// Expenses Dashboard — runs entirely on your iPhone in Scriptable.
// Reads your Notion "Expenses" database directly with your Notion token and shows the dashboard.
// It also checks your bank/card alerts (the "CC Transactions" table, filled by your Google Apps Script):
// each alert is matched to an expense you logged, the card is written onto it, and anything left over
// is shown as "not logged" so you can add it or skip it.
// Nothing goes through Claude or any other server: phone ⇄ Notion only.
//
// Setup (once):
// 1. Scriptable › + (new script) › paste this whole file › name it "Expenses Dashboard".
// 2. Run it. Paste your Notion token (starts with ntn_; the same one your Log Expense shortcut uses),
//    then your Expenses database link (Notion › Expenses › ••• › Copy link). Both are saved in the Keychain.
//    It is stored in the iPhone Keychain.
// 3. Optional: add it to your home screen (Shortcuts › New › Run Script › Expenses Dashboard ›
//    Share › Add to Home Screen), or set Back Tap › Triple Tap to that shortcut.
//
// Home screen widget:
// 4. Long-press the home screen › Edit › Add Widget › Scriptable › pick Small, Medium or Large.
// 5. Long-press the new widget › Edit Widget and set:
//      Script:           Expenses Dashboard
//      When Interacting: Run Script
//    Tapping the widget then opens the dashboard directly in Scriptable — no Shortcuts app in between.
//    (Lock screen widgets: same steps.)
// 6. In your Log Expense shortcut, add Scriptable's "Refresh All Widgets" as the last action,
//    so the widget updates right after you log instead of whenever iOS gets round to it.
//
// Opening the dashboard without the widget:
// - Home screen icon with no app switch: Shortcuts › New › Scriptable "Run Script" › Expenses Dashboard ›
//   tap › and turn OFF "Run In App" › Share › Add to Home Screen. It opens as a sheet over the home screen.
//   If it ever opens blank or closes on its own there, turn "Run In App" back on.
// - Widgets can't run a shortcut without iOS showing the Shortcuts app first, which is why the widget
//   opens Scriptable directly instead (DASHBOARD_SHORTCUT below is empty).

const DATABASE_KEY = "expenses_notion_database"         // your Expenses database ID, asked for on first run
const DEFAULT_DATABASE_ID = ""                           // optional: a database ID to use without asking
let DATABASE_ID = Keychain.contains(DATABASE_KEY) ? Keychain.get(DATABASE_KEY) : DEFAULT_DATABASE_ID
const TOKEN_KEY = "expenses_notion_token"                // same Keychain key as the earlier script
const SETTINGS_KEY = "expenses_dashboard_settings"
const CACHE_FILE = "expenses-dashboard-cache.json"
// Leave empty: tapping the widget opens the dashboard straight in Scriptable (no Shortcuts flash).
// Put a shortcut's exact name here only if you prefer the widget to go through Shortcuts.
const DASHBOARD_SHORTCUT = ""
const DUES = "Credit Card Dues"

const fm = FileManager.local()
const cachePath = fm.joinPath(fm.documentsDirectory(), CACHE_FILE)

// ---------- Notion ----------

// Accepts a Notion link or a bare ID; returns the 32-character ID, or "" if none found.
function parseDatabaseId(text) {
  const clean = String(text || "").split("?")[0].replace(/-/g, "")
  const m = clean.match(/[0-9a-f]{32}(?![0-9a-f])/i)
  return m ? m[0].toLowerCase() : ""
}

// Finds a table you've shared with your Notion integration by its name.
async function findTable(tok, name) {
  try {
    const res = await notion(tok, "POST", "search", { query: name, filter: { property: "object", value: "database" }, page_size: 20 })
    const hit = (res.results || []).find(d => (d.title || []).map(t => t.plain_text).join("").trim().toLowerCase().startsWith(name.toLowerCase()))
    return hit ? hit.id.replace(/-/g, "") : ""
  } catch (e) { log(`search ${name}: ${e.message || e}`); return "" }
}
async function findTables(tok) {
  if (!ALERTS_ID) { ALERTS_ID = await findTable(tok, "CC Transactions"); if (ALERTS_ID) Keychain.set(ALERTS_KEY, ALERTS_ID) }
  if (!BILLS_ID) { BILLS_ID = await findTable(tok, "Card Bills"); if (BILLS_ID) Keychain.set(BILLS_KEY, BILLS_ID) }
  if (!LOANS_ID) { LOANS_ID = await findTable(tok, "Loans"); if (LOANS_ID) Keychain.set(LOANS_KEY, LOANS_ID) }
  if (!POLICIES_ID) { POLICIES_ID = await findTable(tok, "Insurance"); if (POLICIES_ID) Keychain.set(POLICIES_KEY, POLICIES_ID) }
  if (!PEOPLE_ID) { PEOPLE_ID = await findTable(tok, "People"); if (PEOPLE_ID) Keychain.set(PEOPLE_KEY, PEOPLE_ID) }
  if (!DIARY_ID) { DIARY_ID = await findTable(tok, "Money Diary"); if (DIARY_ID) Keychain.set(DIARY_KEY, DIARY_ID) }
}
// Your cards: the options of the Card column in Expenses, written like "HDFC Regalia ••1234".
const CARDS_KEY = "expenses_card_names"
const shortCard = n => String(n).replace(/\s*•+\s*\d+\s*$/, "").trim() || n
function setCards(names) {
  CREDIT_CARDS.splice(0, CREDIT_CARDS.length, ...names)
  for (const k of Object.keys(CARD_SHORT)) delete CARD_SHORT[k]
  for (const k of Object.keys(MARKUP_DEFAULTS)) { delete MARKUP_DEFAULTS[k]; delete MARKUP_NAMES[k] }
  for (const n of names) { CARD_SHORT[n] = shortCard(n); MARKUP_NAMES[n] = shortCard(n); MARKUP_DEFAULTS[n] = /scapia/i.test(n) ? 0 : 3.5 }
}
try { setCards(Keychain.contains(CARDS_KEY) ? JSON.parse(Keychain.get(CARDS_KEY)) : []) } catch (e) {}
async function loadCards(tok) {
  try {
    const db = await notion(tok, "GET", `databases/${DATABASE_ID}`)
    rememberAuto((db.description || []).map(t => t.plain_text).join(""))
    const names = ((db.properties && db.properties.Card && db.properties.Card.select && db.properties.Card.select.options) || []).map(o => o.name).filter(n => !/^example card/i.test(n))
    setCards(names); Keychain.set(CARDS_KEY, JSON.stringify(names))
  } catch (e) { log(`cards: ${e.message || e}`) }
}

async function databaseId(forceAsk = false) {
  if (!forceAsk && DATABASE_ID) return DATABASE_ID
  if (!forceAsk) {
    const tok = Keychain.contains(TOKEN_KEY) ? Keychain.get(TOKEN_KEY) : ""
    const found = tok ? await findTable(tok, "Expenses") : ""
    if (found) { Keychain.set(DATABASE_KEY, found); DATABASE_ID = found; return found }
  }
  while (true) {
    const a = new Alert()
    a.title = "Expenses database"
    a.message = "Paste the link to your Expenses database (Notion › Expenses › ••• › Copy link), or just its 32-character ID."
    a.addTextField("notion.so/Expenses-…")
    a.addAction("Save")
    a.addCancelAction("Cancel")
    if (await a.presentAlert() === -1) return ""
    const id = parseDatabaseId(a.textFieldValue(0))
    if (id) { Keychain.set(DATABASE_KEY, id); DATABASE_ID = id; return id }
    const e = new Alert()
    e.title = "That doesn't look like a database link"
    e.message = "Copy the link from the Expenses page itself (••• › Copy link) and paste it again."
    e.addAction("Try again")
    await e.presentAlert()
  }
}

async function token(forceAsk = false) {
  if (!forceAsk && Keychain.contains(TOKEN_KEY)) return Keychain.get(TOKEN_KEY)
  const a = new Alert()
  a.title = "Notion token"
  a.message = "Paste your Notion integration token (starts with ntn_). It's saved in this iPhone's Keychain and only sent to Notion."
  a.addSecureTextField("ntn_…")
  a.addAction("Save")
  a.addCancelAction("Cancel")
  if (await a.presentAlert() === -1) return null
  const t = a.textFieldValue(0).trim()
  if (t) Keychain.set(TOKEN_KEY, t)
  return t || null
}

const text = p => (p?.title || p?.rich_text || []).map(t => t.plain_text).join("")

// Returns rows in the shape the dashboard page expects.
async function fetchExpenses(tok, sinceISO = null, opts = {}) {
  const rows = []
  let cursor = null, pages = 0
  do {
    if (!DATABASE_ID) throw Object.assign(new Error("No Expenses database saved yet. Tap Retry to add its link."), { code: "no_database" })
    const req = new Request(`https://api.notion.com/v1/databases/${DATABASE_ID}/query`)
    req.method = "POST"
    req.timeoutInterval = 30
    req.headers = {
      "Authorization": `Bearer ${tok}`,
      "Notion-Version": "2022-06-28",
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
    }
    const body = { page_size: 100 }
    if (cursor) body.start_cursor = cursor
    if (sinceISO) body.filter = { property: "Date", date: { on_or_after: sinceISO } }
    if (opts.filter) body.filter = opts.filter
    if (opts.sorts) body.sorts = opts.sorts
    if (opts.max) body.page_size = Math.min(100, opts.max)
    req.body = JSON.stringify(body)
    const res = await req.loadJSON()
    if (res.object === "error") {
      const err = new Error(res.message || "Notion returned an error.")
      err.status = res.status
      err.code = res.code
      throw err
    }
    for (const page of res.results || []) {
      const p = page.properties || {}
      rows.push({
        id: page.id,
        url: page.url,
        "Event": p.Event?.select?.name || "",
        "Created": page.created_time,
        "date:Date:start": p.Date?.date?.start || null,
        "Amount": p.Amount?.number ?? 0,
        "Category": p.Category?.select?.name || "Other",
        "Payment Method": p["Payment Method"]?.select?.name || "",
        "Merchant": text(p.Merchant),
        "Notes": text(p.Notes),
        "Card": p.Card?.select?.name || "",
        "Alerts": (p.Alerts?.relation || []).map(r => r.id),
        "Owed": p.Owed?.number || 0,
        "Owed by": text(p["Owed by"]),
        "Paid back": !!p["Paid back"]?.checkbox,
        "Edited": page.last_edited_time,
        "Logged by": p["Logged by"]?.select?.name || "",
      })
    }
    cursor = res.has_more && !(opts.max && rows.length >= opts.max) ? res.next_cursor : null
  } while (cursor && ++pages < 100)
  return rows
}

// The chart library is downloaded once and kept on this phone, so the dashboard
// never waits on the internet to appear. Without it, everything but the charts still shows.
const CHART_FILE = "chartjs-4.4.1.min.js"
async function chartLibrary() {
  const p = fm.joinPath(fm.documentsDirectory(), CHART_FILE)
  try { if (fm.fileExists(p)) return fm.readString(p) || "" } catch (e) {}
  try {
    const r = new Request("https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/Chart.js/4.4.1/chart.umd.min.js")
    r.timeoutInterval = 8
    const js = await r.loadString()
    if (js && js.length > 50000 && js.includes("Chart")) { fm.writeString(p, js); return js }
  } catch (e) {}
  return ""
}

// ---------- one-off events (event mode) ----------
// An expense with a value in the Notion "Event" column is a one-off (a trip, a renovation):
// it is shown separately and kept out of monthly spending, budget and trends.
// While event mode is on, every new expense is tagged with the event's name automatically.
// (In the code the active event is still called "trip"; users see "event mode".)

const EVENTS_KEY = "expenses_events_state"

// Event icons. Saved choices win; otherwise the name decides; "tag" is the neutral fallback.
const KINDS_KEY = "expenses_event_kinds"
const KIND_WORDS = [
  ["trip", /\b(trip|travel|holiday|vacation|tour|flight|getaway|honeymoon)\b/i],
  ["home", /\b(home|house|flat|apartment|renovat\w*|repair\w*|maintenance|plumb\w*|paint\w*|furniture|interiors?|electrical)\b/i],
  ["celebration", /\b(wedding|marriage|shaadi|party|birthday|anniversary|festival|diwali|holi|christmas|gifts?)\b/i],
  ["health", /\b(hospital|medical|health|surgery|dental|dentist|doctor|treatment)\b/i],
  ["vehicle", /\b(car|bike|vehicle|scooter|motorcycle)\b/i],
  ["education", /\b(course|education|school|college|university|exam|fees|tuition|cfa|mba)\b/i],
]
const KIND_SYMBOLS = { trip: "airplane", home: "house", celebration: "gift", health: "cross.case", vehicle: "car", education: "graduationcap", tag: "tag" }
const guessKind = name => (KIND_WORDS.find(k => k[1].test(name || "")) || [""])[0]
function readKinds() { try { return Keychain.contains(KINDS_KEY) ? JSON.parse(Keychain.get(KINDS_KEY)) || {} : {} } catch (e) { return {} } }
function saveKinds(k) { Keychain.set(KINDS_KEY, JSON.stringify(k)) }
const kindFor = name => readKinds()[name] || guessKind(name) || "tag"
function symbolImage(kind, size) {
  try {
    const s = SFSymbol.named(KIND_SYMBOLS[kind] || "tag") || SFSymbol.named("tag")
    s.applyFont(Font.semiboldSystemFont(size))
    return s.image
  } catch (e) { return null }
}
// An SF Symbol followed by text, in one row. Falls back to text alone.
function iconLine(stack, kind, text, size, color) {
  const row = stack.addStack()
  row.centerAlignContent()
  const img = symbolImage(kind, size)
  if (img) {
    const i = row.addImage(img)
    i.imageSize = new Size(size + 2, size + 2)
    i.tintColor = color
    row.addSpacer(4)
  }
  const t = row.addText(text)
  t.font = Font.semiboldSystemFont(size); t.textColor = color; t.lineLimit = 1; t.minimumScaleFactor = 0.7
  return row
}

function readEvents() {
  try {
    const s = Keychain.contains(EVENTS_KEY) ? JSON.parse(Keychain.get(EVENTS_KEY)) : null
    return { active: (s && s.active) || null, excluded: (s && s.excluded) || {} }
  } catch (e) { return { active: null, excluded: {} } }
}
function saveEvents(s) { Keychain.set(EVENTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(s)) }
// Notion option names can't contain commas.
const cleanEventName = n => String(n || "").replace(/,/g, " ").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().slice(0, 60)

async function notion(tok, method, path, body) {
  const req = new Request(`https://api.notion.com/v1/${path}`)
  req.method = method
  req.timeoutInterval = 30
  req.headers = { "Authorization": `Bearer ${tok}`, "Notion-Version": "2022-06-28", "Content-Type": "application/json" }
  if (body) req.body = JSON.stringify(body)
  if (body && body.archived) forceFull = true   // something was deleted: re-read everything next time
  const res = await req.loadJSON()
  if (res && res.object === "error") throw Object.assign(new Error(res.message || "Notion returned an error."), { code: res.code, status: res.status })
  return res
}

// Adds an "Event" column (Select) to the Expenses table the first time it's needed.
async function ensureEventColumn(tok) {
  const flag = "expenses_event_column_" + DATABASE_ID
  if (Keychain.contains(flag)) return
  const db = await notion(tok, "GET", `databases/${DATABASE_ID}`)
  const existing = db.properties && db.properties.Event
  if (existing && existing.type !== "select") {
    throw Object.assign(new Error("Your Expenses table already has a column called Event that isn't a Select. Rename that column in Notion, then try again."), { code: "event_column" })
  }
  if (!existing) await notion(tok, "PATCH", `databases/${DATABASE_ID}`, { properties: { Event: { select: {} } } })
  Keychain.set(flag, "1")
}

async function setEvent(tok, pageId, name) {
  await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${pageId}`, { properties: { Event: { select: name ? { name } : null } } })
}

// While event mode is on, tag every expense logged since it started (unless removed by hand).
async function autoTag(tok, rows) {
  const st = readEvents()
  if (!st.active) return 0
  let n = 0
  for (const r of rows) {
    if (r.Event || !r.id || st.excluded[r.id]) continue
    if (String(r.Created || "") < st.active.since) continue
    await setEvent(tok, r.id, st.active.name)
    r.Event = st.active.name
    n++
  }
  return n
}

function readCache() {
  try { return fm.fileExists(cachePath) ? JSON.parse(fm.readString(cachePath)) : null } catch (e) { return null }
}
function writeCache(rows, fullAt) {
  try {
    const prev = fullAt ? null : readCache()
    fm.writeString(cachePath, JSON.stringify({ at: Date.now(), syncAt: Date.now(), fullAt: fullAt || (prev && prev.db === DATABASE_ID && prev.fullAt) || 0, db: DATABASE_ID, rows }))
  } catch (e) {}
}
// Expenses: only what changed since the last refresh (plus a 5-minute overlap), merged into the copy on this phone.
// Everything is re-read every 6 hours, when you tap Refresh, or after something was deleted, so removals show up too.
const FULL_EVERY = 6 * 36e5
let forceFull = false, refreshFull = false
async function loadExpenses(tok, full) {
  const c = readCache()
  const ok = !full && !forceFull && c && Array.isArray(c.rows) && c.db === DATABASE_ID && c.fullAt && c.syncAt && Date.now() - c.fullAt < FULL_EVERY && c.rows.every(r => r.Edited)
  if (!ok) { forceFull = false; return { rows: await fetchExpenses(tok), fullAt: Date.now(), changed: null } }
  const since = new Date(c.syncAt - 5 * 6e4).toISOString()
  const changed = await fetchExpenses(tok, null, { filter: { timestamp: "last_edited_time", last_edited_time: { on_or_after: since } } })
  const byId = new Map(c.rows.map(r => [r.id, r]))
  for (const r of changed) byId.set(r.id, r)
  return { rows: [...byId.values()], fullAt: c.fullAt, changed: changed.length }
}
function settings() {
  try { return Keychain.contains(SETTINGS_KEY) ? JSON.parse(Keychain.get(SETTINGS_KEY)) : {} } catch (e) { return {} }
}

function friendly(err) {
  if (err.code === "unauthorized") return "Notion rejected the token. Tap Retry to enter it again."
  if (err.code === "event_column") return err.message
  if (err.code === "restricted_resource") return "Your Notion key isn't allowed to change the Expenses table. On the Notion integrations page, tick Update content and Insert content."
  if (err.code === "validation_error") return "Notion didn't recognise that database ID. Tap Retry to paste the Expenses link again."
  if (err.code === "no_database" || err.code === "no_token") return err.message
  if (err.code === "object_not_found") return "Can't open your Expenses table. Either the saved link is wrong (tap Retry to paste it again) or your Notion key isn't connected: in Notion open the Expense Tracker page › ••• › Connections and add it."
  if (err.code === "rate_limited") return "Notion is rate-limiting requests. Try again in a minute."
  return err.message || "Check your internet connection and try again."
}

// ---------- card & bank alerts: match them to what you logged ----------
// Your Google Apps Script puts every bank/card alert into the Notion table "CC Transactions".
// Each time the dashboard opens it pairs those alerts with the expenses you logged by hand:
// same amount (within ₹1), within 2 days, preferring the same payment method, merchant and time.
// A match writes the card onto the expense (Card column) and links the two (Alerts column).
// Alerts left over are payments you haven't logged; you add them or mark them "not an expense".
// Nothing goes through Claude: phone ⇄ Notion only.

const CREDIT_CARDS = []   // filled from the Card column of your Expenses table
const CARD_SHORT = {}
const DAY_MS = 864e5

// Calendar day (yyyy-mm-dd) in India for a timestamp.
const istDay = ms => new Date(ms + 330 * 6e4).toISOString().slice(0, 10)
const dayNum = iso => Math.round(Date.parse(String(iso).slice(0, 10) + "T00:00:00Z") / DAY_MS)

// Which payment methods you might have picked for an alert's channel.
function methodFits(channel, method) {
  if (!method) return true
  switch (channel) {
    case "Card": return method === "Credit Card"
    case "UPI on credit card": return method === "Credit Card" || method === "UPI"
    case "UPI from bank a/c": return method === "UPI"
    case "Other bank debit": return method === "Debit Card" || method === "UPI"
    default: return method !== "Cash"   // auto-debits, charges, cheques, bill payments
  }
}

const WORD_SKIP = new Set(["the", "and", "pvt", "ltd", "india", "private", "limited", "services", "online", "order", "via", "upi", "payment", "store", "stores", "food", "co"])
const words = s => new Set(String(s || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9 ]+/g, " ").split(/\s+/).filter(w => w.length >= 3 && !WORD_SKIP.has(w)))
const norm = s => String(s || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "")
function sameName(a, b) {
  const A = words(a), B = words(b)
  for (const w of A) for (const v of B) if (w === v || (w.length >= 4 && v.length >= 4 && (w.startsWith(v) || v.startsWith(w)))) return true
  return false
}

// e: { id, date, amount, payment, category, merchant, notes, created, alertIds, card }
// a: { id, time, day, amount, card, channel, type, merchant, ignored, expenseIds, status }
// Returns { links: [{ expenseId, alertIds, card }], open: [alerts not logged], noCard: [card expenses with no alert] }
function matchAlerts(expenses, alerts, opts = {}) {
  const fromDay = opts.fromDay || null
  const nowMs = opts.now || Date.now()
  // Foreign-currency alerts carry an estimated rupee amount, so they match within 3% instead of 0.5%.
  const tol = (amt, fx) => Math.max(1, amt * (fx ? 0.03 : 0.005))
  const isDues = e => e.category === "Credit Card Dues"

  // A spend cancelled by a reversal/refund of the same amount on the same card isn't flagged.
  const cancelled = new Set()
  const backs = alerts.filter(a => a.type === "Reversal" || a.type === "Refund")
  for (const b of backs) {
    const s = alerts.find(a => a.type === "Spend" && !cancelled.has(a.id) && a.card === b.card && Math.abs(a.amount - b.amount) <= 0.01 && b.time - a.time >= -36e5 && b.time - a.time <= 14 * DAY_MS)
    if (s) { cancelled.add(s.id); cancelled.add(b.id) }
  }

  const linkedAlert = new Set(alerts.filter(a => a.expenseIds && a.expenseIds.length).map(a => a.id))
  for (const e of expenses) for (const id of e.alertIds || []) linkedAlert.add(id)
  const freeA = alerts.filter(a => (a.type === "Spend" || a.type === "Bill payment") && !a.ignored && a.status !== "Duplicate" && !linkedAlert.has(a.id) && !cancelled.has(a.id) && (!fromDay || a.day >= fromDay))
  const freeE = expenses.filter(e => !(e.alertIds && e.alertIds.length) && e.payment !== "Cash" && e.date && (!fromDay || e.date >= shiftDay(fromDay, -2)))

  const fits = (e, a) => (a.type === "Bill payment") === isDues(e)
  function score(e, list) {
    const amt = list.reduce((s, a) => s + a.amount, 0)
    const diff = Math.abs(e.amount - amt)
    if (diff > tol(Math.max(e.amount, amt), list.some(a => a.fx))) return null
    let worst = 0, pen = 0, named = false
    for (const a of list) {
      if (!fits(e, a)) return null
      const dd = Math.abs(dayNum(e.date) - dayNum(a.day))
      if (dd > 2) return null
      worst = Math.max(worst, dd)
      if (!methodFits(a.channel, e.payment)) pen += 1.2
      if (sameName(e.merchant + " " + e.notes, a.merchant)) named = true
      // Logged on the day it happened: the closer the logging time is to the alert, the likelier.
      if (e.created && istDay(Date.parse(e.created)) === e.date && a.day === e.date) pen += Math.min(1, Math.abs(Date.parse(e.created) - a.time) / (12 * 36e5)) * 0.6
    }
    const fx = list.some(a => a.fx)
    return worst + pen / list.length + (fx ? diff / Math.max(amt, 1) * 20 : diff * 0.2) - (named ? 0.7 : 0) + (list.length - 1) * 0.4
  }

  const usedA = new Set(), usedE = new Set(), links = []
  const take = (exps, als) => {
    exps.forEach(e => usedE.add(e.id)); als.forEach(a => usedA.add(a.id))
    const credit = als.map(a => a.card).find(c => CREDIT_CARDS.includes(c)) || null
    for (const e of exps) links.push({ expenseId: e.id, alertIds: als.map(a => a.id), card: credit })
  }

  // 1. one alert ↔ one expense, best pairs first
  const pairs = []
  for (const e of freeE) for (const a of freeA) { const s = score(e, [a]); if (s != null && s < 3) pairs.push([s, e, a]) }
  pairs.sort((x, y) => x[0] - y[0])
  for (const [, e, a] of pairs) if (!usedE.has(e.id) && !usedA.has(a.id)) take([e], [a])

  // 2. one payment you split into 2–3 expenses (₹2,545 at Blinkit logged as ₹2,362 + ₹183)
  for (const a of freeA) {
    if (usedA.has(a.id)) continue
    const c = freeE.filter(e => !usedE.has(e.id) && e.amount < a.amount && fits(e, a) && Math.abs(dayNum(e.date) - dayNum(a.day)) <= 1 && methodFits(a.channel, e.payment)).slice(0, 14)
    const hit = combo(c, a.amount, tol(a.amount, a.fx), x => x.amount)
    // only when they're clearly the same shop: all named alike, or named like the alert
    if (hit && (hit.every(e => sameName(e.merchant + " " + e.notes, a.merchant)) || hit.every(e => norm(e.merchant) === norm(hit[0].merchant) && norm(e.merchant)))) take(hit, [a])
  }
  // 3. one expense paid in 2–3 goes (₹80 metro logged once, paid ₹40 + ₹40)
  for (const e of freeE) {
    if (usedE.has(e.id)) continue
    const c = freeA.filter(a => !usedA.has(a.id) && a.amount < e.amount && fits(e, a) && Math.abs(dayNum(e.date) - dayNum(a.day)) <= 1 && methodFits(a.channel, e.payment)).slice(0, 14)
    const hit = combo(c, e.amount, tol(e.amount), x => x.amount)
    if (hit && hit.every(a => norm(a.merchant) === norm(hit[0].merchant))) take([e], hit)   // e.g. two metro rides
  }

  const open = freeA.filter(a => !usedA.has(a.id)).sort((x, y) => y.time - x.time)
  const noCard = expenses.filter(e => e.payment === "Credit Card" && !e.card && !usedE.has(e.id) && !(e.alertIds && e.alertIds.length) && !isDues(e) &&
    (!fromDay || e.date >= fromDay) && dayNum(istDay(nowMs)) - dayNum(e.date) >= 1)
  return { links, open, noCard }
}

// Smallest set of 2–3 items whose amounts add up to target (within tol).
function combo(items, target, tol, val) {
  const n = items.length
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < n; j++) {
    if (Math.abs(val(items[i]) + val(items[j]) - target) <= tol) return [items[i], items[j]]
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < n; j++) for (let k = j + 1; k < n; k++) {
    if (Math.abs(val(items[i]) + val(items[j]) + val(items[k]) - target) <= tol) return [items[i], items[j], items[k]]
  }
  return null
}
function shiftDay(iso, n) { return new Date(Date.parse(iso + "T00:00:00Z") + n * DAY_MS).toISOString().slice(0, 10) }

// The Expenses categories an alert most likely belongs to (used to pre-pick one when you add it).
const ALERT_TO_EXPENSE_CAT = { "Fees & Charges": "Bills & Utilities", "Loan EMI": "Bills & Utilities", "LIC": "Bills & Utilities", "Health insurance": "Health", "Family transfer": "Personal" }
const expenseCategoryFor = a => a.type === "Bill payment" ? "Credit Card Dues" : (ALERT_TO_EXPENSE_CAT[a.category] || a.category || "Other")
const methodFor = a => a.channel === "UPI from bank a/c" ? "UPI" : a.channel === "Other bank debit" ? "Debit Card"
  : /\bdebit\b/i.test(a.card) ? "Debit Card" : CREDIT_CARDS.includes(a.card) || a.type === "Bill payment" ? "Credit Card" : "UPI"


// ---------- Notion side of the alert check ----------
// The Google Apps Script saves the matches every 10 minutes. The dashboard runs the same matching
// (without saving) only so that something you logged a minute ago doesn't show up as "not logged".

const ALERTS_KEY = "expenses_alerts_database"
let ALERTS_ID = Keychain.contains(ALERTS_KEY) ? Keychain.get(ALERTS_KEY) : ""   // CC Transactions (found by name)
const CHECK_CACHE = "expenses-dashboard-check.json"
const BILLS_KEY = "expenses_bills_database"
let BILLS_ID = Keychain.contains(BILLS_KEY) ? Keychain.get(BILLS_KEY) : ""   // Card Bills (found by name)
const LOANS_KEY = "expenses_loans_database"
let LOANS_ID = Keychain.contains(LOANS_KEY) ? Keychain.get(LOANS_KEY) : ""   // Loans (found by name)
const PEOPLE_KEY = "expenses_people_database", DIARY_KEY = "expenses_diary_database"
let PEOPLE_ID = Keychain.contains(PEOPLE_KEY) ? Keychain.get(PEOPLE_KEY) : ""   // People (found by name)
let DIARY_ID = Keychain.contains(DIARY_KEY) ? Keychain.get(DIARY_KEY) : ""      // Money Diary (found by name)
const BILLS_DAYS = 400           // keep about a year of bills, so each month shows its own
const CHECK_DAYS = 60            // same window as the Apps Script
const SYNC_STALE_MIN = 45        // the sync runs every 10 minutes; warn after 45 without a run
const ALERTS_QUIET_DAYS = 3      // warn if no bank/card alert at all for this long

const skipKey = name => String(name || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, " ").trim()

async function fetchAlerts(tok, sinceDay) {
  const out = []
  let cursor = null, pages = 0
  do {
    const body = { page_size: 100, filter: { property: "Date", date: { on_or_after: sinceDay } } }
    if (cursor) body.start_cursor = cursor
    const res = await notion(tok, "POST", `databases/${ALERTS_ID}/query`, body)
    for (const pg of res.results || []) {
      const p = pg.properties || {}
      const start = p.Date?.date?.start || pg.created_time
      const time = Date.parse(start.length === 10 ? start + "T12:00:00+05:30" : start)
      const card = p.Card?.select?.name || "Unknown"
      out.push({
        id: pg.id, url: pg.url, time, day: istDay(time),
        merchant: text(p.Merchant), amount: p.Amount?.number || 0, card, account: p.Account?.select?.name || "",
        channel: p.Channel?.select?.name || (card === "Bank account" ? "Other bank debit" : "Card"),
        type: p.Type?.select?.name || "Spend", category: p.Category?.select?.name || "Other",
        status: p.Status?.select?.name || "", ignored: !!p.Ignored?.checkbox, alwaysSkip: !!p["Always skip"]?.checkbox,
        expenseIds: (p.Expenses?.relation || []).map(r => r.id),
        fx: (c => c && !/^\s*INR\b/i.test(c) ? c : null)(text(p.Currency)),
      })
    }
    cursor = res.has_more ? res.next_cursor : null
  } while (cursor && ++pages < 20)
  return out
}

// ---------- forex markup per card ----------
// Kept on the Card Bills table's description in Notion, so you can change them from the dashboard's
// Settings (or in Notion) without touching any code. New foreign spends use the new number.
const MARKUP_DEFAULTS = {}
const MARKUP_NAMES = {}
const MARKUP_LABEL = "Forex markup (% added to foreign-currency spends, before 18% GST on it):"
function parseMarkups(desc) {
  const out = {}, s = String(desc || "")
  const at = s.indexOf("Forex markup")
  if (at < 0) return null
  const part = s.slice(at)
  for (const [card, name] of Object.entries(MARKUP_NAMES)) {
    const esc = name.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&") + (name === "Scapia" ? "(?!\\s*RuPay)" : "")
    const m = part.match(new RegExp(esc + "\\s*[:=]?\\s*(\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)\\s*%", "i"))
    out[card] = m ? Number(m[1]) : MARKUP_DEFAULTS[card]
  }
  return out
}
const formatMarkups = m => MARKUP_LABEL + " " + Object.entries(MARKUP_NAMES).map(([card, name]) => `${name} ${Number(m[card] != null ? m[card] : MARKUP_DEFAULTS[card])}%`).join(" · ")
// Replaces (or adds) the markup sentence in a description, keeping anything else written there.
function withMarkups(desc, m) {
  const s = String(desc || ""), at = s.indexOf("Forex markup")
  const before = (at < 0 ? s : s.slice(0, at)).trim()
  return (before ? before + "\n" : "") + formatMarkups(m)
}

async function fetchMarkups(tok) {
  const db = await notion(tok, "GET", `databases/${BILLS_ID}`)
  return parseMarkups((db.description || []).map(t => t.plain_text).join("")) || Object.assign({}, MARKUP_DEFAULTS)
}

// Each card's latest bill from the Card Bills table (filled and checked by the Apps Script).
async function fetchBills(tok) {
  const res = await notion(tok, "POST", `databases/${BILLS_ID}/query`, { page_size: 100,
    filter: { property: "Statement date", date: { on_or_after: shiftDay(istDay(Date.now()), -BILLS_DAYS) } },
    sorts: [{ property: "Statement date", direction: "descending" }] })
  const out = []
  for (const pg of res.results || []) {
    const p = pg.properties || {}, card = p.Card?.select?.name
    if (!card) continue
    out.push({ id: pg.id, url: pg.url, card, statementDay: p["Statement date"]?.date?.start?.slice(0, 10) || "",
      dueDay: p["Due date"]?.date?.start?.slice(0, 10) || "", total: p["Total due"]?.number || 0, minDue: p["Minimum due"]?.number || 0,
      difference: p.Difference?.number || 0, result: p.Result?.select?.name || "", details: text(p.Details), seen: !!p.Seen?.checkbox })
  }
  return out.sort((a, b) => a.statementDay < b.statementDay ? 1 : -1)
}

// Your loans (Loans table) and the loan-like debits in your alerts over the last 15 months.
async function fetchLoans(tok) {
  if (!LOANS_ID) return []
  const res = await notion(tok, "POST", `databases/${LOANS_ID}/query`, { page_size: 50 })
  return (res.results || []).map(pg => { const p = pg.properties || {}
    return { id: pg.id, url: pg.url, name: text(p.Loan) || "Loan", type: p.Type?.select?.name || "", match: text(p["Lender match"]) || text(p.Loan),
      emi: p.EMI?.number || 0, day: p["Debit day"]?.number || 1, rate: p.Rate?.number || 0, outstanding: p.Outstanding?.number || 0,
      asOf: p["As of"]?.date?.start?.slice(0, 10) || "", active: !!p.Active?.checkbox,
      closed: p.Closed?.date?.start?.slice(0, 10) || "", start: p.Start?.date?.start?.slice(0, 10) || "",
      rates: parseRates(text(p["Rate history"]), p.Rate?.number || 0), tenure: p.Tenure?.number || 0, basis: /month/i.test(p["Interest basis"]?.select?.name || "") ? "monthly" : "daily",
      docs: (() => { try { return JSON.parse(text(p["Doc figures"]) || "[]") } catch (e) { return [] } })(),
      account: text(p.Account), amount: p["Loan amount"]?.number || 0, disbursed: p.Disbursed?.number || 0, suggested: !!p.Suggested?.checkbox, fromEmail: text(p["From email"]) } })
    // open loans, ones found in your email (to confirm), and ones closed this or last financial year (their interest still counts)
    .filter(l => l.active || l.suggested || (l.closed && l.closed >= finYear(shiftDay(istDay(Date.now()), -366)).start))
    .map(l => l.active || l.suggested ? Object.assign(l, { closed: l.suggested ? l.closed : "" }) : Object.assign(l, { closed: l.closed || istDay(Date.now()) }))
}
async function fetchLoanAlerts(tok) {
  const out = []
  let cursor = null, pages = 0
  do {
    const res = await notion(tok, "POST", `databases/${ALERTS_ID}/query`, Object.assign({ page_size: 100,
      filter: { and: [{ property: "Date", date: { on_or_after: shiftDay(istDay(Date.now()), -460) } }, { property: "Status", select: { does_not_equal: "Duplicate" } },
        { or: [{ property: "Category", select: { equals: "Loan EMI" } }, { property: "Channel", select: { equals: "EMI / auto-debit" } }, { property: "Channel", select: { equals: "Other bank debit" } }] }] } }, cursor ? { start_cursor: cursor } : {}))
    for (const pg of res.results || []) { const p = pg.properties || {}, start = p.Date?.date?.start || pg.created_time
      out.push({ id: pg.id, day: istDay(Date.parse(start.length === 10 ? start + "T12:00:00+05:30" : start)), amount: p.Amount?.number || 0, merchant: text(p.Merchant),
        channel: p.Channel?.select?.name || "", category: p.Category?.select?.name || "", type: p.Type?.select?.name || "Spend" }) }
    cursor = res.has_more ? res.next_cursor : null
  } while (cursor && ++pages < 6)
  return out
}
// Payees you said aren't loans (from "Not a loan" on a suggestion), kept on this phone
const LOAN_IGNORE_KEY = "expenses.loanIgnored"
function loanIgnored() { try { return Keychain.contains(LOAN_IGNORE_KEY) ? JSON.parse(Keychain.get(LOAN_IGNORE_KEY)) : [] } catch (e) { return [] } }
function loanView(loans, loanAlerts) {
  const today = istDay(Date.now())
  return loans.map(l => {
    const pay = loanPayments(l, loanAlerts, loans), lv = learned(l, pay)
    return Object.assign({}, l, { emi: lv.emi, day: lv.day, start: lv.start || l.start, charges: pay.charges, emis: pay.emis, prepayments: pay.prepayments, afterClose: pay.afterClose || [], status: emiStatus(l, pay, today), change: emiChange(l, pay), progress: loanProgress(l, pay, today) })
  })
}

// Card bill payments (from alerts) over the same year, to tell which bills are paid.
async function fetchPayments(tok) {
  const results = []
  let cursor = null, pages = 0
  do {
    const res = await notion(tok, "POST", `databases/${ALERTS_ID}/query`, Object.assign({ page_size: 100,
      filter: { and: [{ property: "Type", select: { equals: "Bill payment" } }, { property: "Date", date: { on_or_after: shiftDay(istDay(Date.now()), -BILLS_DAYS) } }] },
      sorts: [{ property: "Date", direction: "ascending" }] }, cursor ? { start_cursor: cursor } : {}))
    results.push(...(res.results || []))
    cursor = res.has_more ? res.next_cursor : null
  } while (cursor && ++pages < 5)
  return results.map(pg => { const p = pg.properties || {}, start = p.Date?.date?.start || pg.created_time
    return { card: p.Card?.select?.name || "", amount: p.Amount?.number || 0, day: istDay(Date.parse(start.length === 10 ? start + "T12:00:00+05:30" : start)) } })
}

// Marks each bill paid / part-paid: payments on a card go to its oldest unpaid bill first.
// Bill payments you logged yourself (Credit Card Dues with a card, no alert) count too.
function markBillsPaid(bills, payments, rows) {
  const own = rows.filter(r => r.Category === "Credit Card Dues" && r.Card && !(r.Alerts && r.Alerts.length))
    .map(r => ({ card: r.Card, amount: Number(r.Amount) || 0, day: String(r["date:Date:start"] || "").slice(0, 10) }))
  const all = payments.concat(own).filter(p => p.day).sort((a, b) => a.day < b.day ? -1 : 1)
  const byCard = {}
  for (const b of bills) (byCard[b.card] = byCard[b.card] || []).push(b)
  for (const list of Object.values(byCard)) {
    list.sort((a, b) => a.statementDay < b.statementDay ? -1 : 1)
    for (const b of list) { b.paid = 0; b.paidDay = "" }
    for (const p of all.filter(p => p.card === list[0].card && p.day >= list[0].statementDay)) {
      let left = p.amount
      for (const b of list) {
        if (left <= 0 || b.statementDay > p.day) break
        const owe = Math.max(0, b.total - b.paid)
        if (owe <= 1) continue
        const use = Math.min(owe, left); b.paid += use; b.paidDay = p.day; left -= use
      }
    }
    for (const b of list) b.paid = Math.round(b.paid * 100) / 100
  }
  return bills
}

// "Last run …" that the Apps Script writes on the table's description every run.
async function lastSyncRun(tok) {
  const db = await notion(tok, "GET", `databases/${ALERTS_ID}`)
  const desc = (db.description || []).map(t => t.plain_text).join("")
  const m = desc.match(/Last run (\S+)\s*·?\s*(.*)$/)
  return m ? { at: Date.parse(m[1]), note: m[2] || "" } : null
}

const firstDay = rows => rows.map(r => String(r["date:Date:start"] || "").slice(0, 10)).filter(Boolean).sort()[0] || istDay(Date.now())
const checkFromDay = rows => { const f = firstDay(rows), limit = shiftDay(istDay(Date.now()), -CHECK_DAYS); return f > limit ? f : limit }
const toExpense = r => ({
  id: r.id, date: String(r["date:Date:start"] || r.Created || "").slice(0, 10), amount: Number(r.Amount) || 0,
  payment: r["Payment Method"] || "", category: r.Category || "Other", merchant: r.Merchant || "", notes: r.Notes || "",
  created: r.Created || "", alertIds: r.Alerts || [], card: r.Card || "",
})
const alertView = a => ({ id: a.id, url: a.url, day: a.day, time: a.time, merchant: a.merchant, amount: a.amount, card: a.card, channel: a.channel,
  type: a.type, review: a.status === "Needs review", category: expenseCategoryFor(a), fx: a.fx || null })

function syncHealth(lastRun, alerts, now) {
  const newest = alerts.reduce((m, a) => Math.max(m, a.time || 0), 0)
  let warn = null
  if (!lastRun) warn = "The alert sync hasn't run yet. If you set up with a setup link, open it, go to Card alerts and tap Sync now: it shows why. Otherwise finish the Google Apps Script setup on the copy page."
  else if (/^error/i.test(lastRun.note)) warn = `The alert sync hit an error: ${lastRun.note.replace(/^error:\s*/i, "")}`
  else if (now - lastRun.at > SYNC_STALE_MIN * 6e4) warn = `The alert sync last ran ${ago(now - lastRun.at)}. Open script.google.com › your project › Executions to see why it stopped.`
  else if (newest && now - newest > ALERTS_QUIET_DAYS * 864e5) warn = `No bank or card alerts for ${Math.floor((now - newest) / 864e5)} days. If you've been paying as usual, check your bank email alerts and the SMS forwarding automation.`
  return { lastRun: lastRun ? lastRun.at : null, newest: newest || null, warn }
}
function ago(ms) {
  const m = Math.round(ms / 6e4)
  if (m < 90) return `${m} minutes ago`
  const h = Math.round(m / 60)
  return h < 36 ? `${h} hours ago` : `${Math.round(h / 24)} days ago`
}

// What the page shows: payments not logged yet, card expenses with no alert, and whether the sync is healthy.
// Read-only: the Apps Script is what saves matches. Cards from not-yet-saved matches are shown straight away.
async function checkAlerts(tok, rows) {
  if (!ALERTS_ID) throw Object.assign(new Error("No CC Transactions table"), { code: "no_alerts" })
  const fromDay = checkFromDay(rows)
  const loansP = Promise.all([fetchLoans(tok), fetchLoanAlerts(tok)])
  const peopleP = PEOPLE_ID && DIARY_ID ? Promise.all([fetchPeople(tok), fetchDiary(tok)]) : Promise.resolve(null)
  const policiesP = fetchPolicies(tok), insAlertsP = fetchInsuranceAlerts(tok).catch(e => { log(`insurance alerts: ${e.message || e}`); return [] })
  loansP.catch(() => {}); peopleP.catch(() => {}); policiesP.catch(() => {})   // errors are handled below, where they're awaited
  const [alerts, lastRun, bills, markups, payments] = await Promise.all([fetchAlerts(tok, shiftDay(fromDay, -3)), lastSyncRun(tok).catch(() => null),
    fetchBills(tok).catch(e => { log(`bills: ${e.code || ""} ${e.message || e}`); return null }),
    fetchMarkups(tok).catch(() => null), fetchPayments(tok).catch(() => [])])
  // loans: EMI debits and prepayments are a fixed cost, so they never show up as "not logged"
  let loans = null, loanSuggestions = [], loansError = "", loansFound = []
  try {
    const [ls, la] = await loansP
    const tracked = ls.filter(l => !l.suggested)
    loans = loanView(tracked, la)
    loansFound = ls.filter(l => l.suggested && !l.closed).map(l => ({ id: l.id, name: l.name, emi: l.emi, day: l.day, account: l.account, amount: l.amount, disbursed: l.disbursed, fromEmail: l.fromEmail }))
    loanSuggestions = LOANS_ID ? suggestLoans(la, ls, loanIgnored()) : []
  } catch (e) { log(`loans: ${e.code || ""} ${e.message || e}`); loansError = /object_not_found|unauthorized|restricted/i.test(`${e.code} ${e.message}`) ? "no-access" : "failed" }
  let people = null
  try { people = await peopleData(tok, alerts, await peopleP) } catch (e) {
    log(`people: ${e.code || ""} ${e.message || e}`)
    people = /object_not_found|unauthorized|restricted/i.test(`${e.code} ${e.message}`) ? { ok: true, people: [], diary: [], unfiled: [], error: "Can't open the People tables. In Notion, open Friends & Family › ••• › Connections and add your integration." } : (readCheckCache() || {}).people || null
    if (/object_not_found/i.test(`${e.code} ${e.message}`)) { PEOPLE_ID = ""; DIARY_ID = ""; try { Keychain.remove(PEOPLE_KEY); Keychain.remove(DIARY_KEY) } catch (e2) {} }
  }
  let policies = null, policiesError = "", policySugg = []
  try {
    const insAlerts = await insAlertsP, seen = new Set(alerts.map(a => a.id)), all = alerts.concat(insAlerts.filter(a => !seen.has(a.id)))
    const plist = await policiesP
    policies = policyView(plist, all)
    try { policySugg = policySuggestions(plist, insAlerts) } catch (e) { log(`policy suggestions: ${e.message || e}`) } if (!config.runsInWidget) scheduleInsuranceReminders(policies.filter(p => p.active)) } catch (e) {
    log(`insurance: ${e.code || ""} ${e.message || e}`)
    policiesError = /object_not_found|unauthorized|restricted/i.test(`${e.code} ${e.message}`) ? "no-access" : "failed"
    if (/object_not_found/i.test(`${e.code} ${e.message}`)) { POLICIES_ID = ""; try { Keychain.remove(POLICIES_KEY) } catch (e2) {} }
  }
  const loanAlert = a => (loans || []).some(l => matchesLoan(l, a.merchant))
  if (bills) markBillsPaid(bills, payments, rows)
  const skip = new Set(alerts.filter(a => a.alwaysSkip).map(a => skipKey(a.merchant)))
  for (const a of alerts) if (skip.has(skipKey(a.merchant))) a.ignored = true
  const res = matchAlerts(rows.map(toExpense), alerts, { fromDay })
  const byId = Object.fromEntries(rows.map(r => [r.id, r]))
  for (const l of res.links) {
    const r = byId[l.expenseId]
    if (!r) continue
    r.Alerts = [...new Set([...(r.Alerts || []), ...l.alertIds])]
    if (l.card && !r.Card) r.Card = l.card
  }
  const now = Date.now()
  const check = {
    at: now, fromDay, total: alerts.length, pending: res.links.length,
    health: syncHealth(lastRun, alerts, now),
    bills: bills || (readCheckCache() || {}).bills || [],
    markups: markups || (readCheckCache() || {}).markups || null,
    open: res.open.filter(a => !loanAlert(a)).map(alertView),
    loans: loans || (readCheckCache() || {}).loans || [], loanSuggestions, loansError, loansFound, people,
    policies: policies || (readCheckCache() || {}).policies || [], policiesError, policySuggestions: policySugg,
    auto: (() => { try { return autoView(rows, alerts, res.links) } catch (e) { log(`auto view: ${e.message || e}`); return null } })(),
    cardPayments: (payments || []).map(p => ({ card: p.card, day: p.day, amount: p.amount })),
    noCard: res.noCard.map(e => ({ id: e.id, date: e.date, amount: e.amount, merchant: e.merchant, notes: e.notes, category: e.category })),
  }
  try { fm.writeString(fm.joinPath(fm.documentsDirectory(), CHECK_CACHE), JSON.stringify(check)) } catch (e) {}
  return check
}
function readCheckCache() {
  try { const p = fm.joinPath(fm.documentsDirectory(), CHECK_CACHE); return fm.fileExists(p) ? JSON.parse(fm.readString(p)) : null } catch (e) { return null }
}

function alertsFriendly(err) {
  if (err.code === "no_alerts") return null   // card alerts not set up: nothing to show
  if (err.code === "object_not_found" || err.code === "validation_error")
    return "The dashboard can't see your CC Transactions table yet. In Notion open it › ••• › Connections and add the same integration your Expenses table uses."
  if (err.code === "restricted_resource") return "Your Notion integration needs Update content and Insert content ticked."
  return "Couldn't check your bank alerts: " + friendly(err)
}

// "Add a loan document": pick PDFs in Files and email them to yourself tagged [LOAN-DOC]; the Google sync reads them.
// iOS shows the email before it goes, so you tap Send. Password-protected PDFs are flagged first, since Google can't open them.
const MY_EMAIL_KEY = "expenses.myEmail"
async function myEmail(change) {
  const saved = Keychain.contains(MY_EMAIL_KEY) ? Keychain.get(MY_EMAIL_KEY) : ""
  if (saved && !change) return saved
  const a = new Alert(); a.title = "Your Gmail address"
  a.message = "Loan documents are emailed to this address, where your card-alerts sync reads them. It's saved only on this iPhone."
  a.addTextField("name@gmail.com", saved); a.addAction("Save"); a.addCancelAction("Cancel")
  if (await a.presentAlert() === -1) return ""
  const v = a.textFieldValue(0).trim()
  if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(v)) return ""
  Keychain.set(MY_EMAIL_KEY, v); return v
}
// Direct upload: the PDF goes straight to your Google script (published as a private web-app link) and is read at once.
const UPLOAD_KEY = "expenses.uploadLink"
async function uploadLink(change) {
  const saved = Keychain.contains(UPLOAD_KEY) ? Keychain.get(UPLOAD_KEY) : ""
  if (saved && !change) return saved
  const a = new Alert(); a.title = "Upload link"
  a.message = "Paste the link that uploadLink() printed in your Google script (it starts with https://script.google.com and ends with ?k=…). It's saved only on this iPhone."
  a.addTextField("https://script.google.com/macros/s/…/exec?k=…", saved || (() => { const c = (Pasteboard.paste() || "").trim(); return /^https:\/\/script\.google(usercontent)?\.com\/\S+[?&]k=\w+$/.test(c) ? c : "" })()); a.addAction("Save"); a.addCancelAction("Cancel")
  if (await a.presentAlert() === -1) return ""
  const v = a.textFieldValue(0).trim()
  if (!/^https:\/\/script\.google(usercontent)?\.com\/.+[?&]k=\w+/.test(v)) { const e = new Alert(); e.title = "That isn't an upload link"; e.message = "It should start with https://script.google.com and end with ?k= and a key."; e.addAction("OK"); await e.presentAlert(); return "" }
  Keychain.set(UPLOAD_KEY, v); return v
}
const lakh = n => n >= 1e5 ? "₹" + (n / 1e5).toFixed(n >= 1e7 ? 1 : 2).replace(/\.?0+$/, "") + "L" : inr(n)
async function uploadLoanDocs(paths, link) {
  const files = paths.map(p => ({ name: p.split("/").pop(), b64: Data.fromFile(p).toBase64String() }))
  const req = new Request(link); req.method = "POST"; req.timeoutInterval = 180
  req.headers = { "Content-Type": "application/json" }; req.body = JSON.stringify({ files })
  let res, raw = ""
  try { raw = await req.loadString() } catch (e) { throw new Error("Couldn't reach your Google script (no internet, or the link is wrong). Check it under Loans tab › Upload settings.") }
  try { res = JSON.parse(raw) } catch (e) {
    // Google answered with a web page instead of the script's reply: say what it means
    const t = String(raw || "").replace(/<[^>]+>/g, " ").replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    if (/Script function not found: doPost/i.test(t)) throw new Error("Your upload link runs an old version of the script. In Apps Script: Deploy › Manage deployments › pencil › Version: New version › Deploy.")
    if (/authori[sz]ation is required|authori[sz]e|permission/i.test(t)) throw new Error("Your Google script needs permission first. In Apps Script, choose authorizeDocs › Run and allow access, then Deploy › Manage deployments › New version.")
    if (/accounts\.google\.com|sign in|ServiceLogin/i.test(t)) throw new Error("Google asked to sign in, so the web app isn't open to the dashboard. In Apps Script: Deploy › Manage deployments › pencil › Who has access: Anyone › Deploy.")
    if (/not found|404|unable to open the file/i.test(t)) throw new Error("That upload link doesn't exist. Run uploadLink in Apps Script again and paste the new link under Upload settings.")
    throw new Error("Your Google script replied with an error: " + (t.trim().slice(0, 160) || "no reply") + ". Check the execution log in Apps Script (Executions on the left).")
  }
  if (!res || !res.ok) throw new Error((res && res.error) || "Your Google script didn't accept the upload.")
  // the script couldn't tell which loan a document is for: ask, and save it there
  for (const r of res.results.filter(r => r.unmatched && r.entry)) {
    const tok = await token(), loans = ((readCheckCache() || {}).loans || []).filter(l => l.id)
    if (!loans.length) continue
    const a = new Alert(); a.title = "Which loan is this for?"; a.message = `${r.name} doesn't name a loan account I know.`
    for (const l of loans) a.addAction(l.name)
    a.addCancelAction("Skip")
    const k = await a.presentAlert()
    if (k === -1) continue
    const l = loans[k], pg = await notion(tok, "GET", `pages/${l.id}`)
    let docs = []; try { docs = JSON.parse(((pg.properties["Doc figures"] || {}).rich_text || []).map(t => t.plain_text).join("") || "[]") } catch (e) {}
    docs = docs.concat([r.entry]).slice(-4)
    await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${l.id}`, { properties: { "Doc figures": { rich_text: [{ text: { content: JSON.stringify(docs).slice(0, 1990) } }] } } })
    r.unmatched = false; r.loan = l.name
  }
  return res.results.map(r => {
    if (r.locked) return `${r.name}: it has a password, so it couldn't be read.`
    if (r.error) return `${r.name}: ${r.error}`
    const f = r.figures || {}, bits = [f.rate ? `rate ${f.rate}%` : "", f.outstanding ? `outstanding ${lakh(f.outstanding)}` : "", f.fyInterest ? `interest ${lakh(f.fyInterest)}` : "", f.tenureLeft ? `${f.tenureLeft} EMIs left` : ""].filter(Boolean).join(", ")
    return r.unmatched ? `${r.name}: read (${bits}), but it doesn't say which loan it's for. Add its account number to the loan in Notion and upload again.`
      : `${r.name}: read for ${r.loan} (${bits}). Confirm it on the loan's card.`
  }).join("\n")
}
// On this iPhone: read the PDF's text with pdf.js (in a hidden web view), unlocking it with its password if it has one,
// pick out the loan figures, and save them on the loan for you to confirm. No Google script or email needed.
const PDF_PW_KEY = "expenses.pdfPasswords"
const PDFJS = "https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/"
async function pdfTextHere(path, password) {
  const wv = new WebView()
  await wv.loadHTML(`<html><head><script src="${PDFJS}pdf.min.js"></script><script src="${PDFJS}pdf.worker.min.js"></script></head><body></body></html>`)
  const b64 = Data.fromFile(path).toBase64String()
  // The page's text is rebuilt line by line from where each word sits, so table rows ("02-Jul-2026  EMI received  53,783.00")
  // and labels with their values stay together. The script ends with 0, not a Promise: Scriptable can't return a Promise.
  return await wv.evaluateJavaScript(`(async () => { try {
    for (let w = 0; !window.pdfjsLib && w < 50; w++) await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 100))
    if (!window.pdfjsLib) return completion({ error: "nolib" })
    const raw = atob(${JSON.stringify(b64)}), data = new Uint8Array(raw.length)
    for (let i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) data[i] = raw.charCodeAt(i)
    const doc = await pdfjsLib.getDocument({ data, password: ${JSON.stringify(password || "")}, isEvalSupported: false }).promise
    const out = []
    for (let n = 1; n <= Math.min(doc.numPages, 25); n++) {
      const tc = await (await doc.getPage(n)).getTextContent(), rows = []
      for (const it of tc.items) { if (!it.str || !it.str.trim()) continue
        const x = it.transform[4], y = it.transform[5], h = Math.abs(it.transform[3]) || 8
        let row = rows.find(r => Math.abs(r.y - y) <= Math.max(2, h * 0.4)); if (!row) rows.push(row = { y, items: [] })
        row.items.push({ x, w: it.width || 0, s: it.str }) }
      rows.sort((a, b) => b.y - a.y)
      for (const r of rows) { r.items.sort((a, b) => a.x - b.x); let line = "", end = null
        for (const it of r.items) { line += end == null ? "" : (it.x - end > 12 ? "\\t" : it.x - end > 1 ? " " : ""); line += it.s; end = it.x + it.w }
        out.push(line) }
      out.push("") }
    completion({ text: out.join("\\n"), pages: doc.numPages })
  } catch (e) { completion({ error: String((e && e.name) || "error"), message: String((e && e.message) || e) }) } })(); 0`, true)
}
async function readPdfHere(path) {
  const name = path.split("/").pop()
  let saved = []; try { saved = Keychain.contains(PDF_PW_KEY) ? JSON.parse(Keychain.get(PDF_PW_KEY)) : [] } catch (e) {}
  let r = await pdfTextHere(path, "")
  if (r && r.error === "PasswordException") for (const pw of saved) { r = await pdfTextHere(path, pw); if (!r.error) break }
  let tries = 0
  while (r && r.error === "PasswordException" && tries++ < 3) {
    const a = new Alert(); a.title = tries === 1 ? "This PDF has a password" : "That password didn't work"
    a.message = `${name}\n\nLenders and insurers often use your date of birth, PAN or a mix (check the email it came with). It's used only on this iPhone.`
    a.addSecureTextField("Password", ""); a.addAction("Open"); a.addCancelAction("Cancel")
    if (await a.presentAlert() === -1) return { cancelled: true }
    const pw = a.textFieldValue(0)
    r = await pdfTextHere(path, pw)
    if (!r.error) { const keep = new Alert(); keep.title = "Remember this password?"; keep.message = "Saved only on this iPhone, and tried first for your next locked PDF."; keep.addAction("Remember"); keep.addCancelAction("Not now")
      if (await keep.presentAlert() === 0) { saved = [pw].concat(saved.filter(x => x !== pw)).slice(0, 5); Keychain.set(PDF_PW_KEY, JSON.stringify(saved)) } }
  }
  return r || { error: "error" }
}
async function readLoanDocsHere(paths) {
  const tok = await token(), loans = ((readCheckCache() || {}).loans || []).filter(l => l.id), today = istDay(Date.now()), lines = [], leftover = []
  for (const p of paths) {
    const name = p.split("/").pop()
    const r = await readPdfHere(p)
    if (r.cancelled) { lines.push(`${name}: skipped.`); continue }
    if (r.error === "nolib") { leftover.push(p); continue }                       // couldn't load the reader (offline?)
    if (r.error && r.error !== "PasswordException") lines.push(`${name}: couldn't open it here (${r.message || r.error}).`)
    if (r.error === "PasswordException") { lines.push(`${name}: the password didn't work, so it was skipped.`); continue }
    if (r.error || !r.text || r.text.replace(/\s/g, "").length < 40) { leftover.push(p); continue }   // a scan, or unreadable here
    const doc = parseLoanDoc(r.text, { filename: name, mailDay: today })
    if (!doc) { leftover.push(p); continue }                                      // Google reads PDFs a different way: worth a second try
    const lender = lenderOf("", r.text.slice(0, 1500))
    const acct = doc.account || (name.match(/([A-Z]{0,6}\d[A-Z0-9]{5,19})/i) || [])[1] || ""
    let loan = acct && loans.find(l => l.account && (String(l.account).toUpperCase() === acct.toUpperCase() || String(l.account).toUpperCase().endsWith(acct.toUpperCase().slice(-6))))
    if (!loan && lender) { const same = loans.filter(l => matchesLoan(l, lender.name + " " + lender.match)); if (same.length === 1) loan = same[0] }
    if (!loan) {
      const a = new Alert(); a.title = "Which loan is this for?"; a.message = `${name}${lender ? ` (${lender.name})` : ""}`
      for (const l of loans) a.addAction(l.name)
      a.addCancelAction("Skip")
      const k = await a.presentAlert(); if (k === -1) { lines.push(`${name}: skipped.`); continue }
      loan = loans[k]
    }
    // EMIs and part payments the statement lists but your alerts missed: added to CC Transactions, so the loan counts them
    let added = 0
    if (doc.paid && doc.paid.length) {
      const rows = docPaymentRows(doc.paid, await fetchLoanAlerts(tok), loan.account || doc.account || "", (lender && lender.name) || String(loan.name || "").split("·").pop().replace(/\(A\/c[^)]*\)/, "").trim())
      for (const r of rows) {
        await notion(tok, "POST", "pages", { parent: { database_id: ALERTS_ID }, properties: { Merchant: { title: [{ text: { content: r.merchant } }] }, Amount: { number: r.amount },
          Date: { date: { start: r.day } }, Card: { select: { name: "Bank account" } }, Channel: { select: { name: r.channel } }, Type: { select: { name: "Spend" } }, Category: { select: { name: "Loan EMI" } },
          Source: { select: { name: "Statement" } }, Status: { select: { name: "Parsed" } }, "Message ID": { rich_text: [{ text: { content: r.messageId } }] }, Currency: { rich_text: [{ text: { content: "INR" } }] },
          Raw: { rich_text: [{ text: { content: `From ${name}` } }] }, Ignored: { checkbox: true } } })
        added++
      }
    }
    const entry = Object.assign({ file: name.slice(0, 60), lender: lender && lender.name }, doc, { paid: undefined, paidCount: (doc.paid || []).length || undefined, paidAdded: added || undefined })
    const pg = await notion(tok, "GET", `pages/${loan.id}`)
    let docs = []; try { docs = JSON.parse(((pg.properties["Doc figures"] || {}).rich_text || []).map(t => t.plain_text).join("") || "[]") } catch (e) {}
    docs = docs.filter(d => d.file !== entry.file).concat([entry]).slice(-4)
    await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${loan.id}`, { properties: { "Doc figures": { rich_text: [{ text: { content: JSON.stringify(docs).slice(0, 1990) } }] } } })
    const bits = [doc.rate ? `rate ${doc.rate}%` : "", doc.outstanding ? `outstanding ${lakh(doc.outstanding)}${doc.asOf ? ` on ${doc.asOf}` : ""}` : "", doc.fyInterest ? `interest ${lakh(doc.fyInterest)}` : "", doc.tenureLeft ? `${doc.tenureLeft} EMIs left` : "",
      doc.paid ? `${doc.paid.length} payment${doc.paid.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} listed${added ? `, ${added} added that your alerts had missed` : ", all already in your alerts"}` : ""].filter(Boolean).join(", ")
    lines.push(`${name}: read for ${loan.name} (${bits || "some figures"}). Tap Use these on its card.`)
  }
  return { lines, leftover }
}
async function sendLoanDocs() {
  let paths = []
  try { paths = await DocumentPicker.open(["com.adobe.pdf"]) } catch (e) { return null }   // cancelled
  if (!paths || !paths.length) return null
  const name = p => p.split("/").pop()
  // first, read them right here on the phone
  const here = await readLoanDocsHere(paths)
  if (!here.leftover.length) return here.lines.join("\n") || null
  paths = here.leftover
  const pre = here.lines.length ? here.lines.join("\n") + "\n" : ""
  const ask = new Alert(); ask.title = paths.length === 1 ? "Couldn't read this one here" : `Couldn't read ${paths.length} of them here`
  ask.message = `${paths.map(name).join(", ")} couldn't be read here (a scan, or a layout this reader doesn't know). Your Google script reads PDFs a different way, scans included.`
  ask.addAction("Send to Google script"); ask.addCancelAction("Not now")
  if (await ask.presentAlert() === -1) return pre || null
  const locked = paths.filter(p => { try { return /\/Encrypt\b/.test(Data.fromFile(p).toRawString()) } catch (e) { return false } })
  if (locked.length) {
    const a = new Alert(); a.title = locked.length === paths.length ? "This PDF has a password" : "Some PDFs have a password"
    a.message = `${locked.map(name).join(", ")} can't be read by Google while it's locked.\n\nTo send an unlocked copy: open it in Files and enter the password, tap Share › Print, then Share again › Save to Files. Pick that copy here.`
    if (locked.length < paths.length) a.addAction("Send the others"); a.addCancelAction("OK")
    const k = await a.presentAlert()
    if (k === -1) return null
    paths = paths.filter(p => !locked.includes(p))
  }
  // straight to your Google script if you've set up the upload link; otherwise by email
  let link = Keychain.contains(UPLOAD_KEY) ? Keychain.get(UPLOAD_KEY) : ""
  if (!link) {
    const c = new Alert(); c.title = "Send it how?"
    c.message = "Direct upload reads it in seconds, but needs a one-time setup on a computer (see the copy page). Email works now: Mail opens and you tap Send; it's read within 10 minutes."
    c.addAction("Paste upload link"); c.addAction("Send by email"); c.addCancelAction("Cancel")
    const k = await c.presentAlert()
    if (k === -1) return null
    if (k === 0) link = await uploadLink(true)
    if (k === 0 && !link) return null
  }
  if (link) return await uploadLoanDocs(paths, link)
  const to = await myEmail(false); if (!to) return null
  const m = new Mail(); m.toRecipients = [to]
  m.subject = `[LOAN-DOC] ${paths.map(name).join(", ")}`.slice(0, 200)
  m.body = "Loan document sent from the Expenses dashboard. Your card-alerts sync reads it within 10 minutes; the figures then show on the loan's card for you to confirm."
  for (const p of paths) m.addFileAttachment(p)
  try { await m.send() } catch (e) { throw new Error("Couldn't open Mail. Set up the Mail app with an account, or use Share › Send loan doc instead.") }
  return `Sent ${paths.length === 1 ? "it" : paths.length + " documents"}. Your sync reads loan PDFs every 10 minutes; the figures then show on the loan's card for you to confirm.`
}

// Requests from the page: add or skip an alert, pick a card, set what's owed, mark it paid back.
async function alertAction(kind, data) {
  while (refreshing) await sleep(200)
  try {
    const tok = await token()
    if (!tok || !DATABASE_ID) throw new Error("Finish setup first: the dashboard needs your Notion token and database link.")
    if (kind === "alert-skip") {
      const ids = data.ids || []
      for (const id of ids) await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${id}`, { properties: { Ignored: { checkbox: true } } })
      // "Always skip" lives on the alert in Notion, so the Apps Script skips this payee's future alerts too.
      if (data.always && ids[0]) await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${ids[0]}`, { properties: { "Always skip": { checkbox: true } } })
      extraMessage = data.always && data.name ? `Skipped. Payments to ${data.name} won't be flagged again.` : "Skipped. It won't be flagged again."
    } else if (kind === "alert-add") {
      const a = data.alert || {}
      const cat = String(data.category || a.category || "Other")
      const props = {
        "Category Text": { title: [{ text: { content: cat } }] },
        Category: { select: { name: cat } },
        Amount: { number: Number(a.amount) || 0 },
        Date: { date: { start: a.day } },
        Merchant: { rich_text: [{ text: { content: String(data.merchant || a.merchant || "").slice(0, 200) } }] },
        Notes: (n => n ? { rich_text: [{ text: { content: n.slice(0, 500) } }] } : { rich_text: [] })([data.notes, a.fx ? `${a.fx}, rupees estimated` : ""].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")),
        Alerts: { relation: [{ id: a.id }] },
      }
      const method = methodFor(a)
      if (method) props["Payment Method"] = { select: { name: method } }
      if (CREDIT_CARDS.includes(a.card)) props.Card = { select: { name: a.card } }
      await notion(tok, "POST", "pages", { parent: { database_id: DATABASE_ID }, properties: props })
      extraMessage = `Added ${inr(a.amount)} ${a.merchant ? "at " + a.merchant : ""} to your expenses.`
    } else if (kind === "set-card") {
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { properties: { Card: { select: data.card ? { name: data.card } : null } } })
      extraMessage = data.card ? `Tagged to ${CARD_SHORT[data.card] || data.card}.` : null
    } else if (kind === "set-owed") {
      // One update per expense: the people who owe you, each with an amount and whether they've paid back.
      const updates = data.updates || [{ id: data.id, people: data.people || [] }]
      let owedNow = 0, paidNow = 0
      for (const u of updates) {
        const people = (u.people || []).map(p => ({ name: String(p.name || "Someone").replace(/[;₹✓]/g, " ").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().slice(0, 40) || "Someone",
          amount: Math.max(0, Math.round((Number(p.amount) || 0) * 100) / 100), paid: !!p.paid })).filter(p => p.amount > 0)
        const owed = Math.round(people.reduce((s, p) => s + p.amount, 0) * 100) / 100
        const text = people.map(p => `${p.name} ₹${p.amount}${p.paid ? " ✓" : ""}`).join("; ")
        await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${u.id}`, { properties: {
          Owed: { number: owed || null },
          "Owed by": { rich_text: text ? [{ text: { content: text.slice(0, 1900) } }] : [] },
          "Paid back": { checkbox: people.length > 0 && people.every(p => p.paid) },
        } })
        owedNow += people.filter(p => !p.paid).reduce((s, p) => s + p.amount, 0)
        paidNow += people.filter(p => p.paid).length
      }
      extraMessage = data.reason === "paid" ? "Marked as paid back."
        : owedNow ? `Saved. ${inr(owedNow)} is owed to you; only your share counts in spending.` : "Removed the split. The full amount counts as your spending again."
    } else if (kind === "set-markups") {
      const db = await notion(tok, "GET", `databases/${BILLS_ID}`)
      const cur = parseMarkups((db.description || []).map(t => t.plain_text).join("")) || Object.assign({}, MARKUP_DEFAULTS)
      for (const [card, v] of Object.entries(data.markups || {})) if (card in MARKUP_DEFAULTS && Number(v) >= 0 && Number(v) <= 10) cur[card] = Math.round(Number(v) * 100) / 100
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `databases/${BILLS_ID}`, { description: [{ type: "text", text: { content: withMarkups((db.description || []).map(t => t.plain_text).join(""), cur) } }] })
      extraMessage = "Forex markups saved. New foreign-currency spends will use them."
    } else if (kind === "loan-set") {
      const P = Number(data.rate) > 0 ? { Rate: { number: Number(data.rate) } } : {}
      if (data.history) P["Rate history"] = { rich_text: [{ text: { content: String(data.history).slice(0, 1900) } }] }
      if (Number(data.outstanding) > 0) { P.Outstanding = { number: Math.round(Number(data.outstanding)) }; if (data.asOf) P["As of"] = { date: { start: String(data.asOf).slice(0, 10) } } }
      if (Number(data.tenure) > 0) P.Tenure = { number: Math.round(Number(data.tenure)) }
      if (data.basis) P["Interest basis"] = { select: { name: data.basis === "monthly" ? "Equal months" : "Actual days" } }
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { properties: P })
      extraMessage = data.basis && !data.history && !(Number(data.outstanding) > 0) ? `Interest is now worked out on ${data.basis === "monthly" ? "equal months" : "actual days"} for this loan.` : data.rateChanged ? `Saved. ${data.rate}% is used from ${data.rateFromLabel || "that date"}; earlier months keep the old rate.` : "Saved. Loan progress is worked out from your EMIs from here on."
    } else if (kind === "loan-emi") {
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { properties: { EMI: { number: Number(data.emi) } } })
      extraMessage = `EMI updated to ${inr(Number(data.emi))}.`
    } else if (kind === "loan-close") {
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { properties: { Active: { checkbox: false }, Closed: { date: { start: String(data.date || istDay(Date.now())).slice(0, 10) } } } })
      extraMessage = "Loan marked closed. Its interest so far this year stays in the totals."
    } else if (kind === "loan-upload") {
      extraMessage = await sendLoanDocs()
    } else if (kind === "loan-email") {
      const c = new Alert(); c.title = "Upload settings"
      const has = Keychain.contains(UPLOAD_KEY)
      c.message = has ? "PDFs go straight to your Google script." : "PDFs are sent by email unless you add an upload link."
      c.addAction(has ? "Change upload link" : "Add upload link"); if (has) c.addDestructiveAction("Stop direct upload (use email)"); c.addAction("Change email address"); c.addCancelAction("Cancel")
      const k = await c.presentAlert()
      if (k === 0) { const v = await uploadLink(true); extraMessage = v ? "Upload link saved. PDFs now go straight to your Google script." : null }
      else if (has && k === 1) { Keychain.remove(UPLOAD_KEY); extraMessage = "Direct upload off. PDFs will be sent by email." }
      else if (k === (has ? 2 : 1)) { const v = await myEmail(true); extraMessage = v ? `Loan documents will be emailed to ${v}.` : null }
      else extraMessage = null
    } else if (kind === "loan-doc") {
      const P = { "Doc figures": { rich_text: [{ text: { content: JSON.stringify(data.docs || []).slice(0, 1990) } }] } }
      const f = data.fields || {}
      if (f.rate > 0) P.Rate = { number: Number(f.rate) }
      if (f.history) P["Rate history"] = { rich_text: [{ text: { content: String(f.history).slice(0, 1900) } }] }
      if (f.outstanding > 0 && f.asOf) { P.Outstanding = { number: Math.round(Number(f.outstanding)) }; P["As of"] = { date: { start: String(f.asOf).slice(0, 10) } } }
      if (f.tenure > 0) P.Tenure = { number: Math.round(Number(f.tenure)) }
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { properties: P })
      extraMessage = data.used ? "Saved the figures from the document." : null
    } else if (kind === "loan-accept") {
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { properties: { Active: { checkbox: true }, Suggested: { checkbox: false } } })
      extraMessage = "Now tracking it. Its EMIs are kept out of your spending, with a reminder before each one."
    } else if (kind === "loan-dismiss") {
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { properties: { Suggested: { checkbox: false }, Active: { checkbox: false } } })   // kept (inactive) so it isn't found again
      extraMessage = "Removed. It won't be suggested again."
    } else if (kind === "loan-ignore") {
      const list = loanIgnored(); if (data.match && !list.includes(data.match)) list.push(String(data.match))
      Keychain.set(LOAN_IGNORE_KEY, JSON.stringify(list.slice(-50)))
      extraMessage = "Got it. It won't be suggested again."
    } else if (kind === "loan-add") {
      await notion(tok, "POST", "pages", { parent: { database_id: LOANS_ID }, properties: {
        Loan: { title: [{ text: { content: `Loan · ${String(data.name || "EMI").slice(0, 80)}` } }] }, Type: { select: { name: "Other" } },
        "Lender match": { rich_text: [{ text: { content: String(data.match || data.name || "").slice(0, 80) } }] },
        EMI: { number: Number(data.emi) || 0 }, "Debit day": { number: Number(data.day) || 1 }, Active: { checkbox: true } } })
      extraMessage = "Now tracking it. Set the loan type and details in Notion's Loans table if you like."
    } else if (kind === "bill-seen") {
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { properties: { Seen: { checkbox: true } } })
      extraMessage = null
    } else if (kind === "paid-back") {
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { properties: { "Paid back": { checkbox: data.paid !== false } } })
      extraMessage = data.paid !== false ? "Marked as paid back." : "Marked as still owed."
    }
    log(`${kind} done`)
  } catch (e) {
    log(`${kind} failed: ${e.message || e}`)
    extraMessage = null
    await refresh()
    await js(`window.flash && window.flash(${safe("Couldn't save that to Notion: " + friendly(e))})`)
    return
  }
  await refresh()
}


// ---------- friends & family: People and Money Diary ----------
// Two Notion tables on your "Friends & Family" page. The card-alerts sync files bank transfers to people you've
// added; the People tab adds cash, days off and notes, and asks about transfers to people it doesn't know yet.
const PEOPLE_SKIP_KEY = "expenses.peopleSkip"
const HOUSEHOLD = "Household"
function peopleSkip() { try { return Keychain.contains(PEOPLE_SKIP_KEY) ? JSON.parse(Keychain.get(PEOPLE_SKIP_KEY)) || [] : [] } catch (e) { return [] } }
const ppNameKey = s => String(s || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9@. ]+/g, " ").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim()
// A payee that reads like a person ("Sunita Devi", "ramesh@okaxis"), not a business
const BUSINESS_WORDS = /\b(ltd|limited|pvt|private|llp|services|store|stores|mart|enterprises?|traders?|trading|bank|finance|financial|india|foods?|retail|technolog\w*|solutions|hospital|medical|pharma\w*|restaurant|cafe|hotel|motors|agency|agencies|industries|co|corp\w*|company|international|global|systems|payments?|billdesk|razorpay|paytm|phonepe|gpay|amazon|flipkart|swiggy|zomato|uber|ola|airtel|jio|insurance|securities|clearing|mutual|fund|apple|google|netflix|spotify)\b/i
const personish = m => { const s = String(m || "").trim(); if (/^\S+@[\w.]+$/.test(s)) return true; const w = s.split(/\s+/); return /^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z .'-]+$/.test(s) && w.length >= 2 && w.length <= 4 && !BUSINESS_WORDS.test(s) }

async function fetchPeople(tok) {
  const res = await notion(tok, "POST", `databases/${PEOPLE_ID}/query`, { page_size: 100 })
  return (res.results || []).map(pg => { const p = pg.properties || {}
    return { id: pg.id, url: pg.url, name: text(p.Name), group: p.Group?.select?.name || "Other", spend: !!p["Counts as spending"]?.checkbox, names: text(p["Names in bank alerts"]),
      pay: p["Monthly pay"]?.number || 0, payday: p["Pay day"]?.number || 0, leave: p["Paid leave"]?.number || 0, payType: p["Pay type"]?.select?.name || "", rate: p["Rate per day"]?.number || 0, since: p.Since?.date?.start?.slice(0, 10) || "" } })
    .filter(p => p.name)
}
async function fetchDiary(tok) {
  const out = []
  let cursor = null, pages = 0
  do {
    const res = await notion(tok, "POST", `databases/${DIARY_ID}/query`, Object.assign({ page_size: 100, sorts: [{ property: "Date", direction: "descending" }, { timestamp: "created_time", direction: "descending" }] }, cursor ? { start_cursor: cursor } : {}))
    for (const pg of res.results || []) { const p = pg.properties || {}
      const day = (p.Date?.date?.start || pg.created_time || "").slice(0, 10)
      out.push({ id: pg.id, person: (p.Person?.relation || [])[0]?.id || "", day, type: p.Type?.select?.name || "Note", kind: p.Kind?.select?.name || "", amount: p.Amount?.number || 0,
        days: p.Days?.number || 0, source: p.Source?.select?.name || "Manual", notes: text(p.Notes), alert: (p.Alert?.relation || [])[0]?.id || "", expense: (p.Expense?.relation || [])[0]?.id || "" }) }
    cursor = res.has_more ? res.next_cursor : null
  } while (cursor && ++pages < 10)
  return out
}
// People, the diary, and bank transfers to or from people who aren't in your list yet (last 30 days).
async function peopleData(tok, alerts, pre) {
  if (!PEOPLE_ID || !DIARY_ID) return { ok: false }
  const [people, diary] = pre || await Promise.all([fetchPeople(tok), fetchDiary(tok)])
  const filed = new Set(diary.map(e => e.alert.replace(/-/g, "")).filter(Boolean))
  const keys = people.flatMap(p => [p.name].concat(p.names.split(/[,;\n]/)).map(ppNameKey).filter(k => k.length >= 3 && (k.includes("@") || k.includes(" "))))
  const known = m => { const s = " " + ppNameKey(m) + " "; return keys.some(k => s.includes(" " + k + " ") || (k.includes("@") && s.includes(k))) }
  const skip = new Set(peopleSkip()), since = shiftDay(istDay(Date.now()), -30)
  const unfiled = (alerts || []).filter(a => a.card === "Bank account" && (a.type === "Spend" || a.type === "Received") && a.status !== "Duplicate" && a.day >= since &&
      !/EMI|charges|bill/i.test(a.channel) && !a.alwaysSkip && !(a.expenseIds || []).length && !filed.has(String(a.id).replace(/-/g, "")) &&
      personish(a.merchant) && !known(a.merchant) && !skip.has(skipKey(a.merchant)))
    .sort((x, y) => y.time - x.time).slice(0, 30)
    .map(a => ({ id: a.id, day: a.day, amount: a.amount, merchant: a.merchant, type: a.type, ignored: !!a.ignored, expenseIds: a.expenseIds || [] }))
  return { ok: true, people, diary, unfiled }
}

async function addHouseholdExpense(tok, name, amount, day, method, alertId) {
  const props = {
    "Category Text": { title: [{ text: { content: HOUSEHOLD } }] }, Category: { select: { name: HOUSEHOLD } }, Amount: { number: amount }, Date: { date: { start: day } },
    Merchant: { rich_text: [{ text: { content: String(name).slice(0, 200) } }] }, "Payment Method": { select: { name: method || "Cash" } },
    Notes: { rich_text: [{ text: { content: "From Money Diary" } }] },
  }
  if (alertId) props.Alerts = { relation: [{ id: alertId }] }
  return (await notion(tok, "POST", "pages", { parent: { database_id: DATABASE_ID }, properties: props })).id
}
async function createPerson(tok, np) {
  const pg = await notion(tok, "POST", "pages", { parent: { database_id: PEOPLE_ID }, properties: {
    Name: { title: [{ text: { content: np.name.slice(0, 100) } }] }, Group: { select: { name: np.group || "Other" } },
    "Counts as spending": { checkbox: !!np.spend }, Active: { checkbox: true } } })
  return { personId: pg.id, name: np.name, spend: !!np.spend }
}
const PAY_TYPES = ["Monthly salary", "Per day", "Monthly bill", "None"]
let payColumnsOk = false
async function ensurePayColumns(tok) {
  if (payColumnsOk) return
  const db = await notion(tok, "GET", `databases/${PEOPLE_ID}`), have = db.properties || {}, add = {}
  if (!have["Pay type"]) add["Pay type"] = { select: { options: [{ name: "Monthly salary", color: "blue" }, { name: "Per day", color: "green" }, { name: "Monthly bill", color: "orange" }, { name: "None", color: "gray" }] } }
  if (!have["Rate per day"]) add["Rate per day"] = { number: { format: "rupee" } }
  if (Object.keys(add).length) await notion(tok, "PATCH", `databases/${PEOPLE_ID}`, { properties: add })
  payColumnsOk = true
}
const rt = v => ({ rich_text: v ? [{ text: { content: String(v).slice(0, 1900) } }] : [] })

// Requests from the People tab. Each one writes to Notion, then reloads.
async function peopleAction(kind, data) {
  while (refreshing) await sleep(200)
  try {
    const tok = await token()
    if (!tok || !DATABASE_ID) throw new Error("Finish setup first: the dashboard needs your Notion token and database link.")
    if (kind !== "loan-new" && (!PEOPLE_ID || !DIARY_ID)) throw new Error("Can't open the People tables. In Notion, open Friends & Family › ••• › Connections and add your integration, then refresh.")
    if (kind === "person-save") {
      const props = {
        Name: { title: [{ text: { content: String(data.name || "").slice(0, 100) } }] }, Group: { select: { name: data.group || "Other" } },
