        "Counts as spending": { checkbox: !!data.spend }, "Names in bank alerts": rt(data.names), Active: { checkbox: true },
        "Monthly pay": { number: data.pay > 0 ? data.pay : null }, "Pay day": { number: data.payday > 0 ? data.payday : null }, "Paid leave": { number: data.leave > 0 ? data.leave : null },
        Since: { date: data.since ? { start: data.since } : null },
      }
      // drivers, milk, newspaper…: how they're paid; the two columns are added to People the first time they're needed
      if (data.payType || data.rate) {
        await ensurePayColumns(tok)
        props["Pay type"] = { select: data.payType ? { name: data.payType } : null }
        props["Rate per day"] = { number: data.rate > 0 ? data.rate : null }
      }
      if (data.id) await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { properties: props })
      else await notion(tok, "POST", "pages", { parent: { database_id: PEOPLE_ID }, properties: props })
      extraMessage = data.id ? `Saved ${data.name}.` : `Added ${data.name}.${data.names ? " Their bank transfers will be filed within 10 minutes." : ""}`
    } else if (kind === "person-remove") {
      // to Notion's Trash (restorable there for 30 days): the person and their diary entries; expenses they're linked to stay
      for (const id of data.entryIds || []) { try { await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${id}`, { archived: true }) } catch (e) { log(`remove entry: ${e.message || e}`) } }
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { archived: true })
      extraMessage = `Removed ${data.name}.${(data.entryIds || []).length ? ` Their ${(data.entryIds || []).length} diary entr${(data.entryIds || []).length === 1 ? "y is" : "ies are"} gone too.` : ""} Changed your mind? They're in Notion's Trash for 30 days.`
    } else if (kind === "diary-add" || kind === "diary-assign") {
      const who = data.personId ? { personId: data.personId, name: data.name, spend: !!data.spend } : await createPerson(tok, data.newPerson || {})
      const a = kind === "diary-assign" ? data.alert || {} : null
      const type = a ? (a.type === "Received" ? "Received" : "Paid") : data.type || "Note"
      const amount = a ? Number(a.amount) || 0 : Number(data.amount) || 0, day = a ? a.day : data.day || istDay(Date.now())
      let expense = ""
      if (type === "Paid" && who.spend && amount > 0) expense = a && (a.expenseIds || [])[0] ? a.expenseIds[0] : await addHouseholdExpense(tok, who.name, amount, day, a ? "UPI" : data.method, a ? a.id : "")
      const props = {
        Entry: { title: [{ text: { content: `${type} · ${who.name}`.slice(0, 200) } }] }, Person: { relation: [{ id: who.personId }] }, Date: { date: { start: day } },
        Type: { select: { name: type } }, Source: { select: { name: a ? "Bank alert" : "Manual" } }, Notes: rt(data.notes),
      }
      if (amount > 0) props.Amount = { number: amount }
      if (data.kind) props.Kind = { select: { name: data.kind } }
      if (type === "Day off") props.Days = { number: Number(data.days) || 1 }
      if (a) props.Alert = { relation: [{ id: a.id }] }
      if (expense) props.Expense = { relation: [{ id: expense }] }
      await notion(tok, "POST", "pages", { parent: { database_id: DIARY_ID }, properties: props })
      if (a && type === "Paid" && !who.spend && !a.ignored) await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${a.id}`, { properties: { Ignored: { checkbox: true } } })
      if (a && data.remember && a.merchant) {
        const pg = await notion(tok, "GET", `pages/${who.personId}`), cur = text(pg.properties["Names in bank alerts"])
        if (!cur.split(/[,;\n]/).map(ppNameKey).includes(ppNameKey(a.merchant))) await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${who.personId}`, { properties: { "Names in bank alerts": rt([cur, a.merchant].filter(Boolean).join(", ")) } })
      }
      extraMessage = type === "Day off" ? `Day off saved for ${who.name}.` : type === "Note" ? "Note saved." : `${type === "Paid" ? "Payment to" : "Money from"} ${who.name} saved${expense && !(a && (a.expenseIds || [])[0]) ? " and added to your expenses" : ""}.`
    } else if (kind === "loan-new") {
      if (!LOANS_ID) LOANS_ID = await createLoansTable(tok)
      const today = istDay(Date.now()), num = v => ({ number: Number(v) > 0 ? Number(v) : null })
      const props = {
        Loan: { title: [{ text: { content: `${data.type || "Loan"} · ${String(data.lender).slice(0, 80)}` } }] }, Type: { select: { name: data.type || "Other" } },
        "Lender match": rt(data.lender), EMI: num(data.emi), "Debit day": num(data.day), Active: { checkbox: true },
        Rate: num(data.rate), "Loan amount": num(data.amount), Tenure: num(data.tenure),
      }
      if (data.outstanding > 0) { props.Outstanding = num(data.outstanding); props["As of"] = { date: { start: today } } }
      if (data.rate > 0) props["Rate history"] = rt(`${data.rate}% from ${today}`)
      await notion(tok, "POST", "pages", { parent: { database_id: LOANS_ID }, properties: props })
      extraMessage = `Added your ${String(data.type || "loan").toLowerCase()} with ${data.lender}. It's in the Loans tab; EMIs are matched from your bank alerts.`
    } else if (kind === "dayoff-batch") {
      for (const id of data.remove || []) await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${id}`, { archived: true })
      for (const a of data.add || []) await notion(tok, "POST", "pages", { parent: { database_id: DIARY_ID }, properties: {
        Entry: { title: [{ text: { content: `Day off · ${data.name}`.slice(0, 200) } }] }, Person: { relation: [{ id: data.personId }] }, Date: { date: { start: a.day } },
        Type: { select: { name: "Day off" } }, Days: { number: Number(a.days) || 1 }, Source: { select: { name: "Manual" } } } })
      const n = (data.add || []).length, r = (data.remove || []).length
      extraMessage = `${n ? `${n} day${n === 1 ? "" : "s"} off saved` : ""}${n && r ? ", " : ""}${r ? `${r} cleared` : ""} for ${data.name}.`
    } else if (kind === "person-alias") {
      const pg = await notion(tok, "GET", `pages/${data.id}`), cur = text(pg.properties["Names in bank alerts"])
      if (!cur.split(/[,;\n]/).map(ppNameKey).includes(ppNameKey(data.alias))) await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { properties: { "Names in bank alerts": rt([cur, data.alias].filter(Boolean).join(", ")) } })
      extraMessage = `Linked: bills under "${data.alias}" now show under ${text(pg.properties.Name)}.`
    } else if (kind === "diary-skip") {
      const list = peopleSkip(), k = skipKey(data.name)
      if (k && !list.includes(k)) { list.push(k); Keychain.set(PEOPLE_SKIP_KEY, JSON.stringify(list.slice(-300))) }
      extraMessage = "Got it. It won't be asked about again."
    } else if (kind === "diary-delete") {
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { archived: true })
      // an expense the diary made for this entry goes too; one you logged yourself stays
      if (data.expenseId) {
        try { const ex = await notion(tok, "GET", `pages/${data.expenseId}`); if (/From Money Diary/.test(text(ex.properties.Notes))) await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.expenseId}`, { archived: true }) } catch (e) {}
      }
      extraMessage = "Deleted."
    }
  } catch (e) {
    log(`people action failed (${kind}): ${e.message || e}`)
    extraMessage = null
    await refresh()
    await js(`window.setData && window.setData(${safe(latest ? latest.rows : [])}, ${safe(Object.assign({}, latest ? latest.meta : {}, { error: friendly(e) }))})`)
    return
  }
  await refresh()
}


// A Loans table next to your Expenses table, for a copy of the template made before it had one.
async function createLoansTable(tok) {
  const db = await notion(tok, "GET", `databases/${DATABASE_ID}`)
  if (!db.parent || db.parent.type !== "page_id") throw new Error("Couldn't add a Loans table automatically. Copy the Loans table from the Expense Tracker template into your Expense Tracker page, then try again.")
  const opts = names => ({ select: { options: names.map(name => ({ name })) } }), rupee = { number: { format: "rupee" } }
  const res = await notion(tok, "POST", "databases", { parent: { type: "page_id", page_id: db.parent.page_id }, title: [{ type: "text", text: { content: "Loans" } }], properties: {
    Loan: { title: {} }, Type: opts(["Home loan", "Car loan", "Personal loan", "Education loan", "Other"]), "Lender match": { rich_text: {} }, EMI: rupee, "Debit day": { number: {} },
    Active: { checkbox: {} }, Suggested: { checkbox: {} }, Rate: { number: {} }, "Rate history": { rich_text: {} }, Outstanding: rupee, "As of": { date: {} },
    "Loan amount": rupee, Disbursed: rupee, Tenure: { number: {} }, Start: { date: {} }, Closed: { date: {} }, "Interest basis": opts(["Actual days", "Equal months"]),
    Account: { rich_text: {} }, Notes: { rich_text: {} }, "Doc figures": { rich_text: {} }, "From email": { rich_text: {} }, "Reminder sent": { date: {} }, "Late alert sent": { date: {} } } })
  const id = res.id.replace(/-/g, "")
  Keychain.set(LOANS_KEY, id)
  return id
}

// ---------- category learning: what you did before decides what an auto-logged expense becomes ----------
// history: [{ merchant: bank's text, name: what you called it, category, amount }] from expenses linked to bank alerts.
// No model: a vote among your own past choices, so every guess can say why ("Food & Dining: 6 of 6 times for Swiggy").
const LEARN_STOP = new Set(["pvt", "ltd", "private", "limited", "india", "ind", "systems", "system", "online", "order", "payments", "payment", "services", "service", "the", "and", "via", "upi", "pos", "co", "llp", "technologies", "tech", "retail", "marketplace", "priv", "pri", "noida", "mumbai", "bangalore", "bengaluru", "delhi", "boi", "cred"])
const learnKey = s => String(s || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\([^)]*\)/g, " ").replace(/[^a-z ]+/g, " ").split(/\s+/).filter(w => w.length >= 2 && !LEARN_STOP.has(w)).join(" ")
const learnTokens = s => learnKey(s).split(" ").filter(w => w.length >= 3)
// a payee that reads like a person's name (a rickshaw driver, a street stall) rather than a business
const LEARN_BIZ = /\b(enterprises?|traders?|trading|store|stores|mart|foods?|cafe|coffee|restaurant|kitchen|snacks|hub|labs?|medical|pharma|hospital|clinic|motors|auto|services|ltd|pvt|co|kiosk|canteen|shop|bakery|sweets)\b/i
const learnPersonish = s => { const t = String(s || "").trim(); return /^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z .'-]+$/.test(t) && t.split(/\s+/).length >= 2 && t.split(/\s+/).length <= 4 && !LEARN_BIZ.test(t) }
function voteOf(list) {
  const by = {}
  for (const h of list) by[h.category] = (by[h.category] || 0) + 1
  const top = Object.entries(by).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0]
  return top ? { category: top[0], n: top[1], of: list.length } : null
}
const topName = list => { const by = {}; for (const h of list) if (h.name) by[h.name] = (by[h.name] || 0) + 1; const t = Object.entries(by).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0]; return t ? t[0] : "" }
// guess: the category the alert parser's rules would give (fallback when you've never paid this merchant)
function learnCategory(history, alert, guess) {
  const key = learnKey(alert.merchant), toks = learnTokens(alert.merchant)
  const say = (v, how, conf) => ({ category: v.category, confidence: conf, reason: `${how}: ${v.n} of ${v.of} time${v.of === 1 ? "" : "s"}`, name: "" })
  // 1. the same merchant as before
  const same = key ? history.filter(h => learnKey(h.merchant) === key) : []
  if (same.length) {
    const v = voteOf(same), share = v.n / v.of
    const conf = share >= 0.75 && v.n >= 2 ? "high" : share >= 0.6 && (v.n >= 2 || v.category === guess) ? "medium" : "low"
    const out = say(v, `You chose ${v.category} for this merchant`, conf)
    out.name = topName(same)
    return out
  }
  // 2. a close variant: shares a distinctive word ("SWIGGY INSTAMART" after "SWIGGY")
  if (toks.length) {
    const near = history.filter(h => { const t = learnTokens(h.merchant); return t.some(w => w.length >= 4 && toks.includes(w)) })
    if (near.length) { const v = voteOf(near); const out = say(v, `You chose ${v.category} for similar merchants`, v.n / v.of >= 0.75 && v.n >= 2 ? "medium" : "low"); out.name = ""; return out }
  }
  // 3. the built-in rules, when they recognise the merchant (Uber → Travel, Netflix → Subscriptions)
  if (guess && guess !== "Other" && guess !== "Personal") return { category: guess, confidence: "medium", reason: "From the merchant's name", name: "" }
  // 4. a person's name you've never paid: what your other payments to individuals of about this amount were
  if ((guess === "Personal" || learnPersonish(alert.merchant)) && alert.amount > 0) {
    const ppl = history.filter(h => (h.guess === "Personal" || learnPersonish(h.merchant)) && h.amount > 0)
      .map(h => ({ h, d: Math.abs(Math.log(h.amount / alert.amount)) })).sort((a, b) => a.d - b.d).slice(0, 5).filter(x => x.d < 1.2).map(x => x.h)
    if (ppl.length >= 2) { const v = voteOf(ppl); return say(v, `Payments of about this size to individuals were ${v.category}`, "low") }
  }
  // 5. nothing to go on
  return { category: guess === "Personal" ? "Other" : guess || "Other", confidence: "low", reason: "New merchant", name: "" }
}

// ---------- reading a bank SMS on this iPhone (the same parser the Google script uses) ----------
// Your cards come from the Card column of Expenses ("HDFC Regalia ••1234"); your name (Settings › Logging) keeps
// transfers between your own accounts out.
function makeSmsParser(cardNames, myName) {
const MY_CARDS = (cardNames || []).map(n => { const d = (String(n).match(/(\d{4,5})\s*$/) || [])[1]; return { name: n, digits: d ? [d] : [] } })
const MY_NAME = myName || "", FAMILY = []
const BANK_WORDS = [
  [/scapia|federal/i, /scapia|federal bank/i], [/hdfc|diners|regalia|infinia|millennia|tata neu/i, /hdfc bank (credit )?card|diners/i],
  [/axis/i, /axis bank (credit )?card/i], [/amex|american express/i, /\bamex\b|american express/i], [/icici/i, /icici bank (credit )?card/i],
  [/\bsbi\b/i, /\bsbi card\b/i], [/kotak/i, /kotak/i], [/idfc/i, /idfc first/i], [/yes bank/i, /yes bank/i], [/indusind/i, /indusind/i],
  [/hsbc/i, /hsbc/i], [/onecard|one card/i, /onecard|one card/i], [/\bau\b/i, /\bau (small finance )?bank\b/i], [/rbl/i, /\brbl\b/i],
]
const CARDS = MY_CARDS.map(c => {
  const w = BANK_WORDS.find(([test]) => test.test(c.name))
  let words = w ? w[1] : new RegExp(c.name.split(/\s+/)[0].replace(/[^A-Za-z]/g, ""), "i")
  if (/rupay/i.test(c.name)) words = new RegExp(`(${words.source})[^.]{0,60}rupay|rupay[^.]{0,60}(${words.source})`, "i")
  return { name: c.name, digits: c.digits || [], words }
}).sort((x, y) => (/rupay/i.test(y.name) ? 1 : 0) - (/rupay/i.test(x.name) ? 1 : 0))   // RuPay variants checked first
const esc = s => s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&")
const SELF_NAMES = (() => { const p = MY_NAME.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).map(esc); return p.length >= 2 ? new RegExp(`\\b${p[0]}\\s+(?:\\w+\\s+){0,2}${p[p.length - 1]}\\b`, "i") : /$^/ })()
const FAMILY_NAMES = FAMILY.length ? new RegExp(FAMILY.map(n => n.trim().split(/\s+/).map(esc).join("\\s+")).join("|"), "i") : /$^/
const IGNORE_BANKS = /$^/   // add a bank here to ignore its alerts, e.g. /\bkotak\b/i
// Your card for a bank ("amex", "scapia", "hdfc"…), or "Unknown" if you don't have one.
function cardByBank(bank) {
  const rupay = /rupay/.test(bank), key = bank.replace(/\s*rupay/, "")
  const w = BANK_WORDS.find(([test]) => test.test(key))
  const c = MY_CARDS.find(c => (w ? w[0].test(c.name) : new RegExp(key, "i").test(c.name)) && /rupay/i.test(c.name) === rupay)
  return c ? c.name : "Unknown"
}

// ---------- run ----------
// ---------- parsing (plain JavaScript, no Google services; tested on real alerts) ----------

const MON3 = { jan: 0, feb: 1, mar: 2, apr: 3, may: 4, jun: 5, jul: 6, aug: 7, sep: 8, oct: 9, nov: 10, dec: 11 }
const num = s => Number(String(s).replace(/,/g, "").replace(/\.$/, ""))
const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2, "0")
const titleCase = s => s.toLowerCase().replace(/\b[a-z]/g, c => c.toUpperCase())

// IST wall-clock parts → epoch ms
function istTime(y, mo, d, h = 12, mi = 0, s = 0) { return Date.UTC(y, mo, d, h, mi, s) - 330 * 6e4 }
function istIso(ms) {
  const d = new Date(ms + 330 * 6e4)
  return `${d.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad(d.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getUTCDate())}T${pad(d.getUTCHours())}:${pad(d.getUTCMinutes())}:00+05:30`
}
function hms(t) {
  const m = String(t || "").match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?\s*([AP]M)?/i)
  if (!m) return null
  let h = Number(m[1])
  if (m[4]) { const pm = /p/i.test(m[4]); if (h === 12) h = pm ? 12 : 0; else if (pm) h += 12 }
  return [h, Number(m[2]), Number(m[3] || 0)]
}
// Accepts "28 Sep, 2026", "02-10-2026", "02-10-26", "2026-07-23", "02/10/26", "28 September 2026".
function parseDay(s) {
  s = String(s || "").trim()
  let m = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/)
  if (m) return [+m[1], +m[2] - 1, +m[3]]
  m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})[-\/](\d{1,2})[-\/](\d{2,4})/)
  if (m) return [m[3].length === 2 ? 2000 + +m[3] : +m[3], +m[2] - 1, +m[1]]
  m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})[\s-]+([A-Za-z]{3})[A-Za-z]*[,\s-]+(\d{2,4})/)
  if (m && MON3[m[2].toLowerCase()] != null) return [m[3].length === 2 ? 2000 + +m[3] : +m[3], MON3[m[2].toLowerCase()], +m[1]]
  return null
}
// Text date (+ optional time) → ms; date-only uses the email's own time when it's the same day.
function when(dayStr, timeStr, fallbackMs) {
  const d = parseDay(dayStr)
  if (!d) return fallbackMs
  const t = hms(timeStr)
  if (t) return istTime(d[0], d[1], d[2], t[0], t[1], t[2])
  const f = new Date(fallbackMs + 330 * 6e4)
  if (f.getUTCFullYear() === d[0] && f.getUTCMonth() === d[1] && f.getUTCDate() === d[2]) return fallbackMs
  return istTime(d[0], d[1], d[2], 12)
}

const hasDigits = (text, digits) => new RegExp(`(?:^|[^\\d])${digits}(?!\\d)`).test(text)
function cardOf(text, digitsHint) {
  if (digitsHint) {
    const byDigits = CARDS.find(c => c.digits.some(d => d.endsWith(digitsHint) || digitsHint.endsWith(d)))
    if (byDigits) return byDigits.name
  }
  for (const c of CARDS) if (c.words.test(text) && (c.digits.length === 0 || !CARDS.some(o => o !== c && o.digits.some(d => hasDigits(text, d))) || c.digits.some(d => hasDigits(text, d)))) return c.name
  for (const c of CARDS) if (c.digits.some(d => hasDigits(text, d))) return c.name
  return "Unknown"
}
function cardFromName(name) {
  if (/american express|\bamex\b|aebc/i.test(name)) return cardByBank("amex")
  for (const [test] of BANK_WORDS) if (test.test(name)) { const c = MY_CARDS.find(c => test.test(c.name) && !/rupay/i.test(c.name)); if (c) return c.name }
  return "Unknown"
}

function cleanMerchant(s) {
  return String(s || "")
    .replace(/\s+(What.s more\?.*|You can convert this.*|Not you\??|Want to.*|Need help.*|Avl\.?\s*(Bal|Limit).*|Available.*|Info:.*|Ref(?:\s*No)?\.?.*|UPI transaction.*|For more.*)$/i, "")
    .replace(/[\s.,:;-]+$/, "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().slice(0, 80)
}
function stripBalances(s) {
  return String(s || "")
    .replace(/(avl\.?|available|avail\.?|total|current)?\s*(bal(ance)?|limit|credit limit|outstanding)\s*[:\-]?\s*(is\s*)?(Rs\.?|INR|₹)?\s*[\d,]+(\.\d+)?/gi, "[hidden]")
    .replace(/\b\d{4,8}\b(?=[^.]{0,30}\b(otp|one[- ]time))/gi, "[otp]")
}

const CATEGORY_RULES = [
  ["Groceries", /blinkit|zepto|bigbasket|big basket|dmart|d mart|instamart|jiomart|kirana|grocery|nature'?s basket|swiggy instamart/i],
  ["Food & Dining", /swiggy|zomato|dineout|restaurant|cafe|caf[eé]|coffee|pret|starbucks|chai|food|dines\*|kitchen|bakery|pizza|burger|canteen|snacks|eats|bar &|bistro|dhaba|monarch/i],
  ["Travel", /uber|ola\b|olacabs|rapido|mmrda|metro|irctc|confirmtkt|makemytrip|goibibo|agoda|indigo|air india|vistara|akasa|spicejet|airline|airways|\btfl\b|trip\.com|trainpal|\bgrab\b|priority pass|\bfuel|petrol|hpcl|bpcl|indian oil|fastag|redbus|cleartrip|booking\.com|airbnb|\brail/i],
  ["Subscriptions", /netflix|spotify|apple|itunes|google|youtube|prime video|hotstar|jiocinema|icloud|openai|chatgpt|claude|anthropic|notion|microsoft|adobe|linkedin|medium|audible/i],
  ["Entertainment", /bookmyshow|district|pvr|inox|cinema|movie|ticket|\bevents?\b|concert|liquor|wine|beer|spirits/i],
  ["Shopping", /myntra|westside|reliance|amazon|flipkart|ajio|nykaa|zara|h&m|uniqlo|decathlon|croma|tata cliq|meesho|tesco|m&s|marks|duty free|avolta|boots|wh smith|make your o|lifestyle|shoppers stop|pantaloons|ikea/i],
  ["Health", /pharm|medical|chemist|apollo|1mg|netmeds|pharmeasy|hospital|clinic|doctor|diagnostic|\blabs?\b|dental|healthcare/i],
  ["Bills & Utilities", /airtel|jio\b|vodafone|\bvi\b|bsnl|electricity|adani|tata power|bescom|mahanagar gas|mgl|broadband|act fibernet|tata play|recharge|\brent\b|society|maintenance|insurance/i],
]
function guessCategory(merchant) {
  if (FAMILY_NAMES.test(merchant)) return "Family transfer"
  if (/\blic\b|life insurance corp/i.test(merchant)) return "LIC"
  if (/policybazaar|star health|hdfc ergo|niva bupa|care health|icici lombard|aditya birla health|bajaj allianz/i.test(merchant)) return "Health insurance"
  if (/bajaj housing|bajaj fin|home loan|\bemi\b|loan|lic housing|pnb housing|tata capital|aditya birla fin|idfc first|hdfc ltd|hdfc limited|icici home|axis finance|kotak mahindra prime|l ?& ?t finance|cholamandalam|shriram fin|muthoot|mahindra fin|home credit|piramal|aavas|indiabulls|poonawalla|hero fincorp/i.test(merchant)) return "Loan EMI"
  for (const [cat, re] of CATEGORY_RULES) if (re.test(merchant)) return cat
  return /^[a-z]+(\s+[a-z]+){1,3}$/i.test(merchant.trim()) && !/\b(ltd|pvt|private|limited|store|stores|mart|services|enterprises|traders|trading|co)\b/i.test(merchant) ? "Personal" : "Other"
}

// Returns a transaction, or { skip: reason }.
function parseAlert(msg) {
  const subject = String(msg.subject || "")
  const from = String(msg.from || "")
  const isSms = /\[CC-SMS\]/i.test(subject)
  const body = String(msg.body || "").replace(/&amp;/g, "&").replace(/&#39;/g, "'").replace(/\|/g, " ").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim()
  // Currency symbols → codes, so "$53.10" reads like "USD 53.10"
  const sym = s => s.replace(/(?:US\s?\$|\$)\s?(?=\d)/g, "USD ").replace(/€\s?(?=\d)/g, "EUR ").replace(/£\s?(?=\d)/g, "GBP ")
  const text = sym(isSms ? body : subject + " · " + body)
  const emailMs = msg.date instanceof Date ? msg.date.getTime() : Date.parse(msg.date)
  const base = { messageId: msg.id, source: isSms ? "SMS" : "Email", status: "Parsed", raw: "" }
  const out = (o) => {
    const r = Object.assign({}, base, o)
    r.amount = Math.round(num(r.amount) * 100) / 100
    if (!(r.amount > 0)) return { skip: "no amount" }
    r.merchant = cleanMerchant(r.merchant) || (r.type === "Bill payment" ? "Card bill payment" : "Unknown")
    r.time = r.time || emailMs
    r.iso = istIso(r.time)
    r.category = r.category || (r.type === "Bill payment" ? "Other" : guessCategory(r.merchant))
    if (r.status === "Needs review") r.raw = stripBalances(body).slice(0, 300)
    return r
  }

  if (isSms && !body) return { skip: "empty test SMS" }
  if (isSms && /^\s*test\b/i.test(body) && !/(Rs\.?|INR|₹)\s*\d/.test(body)) return { skip: "test SMS" }
  if (/\botp\b|one[- ]time password|verification code/i.test(text) && !/spent|debited/i.test(text)) return { skip: "OTP" }
  if (/statement/i.test(subject)) return { skip: "statement" }
  if (/declined|unsuccessful|could not be processed|has failed|(txn|transaction|payment) failed/i.test(text) && !/was successful/i.test(text)) return { skip: "declined or failed" }
  if (/bill is due|payment (is )?due|is due on|bill (has been )?generated|new bill|minimum amount due|upcoming|will be debited|reminder/i.test(text) && !/has been debited|is debited|spent/i.test(text)) return { skip: "reminder" }
  if (IGNORE_BANKS.test(from + " " + (isSms ? body.slice(0, 40) : ""))) return { skip: "not your bank" }
  let m

  // ----- credit card: spends, refunds, reversals -----
  if ((m = text.match(/(?:Rs\.?|INR)\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?) (?:has been|is) debited from your HDFC Bank Credit Card ending (\d{4}) towards (.+?) on (\d{1,2} \w{3},? \d{4})(?: at (\d{1,2}:\d{2}(?::\d{2})?))?/i)))
    return out({ amount: m[1], card: cardOf(text, m[2]), merchant: m[3], time: when(m[4], m[5], emailMs), type: "Spend", channel: "Card" })
  if ((m = text.match(/Spent (Rs\.?|INR|[A-Z]{3})\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?) On HDFC Bank Card (\d{4}) At (.+?) On (\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}):?(\d{2}:\d{2}(?::\d{2})?)?/i)))
    return out({ amount: m[2], card: cardOf(text, m[3]), merchant: m[4], time: when(m[5], m[6], emailMs), type: "Spend", channel: "Card",
      fx: /^(Rs\.?|INR)$/i.test(m[1]) ? null : { cur: m[1].toUpperCase(), amt: num(m[2]) } })
  if ((m = text.match(/transaction reversal of Rs\.?\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?) has been initiated to your HDFC Bank Credit Card ending (\d{4}) From Merchant:\s*(.+?)\s*Date ?Time:\s*(\d{1,2} \w{3},? \d{4})(?: at (\d{1,2}:\d{2}(?::\d{2})?))?/i)))
    return out({ amount: m[1], card: cardOf(text, m[2]), merchant: m[3], time: when(m[4], m[5], emailMs), type: "Reversal", channel: "Card" })
  if ((m = text.match(/Transaction Reversed!?\s*On HDFC Bank CREDIT Card x*(\d{4}) Amt:?\s*Rs\.?\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?) By (.+?)(?: On | Not you|\.\s|$)/i)))
    return out({ amount: m[2], card: cardOf(text, m[1]), merchant: m[3], type: "Reversal", channel: "Card" })
  if ((m = text.match(/Rs\.?\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?) (?:has been )?refunded by (.+?)(?: on .*?)? (?:and )?(?:has been )?adjusted against (?:your )?HDFC Bank Credit Card (?:ending )?(\d{4})/i)))
    return out({ amount: m[1], card: cardOf(text, m[3]), merchant: m[2], type: "Refund", channel: "Card" })
  if ((m = text.match(/PAYMENT OF Rs\.?\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?) (?:HAS BEEN )?RECEIVED TOWARDS YOUR (?:HDFC BANK )?CREDIT CARD ENDING (?:WITH )?(\d{4})/i)))
    return out({ amount: m[1], card: cardOf(text, m[2]), merchant: "HDFC card payment received", type: "Bill payment", channel: "Card bill payment" })

  if ((m = text.match(/Transaction Amount:?\s*INR\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*Merchant Name:?\s*(.+?)\s*Axis Bank Credit Card No\.?\s*X*(\d{4})\s*Date\s*&\s*Time:?\s*(\d{2}-\d{2}-\d{4}),?\s*(\d{2}:\d{2}(?::\d{2})?)?/i)))
    return out({ amount: m[1], card: cardOf(text, m[3]), merchant: m[2], time: when(m[4], m[5], emailMs), type: "Spend", channel: "Card" })
  if ((m = text.match(/Spent\s*(INR|[A-Z]{3})\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*Axis Bank Card no\.?\s*X*(\d{4})\s*(\d{2}-\d{2}-\d{2,4})\s*(\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2})?\s*(?:IST)?\s*(.+?)\s*(?:Avl|Not you|$)/i)))
    return out({ amount: m[2], card: cardOf(text, m[3]), merchant: m[6], time: when(m[4], m[5], emailMs), type: "Spend", channel: "Card",
      fx: /^INR$/i.test(m[1]) ? null : { cur: m[1].toUpperCase(), amt: num(m[2]) } })

  if (/scapia|federal/i.test(text)) {
    if ((m = text.match(/received your payment of ₹\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)/i)))
      return out({ amount: m[1], card: cardByBank("scapia"), merchant: "Scapia repayment", type: "Bill payment", channel: "Card bill payment" })
    const amt = text.match(/Amount\s*₹\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)/i) || text.match(/(?:txn|transaction) (?:of|for) ₹\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)/i)
    const merch = text.match(/Merchant\s*:?\s*(.+?)(?=\s+(?:Not you|Want to|Need help|You'?ve|Regards|Check)|$)/i) || text.match(/₹\s*[\d,.]+ at (.+?) on your Scapia/i)
    const dt = text.match(/on (\d{2}-\d{2}-\d{4}) at (\d{1,2}:\d{2}\s*[AP]M)/i)
    const card = /rupay/i.test(text) ? cardByBank("scapia rupay") : cardByBank("scapia")
    const channel = card === cardByBank("scapia rupay") && /upi|vpa/i.test(text) ? "UPI on credit card" : "Card"
    if (amt) {
      const type = /reversed/i.test(text) ? "Reversal" : /refund/i.test(text) ? "Refund" : /successful|success/i.test(text) ? "Spend" : null
      if (type) return out({ amount: amt[1], card, merchant: merch ? merch[1] : "", time: dt ? when(dt[1], dt[2], emailMs) : emailMs, type, channel, status: merch ? "Parsed" : "Needs review" })
    }
  }

  if ((m = text.match(/your credit card (?:bill )?payment was successful.*?(HDFC Bank|Axis Bank|Scapia|Federal Bank|American Express|Amex|[A-Z][A-Za-z]+ Bank)\s*•+\s*(\d{4,5}).*?amount paid\s*₹\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)/i)))
    return out({ amount: m[3], card: cardOf(m[1], m[2]) !== "Unknown" ? cardOf(m[1], m[2]) : cardFromName(m[1]), merchant: "Bill payment via CRED", type: "Bill payment", channel: "Card bill payment" })
  if ((m = text.match(/American Express Card Bill Payment was successful.*?Payment Amount \(INR\)\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)/i)))
    return out({ amount: m[1], card: cardByBank("amex"), merchant: "Bill payment via BillDesk", type: "Bill payment", channel: "Card bill payment" })
  if (/amex|american express/i.test(text)) {
    if ((m = text.match(/payment of (?:INR|Rs\.?|₹)\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?) (?:was|has been) (?:received|credited)/i)))
      return out({ amount: m[1], card: cardByBank("amex"), merchant: "Amex payment received", type: "Bill payment", channel: "Card bill payment" })
    if ((m = text.match(/spent (INR|Rs\.?|₹|[A-Z]{3})\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?) on your AMEX card \**\s*(\d{4,5})? ?at (.+?) on (\d{1,2} \w+ \d{4})(?: at (\d{1,2}:\d{2}(?:\s*[AP]M)?))?/i))) {
      const fx = /^(INR|Rs\.?|₹)$/i.test(m[1]) ? null : { cur: m[1].toUpperCase(), amt: num(m[2]) }
      return out({ amount: m[2], card: cardByBank("amex"), merchant: m[4], time: when(m[5], m[6], emailMs), type: "Spend", channel: "Card", fx })
    }
  }

  // ----- bank account: UPI, debit card, auto-debits, charges -----
  let bank = null
  if ((m = text.match(/(?:Rs\.?|INR)\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?) is debited from your account ending (\d{3,6}) towards VPA (\S+) \((.+?)\) on (\d{2}-\d{2}-\d{2,4})/i)))
    bank = { amount: m[1], merchant: m[4], vpa: m[3], time: when(m[5], null, emailMs), channel: "UPI from bank a/c" }
  else if ((m = text.match(/Sent Rs\.?\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*From HDFC Bank A\/?C \*?x*(\d{4})\s*To (.+?)\s*On (\d{2}\/\d{2}\/\d{2,4})/i)))
    bank = { amount: m[1], merchant: m[3], time: when(m[4], null, emailMs), channel: "UPI from bank a/c" }
  else if ((m = text.match(/(?:Rs\.?|INR)\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?) (?:is|has been) debited from your HDFC Bank Debit Card ending (\d{4}) at (.+?) on (\d{1,2} \w{3},? \d{4})(?: at (\d{2}:\d{2}(?::\d{2})?))?/i)))
    bank = { amount: m[1], merchant: m[3], time: when(m[4], m[5], emailMs), channel: /e-?mandate|netflix|apple|google|spotify|youtube|prime/i.test(text) ? "EMI / auto-debit" : "Other bank debit" }
  else if (/set up through E-?mandate|successfully paid using your HDFC Bank Debit Card/i.test(text))
    return { skip: "e-mandate notice (the debit card alert is recorded instead)" }
  else if (/(debited|debit|spent|sent|paid|withdrawn)/i.test(text) && /(a\/?c|account|acct|debit card)\b/i.test(text) && !/credit card/i.test(text)) {
    const amt = text.match(/(?:Rs\.?|INR|₹)\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)/i)
    if (!amt) return { skip: "no amount" }
    if (/credited to your (a\/?c|account)|has been credited|received (Rs|INR|₹)/i.test(text) && !/debited/i.test(text)) return { skip: "money in" }
    const who = text.match(/UPI\/P2[AM]\/\d+\/([^\/\n]+?)(?:\/|\s{2}|\s+Not you|$)/i) || text.match(/credited to (?:VPA )?(?:\S+@\S+ )?\(?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z .&'-]{2,40}?)\)? (?:via|on|UPI|Ref)/i) ||
      text.match(/\bto (?:VPA )?(?:\S+@\S+\s*)?\(?([A-Z][A-Za-z .&'-]{2,40}?)\)?\s+(?:on|via|UPI|Ref|\d)/) || text.match(/towards (.+?) on \d/i) || text.match(/;\s*([A-Z][A-Za-z .&'-]{2,40}) credited/)
    const day = text.match(/(\d{2}[-\/]\d{2}[-\/]\d{2,4}|\d{1,2} \w{3},? \d{4})/)
    const tm = text.match(/\b(\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2})\b/)
    const channel = /ACH|NACH|ECS|e-?mandate|mandate|standing instruction|auto ?pay|autopay|\bSI\b/i.test(text) ? "EMI / auto-debit"
      : /charges?|fee\b|penalty|AMB|min(imum)? bal/i.test(text) && !/UPI/i.test(text) ? "Bank charges"
      : /cheque|chq|clg/i.test(text) ? "Cheque"
      : /UPI|VPA/i.test(text) ? "UPI from bank a/c" : "Other bank debit"
    bank = { amount: amt[1], merchant: who ? who[1] : "", time: day ? when(day[1], tm && tm[1], emailMs) : emailMs, channel, status: who ? "Parsed" : "Needs review" }
  }

  if (bank) {
    const name = cleanMerchant(bank.merchant) + " " + (bank.vpa || "")
    if (SELF_NAMES.test(name)) return { skip: "own account transfer" }
    if (/zerodha|\biccl\b|indian clearing|nse clearing|\bbse\b|mutual fund|\bsip\b|groww|kuvera|coin by|\bnps\b|\bppf\b|digital gold|recurring deposit|fixed deposit/i.test(name + " " + text.slice(0, 200))) return { skip: "investment" }
    if (/credpay|cred.?swiggy/i.test(name)) return out(Object.assign(bank, { card: "Bank account", type: "Spend", merchant: "Swiggy (via CRED UPI)", category: "Food & Dining" }))
    if (/cred\.?club|cred club|dreamplug|american express|aebc\d|\bamex\b|scapia|card ?bill|credit card bill/i.test(name))
      return out(Object.assign(bank, { card: /cred|dreamplug/i.test(name) ? "Unknown" : cardFromName(name), type: "Bill payment", channel: "Card bill payment", merchant: /cred|dreamplug/i.test(name) ? "Bill payment via CRED (bank)" : "Bill payment via UPI" }))
    if (/^\s*cred\s*$/i.test(cleanMerchant(bank.merchant))) return out(Object.assign(bank, { card: "Bank account", type: "Spend", merchant: "CRED bill pay (UPI)", category: "Bills & Utilities" }))
    if (/\blic\b|life insurance corp/i.test(name)) return out(Object.assign(bank, { card: "Bank account", type: "Spend", merchant: "LIC premium", category: "LIC" }))
    const r = Object.assign(bank, { card: "Bank account", type: "Spend" })
    r.merchant = cleanMerchant(r.merchant)
    if (r.merchant && r.merchant === r.merchant.toUpperCase() && /\s/.test(r.merchant) && !/\b(LTD|PVT|INDIA|SERVICES)\b/.test(r.merchant)) r.merchant = titleCase(r.merchant)
    if (r.channel === "Bank charges") r.category = "Fees & Charges"
    if (r.channel === "EMI / auto-debit" && /bajaj housing|loan|emi/i.test(text)) r.category = "Loan EMI"
    return out(r)
  }

  // ----- anything else that looks like a card spend: keep it, but ask you to check -----
  if (/credit card|card (no|ending|xx)/i.test(text) && /spent|debited|charged|transaction of/i.test(text) && (m = text.match(/(?:Rs\.?|INR|₹)\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)/i)))
    return out({ amount: m[1], card: cardOf(text), merchant: "", type: "Spend", channel: "Card", status: "Needs review" })

  return { skip: "not a transaction" }
}
// ---------- recognise your cards, accounts and name from bank emails ----------
// Reads the alert emails/SMS already in your Gmail and lists the credit cards (bank + last digits),
// bank accounts, and your name as banks write it. Nothing to type in.
const BANK_NAMES = { hdfc: "HDFC", axis: "Axis", icici: "ICICI", sbi: "SBI", kotak: "Kotak", idfc: "IDFC FIRST", yes: "YES Bank", indusind: "IndusInd",
  hsbc: "HSBC", rbl: "RBL", au: "AU", federal: "Federal", amex: "Amex", "american express": "Amex", scapia: "Scapia", onecard: "OneCard", citi: "Citi", sc: "Standard Chartered", "standard chartered": "Standard Chartered" }
const bankLabel = s => { const k = String(s || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s*bank$/, "").trim(); return BANK_NAMES[k] || (k.length <= 4 ? k.toUpperCase() : k.replace(/\b\w/g, c => c.toUpperCase())) }
function detectAccounts(msgs) {
  const cards = {}, accounts = {}, names = {}
  const addCard = (bank, digits, rupay) => {
    const b = bankLabel(bank)
    const key = b + "|" + (rupay ? "rupay" : digits)
    // the same card written with 4 or 5 digits (Amex shows 4 in some alerts, 5 in others)
    const same = Object.keys(cards).find(k => k.startsWith(b + "|") && !rupay && k.split("|")[1] !== "rupay" && (k.split("|")[1].endsWith(digits) || digits.endsWith(k.split("|")[1])))
    const c = cards[same || key] || (cards[same || key] = { bank: b, digits: rupay ? "" : digits, rupay: !!rupay, count: 0 })
    if (digits && digits.length > c.digits.length && !rupay) c.digits = digits
    c.count++
  }
  for (const m of msgs) {
    const text = (String(m.subject || "") + " · " + String(m.body || "")).replace(/&amp;/g, "&").replace(/&#39;/g, "'").replace(/\|/g, " ").replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    if (/\botp\b|one[- ]time password/i.test(text) && !/spent|debited/i.test(text)) continue
    let x
    const re = (r, f) => { for (const mm of text.matchAll(r)) f(mm) }
    re(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z]+) Bank Credit Card (?:no\.?|number|ending(?: in| with)?|xx+|x+|\*+)?\s*[x*]*\s*(\d{4})\b/gi, mm => addCard(mm[1], mm[2]))
    re(/\b(SBI|ICICI|Kotak|IDFC FIRST|IDFC|IndusInd|RBL|AU|HSBC|Citi|OneCard|YES|HDFC|Axis) (?:Bank )?Credit Card (?:no\.?|number|ending(?: in| with)?|xx+|x+|\*+)?\s*[x*]*\s*(\d{4})\b/gi, mm => addCard(mm[1], mm[2]))
    re(/\b(?:spent|debited)[^.]{0,60}\bon ([A-Z][A-Za-z]+) Bank Card (\d{4})\b/gi, mm => addCard(mm[1], mm[2]))                       // HDFC SMS
    re(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z]+) Bank Card no\.?\s*x*(\d{4})\b/gi, mm => { if (/spent/i.test(text) && !/debit card/i.test(text)) addCard(mm[1], mm[2]) })   // Axis SMS
    re(/\b(?:AMEX|American Express)(?: Card)?(?: card)?\s*(?:ending(?: in)?|\*+|x+|no\.?)?\s*\**\s*(\d{4,5})\b/gi, mm => addCard("amex", mm[1]))
    re(/\bScapia Federal (?:Visa )?Credit Card ending in (\d{4})\b/gi, mm => addCard("scapia", mm[1]))
    if (/Scapia Federal RuPay credit card/i.test(text)) addCard("scapia", "", true)
    re(/\bSBI Card (?:ending(?: with)?|no\.?|xx+)\s*[x*]*(\d{4})\b/gi, mm => addCard("sbi", mm[1]))
    if (/cred\.club/i.test(m.from || "")) re(/\b(HDFC Bank|Axis Bank|ICICI Bank|SBI Card|Kotak(?: Mahindra)?(?: Bank)?|IDFC First(?: Bank)?|AMEX|American Express|Yes Bank|IndusInd(?: Bank)?|RBL(?: Bank)?|AU(?: Bank)?|HSBC|Federal Bank|OneCard)\s*•+\s*(\d{4,5})\b/gi,
      mm => addCard(/amex|american/i.test(mm[1]) ? "amex" : mm[1].replace(/\s*(Bank|Card|Mahindra)\b/gi, "").trim(), mm[2]))
    re(/\b(?:account|a\/c|acct)\s*(?:no\.?|number)?\s*(?:ending(?: in| with)?)?\s*[x*]*\s*(\d{4,6})\b/gi, mm => { accounts[mm[1]] = (accounts[mm[1]] || 0) + 1 })
    re(/\bDear (?:Mr\.?|Ms\.?|Mrs\.?|Dr\.?)?\s*([A-Z][a-z]+(?: [A-Z][a-z]+){1,3})\b/g, mm => { if (!/customer|cardmember|card member|sir|madam|valued/i.test(mm[1])) names[mm[1]] = (names[mm[1]] || 0) + 1 })
    re(/\b(?:Hey|Dear),?\s+([A-Z]{2,}(?: [A-Z]{2,}){1,3})\b/g, mm => { if (!/CUSTOMER|CARDMEMBER/.test(mm[1])) { const n = mm[1].toLowerCase().replace(/\b\w/g, c => c.toUpperCase()); names[n] = (names[n] || 0) + 1 } })
  }
  const cardList = Object.values(cards).sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count)
    .map(c => ({ name: c.rupay ? `${c.bank} RuPay` : `${c.bank} ••${c.digits}`, bank: c.bank, digits: c.digits ? [c.digits] : [], seen: c.count }))
  // Your name: the fullest form seen (first, middle and last over first and last)
  const nameList = Object.entries(names).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).map(([n]) => n)
  const name = nameList.sort((a, b) => b.split(" ").length - a.split(" ").length || names[b] - names[a])[0] || ""
  const cardDigits = new Set(cardList.flatMap(c => c.digits))
  return { cards: cardList, accounts: Object.keys(accounts).filter(d => !cardDigits.has(d)).sort((a, b) => accounts[b] - accounts[a]), name }
}
// Keeps a name you already gave a card in Notion (e.g. "HDFC Regalia ••1234") when the digits match.
function mergeCardNames(found, existing) {
  return found.map(c => {
    const keep = existing.find(n => c.digits.length ? c.digits.some(d => new RegExp(`(?:^|\\D)${d.slice(-4)}\\s*$`).test(n)) : /rupay/i.test(n) && n.toLowerCase().includes(c.bank.toLowerCase()))
    return keep ? Object.assign({}, c, { name: keep }) : c
  })
}

// ---------- one-tap setup from the Google Sheet ----------
// The sheet's menu: Expense Tracker › Start. It asks for your Notion key once, finds your tables,
// recognises your cards and accounts from your bank emails, and starts the 10-minute sync.

function onOpen() {
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().createMenu("Expense Tracker")
    .addItem("Start", "start")
    .addItem("Look for new cards", "rescanCards")
    .addSeparator()
    .addItem("Stop syncing", "stop")
    .addToUi()
  try { if (!PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("NOTION_TOKEN")) showStatus([["Status", "Not set up yet. Tap Expense Tracker › Start in the menu above."]]) } catch (e) {}
}

function start() {
  const ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(), props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties()
  let token = props.getProperty("NOTION_TOKEN")
  if (!token) {
    const r = ui.prompt("Connect Notion", "Paste your Notion key (it starts with ntn_). It's stored privately in this sheet's script, not in the sheet itself.", ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL)
    if (r.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return
    token = r.getResponseText().trim()
    if (!/^(ntn_|secret_)/.test(token)) { ui.alert("That doesn't look like a Notion key. It should start with ntn_. Copy it again from notion.so/profile/integrations."); return }
    props.setProperty("NOTION_TOKEN", token)
  }
  try { findTables(token) } catch (e) {
    props.deleteProperty("DB_CC Transactions"); props.deleteProperty("DB_Expenses"); props.deleteProperty("DB_Card Bills")
    if (/unauthorized|invalid/i.test(String(e.message))) props.deleteProperty("NOTION_TOKEN")
    ui.alert("Couldn't reach your Notion tables", String(e.message || e), ui.ButtonSet.OK); return
  }
  showStatus([["Status", "Looking through your bank emails for cards…"]])
  const found = detectAndSave(token)
  uninstall()
  ScriptApp.newTrigger("syncAlerts").timeBased().everyMinutes(10).create()
  ScriptApp.newTrigger("syncAlerts").timeBased().after(60 * 1000).create()   // first sync in a minute, with your cards loaded
  showStatus(statusRows(found, "Syncing every 10 minutes. The first sync starts in about a minute."))
  ui.alert("All set", found.cards.length
    ? `Found ${found.cards.length} card${found.cards.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}: ${found.cards.map(c => c.name).join(", ")}. They're now in your Notion Card column. New alerts sync every 10 minutes.`
    : "No credit card alerts found in your email yet. Your cards will be added automatically as alerts arrive. New alerts sync every 10 minutes.", ui.ButtonSet.OK)
}

function rescanCards() {
  const token = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("NOTION_TOKEN")
  if (!token) return start()
  findTables(token)
  const found = detectAndSave(token)
  showStatus(statusRows(found, ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().length ? "Syncing every 10 minutes." : "Stopped."))
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(`Cards: ${found.cards.map(c => c.name).join(", ") || "none found yet"}`)
}

function stop() {
  uninstall()
  showStatus([["Status", "Stopped. Tap Expense Tracker › Start to sync again."]])
}

// Reads the last 4 months of bank emails, saves your cards/name, and adds the cards to Notion's Card columns.
function detectAndSave(token) {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties()
  const msgs = []
  for (const t of GmailApp.search(GMAIL_QUERY.replace(`newer_than:${LOOKBACK}`, "newer_than:120d") + ' OR (from:cred.club "bill summary" newer_than:120d)', 0, 250))
    for (const m of t.getMessages().slice(-10)) msgs.push({ subject: m.getSubject(), from: m.getFrom(), body: m.getPlainBody().slice(0, 3000) })
  const found = detectAccounts(msgs)
  const existing = cardOptions(token, EXPENSES_DB).map(o => o.name).filter(n => !/^example card/i.test(n))
  const merged = mergeCardNames(found.cards, existing)
  // keep cards you added by hand in Notion too
  const saved = JSON.parse(props.getProperty("MY_CARDS") || "[]")
  const all = merged.concat(saved.filter(s => !merged.some(m => m.name === s.name)))
  props.setProperty("MY_CARDS", JSON.stringify(all.map(c => ({ name: c.name, digits: c.digits }))))
  if (found.name && !props.getProperty("MY_NAME")) props.setProperty("MY_NAME", found.name)
  props.setProperty("MY_ACCOUNTS", JSON.stringify(found.accounts))
  props.setProperty("DETECTED_AT", String(Date.now()))
  const names = all.map(c => c.name)
  for (const db of [EXPENSES_DB, ALERTS_DB, BILLS_DB]) if (db) {
    try { addCardOptions(token, db, names.concat(db === ALERTS_DB ? ["Bank account", "Unknown"] : [])) } catch (e) { Logger.log("Card options: " + e) }
  }
  return { cards: all, name: props.getProperty("MY_NAME") || "", accounts: found.accounts }
}
function cardOptions(token, db) {
  const d = notion(token, "get", `databases/${db}`)
  return (d.properties && d.properties.Card && d.properties.Card.select && d.properties.Card.select.options) || []
}
// Adds the cards to a table's Card column (keeps what's there, drops the template's example).
function addCardOptions(token, db, names) {
  const cur = cardOptions(token, db)
  const keep = cur.filter(o => !/^example card/i.test(o.name))
  const add = names.filter(n => !keep.some(o => o.name === n))
  if (!add.length && keep.length === cur.length) return
  notion(token, "patch", `databases/${db}`, { properties: { Card: { select: { options: keep.map(o => ({ id: o.id, name: o.name, color: o.color })).concat(add.map(name => ({ name }))) } } } })
}

function statusRows(found, state) {
  return [["Status", state],
    ["Cards", found.cards.map(c => c.name).join(", ") || "None yet: they're added as alerts arrive"],
    ["Your name (for skipping transfers to yourself)", found.name || "Not found yet"],
    ["Bank accounts", found.accounts.map(a => "••" + a).join(", ") || "None found"],
    ["Updated", new Date().toLocaleString()]]
}
function showStatus(rows) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActive(); if (!ss) return
    const sh = ss.getSheets()[0]
    sh.clear(); sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, 2).setValues([["Expense Tracker", ""]]).setFontWeight("bold").setFontSize(14)
    sh.getRange(3, 1, rows.length, 2).setValues(rows)
    sh.getRange(3, 1, rows.length, 1).setFontWeight("bold")
    sh.setColumnWidth(1, 280); sh.setColumnWidth(2, 520)
    sh.getRange(3 + rows.length + 1, 1).setValue("Menu: Expense Tracker › Start, Look for new cards, or Stop syncing.").setFontColor("#666666")
  } catch (e) { Logger.log("status: " + e) }
}
return { parseAlert, guessCategory }
}

// ---------- auto-logging ----------
// The choice lives on the Expenses table's description ("Auto-log: fill · sms · preview · since …") so the Google script
// and this phone read the same thing. Sources: bank alerts read by the Google script, or bank SMS handed to this script
// by an iPhone Shortcuts automation (Settings › Logging explains how). Nothing else is involved.
const AUTO_KEY = "expenses.autolog", SMS_SEEN_KEY = "expenses.smsSeen", SMS_NAME_KEY = "expenses.myBankName", AUTO_ASKED_KEY = "expenses.autologAsked"
const SMS_QUEUE = "expenses-sms-queue.json", SMS_PREVIEW = "expenses-sms-preview.json"
const AUTO_WAIT_MS = 60 * 6e4
let AUTO = (() => { try { return Keychain.contains(AUTO_KEY) ? JSON.parse(Keychain.get(AUTO_KEY)) : null } catch (e) { return null } })()
function parseAutoDesc(desc) {
  const m = String(desc || "").match(/Auto-log:\s*(off|fill|auto)([^\n]*)/i)
  if (!m) return null
  return { mode: m[1].toLowerCase(), preview: /preview/i.test(m[2]), sms: /\bsms\b/i.test(m[2]), since: Date.parse((m[2].match(/since\s+(\S+)/i) || [])[1] || "") || Date.now() }
}
const autoLine = a => `Auto-log: ${a.mode}${a.sms ? " · sms" : ""}${a.preview ? " · preview" : ""} · since ${new Date(a.since).toISOString()}`
function rememberAuto(desc) { AUTO = parseAutoDesc(desc); try { Keychain.set(AUTO_KEY, JSON.stringify(AUTO)) } catch (e) {} }
const readJSON = (name, d) => { try { const p = fm.joinPath(fm.documentsDirectory(), name); return fm.fileExists(p) ? JSON.parse(fm.readString(p)) : d } catch (e) { return d } }
const writeJSON = (name, v) => { try { fm.writeString(fm.joinPath(fm.documentsDirectory(), name), JSON.stringify(v)) } catch (e) {} }
const smsName = () => Keychain.contains(SMS_NAME_KEY) ? Keychain.get(SMS_NAME_KEY) : ""

// Your past choices, for learnCategory: expenses linked to a bank alert pair the bank's text with your category and name,
// and every expense teaches the names you use.
function autoHistory(rows, alerts) {
  const byId = new Map((alerts || []).map(a => [String(a.id).replace(/-/g, ""), a]))
  const out = []
  for (const r of rows || []) {
    const cat = r.Category || "Other", name = r.Merchant || "", amount = Number(r.Amount) || 0
    if (cat === DUES) continue
    for (const id of r.Alerts || []) { const a = byId.get(String(id).replace(/-/g, "")); if (a) out.push({ merchant: a.merchant, name, category: cat, amount, guess: ALERT_TO_EXPENSE_CAT[a.category] || a.category }) }
    if (name) out.push({ merchant: name, name, category: cat, amount, guess: "" })
  }
  return out
}
// alerts the Google script will log (or would, in preview), and how many you logged yourself
function autoView(rows, alerts, links) {
  if (!AUTO || AUTO.mode === "off") return AUTO ? { mode: "off" } : null
  const view = { mode: AUTO.mode, preview: AUTO.preview, sms: AUTO.sms, since: AUTO.since, wouldAdd: [], matched: 0, total: 0, waiting: 0, earlier: 0 }
  const history = autoHistory(rows, alerts)
  const predict = (merchant, amount, guess) => learnCategory(history, { merchant, amount }, guess)
  if (AUTO.sms) {
    const items = readJSON(SMS_PREVIEW, []).filter(i => i.at >= AUTO.since)
    const dn = d => Date.parse(d + "T00:00:00Z") / 864e5
    for (const i of items) {
      const logged = (rows || []).some(r => Math.abs((Number(r.Amount) || 0) - i.amount) <= 1 && Math.abs(dn(String(r["date:Date:start"] || "").slice(0, 10)) - dn(i.day)) <= 1)
      view.total++
      if (logged) view.matched++
      else if (view.wouldAdd.length < 25) { const p = predict(i.merchant, i.amount, i.guess); view.wouldAdd.push({ day: i.day, amount: i.amount, merchant: p.name || i.merchant, card: i.card, category: p.category, confidence: p.confidence, reason: p.reason }) }
    }
    view.queued = readJSON(SMS_QUEUE, []).length
    return view
  }
  const linked = new Set((links || []).flatMap(l => l.alertIds))
  const backs = (alerts || []).filter(a => a.type === "Refund" || a.type === "Reversal")
  const now = Date.now()
  for (const a of alerts || []) {
    // unlogged payments from before auto-log was turned on: they're in To do, but auto-log never adds them
    if (a.type === "Spend" && !a.ignored && a.status !== "Duplicate" && a.time < AUTO.since && !(a.expenseIds || []).length && !linked.has(a.id) && a.category !== "Loan EMI") view.earlier++
    if (a.type !== "Spend" || a.ignored || a.status === "Duplicate" || a.time < AUTO.since || a.category === "Loan EMI" || /bill payment/i.test(a.channel || "")) continue
    if (backs.some(b => b.card === a.card && Math.abs(b.amount - a.amount) <= 0.01 && b.time >= a.time - 36e5)) continue
    view.total++
    if ((a.expenseIds || []).length || linked.has(a.id)) { view.matched++; continue }
    if (AUTO.mode === "fill" && now - a.time < AUTO_WAIT_MS) { view.waiting++; continue }   // you get an hour to log it yourself
    if (view.wouldAdd.length < 25) { const p = predict(a.merchant, a.amount, ALERT_TO_EXPENSE_CAT[a.category] || a.category); view.wouldAdd.push({ id: a.id, day: a.day, amount: a.amount, merchant: p.name || a.merchant, card: a.card, category: p.category, confidence: p.confidence, reason: p.reason }) }
  }
  return view
}

async function ensureLoggedBy(tok, db) {
  if (db && db.properties && db.properties["Logged by"]) return
  await notion(tok, "PATCH", `databases/${DATABASE_ID}`, { properties: { "Logged by": { select: { options: [{ name: "Auto", color: "blue" }, { name: "Auto · check", color: "orange" }, { name: "SMS", color: "green" }] } } } })
}
// Requests from the page about auto-logging. Each one writes to Notion, then reloads.
async function autoAction(kind, data) {
  while (refreshing) await sleep(200)
  try {
    const tok = await token()
    if (!tok || !DATABASE_ID) throw new Error("Finish setup first: the dashboard needs your Notion token and database link.")
    if (kind === "autolog-set") {
      const db = await notion(tok, "GET", `databases/${DATABASE_ID}`)
      const desc = (db.description || []).map(t => t.plain_text).join("")
      const mode = ["off", "fill", "auto"].includes(data.mode) ? data.mode : "off"
      const next = { mode, sms: !!data.sms, preview: mode !== "off" && !!data.preview, since: Date.now() }
      const text = (/Auto-log:[^\n]*/i.test(desc) ? desc.replace(/Auto-log:[^\n]*/i, autoLine(next)) : [desc.trim(), autoLine(next)].filter(Boolean).join("\n")).slice(0, 1990)
      if (mode !== "off") await ensureLoggedBy(tok, db)
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `databases/${DATABASE_ID}`, { description: [{ type: "text", text: { content: text } }] })
      rememberAuto(text)
      if (data.name != null) { const n = String(data.name).trim(); if (n) Keychain.set(SMS_NAME_KEY, n); else if (Keychain.contains(SMS_NAME_KEY)) Keychain.remove(SMS_NAME_KEY) }
      Keychain.set(AUTO_ASKED_KEY, "1")
      if (data.golive) { writeJSON(SMS_PREVIEW, []) }
      extraMessage = mode === "off" ? "Auto-logging is off. You log expenses yourself." : next.preview ? "Preview is on: nothing is added yet. The Overview shows what would have been." : mode === "fill" ? "Auto-logging is on: anything you don't log within an hour is added for you." : "Auto-logging is on: every payment is added as it comes in."
    } else if (kind === "autolog-dismiss") {
      Keychain.set(AUTO_ASKED_KEY, "1")
      extraMessage = null
    } else if (kind === "auto-review") {
      const cat = String(data.category || "Other")
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { properties: { Category: { select: { name: cat } }, "Category Text": { title: [{ text: { content: cat } }] }, "Logged by": { select: { name: "Auto" } } } })
      extraMessage = data.changed ? `Moved to ${cat}. It'll be remembered.` : "Thanks, marked as checked."
    } else if (kind === "auto-notexpense") {
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { archived: true })
      if (data.alertId) await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.alertId}`, { properties: { Ignored: { checkbox: true } } }).catch(() => {})
      extraMessage = "Removed. That payment won't be added again."
    }
  } catch (e) {
    log(`auto action failed (${kind}): ${e.message || e}`)
    extraMessage = null
    await refresh()
    await js(`window.setData && window.setData(${safe(latest ? latest.rows : [])}, ${safe(Object.assign({}, latest ? latest.meta : {}, { error: friendly(e) }))})`)
    return
  }
  await refresh()
}

// ---------- bank SMS handed over by the Shortcuts automation ----------
const looksLikeSms = p => typeof p === "string" && p.length >= 30 && /(?:Rs\.?|INR|₹)\s*[\d,]+/i.test(p) && /debit|spent|sent|paid|purchase|withdrawn|transaction|txn|credited|received/i.test(p)
const smsHash = t => { let h = 5381; for (let i = 0; i < t.length; i++) h = ((h << 5) + h + t.charCodeAt(i)) | 0; return String(h >>> 0) }
async function smsCreate(tok, item, history, rows) {
  const p = learnCategory(history, { merchant: item.merchant, amount: item.amount }, item.guess)
  const props = {
    "Category Text": { title: [{ text: { content: p.category } }] }, Category: { select: { name: p.category } }, Amount: { number: item.amount },
    Date: { date: { start: item.day } }, Merchant: { rich_text: [{ text: { content: String(p.name || autoName(item.merchant)).slice(0, 200) } }] },
    "Payment Method": { select: { name: item.method } }, Notes: { rich_text: [{ text: { content: `From your bank's SMS · ${p.reason}`.slice(0, 400) } }] },
    "Logged by": { select: { name: p.confidence === "low" ? "Auto · check" : "SMS" } },
  }
  if (CREDIT_CARDS.includes(item.card)) props.Card = { select: { name: item.card } }
  await notion(tok, "POST", "pages", { parent: { database_id: DATABASE_ID }, properties: props })
  history.push({ merchant: item.merchant, name: p.name || autoName(item.merchant), category: p.category, amount: item.amount, guess: item.guess })
}
// "fill in what I miss": SMS payments wait an hour; if you haven't logged one yourself by then, it's added.
async function processSmsQueue(tok, rows) {
  const q = readJSON(SMS_QUEUE, [])
  if (!q.length || !AUTO || AUTO.mode === "off" || AUTO.preview || !AUTO.sms) { if (q.length && (!AUTO || !AUTO.sms || AUTO.mode === "off")) writeJSON(SMS_QUEUE, []); return 0 }
  const due = q.filter(i => Date.now() - i.at >= AUTO_WAIT_MS)
  if (!due.length) return 0
  if (!rows) { const first = due.map(i => i.day).sort()[0]; rows = await fetchExpenses(tok, shiftDay(first, -2)) }
  const dn = d => Date.parse(d + "T00:00:00Z") / 864e5
  const history = autoHistory((readCache() || {}).rows || rows, [])
  let added = 0
  const left = q.filter(i => !due.includes(i))
  for (const i of due) {
    const logged = rows.some(r => Math.abs((Number(r.Amount) || 0) - i.amount) <= 1 && Math.abs(dn(String(r["date:Date:start"] || "").slice(0, 10)) - dn(i.day)) <= 1)
    if (logged) continue
    try { await smsCreate(tok, i, history, rows); added++ } catch (e) { log(`sms add failed: ${e.message || e}`); left.push(i) }
  }
  writeJSON(SMS_QUEUE, left)
  return added
}
async function handleSms(text) {
  const tok = Keychain.contains(TOKEN_KEY) ? Keychain.get(TOKEN_KEY) : ""
  if (!tok || !DATABASE_ID) return "Open the Expenses Dashboard once to connect Notion."
  if (!AUTO || AUTO.mode === "off" || !AUTO.sms) return "Auto-logging from SMS is off (dashboard › gear › Logging)."
  const h = smsHash(text), seen = (() => { try { return JSON.parse(Keychain.get(SMS_SEEN_KEY)) } catch (e) { return [] } })() || []
  if (seen.includes(h)) return "Already seen."
  Keychain.set(SMS_SEEN_KEY, JSON.stringify(seen.concat([h]).slice(-80)))
  const cards = (() => { try { return JSON.parse(Keychain.get(CARDS_KEY)) } catch (e) { return CREDIT_CARDS } })() || []
  setCards(cards)
  const P = makeSmsParser(cards, smsName())
  const r = P.parseAlert({ id: "sms", subject: "[CC-SMS]", from: "", body: text, date: new Date() })
  if (!r || r.skip || r.type !== "Spend" || r.category === "Loan EMI" || /bill payment/i.test(r.channel || "")) return r && r.skip ? `Not a payment (${r.skip}).` : "Not a payment to log."
  if (r.fx) return "Foreign-currency payment: log it yourself, the rupee amount isn't in the SMS."
  const item = { at: Date.now(), day: istDay(r.time || Date.now()), amount: r.amount, merchant: r.merchant, card: r.card, channel: r.channel,
    method: methodFor(r), guess: ALERT_TO_EXPENSE_CAT[r.category] || r.category }
  if (AUTO.preview) { writeJSON(SMS_PREVIEW, readJSON(SMS_PREVIEW, []).filter(i => Date.now() - i.at < 45 * 864e5).concat([item]).slice(-300)); return `Preview: would add ${inr(item.amount)} at ${item.merchant}.` }
  if (AUTO.mode === "auto") {
    await smsCreate(tok, item, autoHistory((readCache() || {}).rows || [], []))
    return `Logged ${inr(item.amount)} · ${item.merchant}`
  }
  writeJSON(SMS_QUEUE, readJSON(SMS_QUEUE, []).concat([item]).slice(-200))
  await processSmsQueue(tok).catch(e => log(`sms queue: ${e.message || e}`))
  return `Noted ${inr(item.amount)} · ${item.merchant}. Added in an hour unless you log it yourself.`
}
function autoName(m) {
  let t = String(m || "").replace(/\b(pvt|private|ltd|limited|llp|india|ind|systems?|technologies|services|online|order)\b\.?/gi, " ").replace(/\b\w*\d\w*\b/g, " ").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim() || String(m || "Unknown")
  if (t === t.toUpperCase()) t = t.toLowerCase().replace(/\b[a-z]/g, c => c.toUpperCase())
  return t
}

// ---------- WhatsApp reminders for money owed ----------
// Numbers are saved only in this iPhone's Keychain (never in Notion). WhatsApp opens with the message
// written; you check it and tap Send. Nothing is sent without you.
const REMIND_KEY = "expenses_owed_whatsapp"
function readRemind() { try { return Keychain.contains(REMIND_KEY) ? JSON.parse(Keychain.get(REMIND_KEY)) || {} : {} } catch (e) { return {} } }
function saveRemind(m) { try { Keychain.set(REMIND_KEY, JSON.stringify(m)) } catch (e) {} }
// What the page may know: when you last reminded someone and whether a number is saved (not the number).
function remindForPage() { const out = {}; for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(readRemind())) out[k] = { reminded: v.reminded || "", hasPhone: !!v.phone }; return out }
function cleanPhone(s) {
  let d = String(s || "").replace(/[^\d+]/g, "")
  if (d.startsWith("+")) return d.slice(1).replace(/\D/g, "")
  d = d.replace(/\D/g, "")
  if (d.length === 11 && d.startsWith("0")) d = d.slice(1)
  if (d.length === 10) d = "91" + d            // an Indian mobile without the country code
  return d.length >= 8 ? d : ""
}
async function askNumber(name) {
  // Offer matching numbers from your contacts first (if you allow access), then typing one in.
  let options = []
  try {
    const first = name.split(" ")[0].toLowerCase()
    const all = await Contact.all(await ContactsContainer.all())
    for (const c of all) {
      const full = `${c.givenName || ""} ${c.familyName || ""}`.trim()
      if (!full.toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).includes(first) && !full.toLowerCase().startsWith(name.toLowerCase())) continue
      for (const p of c.phoneNumbers || []) if (cleanPhone(p.value)) options.push({ label: `${full} · ${p.value}`, phone: cleanPhone(p.value) })
    }
  } catch (e) { log(`contacts: ${e.message || e}`) }
  options = options.filter((o, i) => options.findIndex(x => x.phone === o.phone) === i).slice(0, 6)
  if (options.length) {
    const a = new Alert()
    a.title = `${name}'s WhatsApp number`
    a.message = "Pick the right number. It's saved on this iPhone for next time."
    options.forEach(o => a.addAction(o.label))
    a.addAction("Type a number instead")
    a.addCancelAction("Cancel")
    const i = await a.presentSheet()
    if (i === -1) return ""
    if (i < options.length) return options[i].phone
  }
  while (true) {
    const t = new Alert()
    t.title = `${name}'s WhatsApp number`
    t.message = "With the country code if it's not an Indian number. It's saved only on this iPhone."
    t.addTextField("+91 98765 43210")
    t.addAction("Save")
    t.addCancelAction("Cancel")
    if (await t.presentAlert() === -1) return ""
    const p = cleanPhone(t.textFieldValue(0))
    if (p) return p
    const e = new Alert(); e.title = "That doesn't look like a phone number"; e.addAction("Try again"); await e.presentAlert()
  }
}
async function remind(data) {
  try {
    const key = String(data.key || "").toLowerCase(), name = String(data.name || "them")
    const map = readRemind(), entry = map[key] || {}
    // a person with a linked contact: their number, and a new number picked here is linked to them too
    const linked = data.personId ? readPhones()[data.personId] : null
    if (linked && linked.phone && !data.change) entry.phone = linked.phone
    if (!entry.phone || data.change) {
      const phone = await askNumber(name)
      if (!phone) return
      entry.phone = phone
      map[key] = entry; saveRemind(map)
      if (data.personId) { const pm = readPhones(); pm[data.personId] = Object.assign({}, pm[data.personId], { phone }); savePhones(pm); await js(`window.setPhones && window.setPhones(${safe(phonesForPage())})`) }
      if (data.change) { await js(`window.setReminded && window.setReminded(${safe(key)}, { hasPhone: true }); window.flash && window.flash(${safe(`Saved ${name}'s number.`)})`); return }
    }
    const text = await withPayLink(String(data.text || ""), data.amount)
    Safari.open(`whatsapp://send?phone=${entry.phone}&text=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`)
    entry.reminded = istDay(Date.now())
    map[key] = entry; saveRemind(map)
    await js(`window.setReminded && window.setReminded(${safe(key)}, { reminded: ${safe(entry.reminded)}, hasPhone: true })`)
    log(`reminder opened for ${name}`)
  } catch (e) {
    log(`remind failed: ${e.message || e}`)
    await js(`window.flash && window.flash(${safe("Couldn't open WhatsApp: " + (e.message || e))})`)
  }
}

// ---------- people's phone numbers: linked from your contacts, kept only on this iPhone ----------
// Never written to Notion. The page only learns that a number is linked and the contact's name, not the number.
const PEOPLE_PHONE_KEY = "expenses.peoplePhones"
function readPhones() { try { return Keychain.contains(PEOPLE_PHONE_KEY) ? JSON.parse(Keychain.get(PEOPLE_PHONE_KEY)) || {} : {} } catch (e) { return {} } }
function savePhones(m) { try { Keychain.set(PEOPLE_PHONE_KEY, JSON.stringify(m)) } catch (e) {} }
function phonesForPage() { const out = {}; for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(readPhones())) if (v && v.phone) out[k] = { label: v.label || "" }; return out }
// Contacts whose name shares a word with this one; if none, you can search by any name.
async function askContact(name) {
  let all = []
  try { all = await Contact.all(await ContactsContainer.all()) } catch (e) { log(`contacts: ${e.message || e}`) }
  const find = q => {
    const words = String(q || "").toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter(w => w.length >= 2), out = []
    for (const c of all) {
      const full = `${c.givenName || ""} ${c.familyName || ""}`.trim() || c.organizationName || ""
      const parts = full.toLowerCase().split(/\s+/)
      if (!words.some(w => parts.some(p => p.startsWith(w)))) continue
      for (const p of c.phoneNumbers || []) { const ph = cleanPhone(p.value); if (ph) out.push({ label: full, shown: `${full} · ${p.value}`, phone: ph }) }
    }
    return out.filter((o, i) => out.findIndex(x => x.phone === o.phone) === i).slice(0, 8)
  }
  let q = name
  for (let round = 0; round < 4; round++) {
    const options = all.length ? find(q) : []
    const a = new Alert()
    a.title = options.length ? `Link ${name} to a contact` : all.length ? `No contact matches "${q}"` : `${name}'s number`
    a.message = all.length ? "The number stays on this iPhone; it's never saved to Notion." : "Scriptable can't see your contacts (Settings › Scriptable › Contacts). You can type the number instead."
    options.forEach(o => a.addAction(o.shown))
    if (all.length) a.addAction("Search another name")
    a.addAction("Type a number")
    a.addCancelAction("Cancel")
    const i = await (options.length ? a.presentSheet() : a.presentAlert())
    if (i === -1) return null
    if (i < options.length) return options[i]
    if (all.length && i === options.length) {
      const s = new Alert(); s.title = "Search your contacts"; s.addTextField("Name", q); s.addAction("Search"); s.addCancelAction("Cancel")
      if (await s.presentAlert() === -1) return null
      q = s.textFieldValue(0); continue
    }
    const t = new Alert(); t.title = `${name}'s number`; t.message = "With the country code if it's not an Indian number."
    t.addTextField("+91 98765 43210"); t.addAction("Save"); t.addCancelAction("Cancel")
    if (await t.presentAlert() === -1) return null
    const ph = cleanPhone(t.textFieldValue(0))
    if (ph) return { label: "", phone: ph }
  }
  return null
}
async function contactAction(kind, data) {
  try {
    const map = readPhones(), id = String(data.id || ""), entry = map[id]
    if (kind === "contact-link") {
      const c = await askContact(String(data.name || "them"))
      if (!c) return
      map[id] = { phone: c.phone, label: c.label }
      savePhones(map)
      await js(`window.setPhones && window.setPhones(${safe(phonesForPage())}); window.flash && window.flash(${safe(`Linked ${data.name}${c.label && c.label !== data.name ? " to " + c.label : ""}.`)})`)
      return
    }
    if (kind === "contact-unlink") { delete map[id]; savePhones(map); await js(`window.setPhones && window.setPhones(${safe(phonesForPage())}); window.flash && window.flash("Unlinked.")`); return }
    if (!entry || !entry.phone) { await js(`window.flash && window.flash("Link a contact first.")`); return }
    if (kind === "contact-call") Safari.open(`tel:+${entry.phone}`)
    else if (kind === "contact-sms") Safari.open(`sms:+${entry.phone}`)
    else if (kind === "contact-wa") Safari.open(`whatsapp://send?phone=${entry.phone}${data.text ? "&text=" + encodeURIComponent(String(data.text)) : ""}`)
  } catch (e) {
    log(`contact ${kind} failed: ${e.message || e}`)
    await js(`window.flash && window.flash(${safe("Couldn't do that: " + (e.message || e))})`)
  }
}

// ---------- payback link: your UPI ID added to WhatsApp reminders, kept only on this iPhone ----------
// The friend taps the upi:// link and their UPI app opens with your UPI ID and the amount filled in.
const MY_UPI_KEY = "expenses.myUpi"
function readUpi() { try { return Keychain.contains(MY_UPI_KEY) ? JSON.parse(Keychain.get(MY_UPI_KEY)) || {} : {} } catch (e) { return {} } }
function saveUpi(u) { try { Keychain.set(MY_UPI_KEY, JSON.stringify(u)) } catch (e) {} }
function upiForPage() { const u = readUpi(); return { id: u.id || "", off: !!u.off } }
function cleanUpi(s) {
  const t = String(s || "").trim().replace(/\s+/g, "").toLowerCase()
  return /^[a-z0-9._-]{2,256}@[a-z][a-z0-9.-]{1,64}$/.test(t) ? t : ""
}
// asks for your UPI ID; returns the saved settings, or null if cancelled
async function askUpi(fromReminder) {
  const u = readUpi()
  for (let round = 0; round < 3; round++) {
    const a = new Alert()
    a.title = fromReminder ? "Add a pay link?" : "Your UPI ID"
    a.message = (fromReminder ? "Your reminder can end with a link that opens their UPI app with your UPI ID and the amount filled in. " : "Added to WhatsApp reminders as a tap-to-pay link. ") +
      (round ? "That doesn't look like a UPI ID (it's like name@okhdfc)." : "It stays on this iPhone.")
    a.addTextField("e.g. heet@okhdfc", u.id || "")
    a.addAction(fromReminder ? "Add link" : "Save")
    if (fromReminder) a.addAction("Not now")
    if (fromReminder) a.addAction("Don't ask again")
    else if (u.id) a.addDestructiveAction("Remove")
    a.addCancelAction("Cancel")
    const i = await a.presentAlert()
    if (i === -1) return null
    if (fromReminder && i === 1) return u
    if (fromReminder && i === 2) { u.off = true; saveUpi(u); return u }
    if (!fromReminder && i === 1) { saveUpi({ off: true }); return { off: true } }
    const id = cleanUpi(a.textFieldValue(0))
    if (!id) continue
    const out = { id, off: false }
    saveUpi(out)
    return out
  }
  return null
}
function upiLink(id, amount, note) {
  let pn = ""
  try { if (typeof SMS_NAME_KEY !== "undefined" && Keychain.contains(SMS_NAME_KEY)) pn = Keychain.get(SMS_NAME_KEY) } catch (e) {}
  const q = [`pa=${id}`]  // UPI IDs are checked to plain letters, digits, . _ - and @; some apps reject an encoded @
  if (pn) q.push(`pn=${encodeURIComponent(pn)}`)
  if (amount > 0) q.push(`am=${(Math.round(amount * 100) / 100).toFixed(2)}`)
  q.push("cu=INR")
  if (note) q.push(`tn=${encodeURIComponent(String(note).slice(0, 40))}`)
  return `upi://pay?${q.join("&")}`
}
// the reminder text with the pay lines added (or unchanged when there's no UPI ID)
async function withPayLink(text, amount) {
  let u = readUpi()
  if (!u.id && !u.off) u = (await askUpi(true)) || u
  if (!u.id || u.off) return text
  await js(`window.setUpi && window.setUpi(${safe(upiForPage())})`)
  const amt = Number(amount) || 0
  return `${text}\n\nTap to pay${amt > 0 ? " " + inrPlain(amt) : ""} by UPI:\n${upiLink(u.id, amt, "Split bills")}\nOr pay to UPI ID: ${u.id}`
}
function inrPlain(n) { return "₹" + n.toLocaleString("en-IN", { maximumFractionDigits: 2 }) }
async function upiAction(data) {
  try {
    const u = await askUpi(false)
    if (!u) return
    await js(`window.setUpi && window.setUpi(${safe(upiForPage())}); window.flash && window.flash(${safe(u.id ? "Pay link on: " + u.id : "Pay link removed.")})`)
  } catch (e) { log(`upi failed: ${e.message || e}`) }
}

// ---------- your cards and bank accounts (Settings › Cards & accounts) ----------
// Cards are the options of the Card column (Expenses, CC Transactions and, for credit cards, Card Bills), written
// "HDFC Regalia ••1234" or "HDFC Debit ••1234". Bank accounts are the options of an Account column on CC Transactions
// ("HDFC ••8760"). Your Google script reads both, so alerts from a card or account you add here are recognised by
// its last digits. A removed card is also listed on the Card Bills description ("Removed cards: ••3943"), so the
// script stops tagging it and doesn't add it back when it rescans your email.
const ACCOUNTS_KEY = "expenses_account_names"
const ACCOUNT_NAMES = (() => { try { return JSON.parse(Keychain.get(ACCOUNTS_KEY)) || [] } catch (e) { return [] } })()
async function loadAccounts(tok) {
  if (!ALERTS_ID) return
  try {
    const db = await notion(tok, "GET", `databases/${ALERTS_ID}`)
    const names = ((((db.properties || {}).Account || {}).select || {}).options || []).map(o => o.name)
    ACCOUNT_NAMES.splice(0, ACCOUNT_NAMES.length, ...names); Keychain.set(ACCOUNTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(names))
  } catch (e) { log(`accounts: ${e.message || e}`) }
}
const optionsOf = (db, prop) => ((((db.properties || {})[prop] || {}).select || {}).options || [])
const optList = opts => opts.map(o => ({ id: o.id, name: o.name, color: o.color }))
const lastDigits = n => (String(n).match(/(\d{3,6})\s*$/) || [])[1] || ""
async function addOption(tok, dbId, prop, name) {
  const db = await notion(tok, "GET", `databases/${dbId}`), opts = optionsOf(db, prop)
  if (!(db.properties || {})[prop]) return notion(tok, "PATCH", `databases/${dbId}`, { properties: { [prop]: { select: { options: [{ name }] } } } })
  if (opts.some(o => o.name === name)) return
  await notion(tok, "PATCH", `databases/${dbId}`, { properties: { [prop]: { select: { options: optList(opts).concat([{ name }]) } } } })
}
async function renameOption(tok, dbId, prop, from, to) {
  const db = await notion(tok, "GET", `databases/${dbId}`), opts = optionsOf(db, prop)
  if (!opts.some(o => o.name === from) || opts.some(o => o.name === to)) return false
  await notion(tok, "PATCH", `databases/${dbId}`, { properties: { [prop]: { select: { options: optList(opts).map(o => o.name === from ? { id: o.id, name: to } : o) } } } })
  return true
}
// Notion may keep an option that's still used on a row; that's fine, it just stops being offered here.
async function removeOption(tok, dbId, prop, name) {
  const db = await notion(tok, "GET", `databases/${dbId}`), opts = optionsOf(db, prop)
  if (!opts.some(o => o.name === name)) return true
  await notion(tok, "PATCH", `databases/${dbId}`, { properties: { [prop]: { select: { options: optList(opts.filter(o => o.name !== name)) } } } })
  const after = await notion(tok, "GET", `databases/${dbId}`)
  return !optionsOf(after, prop).some(o => o.name === name)
}
// "Removed cards: ••3943, ••2583." on the Card Bills description (kept before the forex markup sentence)
async function editRemovedCards(tok, add, drop) {
  if (!BILLS_ID) return
  const db = await notion(tok, "GET", `databases/${BILLS_ID}`), desc = (db.description || []).map(t => t.plain_text).join("")
  const m = desc.match(/Removed cards:\s*([^\n]*?)\.?(?:\n|$)/)
  let list = m ? (m[1].match(/\d{3,6}/g) || []) : []
  if (add) list = list.filter(d => d !== add).concat([add])
  if (drop) list = list.filter(d => d.slice(-4) !== drop.slice(-4))
  const rest = desc.replace(/Removed cards:[^\n]*(\n|$)/, "").trim()
  const next = (list.length ? `Removed cards: ${list.map(d => "••" + d).join(", ")}.\n` : "") + rest
  if (next !== desc) await notion(tok, "PATCH", `databases/${BILLS_ID}`, { description: [{ type: "text", text: { content: next.slice(0, 1900) } }] })
}
async function cardAction(kind, data) {
  while (refreshing) await sleep(200)
  try {
    const tok = await token()
    if (!tok || !DATABASE_ID) throw new Error("Finish setup first: the dashboard needs your Notion token and database link.")
    const name = String(data.name || "").replace(/,/g, " ").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().slice(0, 90)
    if (kind === "card-add") {
      if (!name || !lastDigits(name)) throw new Error("Add the card's last 4 digits.")
      const debit = /\bdebit\b/i.test(name)
      for (const id of [DATABASE_ID, ALERTS_ID, debit ? "" : BILLS_ID].filter(Boolean)) await addOption(tok, id, "Card", name)
      await editRemovedCards(tok, null, lastDigits(name))
      extraMessage = `Added ${name}. Its ${debit ? "debit card" : "card"} alerts are recognised from the next sync.`
    } else if (kind === "card-rename") {
      const from = String(data.from || ""), to = name
      if (!to || to === from) return
      if (lastDigits(from) && !lastDigits(to)) throw new Error("Keep the last 4 digits at the end, like HDFC Regalia ••1234.")
      for (const id of [DATABASE_ID, ALERTS_ID, BILLS_ID].filter(Boolean)) await renameOption(tok, id, "Card", from, to)
      // the forex markup follows the card
      if (BILLS_ID) {
        const db = await notion(tok, "GET", `databases/${BILLS_ID}`), desc = (db.description || []).map(t => t.plain_text).join("")
        const cur = parseMarkups(desc)
        if (cur && cur[from] != null) { const v = cur[from]; setCards(CREDIT_CARDS.map(c => c === from ? to : c)); cur[to] = v; delete cur[from]
          await notion(tok, "PATCH", `databases/${BILLS_ID}`, { description: [{ type: "text", text: { content: withMarkups(desc, cur) } }] }) }
      }
      extraMessage = `Renamed to ${to}. Past expenses and alerts show the new name.`
    } else if (kind === "card-remove") {
      const from = String(data.name || "")
      let gone = true
      for (const id of [DATABASE_ID, ALERTS_ID, BILLS_ID].filter(Boolean)) gone = (await removeOption(tok, id, "Card", from)) && gone
      if (lastDigits(from)) await editRemovedCards(tok, lastDigits(from), null)
      extraMessage = `Removed ${from}. Its alerts won't be tagged to it any more.${gone ? "" : " Older rows that use it still show the name."}`
    } else if (kind === "acct-add") {
      if (!name || !lastDigits(name)) throw new Error("Add the account's last 4 digits.")
      if (!ALERTS_ID) throw new Error("Your CC Transactions table isn't connected yet.")
      await addOption(tok, ALERTS_ID, "Account", name)
      extraMessage = `Added ${name}. Bank alerts from it are labelled from the next sync.`
    } else if (kind === "acct-rename") {
      if (ALERTS_ID && name && name !== data.from) await renameOption(tok, ALERTS_ID, "Account", String(data.from || ""), name)
      extraMessage = `Renamed to ${name}.`
    } else if (kind === "acct-remove") {
      if (ALERTS_ID) await removeOption(tok, ALERTS_ID, "Account", String(data.name || ""))
      extraMessage = `Removed ${data.name}.`
    }
    await loadCards(tok); await loadAccounts(tok)
  } catch (e) {
    log(`card action failed (${kind}): ${e.message || e}`)
    extraMessage = null
    await refresh()
    await js(`window.setData && window.setData(${safe(latest ? latest.rows : [])}, ${safe(Object.assign({}, latest ? latest.meta : {}, { error: friendly(e) }))})`)
    return
  }
  await refresh()
}

// ---------- reading a policy document (schedule, renewal notice, premium receipt) ----------
// Shared by the dashboard (PDFs read on the iPhone) and the Google script (scans and emailed PDFs).
// Picks out what's reliable across insurers (policy number, cover, premium, frequency, dates) and, best-effort,
// the benefits worth knowing: bonus, room rent, co-pay, waiting periods, check-ups, riders. Names, dates of birth
// and nominee details are never kept; for who's covered only the relationships are (Self, Spouse, Mother…).
function parsePolicyDoc(text, meta) {
  meta = meta || {}
  const raw = String(text || "").replace(/\u00a0/g, " ").replace(/\r/g, "")
  const lines = raw.split("\n").map(l => l.replace(/[ ]{2,}/g, " ").trim()).filter(Boolean)
  const flat = lines.join("\n")
  const MON = { jan: 1, feb: 2, mar: 3, apr: 4, may: 5, jun: 6, jul: 7, aug: 8, sep: 9, oct: 10, nov: 11, dec: 12 }
  const DATE = "(\\d{1,2}[-\\/. ]\\d{1,2}[-\\/. ]\\d{4}|\\d{1,2}(?:st|nd|rd|th)?[-\\/ ][A-Za-z]{3,9}[-\\/ ,]+\\d{4}|[A-Za-z]{3,9}\\s+\\d{1,2},?\\s+\\d{4}|\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2})"
  const day = s => {
    s = String(s || "").trim()
    let m = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/); if (m) return s
    m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})[-\/. ](\d{1,2})[-\/. ](\d{4})$/); if (m && +m[2] <= 12) return `${m[3]}-${m[2].padStart(2, "0")}-${m[1].padStart(2, "0")}`
