    m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:st|nd|rd|th)?[-\/ ]([A-Za-z]{3,9})[-\/ ,]+(\d{4})$/); if (m && MON[m[2].slice(0, 3).toLowerCase()]) return `${m[3]}-${String(MON[m[2].slice(0, 3).toLowerCase()]).padStart(2, "0")}-${m[1].padStart(2, "0")}`
    m = s.match(/^([A-Za-z]{3,9})\s+(\d{1,2}),?\s+(\d{4})$/); if (m && MON[m[1].slice(0, 3).toLowerCase()]) return `${m[3]}-${String(MON[m[1].slice(0, 3).toLowerCase()]).padStart(2, "0")}-${m[2].padStart(2, "0")}`
    return ""
  }
  const money = s => { const m = String(s || "").match(/(?:₹|rs\.?|inr)?\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d{1,2})?)\s*(lakhs?|lacs?|crores?|cr|l)?\b/i); if (!m) return 0
    let n = Number(m[1].replace(/,/g, "")) || 0; const u = (m[2] || "").toLowerCase()
    if (/^cr/.test(u)) n *= 1e7; else if (/^(l|lakh|lac)/.test(u)) n *= 1e5
    return n }
  // the value next to a label: rest of the same line (after ":" or a tab), else the next line
  const after = re => {
    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
      const m = lines[i].match(re); if (!m) continue
      const rest = lines[i].slice(m.index + m[0].length).replace(/^[\s:\-–|\t.]+/, "").trim()
      if (rest) return rest
      if (lines[i + 1]) return lines[i + 1]
    }
    return ""
  }
  const amountAfter = (re, min) => { for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) { if (!re.test(lines[i])) continue
      const seg = lines[i].replace(re, "\u0001").split("\u0001").slice(1).join(" ") + " " + (/^\s*(₹|rs\.?|inr)?\s*\d/i.test(lines[i + 1] || "") ? lines[i + 1] : "")
      const n = money(seg.replace(/\b(19|20)\d{2}\b(?![,\d])/g, " "))
      if (n >= (min || 1)) return n }
    return 0 }
  // labelled values: docKV() reads tables, labels on their own line and label/value blocks; the older patterns fill gaps
  const kv = docKV(raw), g = (labels, kind, o) => kv.get(labels, kind, o)
  const out = { kind: /premium (paid )?receipt|receipt for premium|we have received|(total )?amount received/i.test(flat) ? "receipt" : /renewal (notice|premium|reminder)|due for renewal/i.test(flat) ? "renewal" : "schedule" }
  // insurer
  const INS = [[/tata aia/i, "Tata AIA Life"], [/hdfc ergo/i, "HDFC ERGO"], [/hdfc life/i, "HDFC Life"], [/icici pru/i, "ICICI Pru Life"], [/icici lombard/i, "ICICI Lombard"], [/star health/i, "Star Health"],
    [/niva bupa|max bupa/i, "Niva Bupa"], [/care health|religare/i, "Care Health"], [/aditya birla (health|capital health)/i, "Aditya Birla Health"], [/life insurance corporation/i, "LIC"],
    [/axis max life|max life/i, "Axis Max Life"], [/sbi life/i, "SBI Life"], [/sbi general/i, "SBI General"], [/bajaj allianz life|bajaj life/i, "Bajaj Life"], [/bajaj allianz|bajaj general/i, "Bajaj Allianz"],
    [/go ?digit/i, "Digit"], [/acko/i, "Acko"], [/kotak (mahindra )?life/i, "Kotak Life"], [/manipal ?cigna/i, "ManipalCigna"], [/tata aig/i, "Tata AIG"], [/pnb met ?life/i, "PNB MetLife"], [/reliance general/i, "Reliance General"]]
  const ins = INS.find(([re]) => re.test(flat.slice(0, 4000))) || INS.find(([re]) => re.test(flat))
  if (ins) out.insurer = ins[1]
  // policy number: only the last 4 digits leave this function
  const no = flat.match(/policy\s*(?:no\.?|number|#)\s*[:.\-]?\s*([A-Z]{0,4}[\/-]?\d[\dA-Z\/-]{5,30})/i) || flat.match(/\bpolicy\s+([A-Z]{0,4}\d{6,20})\b/i) || String(meta.filename || "").match(/([A-Z]?\d{8,20})/)
  const noKV = g(["policy\\s*(?:no\\.?|number|#)", "certificate\\s*(?:no\\.?|number)"], "code", { test: v => v.replace(/\D/g, "").length >= 6 })
  if (noKV) out.last4 = noKV.replace(/[^0-9]/g, "").slice(-4)
  else if (no) out.last4 = no[1].replace(/[^0-9]/g, "").slice(-4)
  // plan name
  const plan = after(/\b(plan name|product name|name of (the )?(plan|product|policy)|product)\b(?!\s*(code|uin))/i).replace(/\s*\(?\buin\b[^)]*\)?/i, "").replace(/[|\t].*$/, "").trim()
  if (plan && plan.length <= 70 && !/^\d/.test(plan) && !/\b(uin|code)\b/i.test(plan)) out.name = plan
  // type
  const t = flat.slice(0, 6000)
  out.type = /griha|home shield|home insurance|householder|dwelling|building sum insured/i.test(t) ? "Home"
    : /motor|private car|two[- ]wheeler|\bidv\b|insured declared value/i.test(t) ? "Motor"
    : /(?<!long\s)\bterm\s*(life|plan|insurance|assurance)\b|sampoorna raksha|iprotect|click 2 protect|smart shield|death benefit/i.test(t) ? "Term life"
    : /health|mediclaim|hospitali[sz]ation|optima|floater|top[- ]?up|cashless/i.test(t) ? "Health"
    : /life insurance|sum assured|maturity/i.test(t) ? "Life" : "Other"
  // cover
  out.cover = g(["total\\s*sum\\s*(?:insured|assured)", "sum\\s*(?:insured|assured)\\s*on\\s*death", "(?:basic|base)\\s*sum\\s*(?:insured|assured)", "sum\\s*(?:insured|assured)(?!\\s*(?:per|for each))", "life\\s*cover", "cover(?:age)?\\s*amount", "insured\\s*declared\\s*value", "\\bIDV\\b"], "money",
    { min: 10000, not: /(building|contents?|rider|accidental[\w\s]*|critical[\w\s]*)\s*$/i }) || amountAfter(/\b(basic |base )?sum\s+(insured|assured)(\s+on\s+death)?\b(?!\s*(per|for each))/i, 10000) || amountAfter(/\b(life cover|cover(age)? amount|insured declared value|\bidv\b)/i, 10000) || undefined
  // premium: the total including GST if it's given
  out.premium = g((out.kind === "receipt" ? ["total\\s*amount\\s*received", "amount\\s*received", "premium\\s*received", "total\\s*amount(?:\\s*paid)?", "amount\\s*paid"] : [])
      .concat(["total\\s*premium\\s*(?:payable|amount)?\\s*(?:\\(?incl[^)\\n]*\\)?)?", "gross\\s*premium", "total\\s*(?:installment|instalment)\\s*premium", "(?:installment|instalment|modal)\\s*premium", "renewal\\s*premium", "premium\\s*payable", "premium\\s*amount", "premium(?!\\s*(?:paying|payment)?\\s*(?:term|frequency|mode|due))"]),
    "money", { min: 100, not: /(basic|base|net|rider|annuali[sz]ed|first\s*year)\s*$/i }) || amountAfter(/\b(total (installment |instalment )?premium( payable| amount)?( \(?incl\w*[^)]*\)?)?|gross premium|installment premium|instalment premium|modal premium|premium amount|renewal premium|premium payable)\b/i, 100)
    || amountAfter(/\bpremium\b(?!\s*(paying|payment) term)/i, 100) || undefined
  if (out.kind === "receipt") { const r = flat.match(new RegExp(`received[^\\n]{0,40}?(?:₹|rs\\.?|inr)\\s*([\\d,]+(?:\\.\\d+)?)[^\\n]{0,80}?\\bon\\s+${DATE}`, "i")); if (r) { out.premium = Number(r[1].replace(/,/g, "")); out.paidDay = day(r[2]) } }
  if (out.kind === "receipt" && !out.paidDay) out.paidDay = g(["receipt\\s*date", "date\\s*of\\s*receipt", "(?:premium\\s*)?pa(?:id|yment)\\s*(?:on|date)", "date\\s*of\\s*payment", "transaction\\s*date", "received\\s*on"], "date") || undefined
  // frequency
  const fr = after(/\b(premium (payment )?(frequency|mode)|mode of (premium )?payment|payment (frequency|mode)|frequency)\b/i)
  const FQ = /\b(monthly|quarterly|half[- ]?yearly|semi[- ]?annual|annual(ly)?|yearly|single|yly|hly|qly|mly)\b/i
  const frKV = g(["premium\\s*(?:payment|paying)?\\s*(?:frequency|mode)", "payment\\s*(?:frequency|mode)", "mode\\s*of\\s*(?:premium\\s*)?payment", "\\bmode\\b", "frequency"], "text", { test: v => FQ.test(v) })
  const fq = ((frKV || "").match(FQ) || [])[0] || (fr.match(/monthly|quarterly|half[- ]?yearly|semi[- ]?annual|annual(ly)?|yearly|single/i) || [])[0] || (flat.match(/\b(monthly|quarterly|half[- ]yearly|yearly|annual) (mode|premium)\b/i) || [])[1] || ""
  if (fq) out.freq = /month|^mly$/i.test(fq) ? "Monthly" : /quarter|^qly$/i.test(fq) ? "Quarterly" : /half|semi|^hly$/i.test(fq) ? "Half-yearly" : /single/i.test(fq) ? "Single" : "Yearly"
  else if (/single premium/i.test(flat)) out.freq = "Single"
  else if (out.type === "Health" || out.type === "Motor") out.freq = "Yearly"
  // dates
  const period = flat.match(new RegExp(`(?:policy period|period of (?:insurance|cover)|policy duration)[^\\n]{0,40}?(?:from)?\\s*:?\\s*${DATE}[^\\n]{0,30}?(?:to|till|-)\\s*(?:midnight of\\s*)?${DATE}`, "i"))
  if (period) { out.start = day(period[1]); out.ends = day(period[2]) }
  const dAfter = re => { const v = after(re); const m = v.match(new RegExp(DATE)); return m ? day(m[1]) : "" }
  out.start = out.start || g(["policy\\s*(?:start|commencement|inception)\\s*date", "date\\s*of\\s*(?:risk\\s*)?commencement(?:\\s*of\\s*(?:risk|policy|cover))?", "risk\\s*commencement\\s*date", "commencement\\s*date", "inception\\s*date", "policy\\s*issue\\s*date", "start\\s*date", "(?:period|cover)\\s*from"], "date") || dAfter(/\b(policy (start|commencement|inception) date|date of (risk )?commencement|risk commencement date|commencement date|start date|policy issue date|issue date)\b/i)
  const endD = g(["policy\\s*(?:end|expiry)\\s*date", "expiry\\s*date", "date\\s*of\\s*expiry", "end\\s*date", "valid\\s*(?:till|upto|until)", "maturity\\s*date", "date\\s*of\\s*maturity"], "date") || dAfter(/\b(policy (end|expiry) date|expiry date|date of expiry|maturity date|date of maturity|policy end date|end date|valid (till|upto|until))\b/i)
  if (endD) out.ends = endD
  out.due = g(["next\\s*(?:premium\\s*)?due\\s*date", "next\\s*due", "due\\s*date\\s*of\\s*next\\s*premium", "next\\s*renewal(?:\\s*date)?", "renewal\\s*(?:due\\s*)?date"], "date") || dAfter(/\b(next (premium )?due date|premium due date|due date of (next )?premium|renewal (due )?date|next renewal)\b/i)
  if (!out.due) {
    // a plain "Due Date": on a receipt it's the instalment just paid, so the next one is a period later
    let dd = g(["premium\\s*due\\s*date", "due\\s*date"], "date", { not: /(last|first|final)\s*(premium\s*)?$/i })
    const step = { Monthly: 1, Quarterly: 3, "Half-yearly": 6, Yearly: 12 }[out.freq]
    if (dd && out.kind === "receipt") { if (step) { const [y, m, d] = dd.split("-").map(Number), t = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1 + step, 1)), last = new Date(Date.UTC(t.getUTCFullYear(), t.getUTCMonth() + 1, 0)).getUTCDate()
      dd = `${t.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(t.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(Math.min(d, last)).padStart(2, "0")}` } else dd = "" }
    if (dd) out.due = dd
  }
  if (!out.due && out.ends && (out.type === "Health" || out.type === "Motor" || out.type === "Home") && out.freq !== "Single") out.due = out.ends
  if (out.kind === "receipt") delete out.ends
  const monYear = iso => { if (!iso) return ""; const [y, m] = iso.split("-").map(Number); return new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, 1)).toLocaleDateString("en-IN", { month: "short", year: "numeric", timeZone: "UTC" }) }
  const lastPrem = dAfter(/\b(due date of last premium|last premium due|premium paying term (ends|till|upto))\b/i)
  // who's covered: relationships only
  const rels = []
  const REL = ["Self", "Spouse", "Wife", "Husband", "Son", "Daughter", "Father", "Mother", "Father-in-law", "Mother-in-law", "Brother", "Sister"]
  const memStart = lines.findIndex(l => /insured (person|member|details)s?|details of (the )?insured|members? covered|persons? (insured|covered)|relationship (with|to) (the )?(proposer|policyholder)/i.test(l))
  if (memStart >= 0) for (const l of lines.slice(memStart, memStart + 25)) for (const r of REL) if (new RegExp(`\\b${r}\\b`, "i").test(l) && !rels.includes(r)) rels.push(r)
  const covers = rels.map(r => r === "Wife" || r === "Husband" ? "Spouse" : r).filter((r, i, a) => a.indexOf(r) === i)
  if (covers.length) out.covers = covers.join(", ")
  else if ((out.type === "Term life" || out.type === "Life") && out.kind === "schedule") out.covers = "Self"
  // benefits, best-effort
  const ben = []
  const add = s => { s = s.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim(); if (s && s.length <= 90 && !ben.some(b => b.toLowerCase() === s.toLowerCase())) ben.push(s) }
  const inrS = n => n >= 1e7 ? `₹${+(n / 1e7).toFixed(2)} Cr` : n >= 1e5 ? `₹${+(n / 1e5).toFixed(1)} L` : "₹" + Math.round(n).toLocaleString("en-IN")
  const plusMonths = (iso, n) => { if (!iso) return ""; const [y, m] = iso.split("-").map(Number), d = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1 + n, 1)); return d.toLocaleDateString("en-IN", { month: "short", year: "numeric", timeZone: "UTC" }) }
  const months = s => { const m = String(s).match(/(\d{1,3})\s*(months?|years?|yrs?|days?)/i); if (!m) return 0; return /day/i.test(m[2]) ? Math.round(+m[1] / 30) : /y/i.test(m[2]) ? +m[1] * 12 : +m[1] }
  const bonus = after(/\b(cumulative bonus|no[- ]claim bonus|ncb|loyalty bonus|bonus sum insured)\b/i)
  if (bonus) { const p = bonus.match(/(\d{1,3})\s*%/), a = money(bonus); add(p ? `No-claim bonus ${p[1]}%` : a >= 10000 ? `No-claim bonus ${inrS(a)}` : "") }
  const room = after(/\broom rent\b/i)
  if (room && !/^\d+$/.test(room)) add(`Room rent: ${room.replace(/[|\t].*$/, "").slice(0, 60)}`)
  const copay = after(/\bco-?pay(ment)?\b/i)
  if (copay) { const p = copay.match(/(\d{1,2})\s*%/); add(p && +p[1] > 0 ? `Co-pay ${p[1]}%` : /\b(nil|no|not applicable|0\s*%)\b/i.test(copay) ? "No co-pay" : "") }
  const ded = amountAfter(/\b(aggregate )?deductible\b/i, 10000)
  if (ded) add(`Deductible ${inrS(ded)} (top-up)`)
  const ped = after(/\bpre-?existing (disease|illness|condition)s?\b[^\n]{0,30}?(waiting)?/i), pedM = months(ped)
  if (pedM) add(`Pre-existing illnesses covered after ${pedM >= 12 && pedM % 12 === 0 ? pedM / 12 + " years" : pedM + " months"}${out.start ? ` (from ${plusMonths(out.start, pedM)})` : ""}`)
  const spec = after(/\bspecific (disease|illness|ailment|treatment)s?\b[^\n]{0,20}?(waiting)?/i), specM = months(spec)
  if (specM) add(`Specific illnesses waiting period ${specM >= 12 && specM % 12 === 0 ? specM / 12 + " years" : specM + " months"}${out.start ? ` (till ${plusMonths(out.start, specM)})` : ""}`)
  const pre = flat.match(/pre[- ]hospitali[sz]ation[^\n]{0,40}?(\d{2,3})\s*days/i), post = flat.match(/post[- ]hospitali[sz]ation[^\n]{0,40}?(\d{2,3})\s*days/i)
  if (pre || post) add(`Hospital expenses ${pre ? pre[1] + " days before" : ""}${pre && post ? " and " : ""}${post ? post[1] + " days after" : ""} a stay`)
  if (/(free|preventive|annual) health check[- ]?up/i.test(flat)) add("Free health check-up")
  if (/\b(restore|recharge|reinstatement|refill|reload) (benefit|of sum insured)|automatic restoration/i.test(flat)) add("Restore benefit: cover refills after a claim")
  if (/\bmaternity\b[^\n]{0,30}(covered|benefit|yes)/i.test(flat)) add("Maternity covered")
  if (/\bayush\b/i.test(flat) && out.type === "Health") add("AYUSH treatment covered")
  if (/terminal illness/i.test(flat) && /life/i.test(out.type)) add("Terminal illness benefit")
  if (/waiver of premium/i.test(flat)) add("Waiver of premium")
  for (const [re, label] of [[/accidental death( benefit)?( rider)?/i, "Accidental death rider"], [/critical illness( benefit)?( rider)?/i, "Critical illness rider"], [/accidental (total and permanent )?disability/i, "Accidental disability rider"]]) {
    const v = amountAfter(re, 10000); if (v) add(`${label} ${inrS(v)}`) }
  const pt = after(/\bpolicy term\b/i), ppt = after(/\bpremium pay(ing|ment) term\b/i)
  if (months(pt) >= 12 && /life/i.test(out.type)) add(`Policy term ${Math.round(months(pt) / 12)} years`)
  if (months(ppt) >= 12 && /life/i.test(out.type)) add(`Premiums for ${Math.round(months(ppt) / 12)} years${lastPrem ? ` (last one ${monYear(lastPrem)})` : ""}`)
  const ncbMotor = after(/\bno claim bonus\b|\bncb\b/i)
  if (out.type === "Motor" && /\d+\s*%/.test(ncbMotor)) add(`NCB ${ncbMotor.match(/\d+\s*%/)[0]}`)
  const bld = amountAfter(/\bbuilding (sum insured|si|value)\b/i, 10000), cnt = amountAfter(/\bcontents? (sum insured|si|value)\b/i, 10000)
  if (bld) add(`Building cover ${inrS(bld)}`); if (cnt) add(`Contents cover ${inrS(cnt)}`)
  if (ben.length) out.benefits = ben.slice(0, 12)
  for (const k of Object.keys(out)) if (out[k] === undefined || out[k] === "" || out[k] === 0) delete out[k]
  const useful = ["cover", "premium", "due", "ends", "benefits", "last4"].filter(k => out[k]).length
  return useful >= 2 || (out.kind === "receipt" && out.premium) ? out : null
}

// ---------- insurance: your policies, premiums due and what each covers ----------
// The Insurance table sits next to your Expenses table. Your Google script fills it from insurers' emails (receipts,
// due and renewal notices); you can add or edit policies on the Loans & insurance tab. Payments are also spotted in
// your bank and card alerts by the insurer's name and the premium amount.
const POLICIES_KEY = "expenses_policies_database"
let POLICIES_ID = Keychain.contains(POLICIES_KEY) ? Keychain.get(POLICIES_KEY) : ""
const POLICY_MONTHS = { Monthly: 1, Quarterly: 3, "Half-yearly": 6, Yearly: 12, Single: 0 }
function addMonthsIso(iso, n) {
  const [y, m, d] = iso.split("-").map(Number), t = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1 + n, 1)), yy = t.getUTCFullYear(), mm = t.getUTCMonth() + 1
  return `${yy}-${String(mm).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(Math.min(d, new Date(Date.UTC(yy, mm, 0)).getUTCDate())).padStart(2, "0")}`
}
const daysBetween = (a, b) => Math.round((Date.parse(b + "T00:00:00Z") - Date.parse(a + "T00:00:00Z")) / 864e5)
async function fetchPolicies(tok) {
  if (!POLICIES_ID) return []
  const res = await notion(tok, "POST", `databases/${POLICIES_ID}/query`, { page_size: 100 })
  const d = x => x?.date?.start?.slice(0, 10) || ""
  return (res.results || []).map(pg => { const p = pg.properties || {}
    return { id: pg.id, url: pg.url, name: text(p.Policy) || "Policy", insurer: text(p.Insurer), type: p.Type?.select?.name || "Other", last4: text(p["Policy no"]).slice(-4),
      cover: p.Cover?.number || 0, premium: p.Premium?.number || 0, freq: p.Frequency?.select?.name || "", due: d(p["Next due"]), grace: p["Grace days"]?.number ?? null,
      autopay: !!p["Auto-pay"]?.checkbox, lastPaid: d(p["Last paid"]), lastPaidAmount: p["Last paid amount"]?.number || 0, start: d(p.Start), ends: d(p.Ends),
      covers: text(p.Covers), benefits: text(p.Benefits), match: text(p.Match) || text(p.Insurer), active: !!p.Active?.checkbox, suggested: !!p.Suggested?.checkbox, notes: text(p.Notes),
      docs: (() => { try { return JSON.parse(text(p["Doc figures"]) || "[]") } catch (e) { return [] } })() } })
    .filter(p => p.active || p.suggested)
}
// premiums seen in your alerts: the insurer's name in the payee, and the premium amount (within 3%)
// Match can list alternatives, comma-separated ("Tata AIA, Policybazaar"): a premium bought through a broker is debited in the broker's name.
const BROKERS = /policybazaar|insurancedekho|ditto|turtlemint|beshak|coverfox|renewbuy/i
function policyPayments(p, alerts) {
  const alts = String(p.match || "").split(/[,;\n]+/).map(m => m.toLowerCase().split(/[^a-z0-9]+/).filter(w => w.length >= 3 && !/^(insurance|company|limited|ltd|general|life)$/.test(w))).filter(w => w.length)
  const amountOk = a => p.premium ? Math.abs(a.amount - p.premium) <= p.premium * 0.03 : true
  return (alerts || []).filter(a => a.type !== "Refund" && a.type !== "Reversal" && a.type !== "Received" && a.amount > 0 && amountOk(a) &&
      (alts.some(words => words.every(w => new RegExp("(^|[^a-z0-9])" + w.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, ""), "i").test(String(a.merchant || "")))) || (p.premium && BROKERS.test(a.merchant || "") && Math.abs(a.amount - p.premium) <= p.premium * 0.01)))
    .map(a => ({ day: a.day, amount: a.amount, card: a.card || "" }))
}
// ---------- premiums seen in your bank alerts (LIC and others whose emails carry no policy number) ----------
// The last 15 months of insurance payments from CC Transactions, read at most every 12 hours. They mark premiums paid
// (yearly ones too), and premiums to an insurer you aren't tracking yet are offered as policies to add.
const INS_ALERTS_FILE = "insurance-alerts.json", POLICY_IGNORE_KEY = "expenses.policyIgnore"
async function fetchInsuranceAlerts(tok) {
  const path = fm.joinPath(fm.documentsDirectory(), INS_ALERTS_FILE)
  try { if (fm.fileExists(path)) { const c = JSON.parse(fm.readString(path)); if (Date.now() - c.at < 12 * 36e5 && !refreshFull) return c.list } } catch (e) {}
  if (!ALERTS_ID) return []
  const out = []
  let cursor = null, pages = 0
  const t = w => ({ property: "Merchant", title: { contains: w } })
  do {
    const res = await notion(tok, "POST", `databases/${ALERTS_ID}/query`, Object.assign({ page_size: 100, filter: { and: [
      { property: "Date", date: { on_or_after: shiftDay(istDay(Date.now()), -460) } }, { property: "Status", select: { does_not_equal: "Duplicate" } },
      { or: [{ property: "Category", select: { equals: "LIC" } }, { property: "Category", select: { equals: "Health insurance" } }, t("LIC"), t("Life Insurance"), t("insurance"), t("Insurance"), t("Policybazaar"), t("POLICYBAZAAR")] }] } },
      cursor ? { start_cursor: cursor } : {}))
    for (const pg of res.results || []) { const p = pg.properties || {}, start = p.Date?.date?.start || pg.created_time
      out.push({ id: pg.id, day: istDay(Date.parse(start.length === 10 ? start + "T12:00:00+05:30" : start)), amount: p.Amount?.number || 0, merchant: text(p.Merchant),
        type: p.Type?.select?.name || "Spend", card: p.Card?.select?.name || "", category: p.Category?.select?.name || "", ignored: !!p.Ignored?.checkbox }) }
    cursor = res.has_more ? res.next_cursor : null
  } while (cursor && ++pages < 4)
  try { fm.writeString(path, JSON.stringify({ at: Date.now(), list: out })) } catch (e) {}
  return out
}
function insurerOfPayee(m) {
  m = String(m || "")
  if (/\blic\b|life insurance corp|lifeincorp/i.test(m)) return "LIC"
  if (BROKERS.test(m)) return (m.match(BROKERS) || ["Policybazaar"])[0].replace(/^\w/, c => c.toUpperCase()).replace(/^Policybazaar.*/i, "Policybazaar")
  const k = [[/star health/i, "Star Health"], [/hdfc ergo/i, "HDFC ERGO"], [/hdfc life/i, "HDFC Life"], [/icici lombard/i, "ICICI Lombard"], [/icici pru/i, "ICICI Pru Life"], [/niva bupa|max bupa/i, "Niva Bupa"],
    [/care health|religare/i, "Care Health"], [/tata aia/i, "Tata AIA Life"], [/tata aig/i, "Tata AIG"], [/max life/i, "Axis Max Life"], [/sbi life/i, "SBI Life"], [/bajaj/i, "Bajaj Allianz"], [/aditya birla/i, "Aditya Birla"], [/digit/i, "Digit"], [/acko/i, "Acko"]].find(([re]) => re.test(m))
  return k ? k[1] : ""
}
function policySuggestions(policies, insAlerts) {
  let ignored = []; try { ignored = Keychain.contains(POLICY_IGNORE_KEY) ? JSON.parse(Keychain.get(POLICY_IGNORE_KEY)) : [] } catch (e) {}
  const pays = (insAlerts || []).filter(a => a.type === "Spend" && a.amount >= 300 && !a.ignored)
  const claimed = new Set(); for (const p of policies) for (const x of policyPayments(p, pays)) claimed.add(x.day + "|" + x.amount)
  const groups = []
  for (const a of pays.filter(a => !claimed.has(a.day + "|" + a.amount)).sort((x, y) => x.day < y.day ? -1 : 1)) {
    const ins = insurerOfPayee(a.merchant) || (a.category === "LIC" ? "LIC" : ""); if (!ins) continue
    let g = groups.find(g => g.insurer === ins && Math.abs(g.amount - a.amount) <= Math.max(g.amount, a.amount) * 0.03)
    if (!g) groups.push(g = { insurer: ins, amount: a.amount, pays: [] })
    g.pays.push(a); g.amount = Math.max(g.amount, a.amount)
  }
  const today = istDay(Date.now())
  return groups.map(g => {
    const days = g.pays.map(p => p.day), gaps = days.slice(1).map((d, i) => daysBetween(days[i], d)).sort((a, b) => a - b), gap = gaps.length ? gaps[Math.floor(gaps.length / 2)] : 0
    const freq = !gap ? "Yearly" : gap < 45 ? "Monthly" : gap < 120 ? "Quarterly" : gap < 240 ? "Half-yearly" : "Yearly"
    const last = days[days.length - 1]
    let due = addMonthsIso(last, POLICY_MONTHS[freq]); while (due < shiftDay(today, -40)) due = addMonthsIso(due, POLICY_MONTHS[freq])
    const sig = `${g.insurer}|${Math.round(g.amount / 100)}`
    return { sig, insurer: g.insurer, amount: Math.round(g.amount * 100) / 100, freq, guessed: !gap, last, due, count: g.pays.length, payee: g.pays[g.pays.length - 1].merchant }
  }).filter(s => !ignored.includes(s.sig) && daysBetween(s.last, today) <= 430 && !(s.freq === "Monthly" && s.count < 2))
}
function policyView(list, alerts) {
  const today = istDay(Date.now())
  return list.map(p => {
    const months = POLICY_MONTHS[p.freq] ?? 0, pays = policyPayments(p, alerts)
    let lastPaid = p.lastPaid, lastAmt = p.lastPaidAmount
    for (const x of pays) if (!lastPaid || x.day > lastPaid) { lastPaid = x.day; lastAmt = x.amount }
    // a premium paid up to 25 days before its due date (or any time after) settles it; the next one is a period later
    let due = p.due
    if (due && months) for (let i = 0; i < 120 && lastPaid && lastPaid >= shiftDay(due, -25); i++) due = addMonthsIso(due, months)
    const grace = p.grace != null ? p.grace : months === 1 ? 15 : 30
    let state = "ok", daysTo = due ? daysBetween(today, due) : null
    if (p.ends && p.ends < today) state = "ended"
    else if (!months && p.freq) state = "single"
    else if (!due) state = "nodate"
    else if (daysTo < 0) state = -daysTo <= grace ? "grace" : "overdue"
    else if (p.autopay) state = daysTo <= 3 ? "autosoon" : "auto"
    else if (daysTo === 0) state = "today"
    else if (daysTo <= 15) state = "soon"
    const yearly = months ? Math.round(p.premium * 12 / months) : 0
    return Object.assign({}, p, { due, grace, daysTo, state, lastPaid, lastPaidAmount: lastAmt, payments: pays.slice(-6), yearly, graceEnds: due ? shiftDay(due, grace) : "" })
  })
}
// Phone reminders before a premium is due (none for auto-pay, just a heads-up the day before).
async function scheduleInsuranceReminders(views) {
  try {
    const pend = await Notification.allPending()
    const ours = pend.filter(n => n.threadIdentifier === "insurance").map(n => n.identifier)
    if (ours.length) Notification.removePending(ours)
    const at = (iso, h) => { const d = new Date(iso + "T00:00:00"); d.setHours(h, 0, 0, 0); return d }
    for (const p of views) {
      if (!p.due || ["ended", "single"].includes(p.state)) continue
      const when = p.autopay ? [[shiftDay(p.due, -1), `${p.name}: ${inrText(p.premium)} auto-debits tomorrow`, "Keep enough balance on the card or account it's set up on."]]
        : [[shiftDay(p.due, -15), `${p.name}: premium due in 15 days`, `${inrText(p.premium)} due on ${p.due}.`], [shiftDay(p.due, -2), `${p.name}: premium due in 2 days`, `${inrText(p.premium)} due on ${p.due}.`],
           [shiftDay(p.graceEnds, -3), `${p.name}: grace period ends in 3 days`, "Pay now to keep the policy (and its benefits) running."]]
      for (const [day, title, body] of when) {
        const t = at(day, 9)
        if (t <= new Date()) continue
        const n = new Notification(); n.title = title; n.body = body; n.threadIdentifier = "insurance"; n.identifier = `ins-${p.id}-${day}`
        n.setTriggerDate(t); await n.schedule()
      }
    }
  } catch (e) { log(`insurance reminders: ${e.message || e}`) }
}
const inrText = n => "₹" + Math.round(n || 0).toLocaleString("en-IN")
function policyColumns() {
  const opts = names => ({ select: { options: names.map(name => ({ name })) } }), rupee = { number: { format: "rupee" } }
  return { Policy: { title: {} }, Insurer: { rich_text: {} }, Type: opts(["Term life", "Life", "Health", "Home", "Motor", "Other"]), "Policy no": { rich_text: {} },
    Cover: rupee, Premium: rupee, Frequency: opts(Object.keys(POLICY_MONTHS)), "Next due": { date: {} }, "Grace days": { number: {} }, "Auto-pay": { checkbox: {} },
    "Last paid": { date: {} }, "Last paid amount": rupee, Start: { date: {} }, Ends: { date: {} }, Covers: { rich_text: {} }, Benefits: { rich_text: {} },
    Match: { rich_text: {} }, Active: { checkbox: {} }, Suggested: { checkbox: {} }, Notes: { rich_text: {} }, "Doc figures": { rich_text: {} } }
}
async function createPoliciesTable(tok) {
  // next to Expenses, or next to Loans or People when Expenses sits at the top of the workspace (the API can't add tables there)
  let page = ""
  for (const id of [DATABASE_ID, LOANS_ID, PEOPLE_ID].filter(Boolean)) {
    try { const d = await notion(tok, "GET", `databases/${id}`); if (d.parent && d.parent.type === "page_id") { page = d.parent.page_id; break } } catch (e) {}
  }
  if (!page) throw new Error("Couldn't add an Insurance table automatically: your tables sit at the top of your Notion workspace. Make a page (say, Money), add a table called Insurance inside it, connect your integration (••• › Connections), then try again.")
  const res = await notion(tok, "POST", "databases", { parent: { type: "page_id", page_id: page }, title: [{ type: "text", text: { content: "Insurance" } }], properties: policyColumns() })
  POLICIES_ID = res.id.replace(/-/g, "")
  Keychain.set(POLICIES_KEY, POLICIES_ID)
  return POLICIES_ID
}
async function policyAction(kind, data) {
  while (refreshing) await sleep(200)
  try {
    const tok = await token()
    if (!tok || !DATABASE_ID) throw new Error("Finish setup first: the dashboard needs your Notion token and database link.")
    const rtx = v => ({ rich_text: v ? [{ text: { content: String(v).slice(0, 1900) } }] : [] })
    if (kind === "policy-save") {
      if (!POLICIES_ID) await createPoliciesTable(tok)
      const props = {
        Policy: { title: [{ text: { content: String(data.name || "Policy").slice(0, 120) } }] }, Insurer: rtx(data.insurer), Type: { select: { name: data.type || "Other" } },
        "Policy no": rtx(String(data.last4 || "").replace(/\D/g, "").slice(-4)), Cover: { number: data.cover > 0 ? data.cover : null }, Premium: { number: data.premium > 0 ? data.premium : null },
        Frequency: data.freq ? { select: { name: data.freq } } : { select: null }, "Next due": { date: data.due ? { start: data.due } : null },
        "Grace days": { number: data.grace >= 0 && data.grace !== "" && data.grace != null ? Number(data.grace) : null }, "Auto-pay": { checkbox: !!data.autopay },
        Ends: { date: data.ends ? { start: data.ends } : null }, Covers: rtx(data.covers), Benefits: rtx(data.benefits), Match: rtx(data.match || data.insurer),
        Active: { checkbox: true }, Suggested: { checkbox: false },
      }
      if (data.id) await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { properties: props })
      else await notion(tok, "POST", "pages", { parent: { database_id: POLICIES_ID }, properties: props })
      extraMessage = data.id ? `Saved ${data.name}.` : `Added ${data.name}.`
    } else if (kind === "policy-paid") {
      const p = (latest && latest.meta && latest.meta.check && latest.meta.check.policies || []).find(x => x.id === data.id) || {}
      const months = POLICY_MONTHS[p.freq] ?? 0, day = data.day || istDay(Date.now())
      const props = { "Last paid": { date: { start: day } }, "Last paid amount": { number: Number(data.amount) || p.premium || null } }
      if (p.due && months) { let due = p.due; while (day >= shiftDay(due, -25)) due = addMonthsIso(due, months); props["Next due"] = { date: { start: due } } }
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { properties: props })
      extraMessage = `Marked ${p.name || "the premium"} paid.`
    } else if (kind === "policy-accept") {
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { properties: { Active: { checkbox: true }, Suggested: { checkbox: false } } })
      extraMessage = "Added to your policies."
    } else if (kind === "policy-ignore") {
      let ig = []; try { ig = Keychain.contains(POLICY_IGNORE_KEY) ? JSON.parse(Keychain.get(POLICY_IGNORE_KEY)) : [] } catch (e) {}
      Keychain.set(POLICY_IGNORE_KEY, JSON.stringify(ig.concat([data.sig]).slice(-60)))
      extraMessage = "Got it. Those payments won't be suggested again."
    } else if (kind === "policy-dismiss" || kind === "policy-delete") {
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { archived: true })
      extraMessage = kind === "policy-delete" ? "Policy removed." : "Dismissed."
    }
  } catch (e) {
    log(`policy action failed (${kind}): ${e.message || e}`)
    extraMessage = null
    await refresh()
    await js(`window.setData && window.setData(${safe(latest ? latest.rows : [])}, ${safe(Object.assign({}, latest ? latest.meta : {}, { error: friendly(e) }))})`)
    return
  }
  await refresh()
}


// ---------- insurance documents: a policy schedule, renewal notice or receipt (PDF) ----------
// Read on this iPhone (password-protected PDFs too: you type the password once), the figures shown on the policy to
// confirm with "Use these". A copy of the PDF is kept in iCloud Drive › Scriptable › Insurance, so it opens from the
// policy when you need it (for a claim, say). Scans go to your Google script, which can read them.
let policyDocColOk = false
async function ensurePolicyDocColumn(tok) {
  if (policyDocColOk || !POLICIES_ID) return
  const db = await notion(tok, "GET", `databases/${POLICIES_ID}`)
  if (!(db.properties || {})["Doc figures"]) await notion(tok, "PATCH", `databases/${POLICIES_ID}`, { properties: { "Doc figures": { rich_text: {} } } })
  policyDocColOk = true
}
function keepPolicyPdf(path) {
  try {
    const dir = icloud.joinPath(icloud.documentsDirectory(), "Insurance")
    if (!icloud.fileExists(dir)) icloud.createDirectory(dir, true)
    let name = path.split("/").pop().replace(/[^\w .()-]+/g, "_"), dest = icloud.joinPath(dir, name), n = 1
    while (icloud.fileExists(dest)) dest = icloud.joinPath(dir, name.replace(/(\.pdf)?$/i, ` (${++n}).pdf`))
    icloud.copy(path, dest)
    return "Insurance/" + dest.split("/").pop()
  } catch (e) { log(`keep pdf: ${e.message || e}`); return "" }
}
// figures → Notion properties (only what the document gave)
function policyPropsFrom(f) {
  const rtx = v => ({ rich_text: [{ text: { content: String(v).slice(0, 1900) } }] }), props = {}
  if (f.cover) props.Cover = { number: f.cover }
  if (f.premium && f.kind !== "receipt") props.Premium = { number: f.premium }
  if (f.freq) props.Frequency = { select: { name: f.freq } }
  if (f.due) props["Next due"] = { date: { start: f.due } }
  if (f.ends) props.Ends = { date: { start: f.ends } }
  if (f.start) props.Start = { date: { start: f.start } }
  if (f.covers) props.Covers = rtx(f.covers)
  if (f.last4) props["Policy no"] = rtx(f.last4)
  if (f.insurer) props.Insurer = rtx(f.insurer)
  if (f.type && f.type !== "Other") props.Type = { select: { name: f.type } }
  if (f.kind === "receipt" && f.paidDay) { props["Last paid"] = { date: { start: f.paidDay } }; props["Last paid amount"] = { number: f.premium || null } }
  return props
}
const docsText = docs => {
  // the newest documents first; figures already used are dropped to fit Notion's 2,000-character limit
  let list = docs.slice(-6)
  for (let i = 0; JSON.stringify(list).length > 1990 && i < list.length; i++) if (list[i].used) delete list[i].fig
  while (JSON.stringify(list).length > 1990 && list.length > 1) list = list.slice(1)
  return JSON.stringify(list).slice(0, 1990)
}
async function savePolicyDoc(tok, policy, entry) {
  const pg = await notion(tok, "GET", `pages/${policy.id}`)
  let docs = []; try { docs = JSON.parse(((pg.properties["Doc figures"] || {}).rich_text || []).map(t => t.plain_text).join("") || "[]") } catch (e) {}
  docs = docs.filter(d => d.file !== entry.file).concat([entry])
  await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${policy.id}`, { properties: { "Doc figures": { rich_text: [{ text: { content: docsText(docs) } }] } } })
}
async function uploadPolicyScans(paths, link) {
  const req = new Request(link); req.method = "POST"; req.timeoutInterval = 180
  req.headers = { "Content-Type": "application/json" }; req.body = JSON.stringify({ kind: "policy", files: paths.map(p => ({ name: p.split("/").pop(), b64: Data.fromFile(p).toBase64String() })) })
  let res; try { res = JSON.parse(await req.loadString()) } catch (e) { throw new Error("Your Google script didn't read it. Update the Card Alerts Sync code and redeploy the web app (Deploy › Manage deployments › New version).") }
  if (!res || !res.ok) throw new Error((res && res.error) || "Your Google script didn't accept the upload.")
  return res.results || []
}
const coverText = n => n >= 1e7 ? `₹${+(n / 1e7).toFixed(2)} Cr` : n >= 1e5 ? `₹${+(n / 1e5).toFixed(1)} L` : inrText(n)
const docSummary = f => [f.cover ? `cover ${coverText(f.cover)}` : "", f.premium ? `premium ${inrText(f.premium)}${f.freq ? " " + f.freq.toLowerCase() : ""}` : "",
  f.due ? `next due ${f.due}` : "", f.covers ? f.covers : "", f.benefits ? `${f.benefits.length} benefit${f.benefits.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}` : ""].filter(Boolean).join(", ")
async function addPolicyDocs(policyId) {
  let paths = []
  try { paths = await DocumentPicker.open(["com.adobe.pdf"]) } catch (e) { return null }
  if (!paths || !paths.length) return null
  const tok = await token()
  if (!POLICIES_ID) await createPoliciesTable(tok)
  await ensurePolicyDocColumn(tok)
  const pols = ((latest && latest.meta && latest.meta.check && latest.meta.check.policies) || []).filter(p => p.id)
  const today = istDay(Date.now()), lines = [], scans = []
  const handle = async (name, path, fig) => {
    let pol = policyId ? pols.find(p => p.id === policyId) : null
    if (!pol && fig && fig.last4) pol = pols.find(p => p.last4 === fig.last4 && (!fig.insurer || !p.insurer || p.insurer.split(" ")[0].toLowerCase() === fig.insurer.split(" ")[0].toLowerCase()))
    if (!pol) {
      const a = new Alert(); a.title = "Which policy is this?"; a.message = `${name}${fig && fig.insurer ? ` (${fig.insurer})` : ""}`
      for (const p of pols) a.addAction(p.name)
      a.addAction("A new policy"); a.addCancelAction("Skip")
      const k = await a.presentAlert()
      if (k === -1) { lines.push(`${name}: skipped.`); return }
      pol = pols[k] || null
    }
    const saved = path ? keepPolicyPdf(path) : ""
    if (!pol) {
      // a new policy straight from the document
      const props = Object.assign(policyPropsFrom(fig || {}), { Policy: { title: [{ text: { content: (fig && fig.insurer ? `${fig.insurer}${fig.name ? " · " + fig.name.replace(new RegExp("^" + fig.insurer.split(" ")[0] + "\\s*", "i"), "") : ""}` : fig && fig.name || name.replace(/\.pdf$/i, "")).slice(0, 120) } }] },
        Match: { rich_text: [{ text: { content: (fig && fig.insurer || "").replace(/ Life$/, "") } }] }, Active: { checkbox: true }, Suggested: { checkbox: false },
        Benefits: { rich_text: fig && fig.benefits ? [{ text: { content: fig.benefits.join("\n").slice(0, 1900) } }] : [] },
        "Doc figures": { rich_text: [{ text: { content: docsText([{ file: name.slice(0, 60), day: today, path: saved, used: true }]) } }] } })
      await notion(tok, "POST", "pages", { parent: { database_id: POLICIES_ID }, properties: props })
      lines.push(`${name}: added as a new policy${fig ? ` (${docSummary(fig)})` : ""}. Check it on the Loans & insurance tab.`)
      return
    }
    await savePolicyDoc(tok, pol, { file: name.slice(0, 60), day: today, path: saved, fig: fig || undefined, used: !fig })
    lines.push(fig ? `${name}: read for ${pol.name} (${docSummary(fig) || "a few details"}). Tap Use these on the policy to save them.` : `${name}: kept with ${pol.name}.`)
  }
  for (const p of paths) {
    const name = p.split("/").pop()
    const r = await readPdfHere(p)
    if (r.cancelled) { lines.push(`${name}: skipped.`); continue }
    if (r.error === "PasswordException") { lines.push(`${name}: the password didn't work, so it was skipped.`); continue }
    if (r.error || !r.text || r.text.replace(/\s/g, "").length < 40) { scans.push(p); continue }
    const fig = parsePolicyDoc(r.text, { filename: name })
    if (!fig) { scans.push(p); continue }          // nothing found here: Google's reader lays the page out differently, worth a second try
    await handle(name, p, fig)
  }
  if (scans.length) {
    const link = Keychain.contains(UPLOAD_KEY) ? Keychain.get(UPLOAD_KEY) : ""
    const a = new Alert(); a.title = scans.length === 1 ? "Couldn't read this one here" : `Couldn't read ${scans.length} of them here`
    a.message = `${scans.map(x => x.split("/").pop()).join(", ")}: a scan, or a layout this reader doesn't know. ` + (link ? "Your Google script reads PDFs a different way, scans included. Send it there?" : "Your Google script reads PDFs a different way, scans included, using the upload link (Loans & insurance › loan documents › Upload settings). Or keep the PDF with the policy without reading it.")
    if (link) a.addAction("Send to Google script"); a.addAction("Just keep the PDF"); a.addCancelAction("Skip")
    const k = await a.presentAlert()
    if (k !== -1) {
      let results = []
      if (link && k === 0) { try { results = await uploadPolicyScans(scans, link) } catch (e) { lines.push(e.message || String(e)) } }
      for (const p of scans) { const name = p.split("/").pop(), r = results.find(x => x.name === name) || {}
        if (r.locked) { lines.push(`${name}: it has a password Google can't open; kept without reading.`) }
        else if (link && k === 0 && !r.figures) lines.push(`${name}: no policy figures found in it, even by Google’s reader.`)
        await handle(name, p, r.figures || null) }
    }
  }
  return lines.join("\n") || null
}
async function policyDocAction(kind, data) {
  while (refreshing) await sleep(200)
  try {
    const tok = await token()
    if (kind === "policy-doc") { extraMessage = await addPolicyDocs(data.id || null); await refresh(); return }
    const pg = await notion(tok, "GET", `pages/${data.id}`), prop = pg.properties || {}
    let docs = []; try { docs = JSON.parse(((prop["Doc figures"] || {}).rich_text || []).map(t => t.plain_text).join("") || "[]") } catch (e) {}
    const d = docs.find(x => x.file === data.file)
    if (!d) throw new Error("That document isn't on this policy any more. Refresh and try again.")
    if (kind === "policy-open") {
      if (d.path) {
        const full = icloud.joinPath(icloud.documentsDirectory(), d.path)
        if (!icloud.fileExists(full)) throw new Error("The PDF isn't on this iPhone. It's kept in iCloud Drive › Scriptable › " + d.path + " on the phone that added it.")
        try { await icloud.downloadFileFromiCloud(full) } catch (e) {}
        await QuickLook.present(full, true)
      } else if (d.link) Safari.open(d.link)
      return
    }
    if (kind === "policy-docuse") {
      const f = d.fig || {}, props = policyPropsFrom(f)
      if (f.benefits && f.benefits.length) {
        const have = ((prop.Benefits || {}).rich_text || []).map(t => t.plain_text).join("").split(/\n+/).map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean)
        const merged = have.concat(f.benefits.filter(b => !have.some(h => h.toLowerCase() === b.toLowerCase())))
        props.Benefits = { rich_text: [{ text: { content: merged.join("\n").slice(0, 1900) } }] }
      }
      d.used = true; delete d.fig
      props["Doc figures"] = { rich_text: [{ text: { content: docsText(docs) } }] }
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { properties: props })
      extraMessage = "Saved the details from the document."
    } else if (kind === "policy-docskip") {
      d.used = true; delete d.fig
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { properties: { "Doc figures": { rich_text: [{ text: { content: docsText(docs) } }] } } })
      extraMessage = null
    } else if (kind === "policy-docdel") {
      if (d.path) { try { const full = icloud.joinPath(icloud.documentsDirectory(), d.path); if (icloud.fileExists(full)) icloud.remove(full) } catch (e) {} }
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${data.id}`, { properties: { "Doc figures": { rich_text: [{ text: { content: docsText(docs.filter(x => x !== d)) } }] } } })
      extraMessage = "Document removed."
    }
  } catch (e) {
    log(`policy doc (${kind}) failed: ${e.message || e}`)
    extraMessage = null
    await refresh()
    await js(`window.setData && window.setData(${safe(latest ? latest.rows : [])}, ${safe(Object.assign({}, latest ? latest.meta : {}, { error: friendly(e) }))})`)
    return
  }
  await refresh()
}
// ---------- reading a bank or credit card statement (PDF text) ----------
// Works on the text of the statement, row by row: a row is a transaction when it starts with a date and has an
// amount with paise ("1,299.00"). Credit card rows are spends unless marked Cr / "payment received" / refund.
// Bank rows use the running balance: if the balance went down, it was money out.
const ST_MON = { jan: 1, feb: 2, mar: 3, apr: 4, may: 5, jun: 6, jul: 7, aug: 8, sep: 9, oct: 10, nov: 11, dec: 12 }
const ST_MONRE = "(jan(?:uary)?|feb(?:ruary)?|mar(?:ch)?|apr(?:il)?|may|june?|july?|aug(?:ust)?|sept?(?:ember)?|oct(?:ober)?|nov(?:ember)?|dec(?:ember)?)"
const ST_BANKS = [[/american express|\bamex\b/i, "Amex"], [/hdfc/i, "HDFC"], [/icici/i, "ICICI"], [/axis/i, "Axis"], [/state bank of india|\bsbi\b/i, "SBI"], [/kotak/i, "Kotak"],
  [/idfc/i, "IDFC First"], [/yes bank/i, "Yes Bank"], [/indusind/i, "IndusInd"], [/\bau small finance|\bau bank/i, "AU"], [/\brbl\b/i, "RBL"], [/hsbc/i, "HSBC"],
  [/standard chartered/i, "Standard Chartered"], [/scapia/i, "Scapia"], [/federal bank/i, "Federal"], [/onecard|one card/i, "OneCard"], [/bank of baroda/i, "Bank of Baroda"],
  [/punjab national/i, "PNB"], [/canara/i, "Canara"], [/union bank/i, "Union Bank"], [/bandhan/i, "Bandhan"], [/idbi/i, "IDBI"], [/bank of india/i, "Bank of India"], [/bank of maharashtra/i, "Bank of Maharashtra"], [/central bank of india/i, "Central Bank"]]
const ST_IFSC = { HDFC: "HDFC", ICIC: "ICICI", UTIB: "Axis", SBIN: "SBI", KKBK: "Kotak", IDFB: "IDFC First", YESB: "Yes Bank", INDB: "IndusInd", AUBL: "AU", RATN: "RBL",
  HSBC: "HSBC", SCBL: "Standard Chartered", FDRL: "Federal", BARB: "Bank of Baroda", PUNB: "PNB", CNRB: "Canara", UBIN: "Union Bank", BDBL: "Bandhan", IBKL: "IDBI", BKID: "Bank of India", MAHB: "Bank of Maharashtra", IOBA: "Indian Overseas", IDIB: "Indian Bank", CBIN: "Central Bank" }
const stPad = n => String(n).padStart(2, "0")
// the first date at the start of a row: { iso, end } or null. year: used when the row has none (Amex "Oct 06")
function stDateAt(s, year) {
  let m
  if ((m = s.match(/^\s*(\d{1,2})[\/\-.](\d{1,2})[\/\-.](\d{4}|\d{2})(?!\d)/))) {
    const y = m[3].length === 2 ? 2000 + +m[3] : +m[3]; if (+m[2] >= 1 && +m[2] <= 12 && +m[1] >= 1 && +m[1] <= 31) return { iso: `${y}-${stPad(m[2])}-${stPad(m[1])}`, end: m[0].length }
  }
  if ((m = s.match(new RegExp("^\\s*(\\d{1,2})[\\s\\-\\/]*" + ST_MONRE + "\\b[\\s\\-\\/,']*(\\d{4}|\\d{2})?(?![\\d:])", "i")))) {
    const y = m[3] ? (m[3].length === 2 ? 2000 + +m[3] : +m[3]) : year; if (y && +m[1] >= 1 && +m[1] <= 31) return { iso: `${y}-${stPad(ST_MON[m[2].slice(0, 3).toLowerCase()])}-${stPad(m[1])}`, end: m[0].length, noYear: !m[3] }
  }
  if ((m = s.match(new RegExp("^\\s*" + ST_MONRE + "\\s+(\\d{1,2})(?:,?\\s+(\\d{4}))?\\b", "i")))) {
    const y = m[3] ? +m[3] : year; if (y && +m[2] >= 1 && +m[2] <= 31) return { iso: `${y}-${stPad(ST_MON[m[1].slice(0, 3).toLowerCase()])}-${stPad(m[2])}`, end: m[0].length, noYear: !m[3] }
  }
  if ((m = s.match(/^\s*(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})\b/))) return { iso: `${m[1]}-${m[2]}-${m[3]}`, end: m[0].length }
  return null
}
const stPlusDays = (iso, n) => new Date(Date.parse(iso + "T00:00:00Z") + n * 864e5).toISOString().slice(0, 10)
function stRowDate(line, year) {
  const d = stDateAt(line, year); if (d) return d
  const sr = line.match(/^\d{1,5}(?:\t|\s+)/); if (!sr) return null
  const d2 = stDateAt(line.slice(sr[0].length), year); return d2 ? Object.assign({}, d2, { end: d2.end + sr[0].length }) : null
}
const stClean = s => s.replace(/\t+/g, " ").replace(/\b\d{1,2}[\/\-.]\d{1,2}[\/\-.]\d{2,4}\b/g, " ").replace(/\b(?=\w*\d)\w{8,}\b/g, " ").replace(/\b(?:ref(?:erence)?\s*(?:no)?\.?|txn\s*(?:id)?|chq\.?(?:\s*no)?)\s*[:#]?\s*\w*\d\w*/gi, " ")
  .replace(/(?:\s*-){2,}/g, " ").replace(/[\s|:;,-]+$/, "").replace(/^[\s|:;,-]+/, "").replace(/\s{2,}/g, " ").trim()
const stNum = s => Number(String(s).replace(/,/g, ""))
function parseStatement(text, meta) {
  meta = meta || {}
  const raw = String(text || "").replace(/[   ]/g, " ").replace(/\r/g, "")
  const head = raw.slice(0, 4000), all = raw
  const cardScore = (head.match(/credit card|card (?:no|number)|minimum (?:amount )?due|total (?:amount )?due|credit limit|available credit|reward points|card statement/gi) || []).length
  const bankScore = (head.match(/savings|current account|account statement|statement of account|opening balance|closing balance|withdrawal|deposit|ifsc|branch|account (?:no|number)/gi) || []).length
  const kind = meta.kind || (cardScore > bankScore ? "card" : "bank")
  const firstRow = raw.split("\n").findIndex(l => stRowDate(l.trim(), 2000) && /\d\.\d{2}\b/.test(l))
  const top = firstRow > 0 ? raw.split("\n").slice(0, firstRow).join("\n").slice(0, 4000) : head
  const ifsc = (top.match(/\b([A-Z]{4})0[A-Z0-9]{6}\b/) || [])[1]
  const bankHit = ST_BANKS.find(([re]) => re.test(top)) || (ifsc && ST_IFSC[ifsc] ? [null, ST_IFSC[ifsc]] : null) || ST_BANKS.find(([re]) => re.test(meta.filename || ""))
  const out = { kind, bank: bankHit ? bankHit[1] : "", last4: "", from: "", to: "", txns: [], skipped: 0 }
  // the card or account number's last digits
  if (kind === "card") {
    const end = head.match(/card\s*(?:no|number|ending)(?:\s*in)?\.?\s*[:\-]?\s*([\dxX*•][\dxX*• -]{2,24})/i)
    const fromEnd = end && (end[1].trim().match(/(\d{4,5})(?!.*\d)/) || [])[1]
    const masked = (head.match(/(?:[\dxX*•]{4}[ -]?){2,3}[\dxX*•]{0,6}[ -]?\d{4,5}\b/g) || []).find(x => /[xX*•]/.test(x))
    out.last4 = fromEnd || (masked ? masked.match(/(\d{4,5})$/)[1] : "")
  } else {
    const m = head.match(/(?:a\/c|acct|account)\s*(?:no\.?|number)?\s*[:\-]?\s*[xX*•]*\s*(\d{6,20}|\d{4})\b/i)
    out.last4 = m ? m[1].slice(-4) : ""
  }
  // statement period, for the year of rows that don't print one
  const per = all.match(/(?:statement period|period|from)\s*[:\-]?\s*(?:from\s*)?([^\n]{6,22}?)\s*(?:\bto\b|\btill\b|\s-\s)\s*[:\-]?\s*([^\n]{6,24})/i)
  if (per) { const a = stDateAt(per[1]), b = stDateAt(per[2]); if (a && b) { out.from = a.iso; out.to = b.iso } }
  if (!out.to) { const sd = all.match(/statement date\s*[:\-]?\s*([^\n]{6,20})/i); const d = sd && stDateAt(sd[1]); if (d) out.to = d.iso }
  const yearRef = (out.to || meta.today || new Date().toISOString()).slice(0, 10)
  const year = Number(yearRef.slice(0, 4))
  const opening = (() => { const m = all.match(/opening balance[^\d\n]{0,30}([\d,]+\.\d{2})/i); return m ? stNum(m[1]) : null })()
  let prevBal = opening
  const AMT = /(?<![\w.,])(\d{1,3}(?:,\d{2,3})+\.\d{2}|\d+\.\d{2})(?![\d])(\s*(?:Cr|CR|Dr|DR|C|D)\b)?/g
  let last = null
  for (const line0 of raw.split("\n")) {
    const line = line0.replace(/\s+\|\s+/g, "\t").trim()
    const d = stRowDate(line, year)
    // no year printed ("December 28" on a January statement): a date well after the statement end is last year's
    if (d && d.noYear && d.iso > stPlusDays(yearRef, 31)) d.iso = `${+d.iso.slice(0, 4) - 1}${d.iso.slice(4)}`
    if (!d) {
      // a description that wrapped onto the next line ("UPI/.../paytm-" + "ptm/Oid223", "HITESH T" + "SUTARIYA")
      if (last && line && line.length <= 45 && !/\d[\d,]*\.\d{2}\b/.test(line) && !/\b(page|statement|balance|total|date|sr\.? ?no|remarks|narration|particulars)\b/i.test(line)) {
        last.raw += (/[-\/]$/.test(last.raw) ? "" : " ") + line; last.t.desc = (stClean(last.raw) || last.t.desc).slice(0, 120)
      }
      last = null; continue
    }
    last = null
    let rest = line.slice(d.end)
    // a second date (value date) or a time right after the first
    rest = rest.replace(/^\s*\d{1,2}:\d{2}(?::\d{2})?\s*(?:am|pm)?/i, "")
    const d2 = stDateAt(rest, year); if (d2 && d2.end <= 12) rest = rest.slice(d2.end)
    const amts = []; let m; AMT.lastIndex = 0
    while ((m = AMT.exec(rest))) amts.push({ v: stNum(m[1]), mark: (m[2] || "").trim().toUpperCase(), at: m.index })
    if (!amts.length) {
      // no amount with decimals: take a last column like "1,299" (or a plain number set off by a tab), never a bare number inside the text
      const w = rest.match(/(?<![\w.,])(\d{1,3}(?:,\d{2,3})+|(?<=\t)\d{1,6})(?![\d.,])(\s*(?:Cr|CR|Dr|DR|C|D)\b)?\s*$/)
      if (w) amts.push({ v: stNum(w[1]), mark: (w[2] || "").trim().toUpperCase(), at: w.index })
    }
    if (!amts.length) continue
    // a bracketed amount "(299.00)": the bracket is not part of the description
    const descRaw = rest.slice(0, amts[0].at).replace(/[(\[]\s*$/, "")
    const desc = stClean(descRaw)
    if (/^(opening|closing) balance|^balance (?:b\/f|c\/f|brought|carried)|^total\b|^previous (?:balance|dues)|^minimum|^b\/f|^c\/f/i.test(desc)) { if (amts.length) prevBal = amts[amts.length - 1].v; continue }
    let amount, credit, bal = null
    if (kind === "card") {
      const a = amts[amts.length - 1]; amount = a.v
      credit = a.mark === "CR" || a.mark === "C" || /\bcr\b/i.test(rest.slice(a.at)) ||
        /payment received|thank you|payment.*(?:recd|received)|bbps|autopay|auto debit|refund|reversal|cashback|credit adjustment|waiver/i.test(desc)
    } else {
      if (amts.length >= 2) { bal = amts[amts.length - 1].v; amount = amts[amts.length - 2].v } else amount = amts[0].v
      const mark = amts[amts.length >= 2 ? amts.length - 2 : 0].mark
      if (mark === "CR" || mark === "C") credit = true
      else if (mark === "DR" || mark === "D") credit = false
      else if (bal != null && prevBal != null && Math.abs(Math.abs(bal - prevBal) - amount) <= 1) credit = bal > prevBal
      else credit = /\b(?:salary|sal\b|neft cr|imps cr|upi cr|\/cr\/|by transfer|by clg|deposit|interest (?:paid|credit)|int\.?\s*pd|refund|reversal|cashback|credited)\b/i.test(desc)
      if (bal != null) prevBal = bal
    }
    if (!(amount > 0)) continue
    const t = { day: d.iso, desc: desc.slice(0, 120) || "Statement entry", amount, credit: !!credit, balance: bal }
    out.txns.push(t); last = { t, raw: descRaw.replace(/\t+/g, " ").trim() }
  }
  return out
}
// The part of a statement description worth showing as the merchant ("UPI-SWIGGY-swiggy@icici-..." → "Swiggy")
function stMerchant(desc) {
  const d = String(desc || "")
  if (stCash(d)) return "Cash withdrawal"
  if (/^cc\b|\bcc\s*\d|autopay|si-tad|credit card (?:payment|bill)/i.test(d)) return "Credit card bill"
  let s = d
  if (/^(?:upi|imps|neft|rtgs|nach|ach)\b/i.test(d)) {
    const parts = d.split(/[\/\-]+/).map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean)
    const pick = parts.find(x => !/^(upi|imps|neft|rtgs|nach|ach|cr|dr|p2a|p2m|in|out|payment|pay|paid|transfer|txn|ref|na|pytm|paytm|razorp|razorpay\.?|ptm|payu|cashfree|billdesk|ccavenue|phonepe|gpay)$/i.test(x) && !/@/.test(x) && !/^[A-Z]{4}0[A-Z0-9]{6}$/i.test(x) && !/\d{4,}/.test(x)
      && !/^(neft|imps|upi|nach|ach|ecs)(\s*(cr|dr))?(\s*(inw|out))?$/i.test(x) && !(x.length === 4 && ST_IFSC[x.toUpperCase()]) && !/^[a-z]{1,3}$/i.test(x) && !/^(fin\s*)?indian clearing/i.test(x))
    if (pick) s = pick
  }
  s = s.replace(/\s\b(?:usd|eur|gbp|aed|sgd|jpy|aud|cad|chf|thb)\b[\s\d.,]*$/i, "").replace(/\S*\.(?:com|co|in|net|io|org)\b\S*/gi, " ")
  s = s.replace(/\b(?:pos|ecom|pur|purchase|txn|ib|mb|bil|bpay|si|debit card|vpa)\b/gi, " ").replace(/@\S+/g, " ").replace(/\b\w*\d{4,}\w*\b/g, " ")
    .replace(/\b(?:mumbai|bangalore|bengaluru|delhi|new delhi|gurgaon|gurugram|noida|pune|chennai|hyderabad|kolkata|in|ind|india)\b\s*$/i, "").replace(/[*_]+/g, " ").replace(/\s{2,}/g, " ").trim()
  if (s === s.toUpperCase()) s = s.toLowerCase().replace(/\b[a-z]/g, c => c.toUpperCase())
  return s.slice(0, 60) || d.slice(0, 60)
}
const ST_RULES = [
  ["Credit Card Dues", /credit card (?:payment|bill)|cc (?:payment|bill)|card payment|cred club|cred\b.*bill|billdesk.*card|autopay|si-tad|^cc\b|\bcc\s*\d/i],
  ["Groceries", /blinkit|zepto|bigbasket|big basket|dmart|d mart|instamart|jiomart|kirana|grocery|nature'?s basket/i],
  ["Food & Dining", /swiggy|zomato|dineout|restaurant|cafe|coffee|starbucks|chai|food|kitchen|bakery|pizza|burger|canteen|snacks|eats|bistro|dhaba|mcdonald|domino|kfc/i],
  ["Travel", /uber|\bola\b|olacabs|rapido|metro|irctc|makemytrip|goibibo|agoda|indigo|air india|vistara|akasa|spicejet|airline|airways|fastag|petrol|fuel|hpcl|bpcl|indian oil|shell/i],
  ["Subscriptions", /\baws\b|amazon web services|netflix|spotify|apple\.com|itunes|google|youtube|prime video|hotstar|jiocinema|icloud|openai|chatgpt|microsoft|adobe|linkedin|audible/i],
  ["Entertainment", /bookmyshow|pvr|inox|cinema|movie|concert|liquor|wine|beer/i],
  ["Shopping", /myntra|amazon|flipkart|ajio|nykaa|zara|h&m|uniqlo|decathlon|croma|tata cliq|meesho|lifestyle|shoppers stop|ikea/i],
  ["Health", /pharm|medical|chemist|apollo|1mg|netmeds|pharmeasy|hospital|clinic|doctor|diagnostic|\blabs?\b|dental/i],
  ["Bills & Utilities", /airtel|\bjio\b|vodafone|\bvi\b|bsnl|electricity|adani|tata power|bescom|bses|\bchs\b|co-?op(?:erative)? (?:housing|hsg)|housing soc|\bpol\b|policy|sud life|premium|mahanagar gas|\bmgl\b|\bcharges?:|broadband|recharge|\brent\b|society|maintenance|insurance|\blic\b|emi\b|loan|finance charge|late fee|gst|annual fee/i],
]
const stCash = d => /\b(atw|atm|nwd|cash withdrawal|cash wdl)\b|^\s*self\s*$|\bto self\b|\bself chq/i.test(d)
// money moved into investments isn't spending: IPO bids, brokers, mutual fund SIPs (NACH to Indian Clearing), FDs, PPF/NPS
const stInvest = desc => /\bipo\b|ipo\.|\basba\b|securiti|broking|stock ?broker|zerodha|groww|upstox|angel ?one|indian clearing|\biccl\b|clearing corp|mutual fund|\bsip\b|\bnps\b|\bppf\b|\basbl\b|fixed deposit|\bfd (?:booking|open)|sweep/i.test(desc)
const stCategory = desc => (ST_RULES.find(([, re]) => re.test(desc)) || ["Other"])[0]
// one id per statement row, so importing the same statement twice adds nothing new
const stImportId = (st, t) => `st|${st.kind}|${st.last4 || st.bank}|${t.day}|${t.amount.toFixed(2)}|${String(t.desc).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "").slice(0, 24)}`
// ---------- statements: import bank and credit card PDFs as expenses ----------
// Each statement row becomes an expense, unless you'd already logged it (by hand, the shortcut or an alert): then the
// logged one just gets the card filled in. Rows that match a card or bank alert waiting in your inbox are linked to it,
// so the alert doesn't ask again. Importing the same statement twice adds nothing (each row carries an Import ID).
// Categories: your merchant rules first, then the merchant's name, then what you picked for that merchant before.
const STMT_CATS = ["Credit Card Dues", "Household", "Groceries", "Food & Dining", "Subscriptions", "Shopping", "Bills & Utilities", "Entertainment", "Health", "Travel", "Personal", "Gifts", "Other"]
const stmtPrivate = () => typeof PRIVATE !== "undefined" && PRIVATE
const stmtRt = v => ({ rich_text: v ? [{ text: { content: String(v).slice(0, 1900) } }] : [] })
const stmtDayDiff = (a, b) => Math.round((Date.parse(a + "T00:00:00Z") - Date.parse(b + "T00:00:00Z")) / 864e5)
const fmtShortDay = iso => new Date(iso + "T00:00:00").toLocaleDateString("en-IN", { day: "numeric", month: "short" })

// ----- merchant → category rules ("Swiggy is always Food & Dining") -----
// Private edition: kept in its data file (so they're in backups). Normal edition: on this iPhone.
// A rule matches when all its words are in the merchant's name; the rule with the most words wins:
// "Swiggy" → Food & Dining, "Swiggy Instamart" → Groceries.
const RULES_KEY = "expenses.merchantRules"
function stmtRules() {
  if (stmtPrivate()) { privLoad(); return (PRIV_DB.rules = PRIV_DB.rules || {}) }
  try { return JSON.parse(Keychain.get(RULES_KEY)) || {} } catch (e) { return {} }
}
function stmtSaveRules(r) {
  if (stmtPrivate()) { PRIV_DB.rules = r; privSave(); return }
  Keychain.set(RULES_KEY, JSON.stringify(r))
}
const stmtWords = s => learnKey(s).split(" ").filter(Boolean)
function ruleFor(...names) {
  const have = new Set(names.flatMap(n => stmtWords(n || "")))
  let best = null
  for (const [key, r] of Object.entries(stmtRules())) {
    const w = key.split(" ").filter(Boolean)
    if (w.length && w.every(x => have.has(x)) && (!best || w.length > best.w)) best = { w: w.length, category: r.category }
  }
  return best ? best.category : ""
}
const ruleMatches = (key, name) => { const have = new Set(stmtWords(name)); const w = key.split(" ").filter(Boolean); return w.length > 0 && w.every(x => have.has(x)) }
// what you typed when logging ("Swiggy dinner", "Uber to office") → the merchant itself ("swiggy", "uber")
const RULE_DROP = new Set(["dinner", "lunch", "breakfast", "brunch", "snack", "snacks", "order", "orders", "food", "groceries", "grocery", "ride", "trip", "cab", "auto", "to", "from", "for", "with", "at", "home", "office", "work", "bill", "recharge", "coffee", "tea", "drinks", "party", "today", "yesterday", "monthly", "weekly", "limited", "bangalore", "bengaluru", "gurgaon", "delhi", "pune", "chennai", "hyderabad", "kolkata"])
const ruleKeyOf = merchant => { const words = stmtWords(stMerchant(merchant) || merchant), core = words.filter(w => !RULE_DROP.has(w)).slice(0, 3); return (core.length ? core : words.slice(0, 3)).join(" ") }
const catProps = cat => ({ Category: { select: { name: cat } }, "Category Text": { title: [{ text: { content: cat } }] } })
const rulesForPage = () => { try { return Object.entries(stmtRules()).map(([key, r]) => ({ key, name: r.name, category: r.category })).sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name)) } catch (e) { return [] } }

async function setCategory(tok, d) {
  const cat = String(d.category || "")
  if (!STMT_CATS.includes(cat)) throw new Error("Pick one of the categories.")
  const pg = await notion(tok, "GET", `pages/${d.id}`)
  const merchant = text(pg.properties.Merchant).trim()
  await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${d.id}`, { properties: catProps(cat) })
  if (!d.always || !merchant) return `Moved to ${cat}.`
  const key = ruleKeyOf(merchant)
  if (!key) return `Moved to ${cat}.`
  const rules = stmtRules(); rules[key] = { name: key.replace(/\b[a-z]/g, c => c.toUpperCase()), category: cat }; stmtSaveRules(rules)
  // the same merchant's other expenses (unless a more specific rule of yours says otherwise)
  const others = latest && latest.rows ? latest.rows : []
  let n = 0
  for (const r of others) {
    if (!r.id || r.id === d.id || !r.Merchant || r.Category === cat || !ruleMatches(key, r.Merchant) || ruleFor(r.Merchant) !== cat) continue
    await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${r.id}`, { properties: catProps(cat) }); n++
  }
  return `${rules[key].name} will always go to ${cat}${n ? `. ${n} earlier expense${n === 1 ? " was" : "s were"} moved too` : ""}.`
}
function removeRule(d) {
  const rules = stmtRules(), r = rules[d.key]
  if (!r) return null
  delete rules[d.key]; stmtSaveRules(rules)
  return `Removed the rule for ${r.name}. Its expenses keep their categories.`
}

// ----- saving one statement -----
// Big bank transfers you said were just moving money: not asked about again when the statement is imported again
const BIG_TRANSFER = 100000, BIG_SKIP_KEY = "expenses.skippedTransfers"
const bigSkipped = () => { try { return JSON.parse(Keychain.get(BIG_SKIP_KEY)) || [] } catch (e) { return [] } }
const bigSkip = iid => { try { Keychain.set(BIG_SKIP_KEY, JSON.stringify(bigSkipped().concat([iid]).slice(-1000))) } catch (e) {} }
async function stmtExpenses(tok, fromDay) {
  const out = []; let cursor = null
  do {
    const res = await notion(tok, "POST", `databases/${DATABASE_ID}/query`, Object.assign({ page_size: 100, filter: { property: "Date", date: { on_or_after: fromDay } } }, cursor ? { start_cursor: cursor } : {}))
    out.push(...res.results); cursor = res.has_more ? res.next_cursor : null
  } while (cursor)
  return out
}
// the Expenses table gets an Import ID column the first time (Notion edition; the private one has it already)
async function ensureImportColumn(tok) {
  const db = await notion(tok, "GET", `databases/${DATABASE_ID}`)
  if (!(db.properties || {})["Import ID"]) await notion(tok, "PATCH", `databases/${DATABASE_ID}`, { properties: { "Import ID": { rich_text: {} } } })
  return db
}
async function saveStatement(tok, st) {
  const db = await ensureImportColumn(tok)
  let label = ""
  if (st.kind === "card") {
    const opts = ((db.properties.Card || {}).select || {}).options || []
    const hit = st.last4 && opts.find(o => o.name.replace(/\D/g, "").endsWith(st.last4.slice(-4)))
    label = hit ? hit.name : `${st.bank || "Card"}${st.last4 ? " ••" + st.last4 : ""}`
    if (!hit && st.last4) for (const id of [DATABASE_ID, ALERTS_ID, BILLS_ID].filter(Boolean)) await addOption(tok, id, "Card", label)
  } else {
    label = `${st.bank || "Bank"}${st.last4 ? " ••" + st.last4 : ""} account`
    if (st.last4 && ALERTS_ID) await addOption(tok, ALERTS_ID, "Account", label.replace(/ account$/, ""))
  }
  const days = st.txns.map(t => t.day).sort()
  if (!days.length) return `${label}: no transactions found.`
  const have = (await stmtExpenses(tok, shiftDay(days[0], -5))).map(pg => { const p = pg.properties
    return { id: pg.id, day: (p.Date.date || {}).start || pg.created_time.slice(0, 10), amount: p.Amount.number || 0, importId: text(p["Import ID"]),
      payment: (p["Payment Method"].select || {}).name || "", card: (p.Card && p.Card.select || {}).name || "", used: false } })
  // what you've logged, all of it: that's what the categories learn from
  const history = ((latest && latest.rows) || []).filter(r => r.Merchant && r.Category).map(r => ({ merchant: r.Merchant, name: r.Merchant, category: r.Category, amount: r.Amount || 0 }))
  // card and bank alerts still waiting in your inbox (Notion edition)
  let waiting = []
  if (ALERTS_ID && !stmtPrivate()) try { waiting = (await fetchAlerts(tok, shiftDay(days[0], -4))).filter(a => !a.ignored && !a.expenseIds.length && a.amount > 0 && a.type !== "Bill payment") } catch (e) { log(`statement alerts: ${e.message || e}`) }
  const addRow = async (t, iid, method, oneOff) => {
    const alert = waiting.filter(a => !a.used && Math.abs(a.amount - t.amount) <= 1 && Math.abs(stmtDayDiff(a.day, t.day)) <= 3)
      .sort((a, b) => Math.abs(stmtDayDiff(a.day, t.day)) - Math.abs(stmtDayDiff(b.day, t.day)))[0]
    let merchant = stMerchant(t.desc)
    if (alert && alert.merchant) merchant = alert.merchant
    const guess = stCategory(t.desc), ruled = ruleFor(merchant, t.desc)
    let cat = ruled || guess
    if (!ruled) try {
      const l = learnCategory(history, { merchant, amount: t.amount }, guess)
      cat = (typeof ALERT_TO_EXPENSE_CAT !== "undefined" && ALERT_TO_EXPENSE_CAT[l.category]) || l.category
      if (l.name) merchant = l.name   // the name you use for it
    } catch (e) {}
    if (!STMT_CATS.includes(cat)) cat = "Other"
    const P = Object.assign(catProps(cat), { Amount: { number: t.amount }, Date: { date: { start: t.day } },
      Merchant: stmtRt(merchant), Notes: stmtRt(`From ${label} statement: ${t.desc}`), "Payment Method": { select: { name: method } }, "Import ID": stmtRt(iid) })
    if (st.kind === "card") P.Card = { select: { name: label } }
    if (alert) { alert.used = true; P.Alerts = { relation: [{ id: alert.id }] }; linked++ }
    if (oneOff) { await ensureEventColumn(tok); P.Event = { select: { name: oneOff } } }
    const made = await notion(tok, "POST", "pages", { parent: { database_id: DATABASE_ID }, properties: P })
    if (made && made.id) log_.added.push(alert ? { id: made.id, alert: true } : { id: made.id })
    have.push({ id: "", day: t.day, amount: t.amount, importId: iid, used: true })
    added++
  }
  const ask = [], log_ = { id: String(Date.now()) + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1e4), at: Date.now(), label, kind: st.kind, from: days[0], to: days[days.length - 1], added: [], matched: [] }
  let spends = 0, added = 0, matched = 0, already = 0, credits = 0, cash = 0, invest = 0, big = 0, linked = 0
  for (const t of st.txns) {
    if (t.credit) { credits++; continue }
    if (st.kind === "bank" && stCash(t.desc)) { cash++; continue }
    if (stInvest(t.desc)) { invest++; continue }
    const iid = stImportId(st, t)
    if (have.some(h => h.importId === iid)) { spends++; already++; continue }
    // lakh-plus bank transfers are often just moving money (to family, a flat, an investment): you're asked about each
    // one after the rest is saved, unless it matches something you logged
    const isBig = st.kind === "bank" && t.amount >= BIG_TRANSFER && !/\b(pos|debit card|ecom|vps|vis)\b/i.test(t.desc)
    if (isBig && bigSkipped().includes(iid)) { big++; continue }
    spends++
    const method = st.kind === "card" ? "Credit Card" : /\bupi\b/i.test(t.desc) ? "UPI" : /\b(pos|debit card|ecom|vps|vis)\b/i.test(t.desc) ? "Debit Card" : "UPI"
    const near = have.filter(h => !h.used && !h.importId && Math.abs(h.amount - t.amount) <= 1 && Math.abs(stmtDayDiff(h.day, t.day)) <= 3)
      .sort((a, b) => Math.abs(stmtDayDiff(a.day, t.day)) - Math.abs(stmtDayDiff(b.day, t.day)))[0]
    if (near) {
      near.used = true
      const P = { "Import ID": stmtRt(iid) }
      if (st.kind === "card" && !near.card) P.Card = { select: { name: label } }
      if (!near.payment) P["Payment Method"] = { select: { name: method } }
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${near.id}`, { properties: P }); matched++
      log_.matched.push({ id: near.id, card: !!P.Card, pay: !!P["Payment Method"] }); continue
    }
    if (isBig) { spends--; ask.push({ t, iid, method }); continue }
    await addRow(t, iid, method)
  }
  // the big transfers, one at a time
  let oneOffs = 0
  for (let k = 0; k < ask.length; k++) {
    const { t, iid, method } = ask[k]
    const a = new Alert()
    a.title = `${inr(t.amount)} on ${fmtShortDay(t.day)} ${t.day.slice(0, 4)}`
    a.message = `${stMerchant(t.desc)}\n${t.desc}\n\nA big transfer from ${label}. Was it spending (rent, a deposit, a purchase), or just moving money (to family, savings, an investment)?${ask.length > 1 ? `\n\n${k + 1} of ${ask.length}` : ""}`
    a.addAction("It was spending"); a.addAction("Spending, but a one-off"); a.addAction("Just moving money (skip)")
    a.addCancelAction(ask.length - k > 1 ? `Skip all ${ask.length - k} for now` : "Skip for now")
    const c = await a.presentAlert()
    if (c === -1) { big += ask.length - k; break }
    if (c === 2) { bigSkip(iid); big++; continue }
    spends++
    await addRow(t, iid, method, c === 1 ? "Big one-offs" : "")
    if (c === 1) oneOffs++
  }
  if (log_.added.length || log_.matched.length) { const all = stmtImports(); all.push(log_); stmtSaveImports(all.slice(-60)) }
  const yr = d => d.slice(0, 4) !== String(new Date().getFullYear()) || days[0].slice(0, 4) !== days[days.length - 1].slice(0, 4) ? " " + d.slice(0, 4) : ""
  const range = `${fmtShortDay(days[0])}${yr(days[0])}–${fmtShortDay(days[days.length - 1])}${yr(days[days.length - 1])}`
  const pl = (n, w) => `${n} ${w}${n === 1 ? "" : "s"}`
  return `${label}, ${range}: ${pl(spends, "spend")} · ${added} added${linked ? ` (${linked} from alerts in your inbox)` : ""} · ${matched} you'd logged (card filled in)` +
    `${already ? ` · ${already} already imported` : ""}${credits ? ` · ${pl(credits, "payment")}/refunds not counted` : ""}${cash ? ` · ${pl(cash, "cash withdrawal")} left out` : ""}` +
    `${invest ? ` · ${pl(invest, "investment")} (IPO, SIP, broker) left out` : ""}${oneOffs ? ` · ${oneOffs} added as one-offs (under "Big one-offs", outside your monthly budget)` : ""}${big ? ` · ${pl(big, "big transfer")} left out` : ""}.`
}

// ----- the imports you've done, so one can be taken back -----
// Private edition: in its data file. Normal edition: on this iPhone (just the Notion page ids, no amounts or names of payees).
const IMPORTS_KEY = "expenses.imports"
function stmtImports() {
  if (stmtPrivate()) { privLoad(); return (PRIV_DB.imports = PRIV_DB.imports || []) }
  try { return JSON.parse(Keychain.get(IMPORTS_KEY)) || [] } catch (e) { return [] }
}
function stmtSaveImports(list) {
  if (stmtPrivate()) { PRIV_DB.imports = list; privSave(); return }
  Keychain.set(IMPORTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(list))
}
const importsForPage = () => stmtImports().slice().reverse().map(i => ({ id: i.id, label: i.label, from: i.from, to: i.to, at: i.at, added: i.added.length, matched: i.matched.length }))
// Remove one import: the expenses it added go to Notion's trash (restorable there for 30 days); the ones you'd logged
// yourself stay, with the card and payment method it filled in cleared again.
async function undoImport(tok, d) {
  const all = stmtImports(), imp = all.find(i => i.id === d.id)
  if (!imp) return "That import is already gone."
  let gone = 0, kept = 0
  for (const a of imp.added) {
    try {
      if (a.alert) await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${a.id}`, { properties: { Alerts: { relation: [] } } })   // the alert goes back to your inbox
      await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${a.id}`, { archived: true }); gone++
    } catch (e) { if (!/object_not_found|archived/i.test(`${e.code} ${e.message}`)) throw e }
  }
  for (const m of imp.matched) {
    const P = { "Import ID": { rich_text: [] } }
    if (m.card) P.Card = { select: null }
    if (m.pay) P["Payment Method"] = { select: null }
    try { await notion(tok, "PATCH", `pages/${m.id}`, { properties: P }); kept++ } catch (e) { if (!/object_not_found|archived/i.test(`${e.code} ${e.message}`)) throw e }
  }
  stmtSaveImports(stmtImports().filter(i => i.id !== d.id))
  const where = stmtPrivate() ? "" : " They're in Notion's Trash for 30 days if you change your mind."
  return `Removed the ${imp.label} import: ${gone} expense${gone === 1 ? "" : "s"} deleted${kept ? `, and the card cleared on ${kept} you'd logged yourself` : ""}.${gone ? where : ""}`
}

// ----- picking PDFs -----
const STMT_LAST_KEY = "expenses.lastImport"
async function importStatements(tok) {
  let paths = []
  try { paths = await DocumentPicker.open(["com.adobe.pdf"]) } catch (e) { return null }
  if (!paths || !paths.length) return null
  const lines = []
  for (const p of paths) {
    const name = p.split("/").pop()
    const r = await readPdfHere(p)
    if (r.cancelled) { lines.push(`${name}: skipped.`); continue }
    if (r.error === "PasswordException") { lines.push(`${name}: the password didn't work, so it was skipped.`); continue }
    if (r.error === "nolib") { lines.push(`${name}: the PDF reader didn't load. It's downloaded the first time, so connect to the internet once and try again.`); continue }
    if (r.error || !r.text || r.text.replace(/\s/g, "").length < 40) { lines.push(`${name}: no text in it. Scanned statements can't be read; download the statement from net banking instead.`); continue }
    const st = parseStatement(r.text, { filename: name, today: istDay(Date.now()) })
    if (!st.txns.length) { lines.push(`${name}: no transactions found in it.`); continue }
    try { lines.push(await saveStatement(tok, st)) } catch (e) { lines.push(`${name}: couldn't save it (${e.message || e}).`) }
  }
  try { Keychain.set(STMT_LAST_KEY, String(Date.now())) } catch (e) {}
  return lines.join("\n")
}
const stmtInfo = () => ({ rules: rulesForPage(), imports: importsForPage(), lastImport: (() => { try { return Number(Keychain.get(STMT_LAST_KEY)) || 0 } catch (e) { return 0 } })() })

async function stmtAction(kind, data) {
  data = data || {}
  while (refreshing) await sleep(200)
  try {
    const tok = await token()
    if (!tok || !DATABASE_ID) throw new Error("Finish setup first: the dashboard needs your Notion token and database link.")
    const msg = kind === "stmt-import" ? await importStatements(tok) : kind === "stmt-category" ? await setCategory(tok, data) : kind === "stmt-rule-remove" ? removeRule(data) : kind === "stmt-undo" ? await undoImport(tok, data) : null
    if (msg) extraMessage = msg
  } catch (e) {
    log(`statement action failed (${kind}): ${e.message || e}`)
    extraMessage = `Couldn't do that: ${e.message || e}`
  }
  refreshFull = true
  await refresh()
}
// ---------- loans & EMIs ----------
// Matches EMI debits from your bank alerts to the loans in your Notion "Loans" table, works out whether this
// month's EMI is paid, due or late, and (once you add the rate and outstanding) the loan's progress:
// outstanding now, months left, interest vs principal, totals for the financial year (April–March),
// and what each prepayment saved. Interest is worked out monthly on the reducing balance, as Indian home
// loans usually are, so figures are close to your lender's statement but not to the rupee.

// Helpers are kept inside so their names can't clash with the rest of the script.
const { loanPayments, emiStatus, emiChange, loanProgress, suggestLoans, finYear, matchesLoan, learned, parseRates, formatRates } = (() => {
const DAY = 864e5
const dnum = iso => Math.round(Date.parse(String(iso).slice(0, 10) + "T00:00:00Z") / DAY)
const monthKeyOf = iso => String(iso).slice(0, 7)
const daysInMonth = (y, m) => new Date(Date.UTC(y, m, 0)).getUTCDate()   // m is 1–12
function dueDateIn(key, day) { const [y, m] = key.split("-").map(Number); return `${key}-${String(Math.min(day, daysInMonth(y, m))).padStart(2, "0")}` }
const shiftDays = (iso, n) => new Date(Date.parse(String(iso).slice(0, 10) + "T00:00:00Z") + n * DAY).toISOString().slice(0, 10)
function addMonths(key, n) { const [y, m] = key.split("-").map(Number); const t = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1 + n, 1)); return t.toISOString().slice(0, 7) }
// Indian financial year containing a day: 2026-10-04 → { start: "2026-04-01", end: "2027-03-31", label: "FY 2026–27" }
function finYear(iso) {
  const [y, m] = String(iso).slice(0, 7).split("-").map(Number), s = m >= 4 ? y : y - 1
  return { start: `${s}-04-01`, end: `${s + 1}-03-31`, label: `FY ${s}–${String(s + 1).slice(2)}` }
}
const wordsOf = s => String(s || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9 ]+/g, " ").split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean)
const matchesLoan = (loan, merchant) => { const w = wordsOf(loan.match || loan.name); const m = " " + wordsOf(merchant).join(" ") + " "; return w.length > 0 && w.every(x => m.includes(" " + x)) }

// alerts: [{ id, day, amount, type, merchant, channel }]. Returns { emis, prepayments, charges } (each [{ id, day, amount }]).
function loanPayments(loan, alerts, others = []) {
  const byAmount = a => loan.emi > 0 && loan.day && /auto-debit|emi/i.test(a.channel || "") && Math.abs(a.amount - loan.emi) <= loan.emi * 0.01 &&
    Math.abs(Number(String(a.day).slice(8, 10)) - loan.day) <= 4 && !others.some(o => o !== loan && matchesLoan(o, a.merchant))
  // two loans with the same lender (a home loan and its top-up): each payment goes to the loan whose EMI is nearest
  const nearest = a => {
    const same = [loan].concat(others.filter(o => o !== loan && matchesLoan(o, a.merchant) && o.emi > 0))
    if (same.length < 2 || !(loan.emi > 0)) return same.length < 2 || !same.some(o => o !== loan && o.emi > 0)
    const gap = o => Math.abs(Math.log(a.amount / o.emi))
    return same.every(o => gap(loan) <= gap(o))
  }
  // a lender receipt names the account ("… EMI · A/c …8406"): it belongs to that loan only
  const tagOf = a => { const m = String(a.merchant || "").match(/A\/c …(\w{4})\b/); return m ? m[1].toUpperCase() : "" }
  const acctIn = (l, a) => { const n = String(l.account || "").toUpperCase(); return n.length >= 6 && String(a.merchant || "").toUpperCase().includes(n) }
  const mineByAcct = a => { if (acctIn(loan, a)) return true; if (others.some(o => o !== loan && acctIn(o, a))) return false
    const t = tagOf(a); if (!t) return null; const own = String(loan.account || "").toUpperCase(); return own ? own.endsWith(t) : (others.some(o => o !== loan && String(o.account || "").toUpperCase().endsWith(t)) ? false : null) }
  // insurance, broking, cards and the like share a bank's name ("HDFC ERGO") but aren't loan payments
  const otherBusiness = a => /\bergo\b|insurance|securities|\bsec\b|mutual fund|\blife\b|credit card|card bill|forex|\bcredila\b/i.test(a.merchant || "")
  const beforeStart = a => loan.start && dnum(a.day) < dnum(loan.start) - 45
  const ours = a => (a.type || "Spend") === "Spend" && !beforeStart(a) && (mineByAcct(a) != null ? mineByAcct(a) : !otherBusiness(a) && ((matchesLoan(loan, a.merchant) && nearest(a)) || byAmount(a)))
  const mine = alerts.filter(a => ours(a) && (!loan.closed || a.day <= loan.closed)).sort((a, b) => a.day < b.day ? -1 : 1)
  // an EMI debited in the days after the loan closed (often refunded later)
