   { const cm = $(\"catsMore\"); if (cm) cm.onclick = () => { state.catsAll = !state.catsAll; render() } }\n    $(\"catCard\").querySelectorAll(\"[data-cat]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.cat = b.dataset.cat; state.pay = null; state.scope = \"month\"; setTab(\"expenses\") })\n\n    // credit card\n    const perCard = {}\n    let unknownCard = 0\n    for (const r of inMonth(state.month)) {\n      if (r.category === DUES) continue\n      if (r.card) perCard[r.card] = (perCard[r.card] || 0) + r.gross\n      else if (r.payment === \"Credit Card\") unknownCard += r.gross\n    }\n    const cardList = Object.entries(perCard).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])\n    $(\"cardCard\").innerHTML = `<h2>Credit cards</h2><div class=\"billhead\"><span>Spent in ${esc(labelOf(state.month, true).split(\" \")[0])}</span></div><dl class=\"kv num\">\n      ${cardList.map(([c, v]) => `<dt>${esc(cardShort(c))}</dt><dd>${inr(v)}</dd>`).join(\"\")}\n      ${unknownCard ? `<dt>Card not identified yet</dt><dd>${inr(unknownCard)}</dd>` : \"\"}\n      ${!cardList.length && !unknownCard ? `<dt>Spent on cards</dt><dd>${inr(0)}</dd>` : \"\"}\n      </dl>\n      <div id=\"billsPart\"></div>\n      <dl class=\"kv num paidrow\"><dt>Card bills paid in ${esc(labelOf(state.month, true).split(\" \")[0])}</dt><dd>${inr(cardPaidIn(state.month, p.cur.dues))}</dd></dl>\n      <p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">${state.countDues ? \"Bill payments are counted as spending (see Settings).\" : \"Bill payments aren't counted as spending, because the purchases are already counted above.\"} Each bill is checked against your card alerts. \"Extra\" means charges with no alert, such as fees, interest, GST or forex markup. Tap a bill for details.</p>`\n\n    renderCheck()\n    renderOwed()\n    renderBills()\n    renderMarkups()\n    renderCardSet()\n    renderPrivate()\n    renderStmt()\n    renderLoans()\n    renderMoney()\n\n    // recently logged\n    const recent = [...state.rows].sort((a, b) => b.created.localeCompare(a.created)).slice(0, 3)\n    $(\"recentCard\").innerHTML = `<h2>Recently logged</h2><ul class=\"tx\">${recent.map(r => txRow(r)).join(\"\")}</ul><button class=\"linkbtn\" id=\"recentAll\">See all expenses</button>`\n    { const ra = $(\"recentAll\"); if (ra) ra.onclick = () => { state.scope = \"month\"; setTab(\"expenses\") } }\n  }\n\n  function txRow(r, tools) {\n    // On the Expenses tab the event shows as its own label below, so don't repeat it here.\n    const rest = esc([r.merchant ? r.category : \"\", r.card ? cardShort(r.card) : r.payment, r.notes].filter(Boolean).join(\" · \"))\n    let sub = r.event && !(tools && r.id) ? evLabel(r.event, 12) + (rest ? \" · \" + rest : \"\") : (rest || esc(r.date))\n    let owedLine = \"\"\n    if (r.owed) {\n      const due = r.people.filter(p => !p.paid)\n      owedLine = `<small class=\"owedline\">${due.length ? inr(due.reduce((s, p) => s + p.amount, 0)) + \" owed by \" + esc(due.map(p => p.name).join(\", \")) : \"Paid back\"} · you paid ${inr(r.gross)}</small>`\n    }\n    const link = `<a href=\"${esc(r.url)}\" title=\"Open in Notion\">\n      <span class=\"dot\" style=\"background:${colorOf(r.category)}\"></span>\n      <span class=\"what\"><div>${esc(shownName(r.merchant) || r.category)}</div><small>${sub}</small>${owedLine}</span>\n      <span class=\"amt num ${counts(r) ? \"\" : \"muted\"}\">${inr(r.amount)}</span></a>`\n    if (!tools || !r.id) return `<li>${link}</li>`\n    const busy = state.busy ? \"disabled\" : \"\"\n    const tag = r.event ? `<span class=\"tag\" title=\"Kept out of your monthly budget\">${iconSvg(kindOf(r.event), 12)}${esc(r.event)}</span>` : \"\"\n    let bar = state.editing === r.id\n      ? tag + (r.event ? `<button class=\"act subtle\" data-untag=\"${esc(r.id)}\" ${busy} aria-label=\"Count ${esc(r.merchant || r.category)} in monthly spending again\">Count in monthly</button>` : `<button class=\"act\" data-oneoff=\"${esc(r.id)}\" ${busy} aria-label=\"Mark ${esc(r.merchant || r.category)} as a one-off expense\">＋ Mark as one-off</button>`) +\n        `<button class=\"act subtle\" data-cat=\"${esc(r.id)}\" ${busy}>Category</button>` +\n        `<button class=\"act subtle\" data-split=\"${esc(r.id)}\" ${busy}>${r.owed ? \"Edit split\" : \"Split / owed\"}</button><button class=\"act subtle\" data-editclose>Close</button>`\n      : tag + `<button class=\"act subtle\" data-edit=\"${esc(r.id)}\" ${busy} aria-label=\"Edit ${esc(r.merchant || r.category)}\">Edit</button>`\n    let editor = \"\"\n    if (state.splitting === r.id) { editor = splitEditor(r); bar = \"\" }\n    if (state.catting === r.id) {\n      const m = shownName(r.merchant) || \"\"\n      editor = `<div class=\"tageditor\" id=\"catBox\" data-id=\"${esc(r.id)}\">\n        <span class=\"small\">Category${m ? \" for \" + esc(m) : \"\"}:</span>\n        <div class=\"chips wrapchips\" style=\"padding:0\">${Object.keys(COLORS).filter(c => !window.__PRIVATE__ || c !== \"Household\").map(c => `<button class=\"chip\" data-catpick=\"${esc(c)}\" aria-pressed=\"${c === r.category}\"><span class=\"dot\" style=\"background:${COLORS[c]}\"></span> ${esc(c)}</button>`).join(\"\")}</div>\n        ${m ? `<label class=\"small catalways\"><input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"catAlways\" checked> Always put ${esc(m)} here, including its earlier expenses and future statements</label>` : \"\"}\n        <button class=\"act subtle\" id=\"catCancel\" style=\"justify-self:start\">Cancel</button></div>`\n      bar = \"\"\n    }\n    if (state.tagging === r.id) {\n      const names = [...new Set(state.rows.filter(x => x.event).map(x => x.event))]\n      editor = `<div class=\"tageditor\">\n        <span class=\"small\">Keep this out of your monthly budget. Put it under:</span>\n        ${names.length ? `<div class=\"chips\">${names.map(n => `<button class=\"chip\" data-tagname=\"${esc(n)}\">${iconSvg(kindOf(n), 13)} ${esc(n)}</button>`).join(\"\")}</div>` : \"\"}\n        <div class=\"row-input\"><input id=\"tagName\" placeholder=\"${names.length ? \"Or a new name\" : \"A name, e.g. House maintenance\"}\" maxlength=\"60\" autocomplete=\"off\"><button class=\"btn\" id=\"tagSave\" ${busy}>Save</button></div>\n        <button class=\"act subtle\" id=\"tagCancel\" style=\"justify-self:start\">Cancel</button>\n      </div>`\n      bar = \"\"\n    }\n    return `<li class=\"txi\">${link}${bar ? `<div class=\"txtools\">${bar}</div>` : \"\"}${editor}</li>`\n  }\n\n  // ---------- split / owed ----------\n  // \"Owed by\" in Notion holds one entry per person, e.g. \"Rahil ₹518 ✓; Jainam ₹518\" (✓ = paid back).\n  // Older entries like \"Rahil, Jainam\" with one total are read as an equal split.\n  function parsePeople(text, owed, paidBack) {\n    const s = String(text || \"\").trim()\n    if (!(owed > 0)) return []\n    const parts = s ? s.split(/\\s*;\\s*/).filter(Boolean) : []\n    const out = []\n    let structured = parts.length > 0\n    for (const p of parts) {\n      const m = p.match(/^(.*?)\\s*₹\\s*([\\d,]+(?:\\.\\d+)?)\\s*(✓)?$/)\n      if (!m) { structured = false; break }\n      out.push({ name: m[1].trim() || \"Someone\", amount: Number(m[2].replace(/,/g, \"\")), paid: !!m[3] })\n    }\n    if (structured && out.length) return out\n    const names = s ? s.split(/\\s*(?:,|&|\\band\\b)\\s*/i).filter(Boolean) : [\"Someone\"]\n    const each = Math.round(owed / names.length * 100) / 100\n    return names.map((name, i) => ({ name, amount: i === names.length - 1 ? Math.round((owed - each * (names.length - 1)) * 100) / 100 : each, paid: !!paidBack }))\n  }\n  const cleanPerson = s => String(s || \"\").replace(/[;₹✓]/g, \" \").replace(/\\s+/g, \" \").trim().slice(0, 40)\n\n  function splitEditor(r) {\n    const d = state.splitDraft && state.splitDraft.id === r.id ? state.splitDraft : (state.splitDraft = { id: r.id, people: r.people.map(p => Object.assign({}, p)) })\n    const owed = d.people.reduce((s, p) => s + (Number(p.amount) || 0), 0)\n    const busy = state.busy ? \"disabled\" : \"\"\n    const pct = (p, n) => Math.round(r.gross * n / 100)\n    const quick = (p, i) => `<div class=\"pquick\" role=\"group\" aria-label=\"Quick amounts for ${esc(p.name || \"this person\")}\">\n        ${[[100, \"Full\"], [90, \"90%\"], [75, \"75%\"], [50, \"50%\"], [25, \"25%\"]].map(([n, t]) => `<button class=\"chip\" data-pq=\"${i}:${n}\" aria-pressed=\"${Math.round(p.amount) === pct(p, n) && p.amount > 0}\">${t}</button>`).join(\"\")}\n        <label class=\"pother\">Other <input class=\"ppct num\" data-ppct=\"${i}\" type=\"number\" inputmode=\"decimal\" min=\"0\" max=\"100\" step=\"1\" placeholder=\"%\" aria-label=\"Percent of ${inr(r.gross)} for ${esc(p.name || \"this person\")}\">%</label></div>`\n    const blanks = d.people.filter(p => !(Number(p.amount) > 0)).length, left = Math.round((r.gross - owed) * 100) / 100\n    const rows = d.people.map((p, i) => `<div class=\"prow\">\n        <input class=\"pname\" data-pi=\"${i}\" placeholder=\"Name\" maxlength=\"40\" autocomplete=\"off\" value=\"${esc(p.name === \"Someone\" ? \"\" : p.name)}\">\n        <input class=\"pamt num\" data-pa=\"${i}\" type=\"number\" inputmode=\"decimal\" min=\"0\" step=\"1\" placeholder=\"₹\" value=\"${p.amount ? Math.round(p.amount * 100) / 100 : \"\"}\">\n        <button class=\"chip paidtoggle\" data-pp=\"${i}\" aria-pressed=\"${!!p.paid}\" title=\"Tap if they've paid you back\">${p.paid ? \"Paid ✓\" : \"Owes\"}</button>\n        <button class=\"mini\" data-prm=\"${i}\" aria-label=\"Remove ${esc(p.name)}\">✕</button></div>${quick(p, i)}`).join(\"\")\n    return `<div class=\"tageditor\" id=\"splitBox\" data-gross=\"${r.gross}\">\n      <span class=\"small\">You paid ${inr(r.gross)}. Who's paying you back? Only your share counts in your spending.</span>\n      <div class=\"chips wrapchips\" style=\"padding:0\">\n        ${[2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map(n => `<button class=\"chip\" data-ways=\"${n}\" aria-pressed=\"${d.people.length === n - 1 && d.people.every(p => Math.round(p.amount) === Math.round(r.gross / n))}\">Split ${n} ways</button>`).join(\"\")}\n        <button class=\"chip\" data-ways=\"all\" aria-pressed=\"${d.people.length === 1 && Math.round(d.people[0].amount) === Math.round(r.gross)}\">One person owes it all</button>\n      </div>\n      <div class=\"people\">${rows || `<span class=\"small\">Nobody added yet. Pick a split above or add a person.</span>`}</div>\n      <div class=\"txtools\" style=\"justify-content:flex-start;padding:0\"><button class=\"act subtle\" id=\"pAdd\">＋ Add person</button>${blanks && left > 0.5 ? `<button class=\"act subtle\" id=\"pRest\">Split the other ${inr(left)} equally${blanks > 1 ? \" between \" + blanks : \"\"}</button>` : \"\"}</div>\n      <div class=\"small\" id=\"owedShare\">${shareLine(r.gross, owed)}</div>\n      <div class=\"txtools\" style=\"justify-content:flex-start;padding:0\">\n        <button class=\"btn\" id=\"owedSave\" ${busy}>Save</button>\n        ${r.owed ? `<button class=\"act subtle\" id=\"owedRemove\" ${busy}>Remove split</button>` : \"\"}\n        <button class=\"act subtle\" id=\"owedCancel\">Cancel</button>\n      </div></div>`\n  }\n  const shareLine = (gross, owed) => owed > gross + 0.01 ? `<span style=\"color:var(--more)\">That's more than you paid (${inr(gross)}).</span>` : `They owe <strong class=\"num\">${inr(owed)}</strong> · your share <strong class=\"num\">${inr(gross - owed)}</strong>`\n\n  function wireSplit(rerender) {\n    const box = $(\"splitBox\"); if (!box) return\n    const d = state.splitDraft, gross = Number(box.dataset.gross) || 0\n    const total = () => d.people.reduce((s, p) => s + (Number(p.amount) || 0), 0)\n    const update = () => { $(\"owedShare\").innerHTML = shareLine(gross, total()) }\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-pi]\").forEach(i => i.oninput = () => { d.people[+i.dataset.pi].name = i.value })\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-pa]\").forEach(i => i.oninput = () => { d.people[+i.dataset.pa].amount = Number(i.value) || 0; update() })\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-pp]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { const p = d.people[+b.dataset.pp]; p.paid = !p.paid; b.setAttribute(\"aria-pressed\", String(p.paid)); b.textContent = p.paid ? \"Paid ✓\" : \"Owes\" })\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-prm]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { d.people.splice(+b.dataset.prm, 1); rerender() })\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-ways]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {\n      const n = b.dataset.ways === \"all\" ? 2 : Number(b.dataset.ways)\n      const count = b.dataset.ways === \"all\" ? 1 : n - 1\n      const each = b.dataset.ways === \"all\" ? gross : Math.round(gross / n)\n      const old = d.people\n      d.people = Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => ({ name: old[i] ? old[i].name : \"\", amount: each, paid: old[i] ? old[i].paid : false }))\n      rerender()\n    })\n    const setAmt = (i, v) => { d.people[i].amount = v; const inp = box.querySelector(`[data-pa=\"${i}\"]`); if (inp) inp.value = v || \"\"; update() }\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-pq]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { const [i, n] = b.dataset.pq.split(\":\").map(Number); d.people[i].amount = Math.round(gross * n / 100); rerender() })\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-ppct]\").forEach(i => i.oninput = () => { const n = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Number(i.value) || 0)); setAmt(+i.dataset.ppct, Math.round(gross * n / 100)) })\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-pa]\").forEach(i => i.addEventListener(\"input\", () => { const o = box.querySelector(`[data-ppct=\"${i.dataset.pa}\"]`); if (o) o.value = \"\" }))\n    const pr = $(\"pRest\"); if (pr) pr.onclick = () => {\n      const blank = d.people.map((p, i) => i).filter(i => !(Number(d.people[i].amount) > 0)), left = Math.round((gross - total()) * 100) / 100\n      if (!blank.length || left <= 0) return\n      const each = Math.floor(left / blank.length)\n      blank.forEach((i, k) => { d.people[i].amount = k === blank.length - 1 ? Math.round((left - each * (blank.length - 1)) * 100) / 100 : each })\n      rerender()\n    }\n    $(\"pAdd\").onclick = () => { d.people.push({ name: \"\", amount: 0, paid: false }); rerender(); const ins = box.ownerDocument.querySelectorAll(\"#splitBox .pname\"); if (ins.length) ins[ins.length - 1].focus() }\n    $(\"owedSave\").onclick = () => {\n      const people = d.people.map(p => ({ name: cleanPerson(p.name) || \"Someone\", amount: Math.round((Number(p.amount) || 0) * 100) / 100, paid: !!p.paid })).filter(p => p.amount > 0)\n      if (people.reduce((s, p) => s + p.amount, 0) > gross + 0.01) { update(); return }\n      const id = d.id; state.splitting = null; state.splitDraft = null; state.editing = null\n      act(\"set-owed\", { updates: [{ id, people }] })\n    }\n    const rm = $(\"owedRemove\"); if (rm) rm.onclick = () => { const id = d.id; state.splitting = null; state.splitDraft = null; state.editing = null; act(\"set-owed\", { updates: [{ id, people: [] }] }) }\n    $(\"owedCancel\").onclick = () => { state.splitting = null; state.splitDraft = null; rerender() }\n  }\n\n  function wireTxTools(rerender) {\n    document.querySelectorAll(\"[data-edit]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.editing = b.dataset.edit; state.splitting = null; state.tagging = null; rerender() })\n    document.querySelectorAll(\"[data-editclose]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.editing = null; rerender() })\n    document.querySelectorAll(\"[data-split]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.splitting = b.dataset.split; state.splitDraft = null; state.tagging = null; rerender(); const i = document.querySelector(\"#splitBox .pname\"); if (i) i.focus() })\n    wireSplit(rerender)\n    document.querySelectorAll(\"[data-cat]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.catting = b.dataset.cat; state.splitting = null; state.tagging = null; rerender() })\n    { const box = $(\"catBox\"); if (box) {\n      box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-catpick]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { const al = $(\"catAlways\"), id = box.dataset.id; state.catting = null; state.editing = null; act(\"stmt-category\", { id, category: b.dataset.catpick, always: !!(al && al.checked) }) })\n      $(\"catCancel\").onclick = () => { state.catting = null; rerender() } } }\n    document.querySelectorAll(\"[data-oneoff]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.tagging = b.dataset.oneoff; state.splitting = null; rerender(); const i = $(\"tagName\"); if (i) i.focus() })\n    document.querySelectorAll(\"[data-untag]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => act(\"event-assign\", { ids: [b.dataset.untag], name: \"\" }))\n    const save = name => { const id = state.tagging; if (!name || !id) return; state.tagging = null; act(\"event-assign\", { ids: [id], name }) }\n    document.querySelectorAll(\"[data-tagname]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => save(b.dataset.tagname))\n    const ts = $(\"tagSave\"); if (ts) ts.onclick = () => { const n = $(\"tagName\").value.trim(); if (!n) { $(\"tagName\").focus(); return } save(n) }\n    const tc = $(\"tagCancel\"); if (tc) tc.onclick = () => { state.tagging = null; rerender() }\n  }\n\n  // Year summary: every rupee, month by month, with a running total for year-end accounts.\n  function yearRange() {\n    const shift = state.yearShift\n    if (state.yearMode === \"fy\") {\n      const start = (today.getMonth() >= 3 ? today.getFullYear() : today.getFullYear() - 1) + shift\n      const keys = Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => keyOf(new Date(start, 3 + i, 1)))\n      return { keys, label: `FY ${start}–${String(start + 1).slice(2)}`, long: `April ${start} – March ${start + 1}` }\n    }\n    const y = today.getFullYear() + shift\n    return { keys: Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => keyOf(new Date(y, i, 1))), label: String(y), long: `January – December ${y}` }\n  }\n\n  function renderYear() {\n    renderYtd()\n    const { keys, label, long } = yearRange()\n    const months = keys.filter(k => k <= NOW_KEY)\n    let ytd = 0, every = 0, once = 0, dues = 0\n    const rows = months.map(k => {\n      const s = sum(inMonth(k))\n      const total = s.total + s.eventTotal\n      ytd += total; every += s.total; once += s.eventTotal; dues += s.dues\n      return { k, everyday: s.total, oneoff: s.eventTotal, total, ytd }\n    })\n    const n = v => Math.round(v).toLocaleString(\"en-IN\")\n    $(\"yearCard\").innerHTML = `<h2>Year summary · ${label}</h2>\n      <p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">${long}</p>\n      ${rows.length ? `<div class=\"yeartable\"><table>\n        <thead><tr><th>Month</th><th>Everyday</th><th>One-off</th><th>Total<br><span class=\"ytdh\">so far</span></th></tr></thead>\n        <tbody>${rows.map(r => `<tr><td>${labelOf(r.k).replace(/ (\\d{2})(\\d{2})$/, \" ’$2\")}</td><td>${n(r.everyday)}</td><td class=\"${r.oneoff ? \"oneoff\" : \"\"}\">${r.oneoff ? n(r.oneoff) : \"–\"}</td><td><strong>${n(r.total)}</strong><span class=\"ytd\">${n(r.ytd)}</span></td></tr>`).join(\"\")}</tbody>\n        <tfoot><tr><td>Year</td><td>${n(every)}</td><td>${n(once)}</td><td>${n(ytd)}</td></tr></tfoot>\n      </table></div>\n      <p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">Amounts in ₹. Total = everyday + one-off: everything you logged. The small grey figure under each total is the running total for the year so far.${dues && !state.countDues ? ` Card bill payments (${inr(dues)} this year) are left out, because the purchases they pay for are already counted.` : \"\"}</p>\n      <button class=\"btn secondary\" id=\"yCopy\">Copy table for Excel</button>`\n      : `<p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">Nothing logged in ${label}.</p>`}`\n    const yc = $(\"yCopy\"); if (yc) yc.onclick = () => {\n      const lines = [[\"Month\", \"Everyday\", \"One-off\", \"Total\", \"Year to date\"].join(\"\\t\")]\n        .concat(rows.map(r => [labelOf(r.k), Math.round(r.everyday), Math.round(r.oneoff), Math.round(r.total), Math.round(r.ytd)].join(\"\\t\")))\n        .concat([[\"Total\", Math.round(every), Math.round(once), Math.round(ytd), \"\"].join(\"\\t\")])\n      location.href = \"app://copy?\" + encodeURIComponent(lines.join(\"\\n\"))\n    }\n  }\n\n  function renderTrends() {\n    renderYearBar()\n    renderYear()\n    const keys = yearRange().keys.filter(k => k <= NOW_KEY)\n    const firstData = state.rows.reduce((m, r) => r.month < m ? r.month : m, \"9999\")\n    const shown = keys.filter(k => k >= firstData)\n    const sums = shown.map(k => sum(inMonth(k)))\n    const catOrder = Object.keys(COLORS).filter(c => sums.some(s => s.cats[c]))\n    const totals = sums.map(s => s.total)\n    const totalLabels = {\n      id: \"totals\",\n      afterDatasetsDraw(c) {\n        const { ctx } = c, meta = c.getDatasetMeta(c.data.datasets.length - 1)\n        ctx.save(); ctx.fillStyle = css(\"--fg\"); ctx.font = `600 ${c.width < 420 ? 9 : 11}px ${css(\"--body\")}`; ctx.textAlign = \"center\"\n        totals.forEach((t, i) => { if (!t) return; const y = c.scales.y.getPixelForValue(t); ctx.fillText(inrShort(t), meta.data[i].x, y - 6) })\n        ctx.restore()\n      },\n    }\n    chart(\"monthsChart\", {\n      type: \"bar\",\n      data: { labels: shown.map(k => MON[Number(k.slice(5)) - 1]), datasets: catOrder.map(c => ({ label: c, data: sums.map(s => s.cats[c] || 0), backgroundColor: colorOf(c), borderWidth: 0, borderRadius: 2, maxBarThickness: 34 })) },\n      plugins: [totalLabels],\n      options: {\n        responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, animation: false, layout: { padding: { top: 18 } },\n        onClick: (e, els) => { if (els.length) { state.month = shown[els[0].index]; setTab(\"overview\") } },\n        plugins: { legend: { position: \"bottom\", labels: { color: css(\"--muted\"), boxWidth: 10, font: { family: css(\"--body\"), size: 11 } } },\n          tooltip: { mode: \"index\", filter: i => i.parsed.y > 0, callbacks: { label: c => `${c.dataset.label}: ${inr(c.parsed.y)}`, footer: i => `Total ${inr(totals[i[0].dataIndex])}` } } },\n        scales: { x: { ...axisStyle(), stacked: true, grid: { display: false } }, y: { ...axisStyle(), stacked: true, beginAtZero: true, suggestedMax: 1000, ticks: { ...axisStyle().ticks, precision: 0, callback: v => inrShort(v) } } },\n      },\n    })\n\n    // this month vs 3-month average, per category\n    const base = keys.length ? keys[keys.length - 1] : NOW_KEY\n    const prev3 = [1, 2, 3].map(i => sum(inMonth(shiftKey(base, -i))))\n    const cur = sum(inMonth(base))\n    const allCats = Object.keys(COLORS).filter(c => cur.cats[c] || prev3.some(s => s.cats[c]))\n    const rows = allCats.map(c => {\n      const avg = prev3.reduce((a, s) => a + (s.cats[c] || 0), 0) / 3\n      return { c, now: cur.cats[c] || 0, avg }\n    }).sort((a, b) => b.now - a.now || b.avg - a.avg)\n    $(\"avgCard\").innerHTML = `<h2>${MON[Number(base.slice(5)) - 1]} vs 3-month average</h2>\n      <dl class=\"kv num\">${rows.map(r => {\n        const d = r.now - r.avg, cls = Math.abs(d) < 1 ? \"\" : d > 0 ? \"color:var(--more)\" : \"color:var(--less)\"\n        return `<dt><span class=\"dot\" style=\"display:inline-block;background:${colorOf(r.c)};margin-right:8px\"></span>${esc(r.c)}</dt>\n          <dd>${inr(r.now)} <span class=\"small\" style=\"${cls};font-weight:500\">${r.avg ? (d >= 0 ? \"+\" : \"−\") + inrShort(Math.abs(d)) : \"new\"}</span></dd>`\n      }).join(\"\")}</dl>\n      <p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">Average of the three months before. ${base === NOW_KEY ? \"This month is still in progress.\" : \"\"}</p>`\n\n    const pays = [\"UPI\", \"Credit Card\", \"Debit Card\", \"Cash\", \"Not set\"].filter(p => sums.some(s => s.pays[p]))\n    chart(\"payChart\", {\n      type: \"bar\",\n      data: { labels: shown.map(k => MON[Number(k.slice(5)) - 1]), datasets: pays.map(p => ({ label: p, data: sums.map(s => s.pays[p] || 0), backgroundColor: PAY_COLORS[p], borderWidth: 0, maxBarThickness: 34 })) },\n      options: {\n        responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, animation: false,\n        plugins: { legend: { position: \"bottom\", labels: { color: css(\"--muted\"), boxWidth: 10, font: { family: css(\"--body\"), size: 11 } } },\n          tooltip: { mode: \"index\", callbacks: { label: c => `${c.dataset.label}: ${inr(c.parsed.y)}` } } },\n        scales: { x: { ...axisStyle(), stacked: true, grid: { display: false } }, y: { ...axisStyle(), stacked: true, beginAtZero: true, suggestedMax: 1000, ticks: { ...axisStyle().ticks, precision: 0, callback: v => inrShort(v) } } },\n      },\n    })\n  }\n\n  function renderExpenses() {\n    renderStmt()\n    const yr = yearRange()\n    const base = state.scope === \"month\" ? inMonth(state.month) : state.scope === \"year\" ? state.rows.filter(r => yr.keys.includes(r.month)) : state.rows\n    const chip = (group, value, label, color) =>\n      `<button class=\"chip\" data-g=\"${group}\" data-v=\"${esc(value ?? \"\")}\" aria-pressed=\"${state[group] === value}\">${color ? `<span class=\"dot\" style=\"background:${color}\"></span>` : \"\"}${esc(label)}</button>`\n    const catsHere = Object.keys(COLORS).filter(c => base.some(r => r.category === c))\n    $(\"catChips\").innerHTML = chip(\"cat\", null, \"All categories\") + catsHere.map(c => chip(\"cat\", c, c, colorOf(c))).join(\"\")\n    const paysHere = [\"UPI\", \"Credit Card\", \"Debit Card\", \"Cash\"].filter(p => base.some(r => r.payment === p))\n    const cardsHere = Object.keys(CARD_SHORT).filter(c => base.some(r => r.card === c))\n    $(\"payChips\").innerHTML = chip(\"pay\", null, \"All payments\") + paysHere.map(p => chip(\"pay\", p, p)).join(\"\") + cardsHere.map(c => chip(\"pay\", \"card:\" + c, CARD_SHORT[c])).join(\"\")\n    $(\"scopeChips\").innerHTML = chip(\"scope\", \"month\", labelOf(state.month, true) + (state.scope === \"month\" ? \" ▾\" : \"\")) + chip(\"scope\", \"year\", yr.label) + chip(\"scope\", \"all\", \"All time\")\n    document.querySelectorAll(\".chip\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {\n      const g = b.dataset.g, v = b.dataset.v || null\n      if (g === \"scope\" && v === \"month\" && state.scope === \"month\") { openMonthSheet(); return }\n      state[g] = g === \"scope\" ? v : (state[g] === v ? null : v)\n      render()\n    })\n\n    const q = state.q.trim().toLowerCase()\n    const list = base.filter(r =>\n      (!state.cat || r.category === state.cat) && (!state.pay || (state.pay.startsWith(\"card:\") ? r.card === state.pay.slice(5) : r.payment === state.pay)) &&\n      (!q || [r.merchant, r.notes, r.category, r.payment, r.owedBy, String(r.amount), String(r.gross)].join(\" \").toLowerCase().includes(q))\n    ).sort((a, b) => (b.date + b.created).localeCompare(a.date + a.created))\n    const s = sum(list)\n    $(\"txCount\").textContent = `${list.length} expense${list.length === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\"}`\n    $(\"txTotal\").textContent = inr(s.total) + (s.dues && !state.countDues ? ` + ${inr(s.dues)} card bill` : \"\") + (s.eventTotal ? ` + ${inr(s.eventTotal)} events` : \"\")\n\n    const LIMIT = 300\n    let html = \"\", lastDay = \"\"\n    list.slice(0, LIMIT).forEach(r => {\n      if (r.date !== lastDay) {\n        lastDay = r.date\n        const d = new Date(r.date + \"T00:00:00\")\n        const dayTotal = list.filter(x => x.date === r.date && counts(x)).reduce((a, x) => a + x.amount, 0)\n        html += `<li class=\"day\"><span>${d.toLocaleDateString(\"en-IN\", { weekday: \"short\", day: \"numeric\", month: \"short\" })}</span><span class=\"num\">${inr(dayTotal)}</span></li>`\n      }\n      html += txRow(r, true)\n    })\n    if (list.length > LIMIT) html += `<li class=\"small\" style=\"padding:12px 2px\">Showing the latest ${LIMIT}. Search or filter to narrow down.</li>`\n    if (!list.length) html = `<li class=\"small\" style=\"padding:16px 2px\">No expenses match.</li>`\n    $(\"txList\").innerHTML = html\n    wireTxTools(renderExpenses)\n  }\n\n  function renderEvents() {\n    const names = [...new Set(state.rows.filter(r => r.event).map(r => r.event))]\n    const busy = state.busy ? \"disabled\" : \"\"\n\n    // Event mode (internally still state.trip)\n    if (state.trip) {\n      const since = new Date(state.trip.since)\n      const spent = state.rows.filter(r => r.event === state.trip.name).reduce((a, r) => a + r.amount, 0)\n      $(\"tripCard\").className = \"card trip trip-on\"\n      $(\"tripCard\").innerHTML = `<h2>Event mode</h2>\n        <div class=\"event-head\"><div class=\"event-title\"><span class=\"kindbtn\" style=\"cursor:default\">${iconSvg(kindOf(state.trip.name), 18)}</span><h3>${esc(state.trip.name)}</h3></div><span class=\"num\">${inr(spent)}</span></div>\n        <p class=\"small\">On since ${since.toLocaleDateString(\"en-IN\", { day: \"numeric\", month: \"short\" })}, ${since.toLocaleTimeString(\"en-IN\", { hour: \"numeric\", minute: \"2-digit\" })}. Everything you log now goes to this event and stays out of your monthly budget.</p>\n        <button class=\"btn secondary\" id=\"tripStop\" ${busy}>Turn off event mode</button>`\n      $(\"tripStop\").onclick = () => act(\"event-stop\")\n    } else {\n      $(\"tripCard\").className = \"card trip\"\n      $(\"tripCard\").innerHTML = `<h2>Event mode</h2>\n        <p class=\"small\">Going on a trip, renovating, or planning a wedding? Turn this on first. Everything you log while it's on is kept separate and doesn't count towards your monthly spending or budget.</p>\n        <p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">Just one expense, like a repair? No need for event mode: find it under <strong>Expenses</strong> and tap <strong>Mark as one-off</strong>.</p>\n        <div class=\"row-input\"><input id=\"tripName\" list=\"eventNames\" placeholder=\"Name it, e.g. Goa trip or Renovation\" maxlength=\"60\" autocomplete=\"off\"><button class=\"btn\" id=\"tripStart\" ${busy}>Start</button></div>\n        <datalist id=\"eventNames\">${names.map(n => `<option value=\"${esc(n)}\">`).join(\"\")}</datalist>`\n      $(\"tripStart\").onclick = () => {\n        const name = $(\"tripName\").value.trim()\n        if (!name) { $(\"tripName\").focus(); return }\n        act(\"event-start\", { name })\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Each event: total, dates, categories, its expenses\n    const events = names.map(name => {\n      const list = state.rows.filter(r => r.event === name).sort((a, b) => (b.date + b.created).localeCompare(a.date + a.created))\n      const total = list.reduce((a, r) => a + r.amount, 0)\n      const cats = {}\n      for (const r of list) cats[r.category] = (cats[r.category] || 0) + r.amount\n      const dates = list.map(r => r.date).sort()\n      return { name, list, total, cats, from: dates[0], to: dates[dates.length - 1] }\n    }).sort((a, b) => b.to.localeCompare(a.to))\n\n    const fmt = d => new Date(d + \"T00:00:00\").toLocaleDateString(\"en-IN\", { day: \"numeric\", month: \"short\", year: \"numeric\" })\n    let html = \"\"\n    if (!events.length) html = `<section class=\"card\"><h2>Events</h2><p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">No events yet. Start event mode above, or add past expenses to an event with the button below.</p></section>`\n    for (const e of events) {\n      const cats = Object.entries(e.cats).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])\n      const open = state.openEvent === e.name\n      html += `<section class=\"card\">\n        <div class=\"event-head\"><div class=\"event-title\"><button class=\"kindbtn\" data-kindfor=\"${esc(e.name)}\" aria-label=\"Change the icon for ${esc(e.name)}\">${iconSvg(kindOf(e.name), 18)}</button><h3>${esc(e.name)}</h3></div><span class=\"num\">${inr(e.total)}</span></div>\n        ${state.kindPicking === e.name ? `<div class=\"kindpick\" role=\"group\" aria-label=\"Choose an icon\">${KINDS.map(([k, label]) => `<button data-setkind=\"${k}\" data-ev=\"${esc(e.name)}\" aria-pressed=\"${kindOf(e.name) === k}\">${iconSvg(k, 20)}${label}</button>`).join(\"\")}</div>` : \"\"}\n        <p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">${e.from === e.to ? fmt(e.from) : `${fmt(e.from)} – ${fmt(e.to)}`} · ${e.list.length} expense${e.list.length === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\"}</p>\n        <div class=\"stack\" aria-hidden=\"true\">${cats.map(([c, v]) => `<span style=\"width:${v / e.total * 100}%;background:${colorOf(c)}\"></span>`).join(\"\")}</div>\n        <dl class=\"kv num\">${cats.slice(0, 4).map(([c, v]) => `<dt><span class=\"dot\" style=\"display:inline-block;background:${colorOf(c)};margin-right:8px\"></span>${esc(c)}</dt><dd>${inr(v)}</dd>`).join(\"\")}</dl>\n        <details class=\"ev\" data-ev=\"${esc(e.name)}\" ${open ? \"open\" : \"\"}><summary>Show expenses</summary>\n          <ul class=\"tx\">${e.list.map(r => `<li class=\"pick\" style=\"grid-template-columns:10px 1fr auto auto\">\n            <span class=\"dot\" style=\"background:${colorOf(r.category)}\"></span>\n            <span class=\"what\"><div>${esc(r.merchant || r.category)}</div><small>${esc(fmt(r.date))}</small></span>\n            <span class=\"amt num\">${inr(r.amount)}</span>\n            <button class=\"rm\" data-rm=\"${esc(r.id)}\" ${busy}>Remove</button></li>`).join(\"\")}</ul>\n        </details>\n      </section>`\n    }\n\n    // Add past expenses to an event\n    if (state.picking) {\n      const since = new Date(); since.setDate(since.getDate() - 120)\n      const cutoff = since.toISOString().slice(0, 10)\n      const cands = state.rows.filter(r => !r.event && r.id && r.date >= cutoff).sort((a, b) => (b.date + b.created).localeCompare(a.date + a.created))\n      html += `<section class=\"card\">\n        <h2>Add expenses to an event</h2>\n        <div class=\"row-input\"><input id=\"pickName\" list=\"eventNames2\" placeholder=\"Event name, e.g. Renovation\" maxlength=\"60\" value=\"${esc(state.picking.name || \"\")}\" autocomplete=\"off\"></div>\n        <datalist id=\"eventNames2\">${names.map(n => `<option value=\"${esc(n)}\">`).join(\"\")}</datalist>\n        <p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">Tick the expenses that belong to it (last 4 months).</p>\n        <ul class=\"tx\">${cands.map(r => `<li class=\"pick\"><input type=\"checkbox\" data-pick=\"${esc(r.id)}\" ${state.picked.has(r.id) ? \"checked\" : \"\"} aria-label=\"Select ${esc(r.merchant || r.category)}\">\n          <span class=\"dot\" style=\"background:${colorOf(r.category)}\"></span>\n          <span class=\"what\"><div>${esc(r.merchant || r.category)}</div><small>${esc(fmt(r.date))} · ${esc(r.category)}</small></span>\n          <span class=\"amt num\">${inr(r.amount)}</span></li>`).join(\"\") || `<li class=\"small\" style=\"padding:10px 2px\">No expenses to add.</li>`}</ul>\n        <div class=\"row-input\"><button class=\"btn\" id=\"pickSave\" ${busy}>Add ${state.picked.size || \"\"} to event</button><button class=\"btn secondary\" id=\"pickCancel\">Cancel</button></div>\n      </section>`\n    } else {\n      html += `<button class=\"btn secondary\" id=\"pickOpen\" style=\"width:100%\">Add past expenses to an event</button>`\n    }\n    $(\"eventList\").innerHTML = html\n\n    document.querySelectorAll(\"[data-kindfor]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.kindPicking = state.kindPicking === b.dataset.kindfor ? null : b.dataset.kindfor; renderEvents() })\n    document.querySelectorAll(\"[data-setkind]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {\n      state.kinds = Object.assign({}, state.kinds, { [b.dataset.ev]: b.dataset.setkind })\n      state.kindPicking = null\n      renderEvents()\n      location.href = \"app://event-icon?\" + encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify({ name: b.dataset.ev, kind: b.dataset.setkind }))\n    })\n    document.querySelectorAll(\"details.ev\").forEach(d => d.ontoggle = () => { if (d.open) state.openEvent = d.dataset.ev; else if (state.openEvent === d.dataset.ev) state.openEvent = null })\n    document.querySelectorAll(\"[data-rm]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => act(\"event-assign\", { ids: [b.dataset.rm], name: \"\" }))\n    const po = $(\"pickOpen\"); if (po) po.onclick = () => { state.picking = { name: \"\" }; state.picked = new Set(); renderEvents() }\n    const pc = $(\"pickCancel\"); if (pc) pc.onclick = () => { state.picking = null; state.picked = new Set(); renderEvents() }\n    const pn = $(\"pickName\"); if (pn) pn.oninput = () => { state.picking.name = pn.value }\n    document.querySelectorAll(\"[data-pick]\").forEach(c => c.onchange = () => {\n      if (c.checked) state.picked.add(c.dataset.pick); else state.picked.delete(c.dataset.pick)\n      $(\"pickSave\").textContent = `Add ${state.picked.size || \"\"} to event`\n    })\n    const ps = $(\"pickSave\"); if (ps) ps.onclick = () => {\n      const name = (state.picking.name || \"\").trim()\n      if (!name) { $(\"pickName\").focus(); return }\n      if (!state.picked.size) return\n      const ids = [...state.picked]\n      state.picking = null; state.picked = new Set()\n      act(\"event-assign\", { ids, name })\n    }\n  }\n\n\n  // ---------- owed to you: grouped by person ----------\n  const personKey = n => String(n || \"\").toLowerCase().replace(/\\s+/g, \" \").trim()\n  const owedNote = key => { const o = inMonth(key).reduce((s, r) => s + r.owed, 0); return o ? ` · ${inr(o)} owed back not counted` : \"\" }\n  function owedPeople() {\n    const by = {}\n    for (const r of state.rows) r.people.forEach((p, i) => {\n      if (p.paid) return\n      const k = personKey(p.name)\n      const g = by[k] || (by[k] = { key: k, name: p.name, total: 0, items: [] })\n      g.total += p.amount; g.items.push({ r, i, amount: p.amount })\n    })\n    return Object.values(by).sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total)\n  }\n  // Marks the given entries (expense id + person index) as paid back, one Notion update per expense.\n  function markPaid(items) {\n    const per = {}\n    for (const it of items) (per[it.r.id] = per[it.r.id] || { r: it.r, idx: [] }).idx.push(it.i)\n    const updates = Object.values(per).map(({ r, idx }) => ({ id: r.id, people: r.people.map((p, i) => Object.assign({}, p, idx.includes(i) ? { paid: true } : {})) }))\n    act(\"set-owed\", { updates, reason: \"paid\" })\n  }\n  // WhatsApp reminder: the message is written here; the script finds their number and opens WhatsApp with it ready to send.\n  state.remind = window.__REMIND__ || {}\n  window.setReminded = (key, info) => { state.remind[key] = Object.assign({}, state.remind[key], info); renderOwed(); if (state.tab === \"people\") renderPeople() }\n  function reminderText(g) {\n    const first = g.name.split(\" \")[0]\n    const items = [...g.items].sort((a, b) => String(a.r.date).localeCompare(String(b.r.date)))\n    const lines = items.slice(0, 8).map(it => `• ${shownName(it.r.merchant) || it.r.category} (${new Date(it.r.date + \"T00:00:00\").toLocaleDateString(\"en-IN\", { day: \"numeric\", month: \"short\" })}): ${inr(it.amount)}`)\n    if (items.length > 8) lines.push(`• and ${items.length - 8} more`)\n    return `Hi ${first}, just a gentle reminder about the ${inr(g.total)} for:\\n${lines.join(\"\\n\")}\\n\\nNo rush, pay whenever you can. Thanks!`\n  }\n  function remindAgo(iso) {\n    const d = Math.round((Date.parse(new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)) - Date.parse(iso)) / 864e5)\n    return d <= 0 ? \"reminded today\" : d === 1 ? \"reminded yesterday\" : `reminded ${d} days ago`\n  }\n  const sendRemind = (g, change) => { location.href = \"app://remind?\" + encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify({ amount: g.total, key: g.key, name: g.name, text: reminderText(g), change: !!change, personId: g.personId || \"\" })) }\n\n  // ---------- credit cards: bills due this month, checked against your alerts, and whether they're paid ----------\n  function renderBills() {\n    const host = $(\"billsPart\"), card = $(\"cardCard\")\n    if (!host) return\n    const seenCard = new Set(), dueOf = b => b.dueDay || b.statementDay\n    const list = ((state.check && state.check.bills) || []).filter(b => String(dueOf(b)).slice(0, 7) === state.month && !seenCard.has(b.card) && seenCard.add(b.card))\n      .sort((a, b) => dueOf(a) < dueOf(b) ? -1 : 1)\n    const fresh = b => b.result === \"Extra charges\" && !b.seen\n    card.classList.toggle(\"attn\", list.some(fresh))\n    if (!list.length) { host.innerHTML = \"\"; return }\n    const busy = state.busy ? \"disabled\" : \"\"\n    const day = iso => iso ? new Date(iso + \"T00:00:00\").toLocaleDateString(\"en-IN\", { day: \"numeric\", month: \"short\" }) : \"\"\n    const todayIso = (() => { const d = new Date(); return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, \"0\")}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, \"0\")}` })()\n    const daysTo = iso => Math.round((Date.parse(iso + \"T00:00:00\") - Date.parse(todayIso + \"T00:00:00\")) / 864e5)\n    const pill = b => b.result === \"Matches\" ? `<span class=\"pill less\">✓ Matches</span>`\n      : b.result === \"Extra charges\" ? `<span class=\"pill more\">${inr(b.difference)} extra</span>`\n      : b.result === \"Lower than expected\" ? `<span class=\"pill flat\">${inr(-b.difference)} lower</span>`\n      : `<span class=\"pill flat\">Can't check</span>`\n    // What's left to pay on this bill\n    const payLine = b => {\n      const left = Math.round((b.total - (b.paid || 0)) * 100) / 100\n      if (b.total <= 0) return `<span class=\"paid\">Nothing to pay</span>`\n      if (left <= 1) return `<span class=\"paid\">✓ Paid ${esc(day(b.paidDay))}</span>`\n      const part = b.paid > 1 ? `${inr(b.paid)} paid · ` : \"\"\n      if (!b.dueDay) return `${part}${inr(left)} to pay`\n      const n = daysTo(b.dueDay)\n      if (n < 0) return `<span class=\"overdue\">${part}${inr(left)} overdue by ${-n} day${n === -1 ? \"\" : \"s\"}</span>`\n      return `${part}${inr(left)} to pay · ${n === 0 ? \"due today\" : n === 1 ? \"due tomorrow\" : `due in ${n} days`}${b.minDue && !b.paid ? ` · min ${inr(b.minDue)}` : \"\"}`\n    }\n    const total = list.reduce((s, b) => s + Math.max(0, b.total - (b.paid || 0)), 0)\n    host.innerHTML = `<div class=\"billhead\"><span>Bills due in ${esc(labelOf(state.month, true).split(\" \")[0])}</span>${total > 1 ? `<span class=\"num\">${inr(total)} to pay</span>` : `<span class=\"paid\">All paid</span>`}</div>\n      <ul class=\"tx\">${list.map(b => {\n        const open = state.billOpen === b.id || (state.billOpen === undefined && fresh(b))\n        return `<li class=\"txi\"><button class=\"personrow\" data-bill=\"${esc(b.id)}\" aria-expanded=\"${open}\">\n          <span class=\"what\"><div>${esc(cardShort(b.card))} <span class=\"billamt num\">${inr(b.total)}</span></div><small>${payLine(b)}</small></span>\n          ${pill(b)}</button>\n          ${open ? `<p class=\"small billnote\">Bill of ${esc(day(b.statementDay))}${b.dueDay ? `, due ${esc(day(b.dueDay))}` : \"\"}. ${esc(b.details)}</p>\n            ${fresh(b) ? `<div class=\"txtools\"><button class=\"act\" data-billseen=\"${esc(b.id)}\" ${busy}>Got it</button></div>` : \"\"}` : \"\"}</li>`\n      }).join(\"\")}</ul>`\n    host.querySelectorAll(\"[data-bill]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { const id = b.dataset.bill, wasOpen = b.getAttribute(\"aria-expanded\") === \"true\"; state.billOpen = wasOpen ? null : id; renderBills() })\n    host.querySelectorAll(\"[data-billseen]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => act(\"bill-seen\", { id: b.dataset.billseen }))\n  }\n  // ---------- settings: forex markup per card (saved to Notion) ----------\n  const FX_OPTS = [0, 0.99, 1, 1.5, 1.75, 1.99, 2, 2.5, 3, 3.5]\n  function renderMarkups() {\n    const box = $(\"markupSet\"), m = state.check && state.check.markups\n    if (!box) return\n    if (!m) { box.hidden = true; return }\n    box.hidden = false\n    if (box.contains(document.activeElement)) return   // don't redraw while you're typing\n    const names = CARD_SHORT\n    box.innerHTML = `<div class=\"setlabel\">Forex markup on foreign-currency spends</div>\n      <div class=\"markgrid\">${Object.entries(names).map(([card, name]) => { const cur = m[card], known = cur == null || FX_OPTS.includes(Number(cur))\n        return `<label>${esc(name)}<span class=\"markin\"><select data-markup=\"${esc(card)}\"><option value=\"\">Not set</option>${FX_OPTS.map(v => `<option value=\"${v}\" ${cur != null && Number(cur) === v ? \"selected\" : \"\"}>${v}%${v === 0 ? \" (no markup)\" : v === 3.5 ? \" (most cards)\" : \"\"}</option>`).join(\"\")}<option value=\"other\" ${known ? \"\" : \"selected\"}>Other…</option></select><input type=\"number\" inputmode=\"decimal\" min=\"0\" max=\"10\" step=\"0.01\" data-markother=\"${esc(card)}\" value=\"${known ? \"\" : esc(String(cur))}\" ${known ? \"hidden\" : \"\"} placeholder=\"%\"></span></label>` }).join(\"\")}</div>\n      <div style=\"display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap\"><button class=\"act\" id=\"markSave\" disabled>Save markups</button><span class=\"small\">Saved to your Card Bills table in Notion. 18% GST is added on top. Only new foreign spends use a changed number.</span></div>`\n    const save = $(\"markSave\")\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-markup]\").forEach(i => i.onchange = () => {\n      const o = box.querySelector(`[data-markother=\"${CSS.escape(i.dataset.markup)}\"]`); if (o) { o.hidden = i.value !== \"other\"; if (!o.hidden) o.focus() }\n      save.disabled = state.busy })\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-markother]\").forEach(i => i.oninput = () => { save.disabled = state.busy })\n    save.onclick = () => {\n      const markups = {}\n      box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-markup]\").forEach(i => { const v = i.value === \"other\" ? (box.querySelector(`[data-markother=\"${CSS.escape(i.dataset.markup)}\"]`) || {}).value : i.value\n        if (v !== \"\" && v != null && !isNaN(Number(v))) markups[i.dataset.markup] = Number(v) })\n      save.disabled = true\n      act(\"set-markups\", { markups })\n    }\n  }\n\n  // ---------- loans & EMIs ----------\n  const loanList = () => (state.check && state.check.loans) || []\n  const shortDate = iso => iso ? new Date(String(iso).slice(0, 10) + \"T00:00:00\").toLocaleDateString(\"en-IN\", { day: \"numeric\", month: \"short\" }) : \"\"\n  const monthYear = key => key ? new Date(key + \"-01T00:00:00\").toLocaleDateString(\"en-IN\", { month: \"short\", year: \"numeric\" }) : \"\"\n  const loanLabel = l => (l.type && l.type !== \"Other\" ? l.type.toLowerCase() : \"loan\") + \" EMI\" + (loanList().length > 1 ? ` (${lenderOf(l)})` : \"\")\n  // \"Home loan · Bajaj Housing Finance (A/c …8406)\" → \"Bajaj Housing Finance top-up\"-style short names\n  const lenderOf = l => { const n = String(l.name || \"\").split(\"·\").slice(1).join(\"·\").trim() || String(l.name || \"Loan\"); return n.replace(/\\s*\\(A\\/c …(\\d{4})\\)/, \" …$1\") }\n  // What each loan was paid in a month: EMIs, prepayments and fees, with the day\n  function paidInMonth(key) {\n    const out = []\n    for (const l of loanList()) {\n      const pick = (list, what) => (list || []).filter(e => String(e.day).slice(0, 7) === key).map(e => ({ loan: l, what, day: e.day, amount: e.amount }))\n      out.push(...pick(l.emis, \"EMI\"), ...pick(l.prepayments, \"Prepayment\"), ...pick(l.charges, \"Fees and charges\"), ...pick(l.afterClose, \"Debited after the loan closed (check for a refund)\"))\n    }\n    return out.sort((a, b) => a.day < b.day ? -1 : 1)\n  }\n  // The EMI line under the month's total: paid that month, or still due this month. Never part of spending.\n  function emiLine(key) {\n    const parts = []\n    for (const l of loanList()) {\n      if (l.closed && !(l.emis || []).some(e => String(e.day).slice(0, 7) === key)) continue\n      if (l.status && l.status.state === \"waiting\") continue\n      const paid = (l.emis || []).filter(e => String(e.day).slice(0, 7) === key).reduce((s, e) => s + e.amount, 0)\n      if (paid) parts.push(`${loanLabel(l)} ${inr(paid)}`)\n      else if (key === NOW_KEY && l.status) parts.push(`${loanLabel(l)} ${inr(l.status.amount)} ${l.status.state === \"late\" || l.status.state === \"expected\" ? \"not debited yet\" : \"due \" + shortDate(l.status.due)}`)\n    }\n    return parts.length ? `<div class=\"evline\">Fixed: ${esc(parts.join(\" · \"))} · not in spending</div>` : \"\"\n  }\n  const ordinal = d => d + ([, \"st\", \"nd\", \"rd\"][d % 10 > 3 || Math.floor(d / 10) === 1 ? 0 : d % 10] || \"th\")\n  const finYearOf = iso => { const [y, m] = iso.split(\"-\").map(Number), s = m >= 4 ? y : y - 1; return { start: `${s}-04-01`, label: `FY ${s}–${String(s + 1).slice(2)}` } }\n  function statusLine(l) {\n    const s = l.status\n    if (!s) return \"\"\n    if (s.state === \"closed\") return `Closed ${esc(shortDate(s.closed))}${s.lastDay ? ` · last EMI ${esc(shortDate(s.lastDay))}` : \"\"}`\n    if (s.state === \"waiting\") return `Waiting for the first EMI debit. It's picked up automatically, with its amount and date.`\n    if (s.state === \"paid\") return `<span class=\"paid\">✓ Paid ${esc(shortDate(s.day))}</span> · next ${esc(shortDate(s.nextDue))}${s.daysTo <= 3 ? ` (in ${s.daysTo} day${s.daysTo === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\"})` : \"\"}`\n    if (s.state === \"today\") return `Debits today · ${inr(s.amount)}`\n    if (s.state === \"soon\") return `<span class=\"soon\">Debits in ${s.daysTo} day${s.daysTo === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\"}</span> (${esc(shortDate(s.due))}) · keep ${inr(s.amount)} in the account`\n    if (s.state === \"upcoming\") return `Next debit ${esc(shortDate(s.due))}`\n    if (s.state === \"expected\") return `Due ${esc(shortDate(s.due))} · no debit alert yet, it may still arrive`\n    return `<span class=\"overdue\">No debit seen for ${esc(shortDate(s.due))}, ${s.daysLate} days late.</span> Check your bank app; a bounced EMI usually brings a charge.`\n  }\n  const monAbbr = key => new Date(key + \"-01T00:00:00\").toLocaleDateString(\"en-IN\", { month: \"short\" })\n  const pct = r => `${Number(r).toFixed(2).replace(/\\.?0+$/, \"\")}%`\n  const hasLoanTab = () => polList().length || polFound().length || polSugg().length || state.polForm || loanList().length || ((state.check && state.check.loansFound) || []).length || ((state.check && state.check.loanSuggestions) || []).length || (state.check && state.check.loansError)\n\n  // Interest vs principal, EMI by EMI, for this financial year (April–March). Later months are estimates.\n  // \"Your EMI went down\": with the same term, the new EMI gives the new rate (Bajaj and many lenders cut the EMI when rates fall)\n  function rateHintHtml(l) {\n    const hs = (l.progress && l.progress.rateHints) || [], busy = state.busy ? \"disabled\" : \"\"\n    return hs.slice(-2).map(h => {\n      const dir = h.to < h.from ? \"down\" : \"up\", from = new Date(Date.parse(h.day + \"T00:00:00Z\") - 20 * 864e5).toISOString().slice(0, 10)\n      if (h.prepaid || !h.rate) return `<p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">EMI went ${dir} from ${inr(h.from)} to ${inr(h.to)} on ${esc(shortDate(h.day))}${h.prepaid ? \", after your prepayment, so the rate may not have changed\" : \"\"}.</p>`\n      return `<div class=\"notice\" style=\"padding:8px 10px\"><span class=\"small\">EMI went ${dir} from ${inr(h.from)} to ${inr(h.to)} on ${esc(shortDate(h.day))}. If the term stayed the same, your rate went from ${pct(h.oldRate)} to about <b>${pct(h.rate)}</b>.</span><button class=\"act subtle\" data-ratehint=\"${esc(JSON.stringify({ id: l.id, rate: h.rate, from }))}\" ${busy}>Use ${pct(h.rate)}</button></div>`\n    }).join(\"\")\n  }\n  // Figures read from the lender's PDFs (statement, schedule, certificate), waiting for you to confirm\n  const docName = d => ({ statement: \"statement\", schedule: \"repayment schedule\", certificate: \"interest certificate\", foreclosure: \"foreclosure statement\", sanction: \"sanction letter\" })[d.kind] || \"document\"\n  function docHtml(l) {\n    const busy = state.busy ? \"disabled\" : \"\"\n    return (l.docs || []).map((d, k) => {\n      if (d.used || d.ignored) return \"\"\n      const who = esc(d.lender || lenderOf(l)), when = d.asOf || d.mailDay\n      if (d.locked) return `<div class=\"notice\" style=\"padding:8px 10px\"><span class=\"small\">${who} sent a password-protected PDF (${esc(d.file || \"document\")}${d.mailDay ? `, ${esc(shortDate(d.mailDay))}` : \"\"}), so it couldn't be read. Open it in Files and enter the password, then Share › Print › Share › <b>Send loan doc</b>.</span><button class=\"act subtle\" data-docskip=\"${esc(l.id)}|${k}\" ${busy}>Dismiss</button></div>`\n      if (d.kind === \"certificate\") return `<p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">${who}'s interest certificate${d.fy ? `, ${esc(d.fy)}` : \"\"}: ${d.fyInterest ? `${inr(d.fyInterest)} interest` : \"\"}${d.fyPrincipal ? `, ${inr(d.fyPrincipal)} principal` : \"\"}. Use these figures for tax; the dashboard's are estimates.</p>`\n      const bits = [d.rate ? `rate ${pct(d.rate)}` : \"\", d.outstanding ? `outstanding ${inr(d.outstanding)}${d.asOf ? ` on ${shortDate(d.asOf)}` : \"\"}` : \"\", d.tenureLeft ? `${d.tenureLeft} EMIs left` : \"\", d.tenure ? `${d.tenure}-month term` : \"\", d.emi ? `EMI ${inr(d.emi)}` : \"\", d.paidAdded ? `${d.paidAdded} missing payment${d.paidAdded === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\"} added` : \"\"].filter(Boolean)\n      if (!bits.length) return \"\"\n      return `<div class=\"notice\" style=\"padding:8px 10px\"><span class=\"small\">From ${who}'s ${docName(d)}${when ? ` (${esc(shortDate(when))} ${String(when).slice(0, 4)})` : \"\"}: ${esc(bits.join(\", \"))}.</span><span class=\"txtools\" style=\"margin:0\"><button class=\"act subtle\" data-docuse=\"${esc(l.id)}|${k}\" ${busy}>Use these</button><button class=\"act subtle\" data-docskip=\"${esc(l.id)}|${k}\" ${busy}>Ignore</button></span></div>`\n    }).join(\"\")\n  }\n  const basisLine = l => (l.progress ? `<p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">Interest charged on ${l.basis === \"monthly\" ? \"equal months\" : \"actual days\"}. <button class=\"linkbtn\" data-basis=\"${esc(l.id)}|${l.basis === \"monthly\" ? \"daily\" : \"monthly\"}\">Switch to ${l.basis === \"monthly\" ? \"actual days\" : \"equal months\"}</button></p>` : \"\")\n  // a loan closed when another began within a few weeks was moved there (a balance transfer)\n  // …and only if the new loan's payout is about the size of this one's balance (a small top-up closed the same week was paid by you)\n  function payoffBy(l) {\n    const owed = (l.progress && l.progress.payoff && l.progress.payoff.principal) || 0\n    const to = loanList().find(o => o !== l && o.start && Math.abs((Date.parse(o.start) - Date.parse(l.closed)) / 864e5) <= 40 &&\n      (!(o.disbursed || o.amount) || !owed || Math.abs((o.disbursed || o.amount) - owed) <= Math.max(o.disbursed || o.amount, owed) * 0.1))\n    return to ? `by ${lenderOf(to)} (balance transfer)` : \"by you\"\n  }\n  function splitChart(l) {\n    const p = l.progress\n    return p ? ipChart((p.schedule) || [], p.fy.interestYear, p.fy.principalYear + p.fy.prepaid, `${p.fy.label}, EMI by EMI`) : \"\"\n  }\n  function ipChart(rows, yi, yp, title) {\n    if (!rows.length) return \"\"\n    const max = Math.max(...rows.filter(r => !r.payoff).map(r => r.interest + Math.max(0, r.principal)), 1), H = 96\n    const cols = rows.map((r, k) => {\n      if (r.payoff) {\n        const tip = `${shortDate(r.day)}: loan closed, ${inr(r.principal)} principal paid off${r.by ? ` ${r.by}` : \"\"}`\n        return `<button class=\"ipcol payoff\" data-tip=\"${esc(tip)}\" aria-label=\"${esc(tip)}\"><span class=\"ipbar\"><span class=\"ipp brk\" style=\"height:${H}px\"></span></span><span class=\"ipm\">✓</span></button>`\n      }\n      const pr = Math.max(0, r.principal), hi = Math.max(1, Math.round(r.interest / max * H)), hp = Math.round(pr / max * H)\n      const tip = `${monthYear(r.key)}${r.est ? \" (estimate)\" : r.assumed ? \" (assumed)\" : \"\"}: ${inr(r.interest)} interest, ${inr(pr)} principal`\n      return `<button class=\"ipcol${r.est ? \" est\" : \"\"}\" data-tip=\"${esc(tip)}\" aria-label=\"${esc(tip)}\"><span class=\"ipbar\"><span class=\"ipi\" style=\"height:${hi}px\"></span>${hp ? `<span class=\"ipp\" style=\"height:${hp}px\"></span>` : \"\"}</span><span class=\"ipm\">${esc(monAbbr(r.key).slice(0, 1))}</span></button>`\n    }).join(\"\")\n    const share = yi + yp > 0 ? Math.round(yi / (yi + yp) * 100) : 0\n    return `<div class=\"ipwrap\">\n      <div class=\"ipttl\"><span>${esc(title)}</span><span class=\"iplegend\"><i class=\"sw i\"></i>Interest <i class=\"sw p\"></i>Principal</span></div>\n      <div class=\"ipchart\" style=\"height:${H + 18}px\">${cols}</div>\n      <div class=\"iptip small\" aria-live=\"polite\">Tap a bar for that month. Faded bars are estimates.</div>\n      <div class=\"ipsplit\" role=\"img\" aria-label=\"${share}% of this year's EMIs go to interest\"><span class=\"i\" style=\"width:${share}%\"></span><span class=\"p\" style=\"width:${100 - share}%\"></span></div>\n      <div class=\"ipsplitlbl small\"><span>${share}% interest · ${inr(yi)}</span><span>${100 - share}% principal · ${inr(yp)}</span></div>\n    </div>`\n  }\n\n  // Top of the Loans tab: what you pay every month, what's left, this year's interest, and what was paid in the month you're looking at\n  function renderLoansTop() {\n    const box = $(\"loansTop\"); if (!box) return\n    const loans = loanList(), openL = loans.filter(l => !l.closed)\n    if (!loans.length) { box.hidden = true; return }\n    box.hidden = false\n    const emi = openL.reduce((t, l) => t + ((l.status && l.status.amount) || l.emi || 0), 0)\n    const withP = loans.filter(l => l.progress), out = openL.filter(l => l.progress).reduce((t, l) => t + l.progress.outstanding, 0)\n    const fyI = withP.reduce((t, l) => t + l.progress.fy.interest, 0), fyP = withP.reduce((t, l) => t + l.progress.fy.principal + l.progress.fy.prepaid, 0)\n    const label = withP.length ? withP[0].progress.fy.label : finYearOf(NOW_KEY + \"-01\").label\n    const paid = paidInMonth(state.month), mon = labelOf(state.month, true).split(\" \")[0]\n    // all loans together, month by month (e.g. Bajaj until September, HDFC from October)\n    const byKey = {}\n    for (const l of withP) for (const r of l.progress.schedule || []) { const x = byKey[r.key] = byKey[r.key] || { key: r.key, interest: 0, principal: 0, est: false, assumed: false }; x.interest += r.interest; x.principal += Math.max(0, r.principal); x.est = x.est || !!r.est; x.assumed = x.assumed || !!r.assumed }\n    const combined = Object.values(byKey).sort((a, b) => a.key < b.key ? -1 : 1)\n    const yI = withP.reduce((t, l) => t + l.progress.fy.interestYear, 0), yP = withP.reduce((t, l) => t + l.progress.fy.principalYear + l.progress.fy.prepaid, 0)\n    box.innerHTML = `<div class=\"label\">Loan EMIs every month</div>\n      ${emi ? `<div class=\"big num\">${inr(emi)}</div>` : \"\"}\n      <div class=\"small\">${emi ? \"\" : `${esc(openL.map(lenderOf).join(\", \") || \"No open loans\")}: ${openL.length ? \"first EMI not debited yet. \" : \"\"}`}${openL.length} open loan${openL.length === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\"} · a fixed cost, not counted in spending</div>\n      ${withP.length > 1 ? `<dl class=\"kv num loankv\" style=\"margin-top:6px\">\n        ${out ? `<dt>Still to repay</dt><dd>${inr(out)}</dd>` : \"\"}\n        <dt>${esc(label)} so far</dt><dd>${inr(fyI)} interest<br><span class=\"sub\">${inr(fyP)} principal</span></dd>\n      </dl>` : \"\"}\n      ${withP.length > 1 && combined.length ? ipChart(combined, yI, yP, `${label}, all loans`) : \"\"}\n      <div class=\"subhead\" style=\"margin-top:8px\">Paid in ${esc(mon)}</div>\n      ${paid.length ? `<dl class=\"kv num loankv\">${paid.map(p => `<dt>${esc(lenderOf(p.loan))}<br><span class=\"sub\">${esc(p.what)} on ${esc(shortDate(p.day))}</span></dt><dd>${inr(p.amount)}</dd>`).join(\"\")}</dl>`\n        : `<p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">No loan payments seen in ${esc(mon)}${state.month === NOW_KEY ? \" yet\" : \"\"}.</p>`}\n      <div class=\"txtools\" style=\"margin-top:6px\"><button class=\"act\" id=\"loanUpload\" ${state.busy ? \"disabled\" : \"\"}>Add a loan document (PDF)</button></div>\n      <p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">A statement, repayment schedule, sanction letter or interest certificate. It goes to your Google script to be read, then the figures show on the loan's card to confirm. PDFs your lender emails are read without this. <button class=\"linkbtn\" id=\"loanEmail\">Upload settings</button></p>`\n    $(\"loanUpload\").onclick = () => act(\"loan-upload\", {})\n    $(\"loanEmail\").onclick = () => act(\"loan-email\", {})\n  }\n\n  // Loans folded into one line (open by tapping), so insurance and the rest are a glance away. Remembered on this phone.\n  function renderLoansFold() {\n    const box = $(\"loansFold\"); if (!box) return\n    const loans = loanList(), openL = loans.filter(l => !l.closed), found = ((state.check && state.check.loansFound) || []).length + ((state.check && state.check.loanSuggestions) || []).length\n    if (!loans.length && !found) { box.hidden = true; return }\n    const open = store.get(\"loansOpen\", false)\n    const emi = openL.reduce((t, l) => t + ((l.status && l.status.amount) || l.emi || 0), 0)\n    const left = openL.filter(l => l.progress).reduce((t, l) => t + l.progress.outstanding, 0)\n    const late = openL.some(l => l.status && l.status.state === \"late\"), soon = openL.find(l => l.status && (l.status.state === \"soon\" || l.status.state === \"today\"))\n    const line = [openL.length ? \"up\" : \"\"}\" aria-hidden=\"true\"></span></button>`\n    $(\"loansFoldBtn\").onclick = () => { store.set(\"loansOpen\", !open); renderLoans(); renderLoansFold() }\n    if (!open) { $(\"loansTop\").hidden = true; $(\"loansCard\").hidden = true }\n  }\n  function renderLoans() {\n    const box = $(\"loansCard\"), loans = loanList(), sugg = (state.check && state.check.loanSuggestions) || [], found = (state.check && state.check.loansFound) || []\n    if (!box) return\n    renderLoansTop()\n    const err = state.check && state.check.loansError\n    if (!loans.length && !sugg.length && !found.length && err) {\n      box.hidden = false\n      box.innerHTML = `<h2>Loans</h2>` + (err === \"no-access\"\n        ? `<p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">Your Notion integration can't open the <b>Loans</b> table yet, so EMIs aren't shown anywhere, including past months.</p><ol class=\"small steps-in\"><li>In Notion, open <b>Loans</b> (sidebar › Private).</li><li>Tap <b>•••</b> › <b>Connections</b> and add the same integration your Expenses table uses.</li><li>Come back and tap <b>Refresh</b>.</li></ol>`\n        : `<p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">Couldn't load your loans just now. Tap <b>Refresh</b> to try again.</p>`)\n      return\n    }\n    if (!loans.length && !sugg.length && !found.length) { box.hidden = true; return }\n    box.hidden = false\n    const busy = state.busy ? \"disabled\" : \"\"\n    box.classList.toggle(\"attn\", loans.some(l => l.status && l.status.state === \"late\"))\n    const openL = loans.filter(l => !l.closed), closedL = loans.filter(l => l.closed)\n    const withP = loans.filter(l => l.progress)\n    const fyAll = withP.length > 1 ? withP.reduce((a, l) => ({ i: a.i + l.progress.fy.interest, p: a.p + l.progress.fy.principal + l.progress.fy.prepaid, label: l.progress.fy.label }), { i: 0, p: 0, label: \"\" }) : null\n    const todayIso = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)\n    box.innerHTML = `<h2>Your loans</h2>` + openL.map(l => {\n      const p = l.progress, open = state.loanOpen === l.id, waiting = l.status && l.status.state === \"waiting\"\n      const hist = (p && p.rateInfo && p.rateInfo.history) || l.rates || []\n      let body = \"\"   // the name, EMI and status sit on the collapsible's header below\n      if (l.amount || l.account) body += `<p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">${[l.amount ? `Loan of ${inr(l.amount)}${l.disbursed && Math.abs(l.disbursed - l.amount) > 1 ? `, ${inr(l.disbursed)} disbursed` : \"\"}` : \"\", l.account ? `A/c …${esc(l.account.slice(-4))}` : \"\"].filter(Boolean).join(\" · \")}</p>`\n      if (l.change) body += `<div class=\"notice\" style=\"padding:8px 10px\"><span class=\"small\">EMI changed from ${inr(l.change.from)} to ${inr(l.change.to)} on ${esc(shortDate(l.change.day))}, usually after a rate change. Update the rate below if it changed.</span><button class=\"act subtle\" data-loanemi=\"${esc(l.id)}\" data-v=\"${l.change.to}\" ${busy}>Use ${inr(l.change.to)}</button></div>`\n      if (p) {\n        const fyl = esc(p.fy.label.replace(/FY 20(\\d\\d)–/, \"FY $1–\"))\n        const prevRate = hist.length > 1 ? hist[hist.length - 2] : null, lastRate = hist[hist.length - 1]\n        const t = p.tenure\n        body += `<dl class=\"kv num loankv\">\n          <dt>Outstanding</dt><dd>${inr(p.outstanding)}</dd>\n          <dt>Rate</dt><dd>${pct(p.rateInfo.current)}${prevRate ? `<br><span class=\"sub\">${pct(prevRate.rate)} until ${esc(shortDate(new Date(Date.parse(lastRate.from + \"T00:00:00Z\") - 864e5).toISOString().slice(0, 10)))}</span>` : \"\"}</dd>\n          <dt>${t ? For the interest part, add its rate and outstanding in Notion's Loans table.</p>` : \"\") + docHtml(l) })()}`, { line: plainText(statusLine(l)) })}</div>`\n    }).join(\"\") + (fyAll ? `<div class=\"loan\"><div class=\"loanhead\"><span class=\"what\"><div>${esc(fyAll.label)}, all home loans</div><small>So far this year, for tax time</small></span><span class=\"amt num\">${inr(fyAll.i)}<small>interest</small></span></div><p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">${inr(fyAll.p)} principal repaid, including prepayments.</p></div>` : \"\") + found.map(f => `<div class=\"loan\"><div class=\"loanhead\"><span class=\"what\"><div>Found in your email: ${esc(f.name)}</div><small>${esc([f.emi ? `EMI ${inr(f.emi)}${f.day ? ` around the ${ordinal(f.day)}` : \"\"}` : \"\", f.amount ? `loan of ${inr(f.amount)}` : \"\", f.account ? `A/c …${f.account.slice(-4)}` : \"\"].filter(Boolean).join(\" · \"))}</small></span></div>\n      <div class=\"txtools\"><button class=\"act\" data-loanaccept=\"${esc(f.id)}\" ${busy}>Track it</button><button class=\"act subtle\" data-loandismiss=\"${esc(f.id)}\" ${busy}>Not mine</button></div></div>`).join(\"\")\n      + sugg.map(s => `<div class=\"loan\"><div class=\"loanhead\"><span class=\"what\"><div>${s.second ? `Another loan with ${esc(s.name)}? You can switch a loan to equal months in its form. Figures are close to your lender's statement; its interest certificate is exact.</p>`\n    document.querySelectorAll(\"#view-loans .ipcol\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { const w = b.closest(\".ipwrap\"); w.querySelectorAll(\".ipcol\").forEach(x => x.classList.toggle(\"on\", x === b)); w.querySelector(\".iptip\").textContent = b.dataset.tip })\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[id^='lr-']\").forEach(inp => inp.oninput = () => { const id = inp.id.slice(3), was = Number(inp.dataset.was || 0), w = $(\"lrfw-\" + id); if (w) w.hidden = !was || Math.abs(Number(inp.value) - was) < 0.005 })\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-loanedit]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.loanOpen = b.dataset.loanedit || null; renderLoans() })\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-loansave]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {\n      const id = b.dataset.loansave, v = k => ($(k + \"-\" + id) || {}).value || \"\", l = loans.find(x => x.id === id) || {}\n      const rate = Number(v(\"lr\")), outstanding = Number(String(v(\"lo\")).replace(/[^\\d.]/g, \"\")), asOf = v(\"la\"), tenure = Number(v(\"lt\")), rateFrom = v(\"lrf\") || todayIso, basis = v(\"lb\") || \"daily\"\n      if (!(rate > 0)) { window.flash && window.flash(\"Fill in the interest rate.\"); return }\n      if (!(outstanding > 0) && !l.disbursed && !l.outstanding) { window.flash && window.flash(\"Fill in the outstanding amount and the EMI date it's right after.\"); return }\n      if (outstanding > 0 && !asOf) { window.flash && window.flash(\"Fill in the EMI date the outstanding is right after.\"); return }\n      // keep a history of rates: a changed rate applies from the date you give; earlier months keep the old one\n      const hist = (l.rates || []).filter(r => r.rate > 0).map(r => ({ rate: r.rate, from: r.from || \"2000-01-01\" }))\n      const cur = hist.length ? hist[hist.length - 1].rate : 0, changed = cur > 0 && Math.abs(rate - cur) >= 0.005\n      if (!hist.length) hist.push({ rate, from: \"2000-01-01\" })\n      else if (changed) { const keep = hist.filter(r => r.from < rateFrom); keep.push({ rate, from: rateFrom }); hist.length = 0; hist.push(...keep) }\n      state.loanOpen = null\n      act(\"loan-set\", { id, rate, outstanding: outstanding || 0, asOf, tenure: tenure || 0, history: hist.map(r => `${r.rate}% from ${r.from}`).join(\"; \"), rateChanged: changed, rateFromLabel: shortDate(rateFrom), basis })\n    })\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-ratehint]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {\n      const h = JSON.parse(b.dataset.ratehint), l = loans.find(x => x.id === h.id) || {}\n      const hist = (l.rates || []).filter(r => r.rate > 0).map(r => ({ rate: r.rate, from: r.from || \"2000-01-01\" })).filter(r => r.from < h.from)\n      if (!hist.length && l.rate) hist.push({ rate: l.rate, from: \"2000-01-01\" })\n      hist.push({ rate: h.rate, from: h.from })\n      act(\"loan-set\", { id: h.id, rate: h.rate, outstanding: 0, tenure: 0, history: hist.map(r => `${r.rate}% from ${r.from}`).join(\"; \"), rateChanged: true, rateFromLabel: shortDate(h.from) })\n    })\n    const docAct = (val, use) => {\n      const [id, k] = val.split(\"|\"), l = loans.find(x => x.id === id) || {}, d = (l.docs || [])[+k]; if (!d) return\n      const docs = (l.docs || []).map((x, j) => j === +k ? Object.assign({}, x, use ? { used: true } : { ignored: true }) : x)\n      const fields = {}\n      if (use) {\n        if (d.rate > 0) {\n          const from = d.asOf || d.mailDay || \"\"\n          const hist = (l.rates || []).filter(r => r.rate > 0).map(r => ({ rate: r.rate, from: r.from || \"2000-01-01\" }))\n          const at = hist.filter(r => r.from <= from).pop()\n          if (!hist.length) hist.push({ rate: d.rate, from: \"2000-01-01\" })\n          else if (!at || Math.abs(at.rate - d.rate) >= 0.005) { hist.push({ rate: d.rate, from: from || \"2000-01-01\" }); hist.sort((a, b) => a.from < b.from ? -1 : 1) }\n          fields.history = hist.map(r => `${r.rate}% from ${r.from}`).join(\"; \"); fields.rate = hist[hist.length - 1].rate\n        }\n        // a newer outstanding replaces yours; an older one is only used if you have none\n        if (d.outstanding > 0 && d.asOf && (!l.outstanding || !l.asOf || d.asOf > l.asOf)) { fields.outstanding = d.outstanding; fields.asOf = d.asOf }\n        if (d.tenure > 0 && !l.tenure) fields.tenure = d.tenure\n      }\n      act(\"loan-doc\", { id, docs, fields, used: use })\n    }\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-basis]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {\n      const [id, basis] = b.dataset.basis.split(\"|\"), l = loans.find(x => x.id === id) || {}\n      const rate = (l.rates && l.rates.length ? l.rates[l.rates.length - 1].rate : l.rate) || 0\n      act(\"loan-set\", { id, rate, outstanding: 0, tenure: 0, basis })\n    })\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-docuse]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => docAct(b.dataset.docuse, true))\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-docskip]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => docAct(b.dataset.docskip, false))\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-loanclose]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.loanOpen = null; act(\"loan-close\", { id: b.dataset.loanclose }) })\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-loanemi]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => act(\"loan-emi\", { id: b.dataset.loanemi, emi: Number(b.dataset.v) }))\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-loanadd]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => act(\"loan-add\", JSON.parse(b.dataset.loanadd)))\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-loanaccept]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => act(\"loan-accept\", { id: b.dataset.loanaccept }))\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-loandismiss]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => act(\"loan-dismiss\", { id: b.dataset.loandismiss }))\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-loanignore]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => act(\"loan-ignore\", { match: b.dataset.loanignore }))\n  }\n\n  // ---------- Overview: one \"Cards, loans and money owed\" card with a summary row each ----------\n  function renderMoney() {\n    const rows = { cards: $(\"cardCard\"), loans: $(\"loansCard\"), owed: $(\"owedCard\") }\n    const sum = {}, dot = {}\n    // credit cards: spent on cards this month, and what's left to pay on bills due this month\n    const spent = inMonth(state.month).filter(r => r.category !== DUES && (r.card || r.payment === \"Credit Card\")).reduce((s, r) => s + r.gross, 0)\n    const bills = ((state.check && state.check.bills) || []).filter(b => String(b.dueDay || b.statementDay).slice(0, 7) === state.month)\n    const left = bills.reduce((s, b) => s + Math.max(0, b.total - (b.paid || 0)), 0)\n    sum.cards = `${inr(spent)} spent${bills.length ? left > 1 ? ` · ${inr(left)} of bills to pay` : \" · bills paid\" : \"\"}`\n    dot.cards = rows.cards.classList.contains(\"attn\")\n    // loans: the first open loan's EMI status\n    const ls = loanList().filter(l => !l.closed), foundN = ((state.check && state.check.loansFound) || []).length\n    const paidThen = paidInMonth(state.month).filter(p => p.what === \"EMI\")\n    if (state.month !== NOW_KEY && paidThen.length) {\n      const top = paidThen.slice().sort((x, y) => y.amount - x.amount)[0]\n      const short = (top.loan.match || lenderOf(top.loan)).replace(/\\b\\w/g, c => c.toUpperCase()) + (/…\\d{4}/.test(lenderOf(top.loan)) ? \" \" + lenderOf(top.loan).match(/…\\d{4}/)[0] : \"\")\n      sum.loans = `${short} · ${inr(top.amount)} paid ${shortDate(top.day)}${paidThen.length > 1 ? ` · +${paidThen.length - 1}` : \"\"}`\n    } else if (state.month < NOW_KEY && loanList().length) {\n      sum.loans = \"No EMI that month\"\n    } else if (ls.length) {\n      const l = ls[0], s = l.status || {}\n      const st = s.state === \"paid\" ? `paid ${shortDate(s.day)}` : s.state === \"late\" ? `not seen, ${s.daysLate} days late` : s.state === \"expected\" ? \"debit expected\" :\n        s.state === \"today\" ? \"debits today\" : s.state === \"waiting\" ? \"waiting for the first EMI\" : s.due ? `next ${shortDate(s.due)}` : \"\"\n      sum.loans = `${s.amount ? \"EMI \" + inr(s.amount) + \" · \" : \"\"}${st}${l.progress ? ` · ${inrShort(l.progress.outstanding)} left` : \"\"}${ls.length > 1 ? ` · +${ls.length - 1} more` : \"\"}`\n    } else sum.loans = foundN ? `${foundN} loan${foundN === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\"} found in your email` : loanList().length ? \"No open loans\" : (state.check && state.check.loansError) ? (state.check.loansError === \"no-access\" ? \"Can't open the Loans table · tap to fix\" : \"Couldn't load · tap Refresh\") : \"\"\n    if (!ls.length && !loanList().length && state.check && state.check.loansError) dot.loans = true\n    dot.loans = rows.loans.classList.contains(\"attn\") || !!dot.loans\n    // owed to you\n    const groups = owedPeople(), owed = groups.reduce((s, g) => s + g.total, 0)\n    sum.owed = groups.length ? `${inr(owed)} from ${groups.length} ${groups.length === 1 ? \"person\" : \"people\"}` : \"\"\n    const ps = peopleSummary()\n    $(\"mrow-people\").hidden = !ps; $(\"msum-people\").textContent = ps\n    const pl = policyMoneyLine()\n    $(\"mrow-ins\").hidden = !pl; $(\"msum-ins\").textContent = pl\n    $(\"mrow-ins\").classList.toggle(\"attn\", polList().some(p => p.state === \"grace\" || p.state === \"overdue\"))\n    $(\"mrow-people\").classList.toggle(\"attn\", !!((state.check && state.check.people && state.check.people.unfiled) || []).length)\n    for (const k of Object.keys(rows)) {\n      const show = k === \"cards\" || (!rows[k].hidden && sum[k] && !(k === \"owed\" && peopleReady()))\n      $(\"mrow-\" + k).hidden = !show\n      $(\"msum-\" + k).textContent = sum[k]\n      $(\"mrow-\" + k).classList.toggle(\"attn\", !!dot[k])\n      if (k === \"owed\") $(\"mrow-owed\").querySelector(\".mhead\").onclick = peopleReady() ? () => setTab(\"people\") : () => { state.mpanel = state.mpanel === \"owed\" ? null : \"owed\"; renderMoney() }\n      const open = state.mpanel === k && !(k === \"owed\" && peopleReady()), body = $(\"mbody-\" + k)\n      if (!body) continue   // Loans opens its own tab\n      body.hidden = !open\n      $(\"mrow-\" + k).querySelector(\".mhead\").setAttribute(\"aria-expanded\", open)\n    }\n  }\n  function openPanel(k) {\n    if (k === \"loans\") { setTab(\"loans\"); return }\n    state.mpanel = k; renderMoney()\n    const el = $(\"mrow-\" + k); if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ block: \"start\", behavior: \"smooth\" })\n  }\n\n  // Things that need you, shown at the top of the To do card (besides payments to review)\n  function todoItems() {\n    const out = [], today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)\n    const days = iso => Math.round((Date.parse(String(iso).slice(0, 10) + \"T00:00:00Z\") - Date.parse(today + \"T00:00:00Z\")) / 864e5)\n    for (const l of loanList().filter(l => !l.closed)) {\n      const s = l.status || {}, label = (l.type || \"Loan\").replace(/ loan$/i, \" loan\") + \" EMI\"\n      if (s.state === \"late\") out.push({ tone: \"bad\", text: `${label} not seen for ${shortDate(s.due)}`, sub: `${s.daysLate} days late. Check your bank app.`, panel: \"loans\" })\n      else if (s.state === \"soon\" || s.state === \"today\") out.push({ tone: \"warn\", text: `${label} ${inr(s.amount)} ${s.state === \"today\" ? \"debits today\" : `debits in ${s.daysTo} day${s.daysTo === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\"}`}`, sub: \"Keep enough balance in the account.\", panel: \"loans\" })\n      if (l.change) out.push({ tone: \"warn\", text: `${label} changed to ${inr(l.change.to)}`, sub: \"Usually after a rate change.\", panel: \"loans\" })\n    }\n    for (const f of (state.check && state.check.loansFound) || []) out.push({ tone: \"info\", text: `Loan found in your email: ${f.name}`, sub: f.emi ? `EMI ${inr(f.emi)}. Track it?` : \"Track it?\", panel: \"loans\" })\n    for (const s of (state.check && state.check.loanSuggestions) || []) out.push({ tone: \"info\", text: s.second ? `Another loan with ${s.name}?` : `${s.sure ? \"Looks like an EMI\" : \"Regular auto-debit\"}: ${s.name}`, sub: `${inr(s.emi)} a month. Track it?`, panel: \"loans\" })\n    for (const b of (state.check && state.check.bills) || []) {\n      const due = b.dueDay || \"\", leftB = Math.max(0, b.total - (b.paid || 0)), d = due ? days(due) : null\n      if (b.result === \"Extra charges\" && !b.seen) out.push({ tone: \"bad\", text: `${cardShort(b.card)} bill: ${inr(b.difference)} extra`, sub: \"Charges with no alert. Tap to see why.\", panel: \"cards\" })\n      if (leftB > 1 && d != null && d < 0 && d > -45) out.push({ tone: \"bad\", text: `${cardShort(b.card)} bill ${inr(leftB)} overdue`, sub: `Was due ${shortDate(due)}.`, panel: \"cards\" })\n      else if (leftB > 1 && d != null && d >= 0 && d <= 3) out.push({ tone: \"warn\", text: `${cardShort(b.card)} bill ${inr(leftB)} due ${d === 0 ? \"today\" : `in ${d} day${d === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\"}`}`, sub: `Due ${shortDate(due)}.`, panel: \"cards\" })\n    }\n    out.push(...policyTodos())\n    return out\n  }\n\n  function cardPaidIn(key, logged) {\n    const fromAlerts = ((state.check && state.check.cardPayments) || []).filter(x => String(x.day).slice(0, 7) === key).reduce((t, x) => t + x.amount, 0)\n    const loggedOnly = inMonth(key).filter(r => r.category === DUES && !r.linked).reduce((t, r) => t + r.gross, 0)\n    return Math.max(logged || 0, fromAlerts + loggedOnly)\n  }\n  function renderOwed() {\n    const box = $(\"owedCard\")\n    const groups = owedPeople()\n    if (!groups.length) { box.hidden = true; return }\n    box.hidden = false\n    const total = groups.reduce((s, g) => s + g.total, 0), busy = state.busy ? Notion keeps the original.\n  const BRANDS = [\"Instamart\", \"Swiggy\", \"Zomato\", \"Uber\", \"Ola\", \"Rapido\", \"Blinkit\", \"Zepto\", \"BigBasket\", \"Amazon\", \"Flipkart\", \"Myntra\",
