\"Jio\", \"Decathlon\", \"IKEA\", \"Croma\"]\n  function niceName(s) {\n    const raw = String(s || \"\").trim()\n    if (!raw) return \"Unknown merchant\"\n    const flat = raw.replace(/[^a-z0-9 ]+/gi, \" \")\n    const b = BRANDS.find(x => new RegExp(\"\\\\b\" + x + \"\\\\b\", \"i\").test(flat))\n    if (b) return b\n    let t = (t === t.toUpperCase()) t = t.toLowerCase().replace(/\\b[a-z]/g, c => c.toUpperCase())\n    return t\n  }\n\n  const shownName = m => { const s = String(m || \"\").trim(); return s && s === added, skipped or tagged leave the queue at once and show as \"working\" rows until the saved data arrives.\n  const pendingMap = () => (state.pending = state.pending || {})\n  function state.pendingTimer = setTimeout(() => { state.pending = {}; renderCheck() }, 45000)   // never leave an item hidden for good\n  }\n  function pendingRows() {\n    return const c = state.check || {}\n    return [...(c.open || []).filter(a => !isPending(a.id)).map(a => ({ kind: \"alert\", id: a.id, a })), ...(c.noCard || []).filter(e => !isPending(e.id)).map(e => ({ function renderCheck() {\n    const box = $(\"checkCard\"), c = state.check\n    if (!c && !state.checkError) { box.hidden = true; return }\n    box.hidden = false\n    const busy = state.busy ? const queue = reviewQueue(), pend = pendingHtml()\n    const todos = todoItems()\n    box.classList.toggle(\"attn\", open.length > 0 || !!warn || todos.some(t => t.tone === \"bad\"))\n    const since = c ? new Date(c.fromDay + \"T00:00:00\").toLocaleDateString(\"en-IN\", { day: \"numeric\", month: \"short\" }) : \"\"\n    let html = `<h2>${queue.length || todos.length || warn ? \"To do\" : \"Bank html += pend\n      if (c && !queue.length && !warn && !todos.length && !pend) html += `<p style=\"margin:0\"><span class=\"ok\">✓ All caught up.</span> <span class=\"small\">Every alert since ${esc(since)} matches something you logged.</span></p>`\n      if (queue.length) {\n        const total = open.reduce((s, a) => s + a.amount, 0)\n        const bits = [open.length ? `${open.length}      }\n      if (todos.length) html += `<ul class=\"todo\">${todos.map((t, i) => `<li><button class=\"todorow ${t.tone}\" data-todo=\"${i}\"><span class=\"tdot\"></span><span box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-todo]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => openPanel(todos[+b.dataset.todo].panel))\n      const rs = $(\"reviewStart\"); if (rs) rs.onclick = () => { state.reviewing = true; state.revIndex = i\n    const q = queue[i]\n    state.revId = q.id\n    html += pend\n    html += `<div class=\"checkhead\"><span class=\"small\">${i + 1} of ${queue.length}</span><button class=\"act if (!el) return\n    const sel = el.querySelector('[aria-selected=\"true\"]')\n    if (sel && el.scrollWidth > el.clientWidth) el.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, sel.offsetLeft - (el.clientWidth - sel.offsetWidth) / 2)\n    el.classList.toggle(\"at-end\", el.scrollLeft + el.clientWidth >= el.scrollWidth - 4)\n  }\n  // ---------- collapsible sections (Settings, People, Loans & insurance) ----------\n  // A section is a header you tap (title, a one-line summary, an arrow) over a body that opens and closes.\n  // Sections start closed; what you open is remembered. A section that needs attention shows a dot and opens by itself\n  // (unless you closed it yourself), so nothing that needs you stays hidden.\n  const plainText = html => { const t = document.createElement(\"div\"); t.innerHTML = html; return t.textContent.replace(/\\s+/g, \" \").trim() }\n  const accState = key => store.get(\"acc_\" + key, null)          // null: never touched, use the default\n  function accHtml(key, title, summary, bodyHtml, o = {}) {\n    const saved = accState(key)\n    const open = saved === null ? (o.closed ? false : (!!o.attention || !!o.open)) : saved   // closed: show the attention dot but stay folded until tapped\n    const id = \"acc-\" + key.replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, \"-\")\n    return `<section class=\"acc${o.attention ? \" attn\" : \"\"}${o.sub ? \" sub\" : \"\"}\">\n      <button class=\"acc-head\" type=\"button\" data-key=\"${esc(key)}\" aria-expanded=\"${open}\" aria-controls=\"${id}\">\n        <span class=\"acc-title${o.line ? \" has-line\" : \"\"}\"><span class=\"acc-main\">${o.dot ? `<span class=\"dot\" style=\"background:${o.dot}\"></span>` : \"\"}${esc(title)}${o.attention ? '<span class=\"acc-flag\" role=\"img\" aria-label=\"needs attention\"></span>' : \"\"}</span>${o.line ? `<small class=\"acc-line\">${esc(o.line)}</small>` : \"\"}</span>\n        <span class=\"acc-sum\">${esc(summary || \"\")}</span><span class=\"acc-chev\" aria-hidden=\"true\"></span>\n      </button>\n      <div class=\"acc-body\" id=\"${id}\" ${open ? \"\" : \"hidden\"}>${bodyHtml}</div>\n    </section>`\n  }\n  document.addEventListener(\"click\", e => {\n    const h = e.target.closest && e.target.closest(\".acc-head\"); if (!h) return\n    const open = h.getAttribute(\"aria-expanded\") !== \"true\"\n    h.setAttribute(\"aria-expanded\", String(open))\n    const body = document.getElementById(h.getAttribute(\"aria-controls\")); if (body) body.hidden = !open\n    store.set(\"acc_\" + h.dataset.key, open)\n  })\n\n  // Settings: each part of a slot (they start with a \".setlabel\" line, or an \"h2\" inside \".autoset\") becomes a collapsible.\n  // The slot's own elements are moved, not rebuilt, so their buttons keep working.\n  function accSummary(nodes, noun) {\n    const root = document.createElement(\"div\"); nodes.forEach(n => root.appendChild(n.cloneNode(true)))\n    const ds = root.querySelector(\"[data-sum]\"); if (ds) return ds.getAttribute(\"data-sum\")\n    const on = root.querySelector('[aria-pressed=\"true\"]'); if (on) return on.textContent.trim()\n    const rows = root.querySelectorAll(\".cs-row\").length\n    if (rows) { const [one, many] = noun || [\"\", \"\"]; return one ? `${rows} ${rows === 1 ? one : many}` : String(rows) }\n    return /none yet|nothing/i.test(root.textContent) ? \"none yet\" : \"\"\n  }\n  const ACC_NOUNS = { \"Cards & bank accounts\": [\"card or account\", \"cards and accounts\"], \"Imported statements\": [\"statement\", \"statements\"], \"Merchant categories\": [\"rule\", \"rules\"] }\n  function accSlot(slot, keyPrefix) {\n    if (!slot || slot.hidden || !slot.firstElementChild) return\n    const host = slot.querySelector(\":scope > .autoset\") || slot\n    if ([...host.children].some(c => c.dataset && c.dataset.top)) return   // already done\n    const kids = [...host.childNodes]\n    const parts = []; let cur = null\n    for (const n of kids) {\n      const isHead = n.nodeType === 1 && (n.classList.contains(\"setlabel\") || (host !== slot && n.tagName === \"H2\"))\n      if (isHead) { cur = { title: n.textContent.trim(), nodes: [] }; parts.push(cur) } else if (cur) cur.nodes.push(n)\n      else if (n.nodeType === 1) { cur = { title: \"\", nodes: [n] }; parts.push(cur) }\n    }\n    if (!parts.some(p => p.title)) return\n    host.textContent = \"\"\n    for (const p of parts) {\n      if (!p.title) { p.nodes.forEach(n => host.appendChild(n)); continue }\n      const key = keyPrefix + \":\" + p.title.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, \"-\")\n      const attention = p.nodes.some(n => n.nodeType === 1 && (n.matches(\".bad, .notice.bad, .attn-mark\") || n.querySelector(\".bad, .notice.bad, .attn-mark\")))\n      const tmp = document.createElement(\"div\"); tmp.innerHTML = accHtml(key, p.title, accSummary(p.nodes, ACC_NOUNS[p.title]), \"\", { attention })\n      const sec = tmp.firstElementChild; sec.dataset.top = \"1\"; const body = sec.querySelector(\".acc-body\")\n      p.nodes.forEach(n => body.appendChild(n))\n      host.appendChild(sec)\n    }\n  }\n  const accSlots = { privSet: \"set-priv\", cardSet: \"set-cards\", ruleSet: \"set-rules\", markupSet: \"set-markup\", autoSet: \"set-auto\", updSet: \"set-upd\" }\n  for (const [id, prefix] of Object.entries(accSlots)) {\n    const slot = $(id); if (!slot) continue\n    const obs = new MutationObserver(() => { obs.disconnect(); try { accSlot(slot, prefix) } finally { obs.observe(slot, { childList: true }) } })\n    obs.observe(slot, { childList: true })\n  }\n  // ---------- Updates (Settings): nothing is contacted unless you tap the button ----------\n  let UPD = window.__UPDATE__ || {}\n  window.setUpdateInfo = (info, msg) => { UPD = info || {}; state.updBusy = false; renderUpdateSet(); if (msg) toast(msg) }\n  function renderUpdateSet() {\n    const box = $(\"updSet\"); if (!box) return\n    const days = UPD.checkedAt ? Math.floor((Date.now() - UPD.checkedAt) / 864e5) : null\n    const busy = state.updBusy ? \"disabled\" : \"\"\n    let h = `<div class=\"setlabel\">Updates</div>`\n    if (!UPD.configured) { box.innerHTML = h + `<p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">Updates aren't set up for this copy yet. To update, paste the newest code from the Copy page.</p>`; return }\n    if (UPD.reopen) {\n      h += `<span data-sum=\"close and reopen\" hidden></span><span class=\"attn-mark\" hidden></span>`\n      box.innerHTML = h + `<div class=\"notice\" style=\"padding:10px 12px\"><strong>${esc(UPD.reopen.replace(/\\. Close.*$/, \"\"))}</strong><span class=\"small\">Close the dashboard and open it again to use it. Until then you are still on the old screen.</span></div>`\n      return\n    }\n    const sum = UPD.available ? `${UPD.available.version} available` : UPD.checkedAt ? \"up to date\" : `version ${UPD.version}`\n    h += `<span data-sum=\"${esc(sum)}\" hidden></span>${UPD.available ? '<span class=\"attn-mark\" hidden></span>' : \"\"}`\n    h += `<p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">Version ${esc(UPD.version)}${UPD.checkedAt ? ` · last checked ${days === 0 ? \"today\" : days === 1 ? \"yesterday\" : days + \" days ago\"}` : \" · never checked\"}.</p>`\n    if (UPD.available) h += `<div class=\"notice\" style=\"padding:10px 12px\"><strong>Version ${esc(UPD.available.version)} is available</strong>${UPD.available.notes ? `<span class=\"small\">${esc(UPD.available.notes)}</span>` : \"\"}</div>`\n    h += `<div class=\"txtools\" style=\"padding:0;justify-content:flex-start\"><button class=\"act\" id=\"updCheck\" ${busy}>Check for updates</button>${UPD.available ? `<button class=\"act\" id=\"updApply\" ${busy}>Update now</button>` : \"\"}${UPD.canRestore ? `<button class=\"act subtle\" id=\"updRestore\" ${busy}>Restore version ${esc(UPD.prevVersion)}</button>` : \"\"}</div>`\n    if (days === null || days >= 30) h += `<p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">${days === null ? \"You haven't checked for updates yet.\" : `It's been ${days} days since you last checked.`} The app never checks by itself.</p>`\n    h += `<p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">Tapping Check contacts the update server and sends nothing about you. Updating downloads the new code, checks it against a fingerprint, and keeps your current version so you can go back.</p>`\n    box.innerHTML = h\n    const go = (id, kind) => { const b = $(id); if (b) b.onclick = () => { state.updBusy = true; b.classList.add(\"is-busy\"); box.querySelectorAll(\"button\").forEach(x => { x.disabled = true }); location.href = \"app://\" + kind } }\n    go(\"updCheck\", \"update-check\"); go(\"updApply\", \"update-apply\"); go(\"updRestore\", \"update-restore\")\n  }\n  // ---------- friends & family: People tab ----------\n  // People and their Money Diary come from Notion (via the script on this phone). Bank transfers to people you've\n  // added are filed by the card-alerts sync; cash, days off and anything else are added here.\n  const PP_KINDS_HELP = [\"Pay\", \"Advance\", \"Bonus\", \"Gift\", \"Other\"]\n  const PP_KINDS = [\"Lent\", \"Paid back\", \"Share\", \"Gift\", \"Other\"]\n  const PP_KINDS_BILL = [\"Pay\", \"Advance\", \"Other\"]\n  // Who someone is (any name you like: Driver, Milk, Newspaper…) and how they're paid\n  const PP_PRESETS = { \"Domestic help\": \"Monthly salary\", Driver: \"Monthly salary\", Cook: \"Monthly salary\", Milk: \"Per day\", Newspaper: \"Per day\", Family: \"None\", Friend: \"None\", Other: \"None\" }\n  const PAY_TYPES = [\"Monthly salary\", \"Per day\", \"Monthly bill\", \"None\"]\n  const PAY_LABEL = { \"Monthly salary\": \"Monthly salary\", \"Per day\": \"Per day (milk, paper)\", \"Monthly bill\": \"Fixed monthly bill\", None: \"Not paid regularly\" }\n  const ppGroups = () => { const seen = Object.keys(PP_PRESETS).filter(g => g !== \"Other\"); for (const p of ((pp() || {}).people || [])) if (p.group && !seen.includes(p.group) && p.group !== \"Other\") seen.push(p.group); return seen.concat(\"Other\") }\n  const defaultPayType = g => PP_PRESETS[g] || \"None\"\n  const groupOpts = sel => ppGroups().map(g => `<option ${g === sel ? \"selected\" : \"\"}>${esc(g)}</option>`).join(\"\") + `<option value=\"__newgroup\">New group…</option>`\n  // how this person is paid; people added before pay types existed: domestic help with a monthly pay is a salary\n  const payTypeOf = p => !p ? \"None\" : p.payType && p.payType !== \"None\" ? p.payType : p.payType === \"None\" ? \"None\" : (p.pay > 0 && PP_PRESETS[p.group] === \"Monthly salary\") ? \"Monthly salary\" : \"None\"\n  const isDaily = p => payTypeOf(p) === \"Per day\" && p.rate > 0\n  const isBill = p => payTypeOf(p) === \"Monthly bill\" && p.pay > 0\n  const hasPay = p => (payTypeOf(p) === \"Monthly salary\" && p.pay > 0) || isDaily(p) || isBill(p)\n  const usesCalendar = p => payTypeOf(p) === \"Monthly salary\" || payTypeOf(p) === \"Per day\"\n  const offWord = p => payTypeOf(p) === \"Per day\" ? \"skipped\" : \"off\"\n  const pp = () => (state.check && state.check.people) || null\n  const hasPeopleTab = () => !!pp()\n  const peopleReady = () => !!(pp() && pp().ok)\n  const ppPeople = () => ((pp() || {}).people || []).slice().sort((a, b) => (hasPay(a) ? 0 : 1) - (hasPay(b) ? 0 : 1) || ppGroups().indexOf(a.group) - ppGroups().indexOf(b.group) || owedFrom(b) - owedFrom(a) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name))\n  const ppDiary = () => ((pp() || {}).diary || []).slice().sort((a, b) => a.day < b.day ? 1 : a.day > b.day ? -1 : 0)\n  const ppEntries = id => ppDiary().filter(e => e.person === id)\n  const ppPerson = id => ((pp() || {}).people || []).find(p => p.id === id)\n  const todayISO = () => { const d = new Date(); return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, \"0\")}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, \"0\")}` }\n  const isHelp = p => payTypeOf(p) === \"Monthly salary\"\n\n  // Domestic help: what each month's pay comes to (less unpaid days off) and what you've paid towards it.\n  // Pay made in the first days of a month for a pay day of the 10th or earlier counts for the month before.\n  // Per day (milk, newspaper): the days delivered this month so far, less skipped days, times the rate. Monthly bill: the same amount each month.\n  // Bills for those are usually paid early the next month, so without a pay day it's taken as the 5th.\n  function helpLedger(p) {\n    if (!p || !hasPay(p)) return null\n    const es = ppEntries(p.id), type = payTypeOf(p), payday = p.payday || (type === \"Monthly salary\" ? 0 : 5)\n    const forMonth = e => e.kind !== \"Advance\" && payday && payday <= 10 && Number(e.day.slice(8, 10)) <= payday + 7 ? shiftKey(e.day.slice(0, 7), -1) : e.day.slice(0, 7)\n    const counts = e => e.type === \"Paid\" && e.kind !== \"Bonus\" && e.kind !== \"Gift\"\n    let start = p.since ? p.since.slice(0, 7) : null\n    for (const e of es) if (counts(e) || e.type === \"Day off\") { const k = e.type === \"Day off\" ? e.day.slice(0, 7) : forMonth(e); if (!start || k < start) start = k }\n    if (!start || start > NOW_KEY) start = NOW_KEY\n    const months = []\n    let bal = 0\n    for (let k = start; k <= NOW_KEY; k = shiftKey(k, 1)) {\n      const off = es.filter(e => e.type === \"Day off\" && e.day.slice(0, 7) === k).reduce((s, e) => s + (e.days || 1), 0)\n      const days = k === NOW_KEY ? Number(todayISO().slice(8, 10)) : daysIn(k)\n      const extra = type === \"Monthly bill\" ? 0 : type === \"Per day\" ? off : Math.max(0, off - (p.leave || 0))\n      const due = type === \"Per day\" ? Math.round(p.rate * Math.max(0, days - off)) : type === \"Monthly bill\" ? Math.round(p.pay) : Math.round(p.pay - extra * p.pay / daysIn(k))\n      const paid = es.filter(e => counts(e) && forMonth(e) === k).reduce((s, e) => s + e.amount, 0)\n      const carry = bal\n      bal += due - paid\n      months.push({ key: k, off, extra, due, paid, carry, left: bal, days })\n      if (months.length > 36) break\n    }\n    return months\n  }\n  function payDayLabel(p, key) {\n    const pd = p.payday || (payTypeOf(p) === \"Monthly salary\" ? 0 : 5)\n    if (!pd) return \"\"\n    const k = pd <= 10 ? shiftKey(key, 1) : key\n    const [y, m] = k.split(\"-\").map(Number)\n    return new Date(y, m - 1, Math.min(pd, daysIn(k))).toLocaleDateString(\"en-IN\", { day: \"numeric\", month: \"short\" })\n  }\n  // Friends and family: money sent vs received (gifts and split shares left out of the net).\n  function flowOf(p) {\n    const es = ppEntries(p.id).filter(e => e.type === \"Paid\" || e.type === \"Received\")\n    const net = es.filter(e => e.kind !== \"Gift\" && e.kind !== \"Share\").reduce((s, e) => s + (e.type === \"Paid\" ? e.amount : -e.amount), 0)\n    const m = es.filter(e => e.day.slice(0, 7) === state.month)\n    return { sent: m.filter(e => e.type === \"Paid\").reduce((s, e) => s + e.amount, 0), got: m.filter(e => e.type === \"Received\").reduce((s, e) => s + e.amount, 0), net, n: es.length }\n  }\n  // Split bills keep the name as you typed it (\"Rahul\"); a person matches by full name, one of their other names, or a first name no one else has.\n  const aliasesOf = p => [p.name].concat(String(p.names || \"\").split(/[,;\\n]/)).map(personKey).filter(Boolean)\n  function ownerOf(g) {\n    const people = (pp() || {}).people || []\n    const exact = people.filter(p => aliasesOf(p).includes(g.key))\n    if (exact.length === 1) return { person: exact[0], cands: exact }\n    const first = g.key.split(\" \")[0], cands = (exact.length ? exact : people).filter(p => aliasesOf(p).some(a => a.split(\" \")[0] === first))\n    return { person: cands.length === 1 ? cands[0] : null, cands }\n  }\n  // One person's open split bills, merged across the spellings that point to them\n  function owedOf(p) {\n    const gs = owedPeople().filter(g => { const o = ownerOf(g); return o.person && o.person.id === p.id })\n    if (!gs.length) return null\n    const items = gs.flatMap(g => g.items).sort((a, b) => String(a.r.date).localeCompare(String(b.r.date)))\n    return { key: gs[0].key, name: gs[0].name, total: items.reduce((t, it) => t + it.amount, 0), items, personId: p.id }\n  }\n  const owedFrom = p => { const o = owedOf(p); return o ? o.total : 0 }\n  const owedUnmatched = () => peopleReady() ? owedPeople().filter(g => !ownerOf(g).person) : owedPeople()\n  function personLine(p) {\n    if (hasPay(p)) {\n      const L = helpLedger(p), cur = L[L.length - 1], daily = payTypeOf(p) === \"Per day\"\n      const offTxt = cur.off && payTypeOf(p) !== \"Monthly bill\" ? ` · ${cur.off} day${cur.off === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\"} ${offWord(p)}` : \"\"\n      const by = payDayLabel(p, NOW_KEY)\n      if (daily) {\n        const lastBy = payDayLabel(p, shiftKey(NOW_KEY, -1))\n        return [cur.carry > 0 ? `${inr(cur.carry)} due${lastBy ? \" by \" + lastBy : \"\"}` : cur.carry < 0 ? `${inr(-cur.carry)} paid ahead` : \"\",\n          `${inr(cur.due)} so far this month`].filter(Boolean).join(\" · \") + offTxt\n      }\n      if (cur.left > 0) return `${inr(cur.left)} ${daily ? \"so far this month\" : \"to pay\"}${by ? \" · due \" + by : \"\"}${offTxt}`\n      if (cur.left < 0) return `${inr(-cur.left)} paid ahead${offTxt}`\n      return `All paid${offTxt}`\n    }\n    const f = flowOf(p), owed = owedFrom(p), bits = []\n    if (f.net > 0) bits.push(`you've sent ${inr(f.net)} more`)\n    else if (f.net < 0) bits.push(`you've got ${inr(-f.net)} more`)\n    if (owed > 0) bits.push(`${inr(owed)} owed from bills`)\n    return bits.join(\" · \") || (f.n ? \"Even\" : \"No entries yet\")\n  }\n  const entryIcon = e => e.type === \"Paid\" ? \"↑\" : e.type === \"Received\" ? \"↓\" : e.type === \"Day off\" ? \"☾\" : \"✎\"\n  const entryAmt = e => e.type === \"Paid\" ? `<span class=\"amt num pp-out\">−${inr(e.amount)}</span>` : e.type === \"Received\" ? `<span class=\"amt num pp-in\">+${inr(e.amount)}</span>`\n    : e.type === \"Day off\" ? `<span class=\"amt muted\">${e.days === 0.5 ? \"½ day\" : (e.days || 1) + \" day\" + ((e.days || 1) === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\")}</span>` : `<span class=\"amt muted\"></span>`\n  function entryRow(e, withName) {\n    const p = ppPerson(e.person), offName = p && payTypeOf(p) === \"Per day\" ? \"No delivery\" : \"Day off\", sub = [e.kind && e.kind !== \"Other\" ? e.kind : \"\", e.notes, e.source === \"Bank alert\" ? \"from bank alert\" : \"\"].filter(Boolean).join(\" · \")\n    return `<li class=\"pp-e\"><span class=\"pp-ico ${e.type === \"Paid\" ? \"pp-out\" : e.type === \"Received\" ? \"pp-in\" : \"\"}\" aria-hidden=\"true\">${entryIcon(e)}</span>\n      <span class=\"what\"><div>${esc(withName ? (p ? p.name : \"Someone\") : e.type === \"Note\" ? (e.notes || \"Note\") : e.type === \"Day off\" ? offName : e.type)}</div><small>${esc(withName ? [e.type === \"Day off\" ? offName : \"\", sub].filter(Boolean).join(\" · \") : sub) || \"&nbsp;\"}</small></span>\n      ${entryAmt(e)}<button class=\"pp-x\" data-ppdel=\"${esc(e.id)}\" aria-label=\"Delete this entry\" ${state.busy ? \"disabled\" : \"\"}>×</button></li>`\n  }\n  // Days off fold into one row per person per month (\"Nitu · 3 days off in October\"), opened like a dropdown.\n  const dayCount = n => n === 0.5 ? \"½ day\" : (Math.round(n * 2) / 2) + \" day\" + (n === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\")\n  function offGroupRow(g, withName) {\n    const p = ppPerson(g.person), perDay = p && payTypeOf(p) === \"Per day\"\n    const total = g.items.reduce((t, e) => t + (e.days || 1), 0)\n    const mon = new Date(g.day.slice(0, 7) + \"-01T00:00:00\").toLocaleDateString(\"en-IN\", { month: \"long\" })\n    const what = perDay ? \"No delivery\" : \"Days off\", open = state.ppOffOpen && state.ppOffOpen[g.key]\n    return `<li class=\"pp-g\"><details data-offkey=\"${esc(g.key)}\" ${open ? \"open\" : \"\"}><summary class=\"pp-e\"><span class=\"pp-ico\" aria-hidden=\"true\">☾</span>\n      <span class=\"what\"><div>${esc(withName ? (p ? p.name : \"Someone\") : what + \" in \" + mon)}</div><small>${esc(withName ? what + \" in \" + mon : g.items.length + \" entries\")}</small></span>\n      <span class=\"amt muted\">${dayCount(total)}</span><span class=\"pp-chev\" aria-hidden=\"true\"></span></summary>\n      <ul class=\"tx pp-sub\">${g.items.map(e => `<li class=\"pp-e\"><span></span><span class=\"what\"><div>${esc(e.day === todayISO() ? \"Today\" : fmtDay(e.day))}</div>${e.notes ? `<small>${esc(e.notes)}</small>` : \"\"}</span>\n        <span class=\"amt muted\">${dayCount(e.days || 1)}</span><button class=\"pp-x\" data-ppdel=\"${esc(e.id)}\" aria-label=\"Delete this day off\" ${state.busy ? \"disabled\" : \"\"}>×</button></li>`).join(\"\")}</ul></details></li>`\n  }\n  function feed(list, withName, limit) {\n    const shown = list.slice(0, limit), rows = [], groups = {}\n    for (const e of shown) {\n      if (e.type !== \"Day off\") { rows.push(e); continue }\n      const k = e.person + \"|\" + e.day.slice(0, 7)\n      if (groups[k]) groups[k].items.push(e); else rows.push(groups[k] = { group: true, key: k, day: e.day, person: e.person, items: [e] })\n    }\n    const byDay = []\n    for (const x of rows.map(x => x.group && x.items.length === 1 ? x.items[0] : x)) {\n      const last = byDay[byDay.length - 1]; if (last && last.day === x.day) last.items.push(x); else byDay.push({ day: x.day, items: [x] })\n    }\n    return byDay.map(g => `<div class=\"pp-day\">${esc(g.day === todayISO() ? \"Today\" : fmtDay(g.day))}</div><ul class=\"tx\">${g.items.map(x => x.group ? offGroupRow(x, withName) : entryRow(x, withName)).join(\"\")}</ul>`).join(\"\")\n  }\n\n  function ppForm() {\n    const f = state.ppForm, people = ppPeople(), busy = state.busy ? \"disabled\" : \"\"\n    if (!f) return \"\"\n    const personSel = (val) => `<select id=\"ppPerson\">${people.map(p => `<option value=\"${esc(p.id)}\" ${p.id === val ? \"selected\" : \"\"}>${esc(p.name)}</option>`).join(\"\")}<option value=\"__new\" ${val === \"__new\" || !people.length ? \"selected\" : \"\"}>New person…</option></select>`\n    const newPerson = `<div id=\"ppNew\" class=\"ff2\" ${people.length && f.personId !== \"__new\" ? \"hidden\" : \"\"}>\n        <label>Name <input id=\"ppNewName\" type=\"text\" autocomplete=\"off\" placeholder=\"e.g. Sunita\"></label>\n        <label>Who <select id=\"ppNewGroup\">${ppGroups().map(g => `<option>${esc(g)}</option>`).join(\"\")}</select></label></div>`\n    if (f.mode === \"person\") {\n      const p = f.id ? ppPerson(f.id) || {} : {}, g = f.group || p.group || (f.prefill ? \"Friend\" : \"Domestic help\")\n      const pt = f.payType || (p.id ? payTypeOf(p) : defaultPayType(g))\n      return `<section class=\"card pp-form\"><h2>${f.id ? \"Edit \" + esc(p.name) : \"Add a person\"}</h2>\n        <label>Name <input id=\"pfName\" type=\"text\" value=\"${esc(f.name != null ? f.name : p.name || f.prefill || \"\")}\" autocomplete=\"off\"></label>\n        <label>Who <select id=\"pfGroup\">${groupOpts(f.newGroup != null ? \"__newgroup\" : g).replace('value=\"__newgroup\">', f.newGroup != null ? 'value=\"__newgroup\" selected>' : 'value=\"__newgroup\">')}</select></label>\n        <label id=\"pfNewGroupBox\" ${f.newGroup != null ? \"\" : \"hidden\"}>Group name <input id=\"pfNewGroup\" type=\"text\" value=\"${esc(f.newGroup || \"\")}\" autocomplete=\"off\" placeholder=\"e.g. Car cleaner, Tuition, Gym\"></label>\n        <label>How you pay them <select id=\"pfPayType\">${PAY_TYPES.map(t => `<option value=\"${t}\" ${t === pt ? \"selected\" : \"\"}>${PAY_LABEL[t]}</option>`).join(\"\")}</select></label>\n        <label class=\"pp-check\"><input id=\"pfSpend\" type=\"checkbox\" ${(p.id ? p.spend : pt !== \"None\") ? \"checked\" : \"\"}> Payments to them count as spending</label>\n        <div class=\"ff2\" ${pt === \"None\" ? Was it for these bills?</span>\n          <div class=\"txtools\" style=\"padding:0\"><button class=\"act subtle\" data-pphintno=\"${esc(got.id)}\">No</button><button class=\"act\" data-pphintyes=\"${pick.join(\",\")}\" data-pphintid=\"${esc(got.id)}\" ${busy}>Mark ${inr(pick.reduce((t, j) => t + g.items[j].amount, 0))} paid back</button></div></div>`\n      }\n    }\n    return `<section class=\"card\" id=\"ppOwed\"><div class=\"checkhead\"><h2>Owed from split bills</h2><strong class=\"num\">${inr(g.total)}</strong></div>${hint}\n      <ul class=\"tx sub\" style=\"margin:0\">${g.items.map((it, j) => `<li class=\"subrow\"><span class=\"what\"><div>${esc(shownName(it.r.merchant) || it.r.category)}</div><small>${esc(fmtDay(it.r.date))} · you paid ${inr(it.r.gross)}</small></span>\n        <span class=\"amt num\">${inr(it.amount)}</span><button class=\"act subtle\" data-ppaid1=\"${j}\" ${busy}>Paid</button></li>`).join(\"\")}</ul>\n      <div class=\"txtools\" style=\"padding:0\"><span>${rem.reminded ? state.calEdit : null\n    const lead = (new Date(y, m - 1, 1).getDay() + 6) % 7, today = todayISO(), cells = []\n    for (let i = 0; i < lead; i++) cells.push(`<span></span>`)\n    for (let d = 1; d <= n; d++) {\n      const iso = `${key}-${String(d).padStart(2, \"0\")}`, has = offs[iso], add = ed && ed.add[iso], rmv = has && ed && has.every(e => ed.remove.includes(e.id))\n      const half = add ? add === 0.5 : has && !rmv && has.reduce((t, e) => t + (e.days || 1), 0) === 0.5\n      const cls = [has && !rmv ? \"off\" : \"\", add ? \"off pend\" : \"\", rmv ? \"rmv\" : \"\", half ? \"half\" : \"\", iso === today ? \"today\" : \"\"].filter(Boolean).join(\" \")\n      cells.push(`<button class=\"cal-d ${cls}\" data-calday=\"${iso}\" ${iso > today ? \"disabled\" : \"\"} aria-pressed=\"${!!((has && !rmv) || add)}\" aria-label=\"${esc(fmtDay(iso))}${has ? (daily ? \", skipped\" : \", day off\") : \"\"}\">${d}</button>`)\n    }\n    const total = Object.entries(offs).reduce((t, [iso, es]) => t + (ed && es.every(e => ed.remove.includes(e.id)) ? 0 : es.reduce((u, e) => u + (e.days || 1), 0)), 0) + (ed ? Object.values(ed.add).reduce((t, v) => t + v, 0) : 0)\n    const extra = daily ? total : Math.max(0, total - (p.leave || 0)), cut = daily ? Math.round(extra * (p.rate || 0)) : p.pay > 0 ? Math.round(extra * p.pay / n) : 0\n    const nAdd = ed ? Object.keys(ed.add).length : 0, nRm = ed ? ed.remove.length : 0\n    return `<section class=\"card\" id=\"calCard\"><div class=\"checkhead\"><h2>${daily ? \"No delivery\" : \"Days off\"}</h2>\n        <div class=\"cal-nav\"><button class=\"icon-btn\" id=\"calPrev\" aria-label=\"Previous month\">‹</button><strong>${esc(labelOf(key, true))}</strong><button class=\"icon-btn\" id=\"calNext\" aria-label=\"Next month\" ${key >= NOW_KEY ? \"disabled\" : \"\"}>›</button></div></div>\n      <div class=\"cal\">${[\"M\", \"T\", \"W\", \"T\", \"F\", \"S\", \"S\"].map(x => `<span class=\"cal-h\">${x}</span>`).join(\"\")}${cells.join(\"\")}</div>\n      <div class=\"chips\"><button class=\"chip\" id=\"calFull\" aria-pressed=\"${!state.calHalf}\">Full day</button><button class=\"chip\" id=\"calHalf\" aria-pressed=\"${!!state.calHalf}\">Half day</button></div>\n      <p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">${total ? `${total} day${total === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\"} ${daily ? \"skipped\" : \"off\"}${!daily && p.leave ? `, ${Math.min(total, p.leave)} of them paid` : \"\"}${cut ? ` · ${daily ? \"bill\" : \"pay\"} ${inr(cut)} less` : \"\"}.` : daily ? \"Delivered every day this month.\" : \"No days off this month.\"} Tap a day to mark it, tap again to clear.</p>\n      ${nAdd || nRm ? `<div class=\"txtools\" style=\"padding:0\"><button class=\"act subtle\" id=\"calCancel\">Cancel</button><button class=\"btn\" id=\"calSave\" ${state.busy ? \"disabled\" : \"\"}>Save${nAdd ? ` ${nAdd} day${nAdd === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\"} off` : \"\"}${nAdd && nRm ? \",\" : \"\"}${nRm ? ` clear ${nRm}` : \"\"}</button></div>` : \"\"}</section>`\n  }\n  function bindCalendar(p) {\n    if (!$(\"calCard\")) return\n    const ed = () => (state.calEdit && state.calEdit.person === p.id) ? state.calEdit : (state.calEdit = { person: p.id, add: {}, remove: [] })\n    const redraw = () => { const y = window.scrollY; e.remove = on ? e.remove.filter(id => !ids.includes(id)) : e.remove.concat(ids) }\n      else e.add[iso] = state.calHalf ? 0.5 : 1\n      redraw()\n    })\n    const c = $(\"calCancel\"); if (c) c.onclick = () => { state.calEdit = null; Set up the automation under the gear › Logging.\" : \"Waiting for your next bank alert.\"}</p>\n        ${v.wouldAdd.length ? `<ul class=\"tx\">${v.wouldAdd.slice(0, state.autoAll ? 25 : 5).map(w => `<li class=\"pp-m\"><span class=\"what\"><div>${esc(shownName(w.merchant))} · <span class=\"${w.confidence === \"low\" ? \"autolow\" : \"\"}\">${esc(w.category)}${w.confidence === \"low\" ? \" ?\" : \"\"}</span></div><small>${esc(fmtDay(w.day))} · ${esc(w.reason)}</small></span><span class=\"amt num\">${inr(w.amount)}</span></li>`).join(\"\")}</ul>\n          ${v.wouldAdd.length > 5 && !state.autoAll ? `<button class=\"linkbtn\" id=\"autoAll\">See all ${v.wouldAdd.length}</button>` : \"\"}` : \"\"}\n        <p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">\"?\" means it's unsure; those would be added marked \"check category\" for you to confirm.${v.earlier ? ` Only payments since ${esc(fmtDay(new Date(v.since).toISOString().slice(0, 10)))} count here: the ${v.earlier} older one${v.earlier === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\"} in To do won't be added automatically, so add or skip ${v.earlier === 1 ? \"it\" : \"them\"} there.` : \"\"}</p>\n        <div class=\"txtools\" style=\"padding:0\"><button class=\"act subtle\" id=\"autoOff\" ${busy}>Turn off</button><button class=\"btn\" id=\"autoLive\" ${busy}>Go live</button></div>`\n    }\n    if (check.length) {\n      const shown = check.slice(0, state.autoCheckAll ? 30 : 4)\n      html += `${html ? `<hr class=\"autohr\">` : \"\"}<div class=\"checkhead\"><h2>Added for you · check the category</h2><span class=\"small\">${check.length}</span></div>\n        <ul class=\"tx\">${shown.map(r => `<li class=\"autochk\"><div class=\"pp-u\" style=\"padding-bottom:6px\"><span class=\"what\"><div>${esc(shownName(r.merchant) || r.category)}</div><small>${esc(fmtDay(r.date))}${r.card ? \" · \" + esc(cardShort(r.card)) : \"\"}</small></span><span class=\"amt num\">${inr(r.gross)}</span></div>\n          <div class=\"autorow\"><select data-autocat=\"${esc(r.id)}\">${autoCats().map(c => `<option ${c === r.category ? `<button class=\"linkbtn\" id=\"autoCheckAll\">See all ${check.length}</button>` : \"\"}`\n    } else if (v && v.mode !== \"off\" && !v.preview) {\n      const mine = state.rows.filter(r => r.month === state.month && /^(Auto|SMS)/.test(r.loggedBy)).length\n      html += `<p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">Auto-logging: ${esc(MODE_LABEL[v.mode])} from ${v.sms ? \"bank SMS\" : \"Gmail alerts\"} · ${mine} added this month${v.queued ? ` · ${v.queued} waiting for the hour to pass` : \"\"}.</p>`\n    }\n    box.hidden = !html\n    box.innerHTML = html\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-autofirst]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {\n      if (b.dataset.autofirst === \"off\") { act(\"autolog-set\", { mode: \"off\" }); return }\n      state.autoDraft = { mode: b.dataset.autofirst, sms: !a.hasSync, preview: true, name: a.name || \"\" }\n      if (typeof openSettingsTo === \"function\") openSettingsTo(\"autoSet\")\n    })\n    const later = $(\"autoLater\"); if (later) later.onclick = () => act(\"autolog-dismiss\", {})\n    const all = $(\"autoAll\"); if (all) all.onclick = () => { state.autoAll = true; renderAuto() }\n    const ca = $(\"autoCheckAll\"); if (ca) ca.onclick = () => { state.autoCheckAll = true; renderAuto() }\n    const off = $(\"autoOff\"); if (off) off.onclick = () => act(\"autolog-set\", { mode: \"off\" })\n    const live = $(\"autoLive\"); if (live) live.onclick = () => act(\"autolog-set\", { mode: v.mode, sms: !!v.sms, preview: false, golive: true, name: v.sms ? (a.name || null) : null })\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-autook]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { const id = b.dataset.autook, sel = box.querySelector(`[data-autocat=\"${CSS.escape(id)}\"]`), r = state.rows.find(x => x.id === id); act(\"auto-review\", { id, category: sel.value, changed: !!r && r.category !== sel.value }) })\n    box.querySelectorAll(\"[data-autono]\").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {\n      if (b.dataset.sure !== \"1\") { b.dataset.sure = \"1\"; b.textContent = \"Remove it?\"; return }\n      act(\"auto-notexpense\", { id: b.dataset.autono, alertId: b.dataset.alert || \"\" })\n    })\n  }\n\n  // ---------- insurance: policies on the Loans & insurance tab ----------\n  const POL_TYPES = [\"Term life\", \"Life\", \"Health\", \"Home\", \"Motor\", \"Other\"]\n  const POL_FREQS = [\"Monthly\", \"Quarterly\", \"Half-yearly\", \"Yearly\", \"Single\"]\n  const allPolicies = () => (state.check && state.check.policies) || []\n  const polList = () => allPolicies().filter(p => p.active && !p.suggested)\n  const polFound = () => allPolicies().filter(p => p.suggested)\n  const polSugg = () => (state.check && state.check.policySuggestions) || []\n  const suggName = g => g.insurer === \"LIC\" ? \"LIC policy\" : `${g.insurer} policy`\n  const polDay = iso => iso ? new Date(iso + \"T00:00:00\").toLocaleDateString(\"en-IN\", { day: \"numeric\", month: \"short\", year: iso.slice(0, 4) === String(new Date().getFullYear()) ? undefined : \"numeric\" }) : \"\"\n  const inrCover = n => n >= 1e7 ? `₹${+(n / 1e7).toFixed(2)} Cr` : n >= 1e5 ? `₹${+(n / 1e5).toFixed(1)} L` : inr(n)\n  const freqWord = f => ({ Monthly: \"a month\", Quarterly: \"a quarter\", \"Half-yearly\": \"every 6 months\", Yearly: \"a year\", Single: \"one-time\" })[f] || \"\"\n  function polStatus(p) {\n    const inDays = n => n === 0 ? \"today\" : n === 1 ? \"tomorrow\" : `in ${n} days`\n    switch (p.state) {\n      case \"ended\": return { tone: \"\", text: `Ended ${polDay(p.ends)}` }\n      case \"single\": return { tone: \"\", text: p.ends ? `Paid up · covered till ${polDay(p.ends)}` : \"Paid up, no regular premium\" }\n      case \"nodate\": return { tone: \"\", text: p.premium ? \"Add the next due date to get reminders\" : \"Add the premium and due date\" }\n      case \"overdue\": return { tone: \"bad\", text: `Premium overdue since ${polDay(p.due)} · grace ended ${polDay(p.graceEnds)}. Check with ${p.insurer || \"the insurer\"}.` }\n      case \"grace\": return { tone: \"bad\", text: `Premium overdue since ${polDay(p.due)} · grace period till ${polDay(p.graceEnds)}` }\n      case \"autosoon\": return { tone: \"warn\", text: `Auto-debits ${inDays(p.daysTo)} (${polDay(p.due)})` }\n      case \"auto\": return { tone: \"\", text: `Auto-debits ${polDay(p.due)}` }\n      case \"today\": return { tone: \"warn\", text: \"Premium due today\" }\n      case \"soon\": return { tone: \"warn\", text: `Premium due ${inDays(p.daysTo)} (${polDay(p.due)})` }\n      default: return { tone: \"\", text: `Next premium ${polDay(p.due)}` }\n    }\n  }\n  const taxNote = p => /life/i.test(p.type) ? \"Premiums can count under section 80C, in the old tax regime only.\" : p.type === \"Health\" ? \"Premiums can count under section 80D, in the old tax regime only.\" : \"\"\n  function policySummary() {\n    const ps = polList().filter(p => p.state !== \"ended\")\n    if (!ps.length) return \"\"\n    const life = ps.filter(p => /life/i.test(p.type)).reduce((t, p) => t + p.cover, 0), health = ps.filter(p => p.type === \"Health\").reduce((t, p) => t + p.cover, 0)\n    const yearly = ps.reduce((t, p) => t + p.yearly, 0)\n    return [life ? `Life cover ${inrCover(life)}` : \"\", health ? `health ${inrCover(health)}` : \"\", yearly ? `${inr(yearly)} a year in premiums (${inr(Math.round(yearly / 12))} a month)` : \"\"].filter(Boolean).join(\" · \")\n  }\n  function polForm(p) {\n    const f = p || {}, v = x => esc(x == null ? \"\" : String(x)), busy = state.busy ? \"disabled\" : \"\"\n    return `<div class=\"pp-form polform\"><h2>${f.id ? \"Edit policy\" : \"Add a policy\"}</h2>\n      <label>Name <input id=\"plName\" type=\"text\" value=\"${v(f.name)}\" placeholder=\"e.g. Star Health family floater\" autocomplete=\"off\"></label>\n      <div class=\"ff2\"><label>Insurer <input id=\"plIns\" type=\"text\" value=\"${v(f.insurer)}\" placeholder=\"e.g. Star Health\" autocomplete=\"off\"></label>\n        <label>Type <select id=\"plType\">${POL_TYPES.map(t => `<option ${t === (f.type || \"Health\") ? \"selected\" : \"\"}>${t}</option>`).join(\"\")}</select></label></div>\n      <div class=\"ff2\"><label>Cover (₹) <input id=\"plCover\" type=\"number\" inputmode=\"numeric\" min=\"0\" value=\"${v(f.cover || \"\")}\" placeholder=\"Sum insured\"></label>\n        <label>Premium (₹) <input id=\"plPrem\" type=\"number\" inputmode=\"decimal\" min=\"0\" value=\"${v(f.premium || \"\")}\"></label></div>\n      <div class=\"ff2\"><label>Paid <select id=\"plFreq\">${POL_FREQS.map(t => `<option ${t === (f.freq || \"Yearly\") ? \"selected\" : \"\"}>${t}</option>`).join(\"\")}</select></label>\n        <label>Next due <input id=\"plDue\" type=\"date\" value=\"${v(f.due)}\"></label></div>\n      <label class=\"pp-check\"><input id=\"plAuto\" type=\"checkbox\" ${f.autopay ? \"checked\" : \"\"}> Paid automatically (auto-debit or standing instruction)</label>\n      <div class=\"ff2\"><label>Grace period (days) <input id=\"plGrace\" type=\"number\" inputmode=\"numeric\" min=\"0\" value=\"${v(f.grace != null ? f.grace : \"\")}\" placeholder=\"15 monthly, else 30\"></label>\n        <label>Policy no. `<button class=\"act subtle\" id=\"plDel\" ${busy}>Remove</button>` : \"\"}<button class=\"act subtle\" id=\"plCancel\">Cancel</button><button class=\"btn\" id=\"plSave\" ${busy}>Save</button></div></div>`\n  }\n  function bindPolForm(p) {\n    const f = p || {}\n    $(\"plCancel\").onclick = () => { state.polForm = null; renderPolicies() }\n    const del = $(\"plDel\"); if (del) del.onclick = () => { if (del.dataset.sure !== \"1\") { del.dataset.sure = \"1\"; del.textContent = \"Remove it?\"; return } state.polForm = null; act(\"policy-delete\", { id: f.id }) }\n    $(\"plSave\").onclick = () => {\n      const val = id => ($(id).value || \"\").trim(), num = id => Number(val(id)) || 0\n      const err = $(\"plErr\"); err.hidden = true\n      if (!val(\"plName\")) { err.hidden = false; err.textContent = \"Give it a name.\"; $(\"plName\").focus(); return }\n      if (!val(\"plDue\") && val(\"plFreq\") !== \"Single\" && num(\"plPrem\")) { if (!err.dataset.warned) { err.hidden = false; err.dataset.warned = \"1\"; err.textContent = \"Add the next due date for reminders, or tap Save again to skip.\"; return } }\n      state.polForm = null\n      act(\"policy-save\", { id: f.id || null, name: val(\"plName\"), insurer: val(\"plIns\"), type: val(\"plType\"), cover: num(\"plCover\"), premium: num(\"plPrem\"), freq: val(\"plFreq\"),\n        due: val(\"plDue\"), autopay: $(\"plAuto\").checked, grace: val(\"plGrace\") === \"\" ? null : num(\"plGrace\"), last4: val(\"plNo\"), covers: val(\"plCovers\"), ends: val(\"plEnds\"),\n        benefits: ($(\"plBen\").value || \"\").trim(), match: val(\"plMatch\") || val(\"plIns\") })\n    }\n  }\n  // a document's figures waiting for \"Use these\": what would change on the policy\n  function docPendingHtml(p) {\n    const d = (p.docs || []).slice().reverse().find(x => x.fig && !x.used); if (!d) return \"\"\n    const f = d.fig, busy = state.busy ? \"disabled\" : \"\", diff = (label, now, then) => then && String(then) !== String(now || \"\") ? `<li>${label}: <b>${esc(String(then))}</b>${now ? ` <span class=\"sub\">(now ${esc(String(now))})</span>` : \"\"}</li>` : \"\"\n    const rows = [diff(\"Cover\", p.cover ? inrCover(p.cover) : \"\", f.cover ? inrCover(f.cover) : \"\"), f.kind !== \"receipt\" ? diff(\"Premium\", p.premium ? inr(p.premium) : \"\", f.premium ? inr(f.premium) : \"\") : \"\",\n      diff(\"Paid\", p.freq, f.freq), diff(\"Next due\", p.due ? polDay(p.due) : \"\", f.due ? polDay(f.due) : \"\"), diff(\"Cover ends\", p.ends ? polDay(p.ends) : \"\", f.ends ? polDay(f.ends) : \"\"),\n      diff(\"Covers\", p.covers, f.covers), f.kind === \"receipt\" && f.paidDay ? \":\" : \" (nothing new in it)\"}</div>\n      ${rows ? `<ul class=\"polchg small\">${rows}</ul>` : \"\"}${newBen.length ? `<div class=\"small\">Benefits to add:</div><ul class=\"polben small\">${newBen.map(b => `<li>${esc(b)}</li>`).join(\"\")}</ul>` : \"\"}\n      <p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">Read automatically: check it's right before saving.</p>\n      <div class=\"txtools\" style=\"padding:0\"><button class=\"act subtle\" data-docskip=\"${esc(p.id)}\" data-file=\"${esc(d.file)}\" ${busy}>Ignore</button>${rows || newBen.length ? `<button class=\"act\" data-docuse=\"${esc(p.id)}\" data-file=\"${esc(d.file)}\" ${busy}>Use these</button>` : \"\"}</div></div>`, { attention: true, closed: true, sub: true })\n  }\n  function docsListHtml(p) {\n    const docs = (p.docs || []).filter(d => d.path || d.link || d.used).slice().reverse()\n    if (!docs.length) return \"\"\n    return `<div class=\"subhead\">Documents</div><ul class=\"poldocs\">${docs.map(d => `<li><button class=\"linkbtn\" data-docopen=\"${esc(p.id)}\" data-file=\"${esc(d.file)}\">${esc(d.file)}</button><span class=\"small\">${esc(polDay(d.day))}${d.link && !d.path ? \"disabled\" : \"\"\n    if (state.polForm) {\n      box.hidden = false\n      const p = state.polForm === \"new\" ? null : typeof state.polForm === \"object\" ? state.polForm : allPolicies().find(x => x.id === state.polForm)\n      box.innerHTML = polForm(p); bindPolForm(p); return\n    }\n    if (!ps.length && !found.length && !sugg.length) {\n      box.hidden = !state.loaded\n      box.innerHTML = `<h2>Insurance</h2>${err === \"no-access\" ? `<p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">Your Notion integration can't open the <b>Insurance</b> table. In Notion open it › <b>•••</b> › <b>Connections</b> and add the integration your Expenses table uses, then Refresh.</p>`\n        : `<p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">Add your life, health and other policies to see premiums coming up, get a reminder before each one, and keep what each policy covers in one place. Policies in your email (premium receipts, renewal notices) are found by your Google script once a day.</p>`}\n        <div class=\"txtools\" style=\"padding:0;justify-content:flex-start\"><button class=\"act\" id=\"polAdd\" ${busy}>Add a policy</button><button class=\"act subtle\" id=\"polPdf\" ${busy}>Add from a PDF</button></div>\n        <p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">From a PDF: pick the policy schedule and its cover, premium, dates and benefits are filled in for you.</p>`\n      const a = $(\"polAdd\"); if (a) a.onclick = () => { state.polForm = \"new\"; renderPolicies() }\n      const pdf = $(\"polPdf\"); if (pdf) pdf.onclick = () => act(\"policy-doc\", {})\n      return\n    }\n    box.hidden = false\n    box.classList.toggle(\"attn\", ps.some(p => p.state === \"grace\" || p.state === \"overdue\"))\n    const sum = policySummary()\n    // folds into one line like Loans; opens by itself when something needs you (due soon, overdue, found in email, a document to check)\n    const needs = found.length || sugg.length || ps.some(p => [\"grace\", \"overdue\", \"soon\", \"today\"].includes(p.state) || (p.docs || []).some(d => d.fig && !d.used))\n    const open = store.get(\"insOpen\", false) || (needs && !store.get(\"insFoldedAt\", 0))\n    const next = ps.filter(p => p.due && p.premium && ![\"single\", \"ended\"].includes(p.state)).sort((a, b) => a.due < b.due ? -1 : 1)[0]\n    const line = [`${ps.length} polic${ps.length === 1 ? \"y\" : \"ies\"}`, next ? `next ${inr(next.premium)} ${next.state === \"grace\" || next.state === \"overdue\" ? \"overdue\" : polDay(next.due)}` : \"\",\n      (() => { const y = ps.filter(p => p.state !== \"ended\").reduce((t, p) => t + p.yearly, 0); return y ? `<p class=\"small\" style=\"margin:0\">${esc(sum)}</p>` : \"\"}`\n    if (sugg.length) html += accHtml(\"polsugg\", \"Premiums seen in your bank alerts\", `${sugg.length} to check`, `<div class=\"polfound\">${sugg.map((g, i) => `<div class=\"polrow\"><span class=\"what\"><div>${esc(g.insurer)} · ${inr(g.amount)}</div><small>${esc(`${g.count > 1 ? Math.max(today.getDate() / new Date(today.getFullYear(), today.getMonth() + 1, 0).getDate(), 0.25) : 1), 0) || 1\n    const avg = s.total / span\n    // the same months a year earlier, up to the same day\n    const prevKeys = months.map(k => shiftKey(k, -12)).filter(k => k >= firstData)\n    let prevLine = \"\"\n    if (prevKeys.length === months.length) {\n      const prev = state.rows.filter(r => prevKeys.includes(r.month) && (r.month !== shiftKey(NOW_KEY, -12) || r.day <= today.getDate()))\n      const p = sum(prev).total\n      if (p > 0) { const d = (s.total - p) / p * 100\n        prevLine = `<span style=\"color:${d > 0 ? \"var(--more)\" : \"var(--less)\"};font-weight:600\">${d >= 0 ? \"▲\" : \"▼\"} ${Math.abs(Math.round(d))}%</span> vs ${inrShort(p)} in the same months last year` }\n    }\n    const cats = Object.entries(s.cats).filter(([, v]) => v > 0).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])\n    const top = cats.length ?
