const SCRIPT_VERSION = "6.14.2"
let wv = null
let refreshing = false
let lastErrorCode = null
let closed = false
let latest = null            // { rows, meta } most recently shown, used to rebuild a blank page
let chartJs = ""

// Diagnostic log, visible in the Files app: iCloud Drive › Scriptable › expenses-dashboard-log.txt
// (Falls back to this phone's own Scriptable storage if iCloud Drive is off for Scriptable.)
let icloud
try { icloud = FileManager.iCloud(); icloud.documentsDirectory() } catch (e) { icloud = FileManager.local() }
const LOG_PATH = icloud.joinPath(icloud.documentsDirectory(), "expenses-dashboard-log.txt")
function log(msg) {
  try {
    const lines = icloud.fileExists(LOG_PATH) ? (icloud.readString(LOG_PATH) || "").split("\n").slice(-79) : []
    lines.push(`${new Date().toLocaleString("en-IN")}  ${msg}`)
    icloud.writeString(LOG_PATH, lines.join("\n"))
  } catch (e) {}
}
function readLog() {
  try { return icloud.fileExists(LOG_PATH) ? icloud.readString(LOG_PATH) || "" : "" } catch (e) { return "" }
}

const sleep = ms => new Promise(r => Timer.schedule(ms, false, r))
const safe = v => JSON.stringify(v).replace(/</g, "\\u003c")

// The page is written as real files on this phone and loaded with loadFile: page, chart library
// and data are separate files instead of one very large text string.
const PAGE_DIR = fm.joinPath(fm.documentsDirectory(), "expenses-dashboard")
const pagePath = name => fm.joinPath(PAGE_DIR, name)

function writePageFiles() {
  if (!fm.fileExists(PAGE_DIR)) fm.createDirectory(PAGE_DIR, true)
  const data = `window.__VERSION__=${safe(SCRIPT_VERSION)};window.__SETTINGS__=${safe(settings())};window.__REMIND__=${safe(remindForPage())};window.__PHONES__=${safe(phonesForPage())};window.__UPI__=${safe(upiForPage())};window.__CARDS__=${safe(CREDIT_CARDS)};window.__ACCOUNTS__=${safe(ACCOUNT_NAMES)};window.__STMT__=${safe(stmtInfo())};window.__UPDATE__=${safe(updateInfo())};window.__OPEN_TAB__=${safe(OPEN_TAB)};` +
    (latest ? `window.__DATA__=${safe(latest.rows)};window.__META__=${safe(latest.meta)};` : "")
  fm.writeString(pagePath("data.js"), data)
  if (chartJs) fm.writeString(pagePath("chart.js"), chartJs)
  fm.writeString(pagePath("index.html"), HTML
    .replace("<!--INJECT-->", () => `<script src="data.js?${Date.now()}"></script>`)
    .replace("<!--CHARTJS-->", () => chartJs ? `<script src="chart.js"></script>` : ""))
}

async function loadPage(reason) {
  try {
    writePageFiles()
    await wv.loadFile(pagePath("index.html"))
    log(`page loaded (${reason})`)
  } catch (e) {
    log(`page load failed (${reason}): ${e.message || e}`)
  }
}

async function js(code) {
  try { return await wv.evaluateJavaScript(code, false) } catch (e) { return undefined }
}

async function show(rows, meta) {
  meta = Object.assign({}, meta, { trip: readEvents().active, kinds: readKinds() })
  const hasSync = !!(meta.check && meta.check.health && meta.check.health.lastRun) || !!(latest && latest.meta && latest.meta.check && latest.meta.check.health && latest.meta.check.health.lastRun)
  meta.autoSetup = { known: true, set: !!AUTO, asked: Keychain.contains(AUTO_ASKED_KEY), mode: AUTO ? AUTO.mode : "off", sms: !!(AUTO && AUTO.sms), preview: !!(AUTO && AUTO.preview), name: smsName(), hasSync }
  if (AUTO && AUTO.sms && AUTO.mode !== "off") { try { meta.autoSms = autoView(rows, [], []) } catch (e) {} }
  latest = { rows, meta }
  await js(`window.setData && window.setData(${safe(rows)}, ${safe(meta)})`)
}

// ---------- updates: a button in Settings, never in the background ----------
// Nothing is contacted unless you tap "Check for updates". The update address answers with the newest version, where to
// download it and its SHA-256 fingerprint. "Update now" downloads the code, checks it against the fingerprint, keeps a copy of
// the current version, and only then replaces this script. "Restore previous version" puts the kept copy back.
const UPDATE_ENDPOINT = "https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/e/2PACX-1vS_YJ9V0bVj-CpB9zLIGyM6NTOmoccEBnl_QDhktLK4D982-h_9OL_VIQRuQ-h52GxNvSdp93NQ4Pn3/pub?gid=0&single=true&output=csv"    // the published Config sheet (or an Apps Script address)
const UPDATE_KEY = "expenses_update"
const UPDATE_EDITION = typeof PRIVATE !== "undefined" ? "private" : "dashboard"
const SHA_K = new Uint32Array([0x428a2f98, 0x71374491, 0xb5c0fbcf, 0xe9b5dba5, 0x3956c25b, 0x59f111f1, 0x923f82a4, 0xab1c5ed5, 0xd807aa98, 0x12835b01, 0x243185be, 0x550c7dc3, 0x72be5d74, 0x80deb1fe, 0x9bdc06a7, 0xc19bf174, 0xe49b69c1, 0xefbe4786, 0x0fc19dc6, 0x240ca1cc, 0x2de92c6f, 0x4a7484aa, 0x5cb0a9dc, 0x76f988da, 0x983e5152, 0xa831c66d, 0xb00327c8, 0xbf597fc7, 0xc6e00bf3, 0xd5a79147, 0x06ca6351, 0x14292967, 0x27b70a85, 0x2e1b2138, 0x4d2c6dfc, 0x53380d13, 0x650a7354, 0x766a0abb, 0x81c2c92e, 0x92722c85, 0xa2bfe8a1, 0xa81a664b, 0xc24b8b70, 0xc76c51a3, 0xd192e819, 0xd6990624, 0xf40e3585, 0x106aa070, 0x19a4c116, 0x1e376c08, 0x2748774c, 0x34b0bcb5, 0x391c0cb3, 0x4ed8aa4a, 0x5b9cca4f, 0x682e6ff3, 0x748f82ee, 0x78a5636f, 0x84c87814, 0x8cc70208, 0x90befffa, 0xa4506ceb, 0xbef9a3f7, 0xc67178f2])
function sha256Hex(str) {
  const bin = unescape(encodeURIComponent(String(str))), n = bin.length
  const total = (((n + 8) >> 6) + 1) << 4
  const w = new Int32Array(total)
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) w[i >> 2] |= bin.charCodeAt(i) << (24 - (i % 4) * 8)
  w[n >> 2] |= 0x80 << (24 - (n % 4) * 8)
  w[total - 1] = n * 8
  const H = new Int32Array([0x6a09e667, 0xbb67ae85, 0x3c6ef372, 0xa54ff53a, 0x510e527f, 0x9b05688c, 0x1f83d9ab, 0x5be0cd19]), W = new Int32Array(64)
  for (let b = 0; b < total; b += 16) {
    for (let t = 0; t < 16; t++) W[t] = w[b + t]
    for (let t = 16; t < 64; t++) {
      const x = W[t - 15], y = W[t - 2]
      W[t] = (W[t - 16] + (((x >>> 7) | (x << 25)) ^ ((x >>> 18) | (x << 14)) ^ (x >>> 3)) + W[t - 7] + (((y >>> 17) | (y << 15)) ^ ((y >>> 19) | (y << 13)) ^ (y >>> 10))) | 0
    }
    let a = H[0], bb = H[1], c = H[2], d = H[3], e = H[4], f = H[5], g = H[6], h = H[7]
    for (let t = 0; t < 64; t++) {
      const t1 = (h + (((e >>> 6) | (e << 26)) ^ ((e >>> 11) | (e << 21)) ^ ((e >>> 25) | (e << 7))) + ((e & f) ^ (~e & g)) + SHA_K[t] + W[t]) | 0
      const t2 = ((((a >>> 2) | (a << 30)) ^ ((a >>> 13) | (a << 19)) ^ ((a >>> 22) | (a << 10))) + ((a & bb) ^ (a & c) ^ (bb & c))) | 0
      h = g; g = f; f = e; e = (d + t1) | 0; d = c; c = bb; bb = a; a = (t1 + t2) | 0
    }
    H[0] = (H[0] + a) | 0; H[1] = (H[1] + bb) | 0; H[2] = (H[2] + c) | 0; H[3] = (H[3] + d) | 0
    H[4] = (H[4] + e) | 0; H[5] = (H[5] + f) | 0; H[6] = (H[6] + g) | 0; H[7] = (H[7] + h) | 0
  }
  return Array.from(H, x => (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0")).join("")
}
const verParts = v => (String(v).match(/\d+/g) || []).map(Number)
function verNewer(a, b) {   // is version a newer than b?
  const x = verParts(a), y = verParts(b)
  for (let i = 0; i < Math.max(x.length, y.length); i++) { const d = (x[i] || 0) - (y[i] || 0); if (d) return d > 0 }
  return false
}
const thisVersion = () => String(SCRIPT_VERSION).replace(/ private$/, "")
function readUpdateState() { try { return Keychain.contains(UPDATE_KEY) ? JSON.parse(Keychain.get(UPDATE_KEY)) || {} : {} } catch (e) { return {} } }
function saveUpdateState(st) { try { Keychain.set(UPDATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(st)) } catch (e) {} }
function updateInfo() {
  const st = readUpdateState(), l = st.latest
  return { configured: !!UPDATE_ENDPOINT, version: thisVersion(), edition: UPDATE_EDITION, checkedAt: st.checkedAt || 0,
    available: l && verNewer(l.version, thisVersion()) ? { version: l.version, notes: l.notes || "" } : null,
    canRestore: !!(st.prev && st.prev.version), prevVersion: (st.prev && st.prev.version) || "" }
}
// Where this script lives. FileManager is reached by name so the private edition's file rewriting leaves it alone.
function scriptFiles() {
  const FM = FileManager
  let path = ""
  try { path = module.filename } catch (e) {}
  const icloud = FM["iCloud"](), local = FM["local"]()
  if (!path) path = local.joinPath(local.documentsDirectory(), Script.name() + ".js")
  let fm = local
  try { if (path.startsWith(icloud.documentsDirectory())) fm = icloud } catch (e) {}
  return { fm, path, dir: fm.joinPath(fm.documentsDirectory(), "Expenses Updates") }
}
// The answer is either JSON, or the Config sheet published as CSV: a header row (edition, version, url, sha256, notes), then one row per edition.
function parseCsv(text) {
  const rows = []; let row = [], cur = "", q = false
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
    const c = text[i]
    if (q) { if (c === '"') { if (text[i + 1] === '"') { cur += '"'; i++ } else q = false } else cur += c }
    else if (c === '"') q = true
    else if (c === ",") { row.push(cur); cur = "" }
    else if (c === "\n" || c === "\r") { if (c === "\r" && text[i + 1] === "\n") i++; row.push(cur); cur = ""; rows.push(row); row = [] }
    else cur += c
  }
  if (cur || row.length) { row.push(cur); rows.push(row) }
  return rows
}
function parseUpdateAnswer(text, edition) {
  const t = String(text || "").trim()
  if (t.startsWith("{")) { try { return JSON.parse(t) } catch (e) { return null } }
  const rows = parseCsv(t); if (rows.length < 2) return null
  const head = rows[0].map(h => h.trim().toLowerCase()), col = n => head.indexOf(n)
  const r = rows.slice(1).find(x => String(x[col("edition")] || "").trim().toLowerCase() === edition)
  if (!r) return null
  return { version: String(r[col("version")] || "").trim(), url: String(r[col("url")] || "").trim(), sha256: String(r[col("sha256")] || "").trim(), notes: String(r[col("notes")] || "").trim() }
}
async function updateCheck() {
  if (!UPDATE_ENDPOINT) return "Updates aren't set up for this copy yet."
  let text
  try { const r = new Request(`${UPDATE_ENDPOINT}${UPDATE_ENDPOINT.includes("?") ? "&" : "?"}e=${UPDATE_EDITION}`); r.timeoutInterval = 20; text = await r.loadString() } catch (e) { return "Couldn't reach the update server. Check your connection and try again." }
  const a = parseUpdateAnswer(text, UPDATE_EDITION)
  if (a && !a.version && !a.url && !a.sha256) return "No update information has been published yet. Try again later."
  if (!a || !/^\d+(\.\d+)*$/.test(String(a.version || "")) || !/^https:\/\//.test(String(a.url || "")) || !/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/i.test(String(a.sha256 || ""))) return "The update server's answer wasn't understood. Nothing was changed."
  const st = readUpdateState()
  st.checkedAt = Date.now(); st.latest = { version: String(a.version), url: String(a.url), sha256: String(a.sha256).toLowerCase(), notes: String(a.notes || "").slice(0, 600) }
  saveUpdateState(st)
  return verNewer(a.version, thisVersion()) ? `Version ${a.version} is available.` : `You're up to date (version ${thisVersion()}).`
}
async function updateApply() {
  const st = readUpdateState(), l = st.latest
  if (!l || !verNewer(l.version, thisVersion())) return "You're already on the newest version."
  let code
  try { const r = new Request(l.url); r.timeoutInterval = 60; code = await r.loadString() } catch (e) { return "The download failed. Nothing was changed." }
  if (!code || code.length < 50000) return "The download looked incomplete. Nothing was changed."
  if (sha256Hex(code) !== l.sha256) return "The download didn't match its fingerprint, so it wasn't installed. Nothing was changed."
  const m = code.match(/const SCRIPT_VERSION = "([^"]+)"/)
  if (!m || m[1].replace(/ private$/, "") !== l.version) return "The download isn't the version it should be. Nothing was changed."
  // the private edition starts a line with "const PRIVATE = true" (this very check mentions it mid-line, so every edition's code contains the words)
  if (/^const PRIVATE = true/m.test(code) !== (UPDATE_EDITION === "private")) return "The download is for a different edition. Nothing was changed."
  try { const AsyncFn = Object.getPrototypeOf(async function () {}).constructor; new AsyncFn(code) } catch (e) { return "The download didn't pass a safety check, so it wasn't installed. Nothing was changed." }
  const { fm, path, dir } = scriptFiles()
  let current
  try { current = fm.readString(path) } catch (e) { return "Couldn't read this script to keep a copy of it. Nothing was changed." }
  try {
    if (!fm.fileExists(dir)) fm.createDirectory(dir, true)
    fm.writeString(fm.joinPath(dir, `previous-${UPDATE_EDITION}.txt`), current)
    fm.writeString(path, code)
    if (sha256Hex(fm.readString(path)) !== l.sha256) { fm.writeString(path, current); return "The update didn't save properly, so the old version was put back." }
  } catch (e) { try { fm.writeString(path, current) } catch (e2) {} ; return "Couldn't save the update. The old version was kept." }
  st.prev = { version: thisVersion() }; saveUpdateState(st)
  return `Updated to version ${l.version}. Close this and open the script again to use it.`
}
async function updateRestore() {
  const st = readUpdateState(), { fm, path, dir } = scriptFiles()
  if (!st.prev) return "There's no previous version to restore."
  let old
  try { old = fm.readString(fm.joinPath(dir, `previous-${UPDATE_EDITION}.txt`)) } catch (e) { return "There's no previous version to restore." }
  if (!old || !/const SCRIPT_VERSION = "[^"]+"/.test(old)) return "The kept copy isn't usable, so nothing was changed."
  let current = ""
  try { current = fm.readString(path) } catch (e) {}
  try { fm.writeString(path, old) } catch (e) { return "Couldn't restore the previous version." }
  st.prev = null; saveUpdateState(st)
  return `Restored version ${(old.match(/const SCRIPT_VERSION = "([^"]+)"/) || [])[1] || ""}. Close this and open the script again.`
}
async function updateAction(kind) {
  let msg
  try { msg = kind === "update-check" ? await updateCheck() : kind === "update-apply" ? await updateApply() : await updateRestore() } catch (e) { msg = "Something went wrong. Nothing was changed." }
  const info = updateInfo()
  if (/^(Updated to|Restored) version/.test(msg)) info.reopen = msg    // the page then says "close and reopen" instead of offering more buttons
  await js(`window.setUpdateInfo && window.setUpdateInfo(${safe(info)}, ${safe(msg)})`)
}
let extraMessage = null   // a confirmation to show after the next refresh

async function refresh() {
  if (refreshing) return
  refreshing = true
  try {
    await js(`window.setLoading && window.setLoading()`)
    const tok = await token(lastErrorCode === "unauthorized")
    if (!tok) throw Object.assign(new Error("No Notion token saved. Tap Retry to add it."), { code: "no_token" })
    if (!(await databaseId(lastErrorCode === "no_database" || lastErrorCode === "validation_error" || lastErrorCode === "object_not_found"))) throw Object.assign(new Error("No Expenses database saved yet. Tap Retry to add its link."), { code: "no_database" })
    const t0 = Date.now(), full = refreshFull
    refreshFull = false
    const [, , got] = await Promise.all([loadCards(tok), findTables(tok), loadExpenses(tok, full)])   // side by side, not one after another
    await loadAccounts(tok)
    await js(`window.setCards && window.setCards(${safe(CREDIT_CARDS)})`)
    await js(`window.setAccounts && window.setAccounts(${safe(ACCOUNT_NAMES)})`)
    await js(`window.setStmtInfo && window.setStmtInfo(${safe(stmtInfo())})`)
    const rows = got.rows
    lastErrorCode = null
    if (AUTO && AUTO.sms && AUTO.mode === "fill" && !AUTO.preview) { try { const n = await processSmsQueue(tok, rows); if (n) { extraMessage = extraMessage || `${n} payment${n === 1 ? "" : "s"} from your bank SMS added.`; forceFull = true } } catch (e) { log(`sms queue: ${e.message || e}`) } }
    let tagged = 0, tagError = null
    try { tagged = await autoTag(tok, rows) } catch (e) { tagError = e; log(`event tagging failed: ${e.message || e}`) }
    writeCache(rows, got.fullAt)
    const tShown = Date.now()
    const meta = Object.assign({ at: Date.now() }, extraMessage ? { message: extraMessage } : {}, tagError ? { error: "Couldn't tag new expenses to your event: " + friendly(tagError) } : {})
    await show(rows, Object.assign({}, meta, latest && latest.meta && latest.meta.check ? { check: latest.meta.check } : {}, { checking: true }))
    extraMessage = null
    log(`refreshed: ${rows.length} expenses${tagged ? `, ${tagged} tagged to event` : ""}`)
    // Then match bank/card alerts and show what's not logged.
    try {
      const check = await checkAlerts(tok, rows)
      writeCache(rows)
      log(`timing: expenses ${tShown - t0} ms (${got.changed == null ? "full read" : got.changed + " changed"}), alerts/loans/people ${Date.now() - tShown} ms`)
      await show(rows, Object.assign({}, meta, { check }))
      log(`alerts: ${check.total} checked, ${check.pending} matches waiting for the sync, ${check.open.length} not logged, ${check.noCard.length} need a card${check.health.warn ? " · " + check.health.warn : ""}`)
    } catch (e) {
      log(`alert check failed: ${e.code || ""} ${e.message || e}`)
      await show(rows, Object.assign({}, meta, latest && latest.meta && latest.meta.check ? { check: latest.meta.check } : {}, { checkError: alertsFriendly(e) }))
    }
  } catch (err) {
    lastErrorCode = err.code || null
    log(`refresh failed: ${err.code || ""} ${err.message || err}`)
    if (err.code === "unauthorized" && Keychain.contains(TOKEN_KEY)) Keychain.remove(TOKEN_KEY)
    const c = readCache()
    await show(c ? c.rows : [], { at: c ? c.at : Date.now(), cached: !!c, error: friendly(err) })
  } finally {
    refreshing = false
  }
}

// Event mode and event changes requested by the page. Each one writes to Notion, then reloads.
async function eventAction(kind, data) {
  while (refreshing) await sleep(200)
  try {
    const tok = await token()
    if (!tok || !DATABASE_ID) throw new Error("Finish setup first: the dashboard needs your Notion token and database link.")
    const st = readEvents()
    if (kind === "event-start") {
      const name = cleanEventName(data.name)
      if (!name) return
      await ensureEventColumn(tok)
      const now = new Date(); now.setSeconds(0, 0)   // Notion records times to the minute
      st.active = { name, since: now.toISOString() }
      saveEvents(st)
      const kinds = readKinds()
      if (!kinds[name]) { kinds[name] = guessKind(name) || "trip"; saveKinds(kinds) }
      extraMessage = `Event mode is on for "${name}". Everything you log now goes there and stays out of your monthly budget.`
      log(`event mode on: ${name}`)
    } else if (kind === "event-stop") {
      // Tag anything logged since the last refresh before switching off.
      const rows = await fetchExpenses(tok)
      await autoTag(tok, rows)
      const name = st.active ? st.active.name : ""
      st.active = null
      saveEvents(st)
      extraMessage = name ? `Event mode is off. "${name}" is saved under Events.` : null
      log(`event mode off: ${name}`)
    } else if (kind === "event-assign") {
      const name = cleanEventName(data.name)
      const ids = (data.ids || []).filter(Boolean)
      if (name) await ensureEventColumn(tok)
      for (const id of ids) {
        await setEvent(tok, id, name)
        if (name) delete st.excluded[id]; else st.excluded[id] = true
      }
      saveEvents(st)
      extraMessage = name ? `Added ${ids.length} expense${ids.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} to "${name}".` : "Removed from the event. It now counts towards your monthly spending."
      log(`event-assign ${ids.length} → ${name || "(none)"}`)
    }
  } catch (e) {
    log(`event action failed (${kind}): ${e.message || e}`)
    extraMessage = null
    await refresh()
    await js(`window.setData && window.setData(${safe(latest ? latest.rows : [])}, ${safe(Object.assign({}, latest ? latest.meta : {}, { error: friendly(e) }))})`)
    return
  }
  await refresh()
}

// "ok" when the page is on screen; anything else means it is missing.
// (Asks for a word, not true/false: iOS can hand booleans back as 1/0.)
async function pageState() {
  try {
    const r = await wv.evaluateJavaScript(`!document.getElementById("hero") ? "empty" : (window.__ready === true ? "ok" : "ok-starting")`, false)
    return r === "ok" || r === "ok-starting" ? "ok" : "not-ready:" + r
  } catch (e) {
    return "no-response"
  }
}

// If the page hasn't appeared after ~8 seconds, say so instead of leaving a black screen.
async function startupCheck() {
  // check often, so loading from Notion starts the moment the page is up (it used to wait a full second)
  for (let i = 0; i < 54 && !closed; i++) {
    await sleep(150)
    const state = await pageState()
    if (state === "ok") { log(`page visible after ${(i + 1) * 150} ms`); return true }
    if (i === 26) { log(`page not visible yet (${state}); reloading`); await loadPage("startup retry") }
  }
  if (closed) return false
  log("page still not visible after 8s")
  while (!closed) {
    const a = new Alert()
    a.title = "The dashboard didn't load"
    a.message = "Tap Reload to try again. If it keeps happening, tap Show log and send it to Claude."
    a.addAction("Reload")
    a.addAction("Show log")
    a.addCancelAction("Close")
    const choice = await a.presentAlert()
    if (choice === 0) {
      await loadPage("reload button")
      await sleep(2500)
      if (await pageState() === "ok") { log("page visible after reload"); return true }
    } else if (choice === 1) {
      await QuickLook.present(readLog() || "The log is empty.", true)
    } else {
      return false
    }
  }
  return false
}

// Later on, iOS can stop the page (low memory, or after switching apps); rebuild it if that happens.
async function watchdog() {
  let reloads = 0, misses = 0
  while (!closed) {
    await sleep(3000)
    if (closed) return
    const state = await pageState()
    if (state === "ok") { misses = 0; continue }
    if (++misses < 2) continue
    if (reloads >= 3) { log(`page not responding (${state}); stopped after 3 reloads`); return }
    reloads++; misses = 0
    log(`page not responding (${state}); reloading #${reloads}`)
    await loadPage("watchdog")
  }
}

let OPEN_TAB = ""
async function runDashboard() {
  const qp = (typeof args !== "undefined" && args.queryParameters) || {}
  OPEN_TAB = qp.openTab || ((typeof args !== "undefined" && args.widgetParameter) ? "events" : "")
  const where = config.runsInApp ? "Scriptable app" : config.runsWithSiri ? "Shortcuts/Siri (Run In App off)" : "other"
  log(`— v${SCRIPT_VERSION} opened from ${where}`)
  wv = new WebView()
  // Requests from the page: refresh, save settings, open an expense in Notion.
  wv.shouldAllowRequest = req => {
    const url = req.url || ""
    if (url.startsWith("app://refresh")) { if (/full=1/.test(url)) refreshFull = true; refresh(); return false }
    if (url.startsWith("app://event-icon?")) {
      try {
        const d = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(url.slice("app://event-icon?".length)))
        if (d.name && KIND_SYMBOLS[d.kind]) {
          const kinds = readKinds(); kinds[d.name] = d.kind; saveKinds(kinds)
          if (latest) latest.meta = Object.assign({}, latest.meta, { kinds })
          log(`icon for ${d.name}: ${d.kind}`)
        }
      } catch (e) { log(`icon save failed: ${e.message || e}`) }
      return false
    }
    if (url.startsWith("app://copy?")) {
      try {
        Pasteboard.copy(decodeURIComponent(url.slice("app://copy?".length)))
        js(`window.flash && window.flash("Copied. Paste it into Excel or Numbers: each month lands in its own row.")`)
      } catch (e) { log(`copy failed: ${e.message || e}`) }
      return false
    }
    if (url.startsWith("app://remind?")) {
      let data = {}
      try { data = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(url.slice("app://remind?".length))) || {} } catch (e) {}
      remind(data)
      return false
    }
    const al = url.match(/^app:\/\/(alert-add|alert-skip|set-card|set-owed|paid-back|bill-seen|set-markups|loan-set|loan-emi|loan-add|loan-close|loan-accept|loan-dismiss|loan-ignore|loan-doc|loan-upload|loan-email)(?:\?(.*))?$/)
    if (al) {
      let data = {}
      try { data = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(al[2] || "")) || {} } catch (e) {}
      alertAction(al[1], data)
      return false
    }
    if (/^app:\/\/upi-set/.test(url)) { upiAction({}); return false }
    const pdq = url.match(/^app:\/\/(policy-doc|policy-docuse|policy-docskip|policy-docdel|policy-open)(?:\?(.*))?$/)
    if (pdq) {
      let data = {}
      try { data = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(pdq[2] || "")) || {} } catch (e) {}
      policyDocAction(pdq[1], data)
      return false
    }
    const polq = url.match(/^app:\/\/(policy-save|policy-paid|policy-accept|policy-dismiss|policy-delete|policy-ignore)(?:\?(.*))?$/)
    if (polq) {
      let data = {}
      try { data = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(polq[2] || "")) || {} } catch (e) {}
      policyAction(polq[1], data)
      return false
    }
    const cq = url.match(/^app:\/\/(contact-link|contact-unlink|contact-call|contact-sms|contact-wa)(?:\?(.*))?$/)
    if (cq) {
      let data = {}
      try { data = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(cq[2] || "")) || {} } catch (e) {}
      contactAction(cq[1], data)
      return false
    }
    const aq = url.match(/^app:\/\/(autolog-set|autolog-dismiss|auto-review|auto-notexpense)(?:\?(.*))?$/)
    if (aq) {
      let data = {}
      try { data = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(aq[2] || "")) || {} } catch (e) {}
      autoAction(aq[1], data)
      return false
    }
    const stq = url.match(/^app:\/\/(stmt-import|stmt-category|stmt-rule-remove|stmt-undo)(?:\?(.*))?$/)
    if (stq) {
      let data = {}
      try { data = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(stq[2] || "")) || {} } catch (e) {}
      stmtAction(stq[1], data)
      return false
    }
    const upd = url.match(/^app:\/\/(update-check|update-apply|update-restore)$/)
    if (upd) { updateAction(upd[1]); return false }
    const crd = url.match(/^app:\/\/(card-add|card-rename|card-remove|acct-add|acct-rename|acct-remove)(?:\?(.*))?$/)
    if (crd) {
      let data = {}
      try { data = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(crd[2] || "")) || {} } catch (e) {}
      cardAction(crd[1], data)
      return false
    }
    const pq = url.match(/^app:\/\/(person-save|person-remove|person-alias|dayoff-batch|loan-new|diary-add|diary-assign|diary-skip|diary-delete)(?:\?(.*))?$/)
    if (pq) {
      let data = {}
      try { data = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(pq[2] || "")) || {} } catch (e) {}
      peopleAction(pq[1], data)
      return false
    }
    const ev = url.match(/^app:\/\/(event-start|event-stop|event-assign)(?:\?(.*))?$/)
    if (ev) {
      let data = {}
      try { data = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(ev[2] || "")) || {} } catch (e) {}
      eventAction(ev[1], data)
      return false
    }
    if (url.startsWith("app://settings?")) {
      try { Keychain.set(SETTINGS_KEY, decodeURIComponent(url.slice("app://settings?".length))) } catch (e) {}
      return false
    }
    if (/^https:\/\/(www\.)?notion\.so\/|^https:\/\/app\.notion\.com\//.test(url)) { Safari.open(url); return false }
    return true
  }

  // Open with the last data saved on this phone, then refresh from Notion.
  const cached = readCache()
  const cachedCheck = readCheckCache()
  if (cached) latest = { rows: cached.rows, meta: Object.assign({ at: cached.at, cached: true }, cachedCheck ? { check: cachedCheck } : {}) }
  chartJs = await chartLibrary()
  log(`chart library ${chartJs ? Math.round(chartJs.length / 1024) + " KB" : "missing"}; saved data ${cached ? cached.rows.length + " expenses" : "none"}`)
  await loadPage("open")
  const shown = wv.present(true).then(() => { closed = true; log("closed") })
  const ok = await startupCheck()
  if (ok) {
    watchdog().catch(e => log(`watchdog stopped: ${e.message || e}`))
    await refresh()
  }
  await shown
}

// ---------- widget ----------
// What counts as your spending: the amount minus anything others owe you back.
const share = r => Math.max(0, (Number(r.Amount) || 0) - (Number(r.Owed) || 0))

const MON = ["Jan","Feb","Mar","Apr","May","Jun","Jul","Aug","Sep","Oct","Nov","Dec"]
const CAT_COLORS = {
  "Credit Card Dues": "#2F6FDB", "Groceries": "#2E9E5B", "Food & Dining": "#E8873A", "Subscriptions": "#D6458F",
  "Shopping": "#1AA39A", "Bills & Utilities": "#7D8590", "Entertainment": "#8B55D6", "Health": "#D9473F",
  "Travel": "#E6B530", "Personal": "#9A6B3F", "Gifts": "#C2602B", "Other": "#A9AEB5",
}
const pad2 = n => String(n).padStart(2, "0")
const inr = n => "₹" + Math.round(n).toLocaleString("en-IN")
const inrShort = n => n >= 1e5 ? "₹" + +(n / 1e5).toFixed(1) + "L" : n >= 1e3 ? "₹" + +(n / 1e3).toFixed(1) + "k" : inr(n)

function summarize(rows, now, countDues) {
  const key = `${now.getFullYear()}-${pad2(now.getMonth() + 1)}`
  const prev = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - 1, 1)
  const prevKey = `${prev.getFullYear()}-${pad2(prev.getMonth() + 1)}`
  const prevDays = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 0).getDate()
  const cutoff = Math.min(now.getDate(), prevDays)
  const out = { events: {}, total: 0, lastSoFar: 0, lastTotal: 0, cats: {}, recent: [], today: 0, byDay: Array(32).fill(0), prevLabel: MON[prev.getMonth()], cutoff,
    day: now.getDate(), days: new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() + 1, 0).getDate(), mon: MON[now.getMonth()] }
  for (const r of rows) {
    const date = String(r["date:Date:start"] || r.Created || "").slice(0, 10)
    const cat = r.Category || "Other"
    if (r.Event) { if (date.startsWith(key)) out.events[r.Event] = (out.events[r.Event] || 0) + share(r); continue }
    if (!countDues && cat === DUES) continue
    const amt = share(r)
    if (date.startsWith(key)) {
      out.total += amt; out.cats[cat] = (out.cats[cat] || 0) + amt
      const d = Number(date.slice(8, 10)) || 0
      out.byDay[d] += amt
      if (d === now.getDate()) out.today += amt
    }
    if (date.startsWith(prevKey)) { out.lastTotal += amt; if (Number(date.slice(8, 10)) <= cutoff) out.lastSoFar += amt }
  }
  out.recent = [...rows].filter(r => !r.Event).sort((a, b) => String(b.Created).localeCompare(String(a.Created))).slice(0, 4)
  return out
}

function barImage(cats, total, w, h) {
  const ctx = new DrawContext()
  ctx.size = new Size(w, h); ctx.opaque = false; ctx.respectScreenScale = true
  ctx.setFillColor(new Color("#8E8E93", 0.22)); ctx.fillRect(new Rect(0, 0, w, h))
  let x = 0
  for (const [c, v] of Object.entries(cats).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])) {
    const seg = total > 0 ? w * v / total : 0
    ctx.setFillColor(new Color(CAT_COLORS[c] || CAT_COLORS.Other))
    ctx.fillRect(new Rect(x, 0, Math.max(0, seg > 3 ? seg - 1 : seg), h)); x += seg
  }
  return ctx.getImage()
}

function meterImage(spent, limit, frac, color, w, h) {
  const ctx = new DrawContext()
  ctx.size = new Size(w, h); ctx.opaque = false; ctx.respectScreenScale = true
  ctx.setFillColor(new Color("#8E8E93", 0.22)); ctx.fillRect(new Rect(0, 0, w, h))
  ctx.setFillColor(color); ctx.fillRect(new Rect(0, 0, w * Math.min(1, spent / limit), h))
  ctx.setFillColor(new Color("#8E8E93")); ctx.fillRect(new Rect(Math.min(w - 2, w * frac), 0, 2, h))
  return ctx.getImage()
}

function dayBarsImage(byDay, days, today, w, h, accent, faint) {
  const ctx = new DrawContext()
  ctx.size = new Size(w, h); ctx.opaque = false; ctx.respectScreenScale = true
  const max = Math.max(1, ...byDay.slice(1, days + 1))
  const slot = w / days, bw = Math.max(2, slot - 2)
  for (let d = 1; d <= days; d++) {
    const v = byDay[d] || 0
    const bh = v > 0 ? Math.max(2, (h - 1) * v / max) : 1
    ctx.setFillColor(d <= today ? (v > 0 ? accent : faint) : faint)
    ctx.fillRect(new Rect((d - 1) * slot, h - bh, bw, bh))
  }
  return ctx.getImage()
}

function dotImage(hex) {
  const ctx = new DrawContext()
  ctx.size = new Size(8, 8); ctx.opaque = false; ctx.respectScreenScale = true
  ctx.setFillColor(new Color(hex)); ctx.fillEllipse(new Rect(0, 0, 8, 8))
  return ctx.getImage()
}

async function runWidget() {
  // payments from bank SMS waiting their hour get added in the background too
  try { if (AUTO && AUTO.sms && AUTO.mode === "fill" && !AUTO.preview && Keychain.contains(TOKEN_KEY)) await processSmsQueue(Keychain.get(TOKEN_KEY)) } catch (e) { log(`sms queue (widget): ${e.message || e}`) }
  // Widget Parameter (long-press › Edit Widget › Parameter):
  //   empty         → the normal monthly widget
  //   event (or trip) → the event that's on now, or your most recent event
  //   an event name → that event, e.g. House maintenance
  const param = String((typeof args !== "undefined" && args.widgetParameter) || "").trim()
  if (param) return runTripWidget(param)
  const fam = config.widgetFamily || "medium"
  const now = new Date()
  const st = settings()
  const prev = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - 1, 1)
  const since = `${prev.getFullYear()}-${pad2(prev.getMonth() + 1)}-01`

  let rows = null, stale = false, problem = null, missed = 0, syncWarn = false
  try {
    const tok = Keychain.contains(TOKEN_KEY) ? Keychain.get(TOKEN_KEY) : null
    if (!tok || !DATABASE_ID) throw Object.assign(new Error("Open the dashboard once to finish setup (Notion token and database link)."), { code: "no_token" })
    rows = await fetchExpenses(tok, since)   // only this month and last: fast enough for a widget
    try { await autoTag(tok, rows) } catch (e) { log(`widget event tagging failed: ${e.message || e}`) }
    try { const c = await checkAlerts(tok, rows); missed = c.open.length; syncWarn = !!c.health.warn } catch (e) { const c = readCheckCache(); missed = c ? c.open.length : 0 }
  } catch (err) {
    const c = readCache()
    if (c) { rows = c.rows; stale = true } else problem = friendly(err)
  }

  const w = new ListWidget()
  w.url = DASHBOARD_SHORTCUT ? "shortcuts://run-shortcut?name=" + encodeURIComponent(DASHBOARD_SHORTCUT) : URLScheme.forRunningScript()
  w.refreshAfterDate = new Date(Date.now() + 30 * 60 * 1000)
  const fg = Color.dynamic(new Color("#16201B"), new Color("#E7EDEA"))
  const muted = Color.dynamic(new Color("#5F6B65"), new Color("#95A39C"))
  const MORE = Color.dynamic(new Color("#B9443A"), new Color("#F08075"))
  const LESS = Color.dynamic(new Color("#2C8455"), new Color("#62C98F"))
  const WARN = Color.dynamic(new Color("#B7791F"), new Color("#E3B05A"))
  if (!fam.startsWith("accessory")) w.backgroundColor = Color.dynamic(new Color("#FFFFFF"), new Color("#171E1B"))

  if (problem) {
    const t = w.addText(problem); t.font = Font.systemFont(12); t.textColor = muted
    Script.setWidget(w); return
  }

  const s = summarize(rows, now, !!st.countDues)
  const diff = s.total - s.lastSoFar
  const paceText = s.lastTotal ? `${diff > 0 ? "▲" : "▼"} ${inr(Math.abs(diff))} vs ${s.cutoff} ${s.prevLabel}` : `No ${s.prevLabel} data`
  const paceColor = !s.lastTotal ? muted : diff > 0 ? MORE : LESS
  const limit = Number(st.budget) || 0

  if (fam === "accessoryInline") {
    w.addText(`${s.mon} ${inr(s.total)} · ${limit ? inr(Math.max(0, limit - s.total)) + " left" : paceText}`)
    Script.setWidget(w); return
  }
  if (fam === "accessoryCircular") {
    w.addAccessoryWidgetBackground = true
    const a = w.addText(s.mon); a.font = Font.mediumSystemFont(10); a.centerAlignText()
    const b = w.addText(inrShort(s.total)); b.font = Font.boldRoundedSystemFont(14); b.minimumScaleFactor = 0.5; b.centerAlignText()
    Script.setWidget(w); return
  }
  if (fam === "accessoryRectangular") {
    const a = w.addText(`${s.mon} so far`); a.font = Font.mediumSystemFont(12)
    const b = w.addText(inr(s.total)); b.font = Font.boldRoundedSystemFont(20); b.minimumScaleFactor = 0.6
    const c = w.addText(limit ? `${inr(Math.max(0, limit - s.total))} left of ${inrShort(limit)}` : paceText)
    c.font = Font.systemFont(12); c.lineLimit = 1; c.minimumScaleFactor = 0.8
    Script.setWidget(w); return
  }

  // Home screen: small, medium, large — content centred vertically
  if (fam !== "large" && fam !== "extraLarge") w.addSpacer()
  const top = fam === "small" ? w : w.addStack()
  const left = fam === "small" ? w : top.addStack()
  if (fam !== "small") left.layoutVertically()
  const barW = fam === "small" ? 110 : 130

  const label = left.addText(`${s.mon} so far${stale ? " · saved" : ""}`)
  label.font = Font.mediumSystemFont(12); label.textColor = muted
  left.addSpacer(2)
  const total = left.addText(inr(s.total))
  total.font = Font.boldRoundedSystemFont(26); total.textColor = fg; total.minimumScaleFactor = 0.6; total.lineLimit = 1
  const pace = left.addText(paceText)
  pace.font = Font.mediumSystemFont(11); pace.textColor = paceColor; pace.lineLimit = 1; pace.minimumScaleFactor = 0.8
  if (missed || syncWarn) {
    const mt = left.addText(syncWarn ? "Alert sync needs a look" : `${missed} payment${missed === 1 ? "" : "s"} not logged`)
    mt.font = Font.semiboldSystemFont(11); mt.textColor = WARN; mt.lineLimit = 1; mt.minimumScaleFactor = 0.8
  }
  left.addSpacer(8)
  if (limit) {
    const over = s.total > limit, ahead = s.total > limit * s.day / s.days
    const col = over ? MORE : ahead ? WARN : LESS
    left.addImage(meterImage(s.total, limit, s.day / s.days, col, 300, 10)).imageSize = new Size(barW, 8)
    left.addSpacer(4)
    const cap = left.addText(over ? `${inr(s.total - limit)} over budget` : `${inr(limit - s.total)} left`)
    cap.font = Font.systemFont(11); cap.textColor = col
  } else {
    left.addImage(barImage(s.cats, s.total, 300, 10)).imageSize = new Size(barW, 8)
    left.addSpacer(4)
    const cap = left.addText(`${s.prevLabel} total ${inr(s.lastTotal)}`)
    cap.font = Font.systemFont(11); cap.textColor = muted
  }
  // Event mode, or this month's one-off events, shown separately from the total above.
  const trip = readEvents().active
  const evTotal = Object.values(s.events).reduce((a, v) => a + v, 0)
  if (trip || evTotal) {
    left.addSpacer(3)
    const tripAmt = trip ? (s.events[trip.name] || 0) : evTotal
    const blue = Color.dynamic(new Color("#2F55C9"), new Color("#8EA6FF"))
    if (trip) iconLine(left, kindFor(trip.name), `${trip.name} ${inr(tripAmt)}`, 10, blue)
    else { const e = left.addText(`+ ${inr(evTotal)} one-off`); e.font = Font.mediumSystemFont(10); e.textColor = blue; e.lineLimit = 1; e.minimumScaleFactor = 0.7 }
  }

  if (fam !== "small") {
    top.addSpacer(18)
    const right = top.addStack()
    right.layoutVertically()

    // Always-present figures, so the widget looks complete even on day 1.
    const stats = right.addStack()
    const stat = (title, value) => {
      const col = stats.addStack(); col.layoutVertically()
      const a = col.addText(title); a.font = Font.semiboldSystemFont(10); a.textColor = muted
      const b = col.addText(value); b.font = Font.boldRoundedSystemFont(15); b.textColor = fg; b.lineLimit = 1; b.minimumScaleFactor = 0.7
    }
    stat("TODAY", inr(s.today))
    stats.addSpacer()
    stat("PER DAY", inr(s.total / Math.max(1, s.day)))
    right.addSpacer(10)

    const cats = Object.entries(s.cats).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 3)
    if (!cats.length) { const t = right.addText("Nothing logged yet this month"); t.font = Font.systemFont(11); t.textColor = muted }
    cats.forEach(([c, v], i) => {
      const r = right.addStack(); r.centerAlignContent()
      r.addImage(dotImage(CAT_COLORS[c] || CAT_COLORS.Other)).imageSize = new Size(8, 8)
      r.addSpacer(6)
      const n = r.addText(c); n.font = Font.systemFont(12); n.textColor = fg; n.lineLimit = 1
      r.addSpacer()
      const a = r.addText(inrShort(v)); a.font = Font.mediumSystemFont(12); a.textColor = fg
      if (i < cats.length - 1) right.addSpacer(5)
    })
  }

  if (fam === "large" || fam === "extraLarge") {
    w.addSpacer(16)
    const dh = w.addText(`${s.mon.toUpperCase()} BY DAY`); dh.font = Font.semiboldSystemFont(11); dh.textColor = muted
    w.addSpacer(6)
    w.addImage(dayBarsImage(s.byDay, s.days, s.day, 600, 80,
      Color.dynamic(new Color("#2F55C9"), new Color("#8EA6FF")), new Color("#8E8E93", 0.25))).imageSize = new Size(290, 38)
    w.addSpacer(14)
    const h = w.addText("RECENTLY LOGGED"); h.font = Font.semiboldSystemFont(11); h.textColor = muted
    w.addSpacer(6)
    for (const r of s.recent) {
      const row = w.addStack(); row.centerAlignContent()
      row.addImage(dotImage(CAT_COLORS[r.Category] || CAT_COLORS.Other)).imageSize = new Size(8, 8)
      row.addSpacer(8)
      const n = row.addText(r.Merchant || r.Category || "Expense"); n.font = Font.systemFont(13); n.textColor = fg; n.lineLimit = 1
      row.addSpacer()
      const a = row.addText(inrshare(r)); a.font = Font.mediumSystemFont(13); a.textColor = fg
      w.addSpacer(6)
    }
  }
  w.addSpacer()
  Script.setWidget(w)
}

// ---------- event widget ----------

const isoDay = d => `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getDate())}`

async function latestEventName(tok) {
  const rows = await fetchExpenses(tok, null, {
    filter: { property: "Event", select: { is_not_empty: true } },
    sorts: [{ property: "Date", direction: "descending" }], max: 1,
  })
  return rows[0] ? rows[0].Event : ""
}

const shortDay = iso => { const d = new Date(iso + "T00:00:00"); return `${d.getDate()} ${MON[d.getMonth()]}` }

async function runTripWidget(param) {
  const fam = config.widgetFamily || "small"
  const st = readEvents()
  const followActive = /^(trip|event|current)$/i.test(param)
  let name = followActive ? (st.active ? st.active.name : "") : cleanEventName(param)
  let rows = [], stale = false, problem = null, noColumn = false

  try {
    const tok = Keychain.contains(TOKEN_KEY) ? Keychain.get(TOKEN_KEY) : null
    if (!tok || !DATABASE_ID) throw Object.assign(new Error("Open the dashboard once to finish setup (Notion token and database link)."), { code: "no_token" })
    // While event mode is on, tag anything logged since it started before counting.
    if (st.active && (followActive || name === st.active.name)) {
      try { await autoTag(tok, await fetchExpenses(tok, st.active.since.slice(0, 10))) } catch (e) { log(`event widget tagging failed: ${e.message || e}`) }
    }
    if (!name) name = await latestEventName(tok)
    if (name) rows = await fetchExpenses(tok, null, { filter: { property: "Event", select: { equals: name } } })
  } catch (err) {
    if (err.code === "validation_error" && /Event/i.test(err.message || "")) { noColumn = true; name = "" }
    else {
      const c = readCache()
      if (c) {
        if (!name) name = (c.rows.filter(r => r.Event).sort((a, b) => String(b["date:Date:start"]).localeCompare(String(a["date:Date:start"])))[0] || {}).Event || ""
        rows = c.rows.filter(r => r.Event && r.Event === name)
        stale = true
      } else problem = friendly(err)
    }
  }

  const w = new ListWidget()
  const base = DASHBOARD_SHORTCUT ? "shortcuts://run-shortcut?name=" + encodeURIComponent(DASHBOARD_SHORTCUT) : URLScheme.forRunningScript()
  w.url = DASHBOARD_SHORTCUT ? base : base + (base.includes("?") ? "&" : "?") + "openTab=events"
  w.refreshAfterDate = new Date(Date.now() + 30 * 60 * 1000)
  const fg = Color.dynamic(new Color("#16201B"), new Color("#E7EDEA"))
  const muted = Color.dynamic(new Color("#5F6B65"), new Color("#95A39C"))
  const accent = Color.dynamic(new Color("#2F55C9"), new Color("#8EA6FF"))
  if (!fam.startsWith("accessory")) w.backgroundColor = Color.dynamic(new Color("#FFFFFF"), new Color("#171E1B"))

  const isOn = !!(st.active && st.active.name === name)
  const total = rows.reduce((a, r) => a + share(r), 0)
  const dates = rows.map(r => String(r["date:Date:start"] || r.Created || "").slice(0, 10)).filter(Boolean).sort()
  const from = isOn ? st.active.since.slice(0, 10) : dates[0]
  const to = isOn ? isoDay(new Date()) : dates[dates.length - 1]
  const span = from && to ? Math.max(1, Math.round((new Date(to + "T00:00:00") - new Date(from + "T00:00:00")) / 864e5) + 1) : 1
  const todayISO = isoDay(new Date())
  const todaySpend = rows.filter(r => String(r["date:Date:start"] || "").slice(0, 10) === todayISO).reduce((a, r) => a + share(r), 0)
  const cats = {}
  for (const r of rows) cats[r.Category || "Other"] = (cats[r.Category || "Other"] || 0) + share(r)
  const when = !from ? "" : isOn ? `since ${shortDay(from)}` : from === to ? shortDay(from) : `${shortDay(from)} – ${shortDay(to)}`

  // Lock screen
  if (fam === "accessoryInline") {
    w.addText(name && (rows.length || isOn) ? `${name} ${inr(total)}` : "No event yet")
    Script.setWidget(w); return
  }
  if (fam === "accessoryCircular") {
    w.addAccessoryWidgetBackground = true
    const img = symbolImage(name ? kindFor(name) : "tag", 12)
    if (img) { const row = w.addStack(); row.addSpacer(); const i = row.addImage(img); i.imageSize = new Size(13, 13); row.addSpacer() }
    const b = w.addText(name ? inrShort(total) : "–"); b.font = Font.boldRoundedSystemFont(14); b.minimumScaleFactor = 0.5; b.centerAlignText()
    Script.setWidget(w); return
  }
  if (fam === "accessoryRectangular") {
    if (name) iconLine(w, kindFor(name), name, 12, Color.white())
    else { const a = w.addText("No event yet"); a.font = Font.mediumSystemFont(12) }
    const b = w.addText(name ? inr(total) : "Start one in the dashboard"); b.font = name ? Font.boldRoundedSystemFont(20) : Font.systemFont(12); b.minimumScaleFactor = 0.6
    if (name) { const c = w.addText(`${rows.length} expense${rows.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}${when ? " · " + when : ""}`); c.font = Font.systemFont(12); c.lineLimit = 1; c.minimumScaleFactor = 0.8 }
    Script.setWidget(w); return
  }

  // Home screen: empty and error states
  const unknown = name && !rows.length && !isOn
  if (problem || !name || unknown) {
    iconLine(w, "tag", "Events & one-offs", 13, accent)
    w.addSpacer(6)
    const t = w.addText(problem || (unknown ? `Nothing tagged “${name}” yet.` : "No event yet."))
    t.font = Font.mediumSystemFont(14); t.textColor = fg
    if (!problem) {
      w.addSpacer(4)
      const s = w.addText("Start event mode in the dashboard's Events tab, or tap Mark as one-off on an expense.")
      s.font = Font.systemFont(11); s.textColor = muted
    }
    w.addSpacer()
    Script.setWidget(w); return
  }

  w.addSpacer()
  const top = fam === "small" ? w : w.addStack()
  const left = fam === "small" ? w : top.addStack()
  if (fam !== "small") left.layoutVertically()
  const barW = fam === "small" ? 110 : 130

  iconLine(left, kindFor(name), name, 13, accent)
  const status = left.addText(isOn ? `Event mode on${stale ? " · saved" : ""}` : `Ended${stale ? " · saved" : ""}`)
  status.font = Font.mediumSystemFont(10); status.textColor = muted
  left.addSpacer(4)
  const amt = left.addText(inr(total))
  amt.font = Font.boldRoundedSystemFont(26); amt.textColor = fg; amt.minimumScaleFactor = 0.6; amt.lineLimit = 1
  const sub = left.addText(`${rows.length} expense${rows.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}${when ? " · " + when : ""}`)
  sub.font = Font.systemFont(11); sub.textColor = muted; sub.lineLimit = 1; sub.minimumScaleFactor = 0.8
  left.addSpacer(8)
  left.addImage(barImage(cats, total, 300, 10)).imageSize = new Size(barW, 8)
  left.addSpacer(4)
  const cap = left.addText(isOn ? `Today ${inr(todaySpend)}` : `${inr(total / span)} a day`)
  cap.font = Font.systemFont(11); cap.textColor = muted

  if (fam !== "small") {
    top.addSpacer(18)
    const right = top.addStack()
    right.layoutVertically()
    right.addSpacer(4)
    Object.entries(cats).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 4).forEach(([c, v], i, arr) => {
      const r = right.addStack(); r.centerAlignContent()
      r.addImage(dotImage(CAT_COLORS[c] || CAT_COLORS.Other)).imageSize = new Size(8, 8)
      r.addSpacer(6)
      const n = r.addText(c); n.font = Font.systemFont(12); n.textColor = fg; n.lineLimit = 1
      r.addSpacer()
      const a = r.addText(inrShort(v)); a.font = Font.mediumSystemFont(12); a.textColor = fg
      if (i < arr.length - 1) right.addSpacer(6)
    })
  }

  if (fam === "large" || fam === "extraLarge") {
    // Spend per day of the event (the last 31 days if it ran longer).
    const days = Math.min(span, 31)
    const startDay = new Date(to + "T00:00:00"); startDay.setDate(startDay.getDate() - days + 1)
    const byDay = Array(days + 1).fill(0)
    for (const r of rows) {
      const d = new Date(String(r["date:Date:start"] || "").slice(0, 10) + "T00:00:00")
      const i = Math.round((d - startDay) / 864e5) + 1
      if (i >= 1 && i <= days) byDay[i] += share(r)
    }
    w.addSpacer(14)
    const dh = w.addText("BY DAY"); dh.font = Font.semiboldSystemFont(11); dh.textColor = muted
    w.addSpacer(6)
    w.addImage(dayBarsImage(byDay, days, days, 600, 80, accent, new Color("#8E8E93", 0.25))).imageSize = new Size(290, 38)
    w.addSpacer(12)
    const rh = w.addText("LATEST"); rh.font = Font.semiboldSystemFont(11); rh.textColor = muted
    w.addSpacer(6)
    for (const r of [...rows].sort((a, b) => String(b.Created).localeCompare(String(a.Created))).slice(0, 4)) {
      const row = w.addStack(); row.centerAlignContent()
      row.addImage(dotImage(CAT_COLORS[r.Category] || CAT_COLORS.Other)).imageSize = new Size(8, 8)
      row.addSpacer(8)
      const n = row.addText(r.Merchant || r.Category || "Expense"); n.font = Font.systemFont(13); n.textColor = fg; n.lineLimit = 1
      row.addSpacer()
      const a = row.addText(inrshare(r)); a.font = Font.mediumSystemFont(13); a.textColor = fg
      w.addSpacer(5)
    }
  }
  w.addSpacer()
  Script.setWidget(w)
}

// ---------- log an expense from a Shortcut ----------
// The shortcut asks for the details and runs this script with them (Scriptable › Run Script, "Run In App" off).
// Accepts a dictionary like { Amount, Category, Merchant, Notes, "Payment Method", Date },
// or the Notion request body an older shortcut used to send (its table ID is replaced with yours).
async function logFromShortcut(input) {
  let p = input
  if (typeof p === "string") { try { p = JSON.parse(p) } catch (e) { const n = Number(String(p).replace(/[^\d.]/g, "")); p = n ? { Amount: n } : {} } }
  const tok = Keychain.contains(TOKEN_KEY) ? Keychain.get(TOKEN_KEY) : ""
  if (!tok || !DATABASE_ID) return "Open the Expenses Dashboard once to connect Notion, then try again."
  let props
  if (p && p.properties) props = p.properties
  else {
    const get = k => { const key = Object.keys(p || {}).find(x => x.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z]/g, "") === k); return key != null ? p[key] : undefined }
    const amount = Number(String(get("amount") ?? "").replace(/[^\d.]/g, ""))
    if (!(amount > 0)) return "No amount, so nothing was logged."
    const cat = String(get("category") || "Other"), pay = get("paymentmethod") || get("payment"), day = get("date")
    props = {
      "Category Text": { title: [{ text: { content: cat } }] }, Category: { select: { name: cat } }, Amount: { number: amount },
      Date: { date: { start: day ? String(day).slice(0, 10) : istDay(Date.now()) } },
      Merchant: { rich_text: get("merchant") ? [{ text: { content: String(get("merchant")).slice(0, 200) } }] : [] },
      Notes: { rich_text: get("notes") ? [{ text: { content: String(get("notes")).slice(0, 500) } }] : [] },
    }
    if (pay) props["Payment Method"] = { select: { name: String(pay) } }
  }
  await notion(tok, "POST", "pages", { parent: { database_id: DATABASE_ID }, properties: props })
  const amt = props.Amount && props.Amount.number
  return amt ? `Logged ${inr(amt)}${props.Category && props.Category.select ? " · " + props.Category.select.name : ""}` : "Logged."
}

// ---------- run ----------

if (typeof args !== "undefined" && args.shortcutParameter != null && !config.runsInWidget) {
  let out
  try { out = looksLikeSms(args.shortcutParameter) ? await handleSms(args.shortcutParameter) : await logFromShortcut(args.shortcutParameter) } catch (e) { out = "Couldn't log it: " + friendly(e) }
  Script.setShortcutOutput(out)
} else if (config.runsInWidget) await runWidget()
else await runDashboard()
Script.complete()
